  > - It never copies `message`, `input`, free text or the input value. Path keys and rule names that do not look like names become `*` or `invalid`. It shows at most 5 issues and 8 path segments.
- fix: show instruction saves in history, credit the real writer, and speed up the agent tool-rule list (#19515)
  > - `agent-instruction-revisions.ts`: history merges the legacy revision rows with the `agent.files_updated` save records for the entry file. The order is `(created_at, id)` descending, and cursors keep PostgreSQL timestamp precision.
  > - `agent-instruction-revisions.ts`: the current revision takes its writer, run, source and time from the latest save of the same content hash. A reader is never the writer. Bytes with no save record have no writer.
  > - `agent-instruction-revisions.ts`: an identical re-save over bytes with no record is marked `source: "adopted"`. It repairs the runtime journal but is not listed as a save.
- fix(grok-local): copy SSH skill contents into the created directory (#22120)
  > - `packages/adapters/grok-local/src/server/remote-project-assets.ts`: after the script creates a skill directory, it now copies each child of the source skill into that directory. Before, it copied the skill directory onto itself. The copy keeps `force: false` and `errorOnExist: true`, so it still does not overwrite files.
- fix(tasks): require merged PR deliverables before native completion (#22244)
  > - Preserve delivery guidance in initial and follow-up task contracts.
  > - Verify non-archived PR work products and declared GitHub PR artifacts with the existing company-scoped resolver. Ignore agent-written merge status as proof.
  > - Reject open, draft, closed without merge, unavailable, and malformed declared PR outputs. Exclude review-comment artifacts from delivery checks.
- fix(ui): keep artifact previews in agent chat (#22252)
  > - Open sidebar document links on the current Chat route. Keep the requested revision and document owner, including links inside another task’s preview. Provide an **Open source task** link.
  > - Show one title when the document title matches the opening Markdown heading.
  > - Collapse the desktop chat list when an artifact tab would leave less than 420 px for the conversation. Keep **Browse chats** in the header with access to saved chat history. Restore the list when there is room.
- fix(recovery): keep a task's lock while its run's executor still settles it (#22248)
  > - `recovery/service.ts`: `sweepStaleIssueLocks` keeps an open task's lock while a run that holds it is still being settled by its executor, using the existing `terminalRunSettlingUntil`. That is a live execution in this process, or an unexpired controller lease in another one. The sweep reports the deadline through `onDeferredDeadline`, so it looks again once the executor is done. A closed task's lock is still cleared at once.
  > - Tests:
- fix(connectors): require an install before an agent reaches a plugin connector (#22237)
  > - `server/src/services/tool-access-policy.ts`: for an agent caller on a connection whose stored transport is `plugin`, deny with the new reason `deny_connection_not_installed` unless an install exists for that agent or the company. The check runs before profiles, grants, trust rules and allow policies, so none of them can replace the install. It reads the transport from the stored connection, not from the caller. The install read uses the decision cache, so a listing batch reads it once per connection.
  > - `packages/shared/src/types/tool-access.ts`: add `deny_connection_not_installed` to `ToolAccessReasonCode`.
  > - `doc/connections/CONNECTOR-PLAYBOOK.md`: document the rule.
- fix(native): stop retrying a run that filled its sandbox disk (#22239)
  > - Server: each run's diagnostic lease now records when a runner diagnostic reports a full disk (`ENOSPC`, `EDQUOT`, "No space left on device", "Disk quota exceeded"). The runner's exit detail already reaches the server as a diagnostic, so no runner change is needed. The fact belongs to that run's diagnostic epoch only. A later run on the same warm session, or a diagnostic after the epoch retires, does not see it.
  > - Server: native failure classification returns `sandbox_disk_full` when either (a) the run's lease saw a full-disk report, or (b) the error chain carries the runner's existing `sandbox_disk_full` launch code (a relaunch that cannot stage on the full disk). A fixed list of failures keeps its own meaning: permission, approval, usage limit, model, runtime, integrity, cleanup quarantine, ownership, saved-state and admission failures.
  > - Server: `sandbox_disk_full` is a permanent native failure. The run does not take another same-run attempt. Native safe replacement does not pick it up, because it is not one of its failure codes. The task becomes blocked with a board-owned recovery action and a plain-language next action.
- fix: restore master's typecheck and runner SDK input pin (#22242)
  > - The `startTurn` mock in the new test uses a local call counter instead of reading its own `mock.calls`.
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.json`: refresh the pin for `packages/shared/src/app-definitions/github.json`, which #22234 changed without updating it. The Runner support suite fails on master without this.
- fix(runner): keep Claude MCP bearer tokens out of process arguments (#22206)
  > - For the Claude ACPX profile, each HTTP MCP header now has the value `Bearer ${PAPERCLIP_ACPX_MCP_BEARER_<n>}`.
  > - The runner puts each token in the Claude launch environment under the same name. Claude Code expands `${VAR}` in `--mcp-config` when it connects to the server.
  > - Other ACPX agents keep the literal header. Their CLIs do not have a known `${VAR}` expansion.
- fix(connections): explain failed app sign-ins and guide GitHub app install (#22234)
  > - The connection-intent sign-in page now posts a `code` field with a `failed` outcome. Only codes that match `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,63}$` are sent. Connected and declined outcomes send no code.
  > - Both OAuth callback catch blocks pass their failure code to that page.
  > - OAuth state errors now carry codes: `oauth_state_expired`, `oauth_state_missing` and `oauth_session_mismatch`.
- fix(runner): continue a turn lost to a mid-run runnerd restore (#22235)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/native-session-runtime.ts`: the execution records the turn id of the turn it starts. `resumeInterruptedTurn` now accepts two owners of a turn lost on restore:
  > - All other guards stay: one continuation per run, the provider allowlist, no compaction or goal runs, no pending runtime requests, and the snapshot must show that same turn as restart-interrupted.
  > - `native-session-runtime.test.ts`: a new regression for an ACPX session that loses its own turn to a mid-execution `runnerd` restore, plus a negative case for a lost turn this execution never started.
- fix(native): let the outgoing server checkpoint its idle warm session while its follow-up has not started (#21887)
  > - `native-runner-checkpoint.ts`: the parked-follow-up check is now `unstartedSuccessorOfOwner`. It takes `FOR SHARE` on the follow-up's finalization row (if any), then on its run row, in that order, inside a savepoint. The claim and defer paths use the same order. If native selection created the finalization row while the fence waited for the run lock, the fence rolls the savepoint back, which releases the run lock, and takes both locks again in the same order (at most twice). A last statement then reads the current state again and accepts one of these states:
  > - These conditions apply to every state and are unchanged: running; no own process-start event; no own checkpoint rows. For the native states, also: same company, task and native session; `attempt = 0`; no result; no process, unless it is the identity inherited from the outgoing run (`inheritedProcessIdentityRunId`). The fence still requires an accepted result and no new work.
  > - A `waitingForOwner` that names another run or boot is still refused.
- fix(issues): show tasks held by a resolved no-replay hold as Blocked (#22215)
  > - `server/src/services/issue-display-status.ts`: An agent task with lifecycle status `in_progress` now shows `blocked` when it has a recovery record with these properties:
  > - For these records, `executionBlockerPredicate` is always true and `restoreHoldReleasedForCurrentOwnerPredicate` is always false. Thus "Blocked" never shows for a task that wake admission would wake. The exceptions of the full predicate apply only to legacy holds, legacy workspace-restore holds and conversation boundaries. Those keep their current display.
  > - The full admission predicate is not used in the projection. I tried it first, and list queries became 3 to 4 times slower, because every list, search and count query includes this projection.
- fix(railway): report restricted environments instead of a consent error (#22214)
  > - `server/src/services/railway.ts`: When the target query fails with an authorization denial, the client reads the project's environments with `canAccess`. If the requested environment has `canAccess: false`, the client throws `railway_environment_restricted` (HTTP 403) with the admin-access remedy. In all other cases, the client throws the original error. If the access check fails, the client also throws the original error. The access check is a read query. No mutation runs after a denied target check.
  > - `server/src/services/railway.ts`: `list-environments` now returns `canAccess` for each environment, so agents can choose an accessible environment before they call a target tool.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/railway.test.ts`: Adds a regression test for the restricted, accessible, and unknown cases.
- fix(connections): name an expired sign-in in connection search (#22211)
  > - `server/src/services/connection-intents.ts`: connection search reads the grants of each matching connection. When a grant in the caller's audience (the organization, the responsible user, or the agent) has status `needs_reauthorization`, and no grant in that audience is active, the reason names that connection. The reason says that the connection must be signed in again, and that a person who manages it must reconnect it on its app page.
  > - The search uses the same audience rule that protects indexed tool descriptions. A grant of another person does not show the name of that connection.
  > - The state, `connectionId`, and all access checks do not change.
- fix(native): back off and retry a provider rate limit that reports no reset (#22216)
  > - `readNativeUsageLimitReset` (server, native runtime) now gives a `rate_limit` turn failure without a reported reset an estimated reset. The estimated reset is the failure time plus `PROVIDER_LIMIT_DEFAULT_COOLDOWN_MS` (one hour). This is the same wait that already holds the agent's new work after the same failure, so both holds end at about the same time.
  > - The reset carries an `estimated` flag. The hold text says that the provider did not report the reset, and it gives the time when Paperclip will retry.
  > - A `quota_exhausted` failure without a reported reset does not change. It still waits for a person, because an exhausted allowance does not pass by itself.
- fix(native-runtime): resolve assigned tool names to the authorized alias (#22213)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/assigned-mcp-tools.ts`: add `resolveAssignedName`. It accepts the exact alias. If the value is not an alias, it looks for tools whose gateway name or display name is equal to the value. It resolves only when exactly one tool in the session's pinned catalog matches in either field. It trims surrounding white space.
  > - Search with `schemaTool` uses the resolved alias. The fresh-grant and work-mode filters still apply to the result.
  > - `paperclip_call_assigned_tool` uses the resolved alias. An unknown name returns `assigned_mcp_tool_unknown: name must be an exact name returned by paperclip_search_assigned_tools. Search assigned tools again and use the returned name.`
- fix(tools): keep audit summaries valid jsonb when the cut lands inside an emoji (#22207)
  > - `server/src/services/tool-content-guards.ts`: `summarizeToolValue` now cuts long summaries with a small `cutWholeCharacters` helper. If the last kept code unit is a high surrogate, the helper drops it. The summary is still at most 4000 code units. The `sha256` and `sizeBytes` values do not change, because they use the full serialized value.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/tool-content-guards.test.ts`: add a regression test. It puts an emoji across the cut point and checks that the summary has no lone surrogate.
- fix(adapter-utils): wait for a replacement GitHub broker after a controller restart (#22209)
  > - `packages/adapter-utils/src/github-launcher.ts`: Broker-file launchers retry for up to 60 seconds. Before each retry, they read `broker.json` again. A replacement broker is used when the restored controller publishes it.
  > - The launcher retries two failure types in this wait: a refused or failed connection, and an HTTP 503 answer. With HTTP/2, the sandbox gateway can continue to listen after its controller connection closes. It then answers `503 {"error":"bridge_unavailable"}`.
  > - HTTP 401 and 403 answers are real denials. The launcher does not wait for them.
- fix(monitors): let low-trust reviewers schedule a self-wake on their own task (#22212)
  > - `assertCanManageIssueMonitor` accepts an optional own-task scope. With this scope, a `deny_low_trust_boundary` result for `runtime:manage` does not block the assignee when both the requested monitor and the stored monitor are the assignee's own self-wake (`wake_owner` recovery, scheduled by the assignee, not the provider-quota service) or are empty.
  > - The native `set_task_monitor` tool passes this scope only when the agent, project and task policies alone resolve the same low-trust boundary as the current run. A monitor wake starts a run from those sources, not from the run snapshot. Thus the woken run keeps the same low-trust limits. A low-trust boundary that only the run snapshot carries is still denied.
  > - The tool still checks `issue:read` and `issue:mutate` on the target first, and still requires an in-progress or in-review task assigned to the calling agent.
- fix(runner): fail fast when an artifact storage read stalls (#22210)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/paperclip-runner-tool-authority.ts`: the artifact branch of `#readApiFile` now uses a 60-second `AbortSignal.timeout`. It sends the signal to `storage.getObject`. It destroys the stream when the deadline expires. Every storage stream gets an error listener before it can be destroyed, so a late reply cannot raise an uncaught `AbortError`. It returns HTTP 504 with code `artifact_storage_timeout` and the message "Artifact storage read did not finish within 60 seconds. No file was written; retry the same download."
  > - A new optional binding field, `storageReadTimeoutMs`, sets the deadline for tests. The default is 60 seconds. The production callers do not change.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/artifact-transfer.integration.test.ts`: a new regression test covers a storage response that never arrives and a stream that stops after one chunk.
- fix(runner): keep current task requests readable in OpenCode traces (#21584)
  > - Select task identity and prompt for known native envelopes. Put pending user messages first in continuation excerpts.
  > - Omit only a valid leading generated handoff packet. Keep plain text and unknown formats on the existing path.
  > - Add a relative run-events reference under the prompt capture switch. Readers must sign in to the source instance and page to the matching turn.
- fix(github): let a queued push start the next review turn instead of a blocker continuation (#22223)
  > - `status-arbiter.ts`: new rule before the blocker continuation lanes. When a blocked run has a queued chat request for the same task and agent, the decision is `preserve` with only `release_checkout` (reason `native_blocker_superseded_by_queued_request`). The normal release drain then starts the queued request. A pause hold or a board-owned blocker keeps the existing behavior.
  > - `native-runtime/queued-chat-request.ts`: new `hasQueuedChatRequest` query. It uses the same chat-wake test that keeps chat wakes out of first turns (`chatConversationId` or `externalChatProvider`).
  > - `native-run-finalizer.ts`: supplies `hasQueuedChatRequest` to the arbiter, only for a blocked report.
- fix(ui): keep GitHub identity widgets out of replies (#22217)
  > - Omit a view from the reply index only when its upstream tool is `get_me` and its resource is `ui://github-mcp-server/get-me`.
  > - Apply the filter before the per-run widget limit. This covers saved history and live replies through their shared index.
  > - Add tests for repeated saved identity calls, widget pagination, and other GitHub and app views.
- fix(native): retry a fresh launch whose provider pack could not be verified (#22106)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`:
  > - `doc/native-workspace-finalization-recovery.md`: describe the fresh-launch retry.
  > - Tests: the disposition retries attempts 1 and 2 of a fresh launch and stops at attempt 3, and the existing reattachment hold test is unchanged. An integration test shows `bootstrap_retry` for a fresh launch with no checkpoint, and `ambiguous_state` without `bootstrap`.
- fix(recovery): wait out a sandbox the provider could not start, with no Retry (#22194)
  > - Plugin SDK (`environment-creation-cleanup.ts`, `environment-acquire-error.ts`, `index.ts`): new `PluginEnvironmentAcquisitionError`. It carries the acquisition diagnostic (step, elapsed time, budget, provider state) for a failed allocation that the provider already removed, with no cleanup envelope. The acquire RPC error data (`paperclip/environment-acquire-error/v1`) now carries this diagnostic. `readEnvironmentAcquisitionDiagnostic` reads it from that envelope. The existing cleanup envelope path is unchanged. No provider text crosses the RPC.
  > - Daytona plugin (`plugin.ts`): `destroyFailedCreation` returns the state of the sandbox that it deleted. When a create fails without an HTTP status and the deleted sandbox was in the `error` state, the plugin throws `PluginEnvironmentAcquisitionError` with `providerState: "error"`. A `build_failed` sandbox (a deterministic snapshot build failure) and any other state keep the original error.
  > - Server (`environment-runtime.ts`): the acquire path records the diagnostic for this run also when the error has no cleanup envelope. A cleanup envelope for another run, company or environment still records nothing.
- fix(ui): clarify dashboard proposal hierarchy (#22180)
  > - Put area changes and their controls in the area heading.
  > - Nest responsibility rows under each area. Label additions, rewords, moves and removals.
  > - Keep the drop/keep dependency behavior and the existing apply payload.
- fix(connectors): activate plugin connectors with agent grants and no sign-in (#22201)
  > - `server/src/services/tool-access.ts`: `connectGalleryApp` writes an active agent grant for `subjectAgentId` when a curated method with `auth` `none` or `api_key` uses `grantKind: "agent"`. The API key goes on that grant, not on the connection. A resume without `subjectAgentId` keeps the agent of the existing grant and keeps its saved key. A second agent on the same connection is a 409 conflict. The grant write is audited and joins the existing revive rollback.
  > - Finish does not create agent grants. Finish carries no credential, and it can list many agents while a per-agent connection has one identity. A credential-less grant would also claim a connected OAuth identity that does not exist. The grant comes from connect (or the OAuth callback). Finish creates only the install.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-preparation.ts` and `native-runtime/runtime-context.ts`: include the `plugin` transport in the runner assignment and in its digest (the two lists must match).
- fix(ephemeral): save retained runtime skill bundles at startup and never strand a volume-free server on lost ones (#22190)
  > - `runtime-skill-preservation.ts`:
  > - `runtime-skill-preservation-reference.ts`: the scan matches only null pins, which is exactly what preservation copies. A non-null pin is a version pin and cannot become an obligation that nothing can settle.
  > - `file-manifest-cache.ts`: new `inspectFileManifest` returns `verified`, `missing` or `changed` from the same walk. `verifyFileManifest` wraps it.
- fix(auth): preserve identity across connection request handoffs
- fix(routines): preserve the manual caller during execution dispatch (#22177)
  > - Add an optional responsible user to the assignment wake helper. Other callers keep their current behavior.
  > - Pass the user already selected by routine dispatch into the wake context. Keep the dispatch lock through queue admission.
  > - Add a regression that runs another person's routine with the caller's selected agent through the real heartbeat service. Check both queued and completed execution identity.
- fix(ui): forward unprefixed links to Connectors, Chat, Inbox and Dashboard (#22100)
  > - `ui/src/App.tsx`: add `dashboard`, `apps`, `apps/connect`, `chats` and `inbox` to the routes that use `UnprefixedBoardRedirect`.
  > - `ui/src/App.deep-link-routing.test.tsx`: add a test that renders the real `<App>` route table. It checks each new path. It checks that a connect link keeps its query string and opens the connect page. It checks that a connect link with no connector goes to the Connectors list. It checks that a path that is not registered stays unresolved.
- fix(native): never rest a task blocked on the agent's own blocker (#22140)
  > - `native-environment-blocker.ts`: a system-owned blocker now uses the bounded fresh-session environment lane for any reason code, except the person-only codes `permission_denied`, `credential_missing`, `policy_restricted` and `budget_exhausted`. An agent-owned blocker with `environment_unavailable` or `tool_unavailable` also uses it. The lane is unchanged: at most 2 retries on the shared failure counter, then a board blocker.
  > - New `native-agent-blocker.ts` and a new arbiter branch for a task-wide blocker that is not person-owned, from the current owner's own succeeded run (not a chat, not a review):
  > - `native-provider-capacity-retry.ts`, `status-decision-committer.ts`, `native-run-finalizer.ts`: materialize the continuation through the existing fresh-session retry lane (`forceFreshSession`, `retryOfRunId`, 30 s delay, refuses a stopped run). Both new reason codes need the provider-failure owner recheck under the issue lock. The finalizer reads the pause hold for every blocked result.
- fix(recovery): wait out a sandbox provider outage with no Retry (#22139)
  > - `server/src/services/environment-provider-wait.ts` (new): the `environment_provider_wait` retry lane. The delays are 1 min, 2 min, then 5 min. The lane stops at 30 minutes after the wait started, with at most 12 attempts. It also builds the task text "Waiting for "<environment name>" to come back. Paperclip continues this task automatically when it does."
  > - `scheduleBoundedRetryForRun` (`heartbeat/retries.ts`): when a run fails with `environment_provider_unavailable` after the fast retries are spent, it schedules the next attempt in the wait lane. The retry context records the environment and the time the wait started. The time is kept across attempts. The lane is uncharged (`execution-recovery-attempt.ts`), so it does not spend the failure budget. Other lanes drop the wait context.
  > - The environment orchestrator puts the environment id on `EnvironmentProviderUnavailableError`, and the setup-failure path saves it in `resultJson.environmentProvider`.
- fix(restart-chaos): exempt a continuation of an uncommitted follow-up run from completion_once (#22137)
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/follow-up-delivery.ts`: add `continuedRunId` (reads `retryOfRunId` from the run context or the wake payload) and `followUpAnsweringRuns`. The new function returns the runs that answered a follow-up, plus each continuation of an answering run that did not commit a `done` result.
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/staging-mix-retry.ts`: `evidenceCompletion` and `runsAfterDone` use `followUpAnsweringRuns` to set `answersFollowUp` and to separate answered runs from extra turns. `followUpsAnsweredLater` and the wait statistics are not changed, so one follow-up still counts as one answer.
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/wake-evidence.ts`: `completionEvidence` (the record that the wave grade uses) uses the same function.
- fix(runner): accept a new controller when the listener's controller goes silent (#22088)
  > - `AuthenticatedTransport` records when it last read any frame (text, ping, or pong).
  > - On a listener-mode transport, a control read returns a transport error when no frame arrived within `DurableRunnerConfig::controller_silence` (default 90 s). The Runner then takes its existing disconnect path: it records a diagnostic, closes the socket, and accepts the next controller within its reconnect grace. A dial-mode transport is unchanged.
  > - The silence rule applies only to steady-state control reads (`receive_json` and `receive_queued_json`). The welcome read during connection setup keeps its own deadline and timeout message, and the silence window restarts once the welcome validates, so a slow welcome is never reported as controller silence (which, after `auth_response`, would fail a bootstrap closed).
- fix(recovery): continue a setup failure after work in seconds, with no Retry (#22128)
  > - `continuation-cause.ts`: export `SETUP_FAILURE_CODES` (`setup_failed` and the transient setup codes).
  > - New `fresh-continuation-eligibility.ts`: the held-record and eligibility predicates move here from `fresh-continuation-fallback.ts` with no behavior change (`heldExecutionWhere`, `interruptionEligibility`, `recentInterruptionSourceSql`, the backlog age constants, and the Blocked-ownership check the fallback uses). The fallback re-exports the public names. The fallback file goes from 992 to about 900 lines.
  > - Eligibility: the backlog pass now continues a system-owned setup failure of a run that did provider work (`executionRecovery.providerWorkStarted` is not `false`) whose files are held for saved-work recovery or were recovered by it. A failure before provider work, a person-owned code, and an exhausted saved-work recovery keep their Retry.
- fix(native): retry transient provider refusals while reattaching a handed-off run (#22118)
  > - `environment-run-orchestrator.ts`: the exact-lease resume during reattachment goes through `retryTransientResumeCall`. The first call keeps the provider's configured start deadline. A first call that used up that deadline (plugin RPC TIMEOUT) is not repeated. After a transient refusal, a 90 s budget (`REUSABLE_SANDBOX_RESUME_BUDGET_MS`) bounds each later wait and passes the remaining budget as `timeoutMs`. A confirmed missing sandbox and a definite refusal are not retried.
  > - `packages/plugins/sdk/src/environment-resume-error.ts`: owns one retry policy for provider failures: `isRetryableEnvironmentHttpStatus` (408, 425, 429, 500, 502-504 and gateway/edge 520-527, 529, 598, 599; never 501 or 505) and `isRetryableEnvironmentErrorCode(code, scope)`. ABORT_ERR is never retried. ENOTFOUND is retried only in the `known_host` scope (an exact reattach to a host this run already reached, the DNS-outage case). Lease acquisition keeps its canonical semantics in the `first_acquisition` scope.
  > - `environment-provider-unavailable-error.ts`: lease acquisition uses the SDK policy instead of its own sets.
- fix(native): keep recovery workers off a run its live heartbeat is finishing (#22101)
  > - Add `server/src/services/native-runtime/live-native-finalization.ts`. It keeps an in-process reservation for each run whose live heartbeat is still running.
  > - The heartbeat (`runtime-execution.ts`) reserves a native run before it dispatches. It releases the reservation when its execution ends. The release wakes both worker queues for that run.
  > - `withNativeWorkspaceFinalizationOwnership` returns `owner_active` to every caller except the run's own heartbeat while the run is reserved. The workspace finalization worker already waits for a release notification on `owner_active`.
- fix(server): keep chat titles current and space verification retries (#22102)
  > - Update matching generated chat titles in the agent configuration transaction. Preserve custom chat titles, ordinary task titles, and other agents' chats.
  > - Record each changed chat as `issue.updated` and publish its event after commit so open pages refresh. Roll back the rename and queued events if audit persistence fails.
  > - Derive new chat titles from the current agent name under the existing company transaction lock. Both user-opened and proactive chats use this path, including a create that read the old name before a rename committed.
- fix(ssh): support all Cloud SSH harnesses in remote task workspaces (#22078)
  > - Use the existing shared adapter capability registry for durable SSH task admission. Keep company, lease, writer, and workspace authority checks with their current owners.
  > - Share the controller workspace check across adapters. Skip local access for authoritative remote workspaces. Default runtime preparation to no workspace copy in that mode. Preserve local and copy behavior.
  > - Keep the local ACP relay in the controller state directory. Do not create the remote task directory or write Claude project settings on the controller. Preserve the previous Claude settings fingerprint calculation.
- fix(recovery): continue a recovered preparation that failed with a transient code (#22096)
  > - `failed-preparation-retry.ts` (new): `isTransientWorkspacePreparationCode` and `failedPreparationOwnsBoundedRetry` move here unchanged from `heartbeat/retries.ts`, which re-exports them. The code list is now an array, so SQL can use it. The move lets `cancelled-native-startup.ts` use the predicate without an import cycle, because `retries.ts` already imports that file.
  > - `cancelled-native-startup.ts`: `isInterruptedNativePreparationCandidate` also accepts a failed preparation that has a required workspace recovery and whose own bounded retry owns it. All other conditions stay the same: legacy Runner, still preparing, no provider evidence, released leases with stop receipts, and a verified workspace repair before replay.
  > - `workspace-recovery.ts`: the recovery sweep lists the same runs, so their continuation is delivered after the files are saved.
- fix(daytona): retry lease acquisition after a provider connection failure (#22087)
  > - The Daytona plugin wraps a `DaytonaConnectionError` from lease acquisition in an error with code `CONNECTION_FAILED`, and a `DaytonaConnectionTimeoutError` with code `ETIMEDOUT`. The message is unchanged. It reads the SDK class name only, never message text.
  > - The plugin SDK closed diagnostic set accepts `CONNECTION_FAILED`, so the code crosses the worker RPC.
  > - The host treats `CONNECTION_FAILED` as a transient connection failure. The run becomes `environment_provider_unavailable` and uses the existing bounded bootstrap retry (30 s, 60 s) and the automatic-run brake.
- fix(server): give a slow remote more time to exclude agent files from Git (#22039)
  > - `agent-instruction-files.ts`: add `excludeRuntimeFromRemoteGit`. Each attempt gets 30 seconds. A timed-out attempt is retried, up to three attempts. Any other failure throws at once. The error keeps the closed `reason=` code from #21499.
  > - `agent-directory-working-copies.ts` and `agent-instruction-working-copies.ts`: both remote call sites use the helper, so the private instruction copy also names a reason.
  > - `execution-target.ts`: `runSshCommand` rejects a command its timeout killed with `code: null` and `killed: true`, but only `ETIMEDOUT` was treated as a timeout, so an SSH timeout was thrown instead of returned. A killed command now returns `timedOut: true`, as the sandbox path already does.
- fix(server): name why agent files could not be excluded from task Git staging (#21499)
  > - `agent-instruction-files.ts`: add `instructionGitExcludeFailureReason`. It maps the result to a closed reason: `timed_out`, `unsafe_directory`, `unsafe_file`, a Node error code such as `enoent` or `eacces`, `node_unavailable`, `signal_<name>`, `exit_<code>` or `unknown`.
  > - `agent-directory-working-copies.ts`: append `(reason=<code>)` to the existing error message.
  > - The reason never copies sandbox output, paths or user names.
- fix(runner): keep a disk reserve so a full sandbox disk does not strand the runner (#22068)
  > - New `server/src/services/native-runtime/remote-runner-disk-reserve.ts` owns the reserve: hold/release scripts, the full-disk classifier, and `createRemoteRunnerDiskReserve`. A release waits for an in-flight hold, and no hold starts after a release.
  > - Both remote runner launchers (agent runs and Dot) get a reserve path in managed sandboxes only: `<workspace>/.paperclip-runtime/paperclip-runner/disk-reserve`. Workspace saves and Git already exclude `.paperclip-runtime`. SSH hosts get no reserve.
  > - After the first launch's identity is confirmed, the launcher holds 256 MiB in the background, only while 2 GiB more is free. It is off the startup path.
- fix(ssh): support ACP with durable Cloud workspaces (#22063)
  > - Admit managed SSH ACP with an in-place workspace in both adapters.
  > - Keep ACP's session directory and fingerprint bound to the remote task directory.
  > - Stage private runtime assets with workspace copying disabled. The remote workspace owner still saves task files.
- fix(posthog-proxy): forward the visitor IP behind a private proxy hop (#22052)
  > - Add `posthogVisitorIp()` in `server/src/routes/posthog-proxy.ts`.
  > - Use it for the forwarded `X-Forwarded-For` header and for the per-visitor in-flight limit.
  > - Add tests for the helper and for the forwarded header.
- fix(ui): skip the GitHub star prompt for established companies (#22031)
  > - Record eligibility from the first successful completed-task read. First reads at five or more do not qualify.
  > - Save that decision per company in browser storage. Preserve eligibility across reloads and company switches.
  > - Keep the existing cloud, onboarding, task-focus, retry, other-dialog, and one-time display guards.
- fix(native): post the agent's reply when the result commit worker finishes the run (#22043)
  > - The result commit worker no longer passes `skipNativeResponsePresentation`. After it commits and projects the run, it runs the same idempotent reply presentation step that the live heartbeat and the retry paths use.
  > - The presentation worker stays the retry owner. If the inline attempt fails or is refused, the run stays a presentation candidate, as before.
  > - Review responses are still left to their own worker (`skipNativeReviewResponse` is unchanged).
- fix(ui): make OpenRouter setup models searchable (#22034)
  > - Enable the existing searchable picker for multi-provider and routed models.
  > - Add seven setup tests with the real model picker. Cover filtered selection, saved IDs, custom IDs during catalog errors, the OpenCode routing prefix, single-provider routing, and a linear work limit for 600 models.
- fix(tool-gateway): preserve safe remote MCP error messages (#22025)
  > - Decode percent/Base64 credential-bearing spans before reflecting provider error prose. Keep this capability in the existing submitted-value redactor, shared with HTTP error responses, rather than duplicating MCP-specific secret policy.
  > - Add one MCP message helper that uses the existing redactors. Remove known credentials, sensitive request header and query values, and sensitive arguments before truncation.
  > - Limit the upstream message to 1,000 characters. Remove control characters and label the text as untrusted. Use the generic fallback for missing or malformed messages.
- fix(ssh): allow CLI agents on volume-free servers (#21957)
  > - Reuse remote workspace preparation, archives, saved versions, and single-writer admission for SSH targets.
  > - Add an explicit file-sync capability to the existing SSH command runner. It streams archive files with bounded deadlines and private temporary files. Ordinary SSH runners keep their previous capabilities.
  > - Run the existing CLI adapter in the remote workspace. Bind both the target and its SSH command specification to that directory.
- fix(ui): keep automated work out of personal recents (#22024)
  > - Add a viewer participation field to bounded navigation summaries. Reuse the canonical Mine query and existing access checks. The field is a read projection; no database migration is needed.
  > - Show only confirmed personal tasks. Remove old nonparticipants after a successful read. Do not trust participation from local storage or full task caches.
  > - Keep status and title snapshots current without changing personal order. Explicit local edits and comments can advance recency.
- fix(apps): show the selected ChatGPT workspace on permissions (#22023)
  > - Add a provider module for OpenAI's Codex accounts endpoint. Match the saved workspace ID in both supported response formats. Do not choose the first or default account.
  > - Use the existing credential audience, company access, audit, timeout, and response-size controls. Keep provider IDs, bearer tokens, and other workspace names out of the response.
  > - Enable the OpenAI subscription account lookup on Permissions. Keep the saved email and account controls when the lookup fails. Skip API keys and routed connections.
- fix(google-workspace): request free/busy with read & write Calendar, and update connector docs (#22026)
  > - `packages/shared/src/google-workspace-connectors.ts`:
  > - Counts: default all-read-and-write selection is now 10 scopes (was 9); all read-only stays 8; the union of all 27 selections stays exactly the 14.
  > - Tests (written first and seen failing on `master`):
- fix(heartbeat): record environment drift once for plugin-owned workspaces (#21947)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/workspaces.ts`: `resolveExecutionWorkspaceConfigFreshness` takes an optional `providerOwnedStorage` flag. With the flag, `environment` drift does not require replacement. Other replacement-class drift (repo, strategy, mode, project workspace, realization) still gives `replace`.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/workspace-preparation.ts`:
  > - `doc/heartbeat-internals.md`: describe the provider-owned storage rule.
- fix(ui): clean up routines controls and folder loading (#22009)
  > - Remove the selection control, its unused bulk mode, and the repeated folder summary.
  > - Use icon buttons with accessible names for Sort and Group. Keep them beside search on small screens.
  > - Load folder data before the initial folder list appears. Keep cached rows visible during a folder refresh. Show folder read errors.
- fix(ui): keep connector permission forms responsive (#22008)
  > - Show 50 actions per group at first. Keep full-list search and bulk changes. Keep direct links to hidden actions usable.
  > - Mount each test dialog only when its Test button is used. Memoize action rows and catalog groups.
  > - Show pending permission choices at once. Restore saved values after an error. Update query data from the save response before the next edit.
- fix(google): keep Paperclip's Google client inside the 14 submitted scopes (#22001)
  > - `packages/shared/src/google-workspace-connectors.ts`: add `GOOGLE_WORKSPACE_SUBMITTED_SCOPES` (the 14, written out by hand). `workspace.all` now uses it. `isGoogleWorkspaceScopeGrant` checks against it. Add `googleWorkspaceProfileWithinSubmittedScopes`.
  > - `server/src/services/paperclip-cloud-connector.ts`: add `paperclipCloudConnectorConsentAllowed`. `startAuthorization` refuses a Google profile outside the 14 before any broker call. `appWithPaperclipCloudConnectorAvailability` does not offer those methods for new setup, even when the broker advertises them.
  > - `authorization-start.ts`: a saved connection on such a profile cannot restart consent (`paperclip_cloud_connector_scope_not_offered`, 422).
- fix(auth): stop holding a signed-in user's requests on an unchanged Cloud tenant sync (#21997)
  > - `resolveCloudTenantActor` reads the user's memberships first. When the stored primary membership is active and has the requested role, the request does not wait for the tenant write sync. The sync finishes in the background, a failure is logged, and idle and drain checks count it until it settles.
  > - A new user, a role change, a status change or a failed membership read still waits for the sync, as before.
  > - New PostgreSQL regression test: another transaction holds the user's rows, and an already-synced user still authenticates in under 3 s with the same companies and role. The existing grant-repair assertion now waits for the background repair.
- fix(tool-gateway): screen MCP resource reads for prompt injection; prove Google Workspace results are screened (#21999)
  > - `server/src/services/tool-gateway.ts`: `namedGatewayContextProtocol` runs `validateToolContent` (block mode, with the company's optional model check) on `resources/read` results. A block writes an audit row and returns 422 `prompt_injection_blocked`. An allowed read returns the guard's redacted value with URI rewriting on top, the same value a tool call delivers, so text hidden from the model check never reaches the agent. `resultClassifier` now takes only the tool name and provider type it uses.
  > - `server/src/routes/tool-gateway.ts`: both named-gateway context routes use `cancellableRequest` and pass the request signal, so a client disconnect cancels the resource read's model check.
  > - New `server/src/__tests__/google-workspace-injection-screening.test.ts`: injected email, document and event content through the `google-workspace` connection on each path. Benign email passes.
- fix(native): report a Runner caught mid-exit as not running (#21987)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/remote-runner-recovery-diagnostics.ts`: in `REMOTE_RECOVERY_PROCESS_CHECK_SCRIPT`, when the command line lacks the runner id, is empty, and the saved process generation matched, the check polls `/proc/<pid>/stat` and `kill -0` every 50 ms for up to 1 second. It exits 45 when the process becomes `Z` or `X`, or is gone. Otherwise it exits 48, as before.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/remote-runner-recovery-diagnostics.test.ts`: two regression tests. A process of the saved generation with an empty command line and state `R` becomes a zombie, or is reaped, while the check runs: the check exits 45. The existing cases are unchanged: a live process with an empty command line (state `S`) still exits 48, and without a saved generation a zombie still exits 48. The helper timeout goes from 2 to 5 seconds, because the unchanged empty-command-line case now waits 1 second.
- fix(native): read "as the file reads" as a comparison, not a file request (#21983)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-deliverable-feedback.ts`: in `explicitlyRequestsFileOutputBySentence`, a comparison to an existing file is not a file request. A comparison needs all of these: the object is a generic container noun ("file", "files"); it comes right after "as the"; and it is followed by an optional adverb (actually, really, still, previously, currently, now, already) and a verb ("reads", "read", "is", "are", "was", "were", "appears", "looks", "stands", "says", "shows", "exists"). A format or file name (CSV, PDF, JSON, output.json) is never a comparison. Past-participle "read" followed by "by", "from" or "into" describes the requested output and is not a comparison. "Is / was [adverb] named, called or titled" after the object still marks a destination; both patterns share one adverb list.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-deliverable-feedback.test.ts`: comparison sentences that must not require a file: the exact chaos-task sentence, "as the file reads", "as the files are", "as the file actually / really reads", "as the file read before the last update", "as the file reads from the log". Destination and description sentences that must still require a file: "as a file", "as the file named ledger.csv", "as the file is named / called / currently titled / actually named ledger.csv", "as a CSV that is compatible with Excel", "as a PDF that is printable", "as output.json that is valid JSON", "as a file read from the log", "as the CSV read by the dashboard", "as the file read by the dashboard", "as the CSV is described above".
- fix(runner): tell a provider its accepted result is final when it reports again (#21984)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/contracts/completion-result.ts`: replace the bare `PRP_SEMANTIC_RESULT_CONFLICT_MESSAGE` with `PRP_RESULT_ALREADY_ACCEPTED_MESSAGE`. `prpSemanticResultConflictMessage` returns it for every committed result that is not a blocker. The blocker messages share the same stop instruction, which now names both `paperclip_finish` and `paperclip_block`, and ask for extra notes in the final response.
  > - The OpenCode, Codex and Codex ACPX drivers already return `prpSemanticResultConflictMessage(...)`, so all three get the new text with no driver change.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/test/fixtures/fake-opencode-server.mjs`: add a `finish-after-finish` scenario. After the accepted `paperclip_finish`, the fake provider sends an identical repeat, a changed `paperclip_finish` and a late `paperclip_block`.
- fix(chaos): confirm a task that rests just before its deadline (#21979)
  > - `TaskObserver.poll` now skips the deadline timeout while a rest candidate began at or before the deadline and has not yet had REST_CONFIRM_MS.
  > - Added three tests in `observer.test.ts`: rest just before the deadline passes, a running task times out, and a task that starts resting after the deadline times out.
- fix(native): finish a failed warm session's cleanup by itself and continue the task (#21977)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: the remote checkpoint resolves its backup owner from the current scope authority, and falls back to the backend's own authority epoch when the scope has none. The controller-persistence `authorityRunId` closure uses the same fallback. A newer run that took over the session still wins.
  > - `paperclip-runner` `native-session-runtime.ts`: new `nativeSessionCleanupRemoteScopes` lists the remote resources whose quarantined cleanup holds a task. New `completeTerminatedRemoteNativeSessionCleanupForTask` retires settled quarantines of the task in one terminated remote resource, whichever run's session they hold. An in-flight close keeps its entry.
  > - `remote-execution-termination.ts`: new `terminatedRemoteCleanupScope` confirms that the company's newest lease on the remote resource is released with its termination receipt and that no lease on it is still held.
- fix(native): continue a run that reports a system environment blocker (#21959)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-environment-blocker.ts`: new lane constants and `isNativeEnvironmentBlocker`. A blocker qualifies when its owner kind is `system` and its reason code is `environment_unavailable` or `tool_unavailable`.
  > - `status-arbiter.ts`: for the current owner's environment blocker (not a chat, not paused), schedule a fresh-session retry (`native_environment_blocker`). After 2 retries on the shared failure counter, bind a board-owned blocker (`native_environment_blocker_exhausted`) with a clear next step. Other blockers keep their current behavior.
  > - `native-run-finalizer.ts`: compute the environment-blocker fact from the accepted result, only for a succeeded run that the task's owner still holds and that nobody stopped. Both new reason codes require the provider-failure owner recheck under the issue lock.
- fix(ui): scope the inbox badge to the signed-in user (#21953)
  > - Read `deskBadgeCount` from the newest attention snapshot for the signed-in user.
  > - Observe the same user-scoped For you feed as the Inbox page.
  > - Scope summary and task-index cache entries by user. Wait for the user ID before reading personal counts.
- fix(server): name the blocking database sessions whenever a run's event writes stall (#21956)
  > - New `server/src/services/run-intake-stall-watch.ts`. It keeps an in-memory list of pending run operations: event receipts (`PaperclipControlPlanePort.appendEvent`), event writes (`appendHeartbeatRunEvent`, `appendHeartbeatRunEventBatch`), and process identity writes (`persistHeartbeatRunProcessMetadata`).
  > - When an operation stays pending for 30 s, the watch logs one warning per stalled run. The warning has: the pending operations and their age, the pending count and oldest age per run, the pool size, the database sessions per application name and state, the lock waits and holders (`pg_blocking_pids`, query without literals, cut to 160 characters), and the event-loop lag. Reports are at most one per 30 s. A second line logs when a reported operation finishes.
  > - The watch arms a timer only while an operation is pending. It reads the database only when it reports, through the private liveness pool with a 5 s statement timeout.
- fix(ci): skip reviewed-lock checks for a pending Dependabot lock in the Runner support suite (#21942)
  > - `.github/workflows/pr-trusted.yml`: the `verify_runner_e2e_support` job sets `PAPERCLIP_CI_PR_AUTHOR` from the pull request author, like the general test job.
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/prepare-report-lock.test.ts`, `source-sdk-evidence.test.ts`, `source-sdk-public-inputs.test.mjs`: the test cases that need a reviewed committed lock skip when `PAPERCLIP_CI_PR_AUTHOR` is `dependabot[bot]`. This uses the same check as `server/scripts/reporting-lock-policy.test.mjs`. The other cases in these files still run.
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/vitest.config.ts`: on a Dependabot pull request, the suite excludes `source-sdk-public-type.test.ts`. Its shared `beforeAll` prepares the reviewed SDK reference, so every case in the file needs a reviewed lock.
- fix(issues): release a done blocker whose ended run already finalized (#21948)
  > - `pendingFinalizeBlockerCondition` (`server/src/services/issues.ts`) has one more release rule. The latest blocker operation is settled (`succeeded`, `failed` or `skipped`). Its heartbeat run is terminal. That run recorded a successful `workspace_finalize` for the blocker issue on the same workspace (or with no workspace id). Readiness, the queue dependency gate, blocker attention and the periodic backstop all read this condition.
  > - Live runs keep the old behavior. A save that ends after its finalize, or an operation that starts after it, still holds dependents while the run is running.
  > - Foreign-run operations, unfinished operations and failed finalizes with no success still hold dependents.
- fix(heartbeat): name the blocking database sessions when reattached runs are never reached (#21946)
  > - `reattach-phase.ts`: when the reattach phase ends at its cap and some runs authenticated but were never reached, the server reads `pg_stat_activity` with `pg_blocking_pids` on the private liveness pool. It logs the waiting sessions, their blockers, long transactions, and the dedicated connection stats.
  > - The read uses a 5 s statement timeout and a pool that a stalled main pool cannot starve.
  > - Test: an embedded Postgres test holds a run row lock, starts a second waiting transaction, ends the phase at the cap with an authenticated run, and checks that the log names the holder and the waiter.
- fix(ci): review the instance settings cache reset in the server test setup (#21943)
  > - `scripts/ci/vitest-selection-graph.mjs`:
  > - `scripts/ci/test-selection.integration.test.mjs`: the fixture copies the cache module with the reviewed setup files. It also checks that a change to the cache module selects every test.
- fix(connections): retire satisfied connection cards on waiting tasks (#21940)
  > - `server/src/services/satisfied-connection-intents.ts`:
  > - `server/src/__tests__/satisfied-connection-intents.test.ts`: two new cases, described below.
- fix(tasks): confirm Stop after preparation settles (#21906)
  > - Complete the Stop receipt in `settleCancelledLegacyPreparation`. Keep its existing checks for process, session, events, leases and saved files.
  > - Require no saved `budgetDispatch.handedOffAt` marker under the run lock. This marker can exist before an adapter records its start. A stale controller must keep the recovery hold after this handoff.
  > - Write and require the `preparation_settled` proof before clearing the failed-attempt notice. Both page queries use one bounded SQL projection. Unknown and foreign proof labels keep the notice.
- fix(release): retry the finalize install while a large tarball lags its npm metadata (#21925)
  > - `scripts/release-lib.sh`: `wait_for_npm_installable` retries `verify_npm_installable` up to N attempts with a fixed delay.
  > - `scripts/confirm-canary-publication.sh`: uses it with 40 attempts × 30 s (`NPM_INSTALL_VERIFY_ATTEMPTS`, `NPM_INSTALL_VERIFY_DELAY_SECONDS`).
  > - `.github/workflows/canary-finalize.yml`: `confirm_canary` timeout 45 → 75 min, to fit the existing 30-minute visibility budget plus the 20-minute install budget.
- fix(acpx): only treat an error result as a Claude sign-in failure (#21921)
  > - `patches/@agentclientprotocol__claude-agent-acp@0.73.0.patch`: port [agentclientprotocol/claude-agent-acp#1103](https://github.com/agentclientprotocol/claude-agent-acp/pull/1103) (commit [`6e97e07`](https://github.com/agentclientprotocol/claude-agent-acp/commit/6e97e071c581002b43d9556dbd1d1376ebf9516f), released in 0.81.1). It has two parts, the same as upstream:
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/acpx/acpx-runtime-adapter.claude-login-text.test.ts`: a new test. It runs the real bridge and the bundled Claude Code against a local API stand-in. The model's reply quotes the sign-in hint, and the test expects the turn to complete.
- fix(runtime): keep managed GitHub access across a controller restart (#21928)
  > - `createNativeGitHubAccess` takes an optional `launcherKey`. The launcher directory is `native-github-<sha256(key)>`, so the same provider session and task always get the same directory.
  > - The native session executor passes a key built from `nativeSessionScopeKey` and the issue id.
  > - `prepareGitHubOperationLaunchers` takes an optional `broker`. It then stages broker-file launchers and an owner-only `broker.json`. The launchers already read `broker.json` on each operation in this mode (`githubLauncherSource({ brokerFile: true })`). A restaged directory therefore gives the running provider the new endpoint.
- fix(native): pipeline tool progress events with text deltas (#21533)
  > - `paperclip-control-plane-port.ts`:
  > - `contracts/control-plane-port.ts`: optional `pipelinedEventTypes`, and the `streaming` option documents that it applies to those types.
  > - `native-session-runtime.ts`: the runtime pipelines the port's `pipelinedEventTypes`, and only `item.delta` when the port does not name them.
- fix(runner): reject only the overflow call when parallel tool calls exceed the result reserve (#21913)
  > - `provider_bridge.rs`: the two `begin_call` branches that fail only because of the per-call result reserve now return a new retryable error (`tool_calls_in_flight`). The provider sees "Too many tool calls are in flight. This call was not executed. Wait for the pending tool results, then retry it." The branches that set `durable_run_receipt_limit_reached` (real held bytes, exact identity capacity, saturated replay history) still stop the turn.
  > - `provider_backend.rs`: the invalid tool call result sets `retryable` from the error, so this rejection is `retryable: true`. Other rejections stay `false`.
  > - `native-tool-receipt-limit.ts` (new): reads the persisted, source-bound `harness.diagnostic` event with code `semantic_tool_turn_receipt_limit` for the run, and holds the lane constants and the hint for the next session.
- fix(wake-queue): keep the child-completion reason when a person's comment merges into its held wake (#21918)
  > - `admitWakeBehindIssueExecution` records `wakeReasons` in the merged held-wake context when two different reasons merge. `wakeReason` keeps the latest reason, as before, so comment delivery and person-wake handling do not change.
  > - New `withMergedWakeReasons` helper in the wake-queue domain context module.
  > - `hasPendingNativeChildCompletion` matches a held wake when `wakeReason` is `issue_children_completed` or when `wakeReasons` contains it.
- fix(dispatch): start work on a task tree a person created in the person tier (#21916)
  > - `queuedRunDispatchClass` puts a run with `personTask` in the person tier. System recovery stays in the recovery tiers, also on a person's tree.
  > - `personTask` now means "the task, or a task above it, was created by a person". The existing `createdByUserId` marker is the source.
  > - New `listPersonTreeIssueIds`: one recursive query per dispatch pass. It walks each queued task's parent chain through the `(company_id, id)` unique index. It stops at the first person-created task, at most 32 levels. It runs only when the agent has more than one queued run. The start-history read uses the same query in place of its old direct `createdByUserId` read, so the query count does not change there.
- fix(heartbeat): keep saved sessions across config edits the provider thread cannot see (#21862)
  > - New `server/src/services/heartbeat/session-fingerprint-inputs.ts`. It classifies each fingerprint input as kept or excluded, with the reason:
  > - The session fingerprint no longer hashes these inputs:
  > - Unknown adapter and runtime config keys are still hashed. A missing classification can only keep the old reset behavior; it cannot skip a reset.
- fix(wake-queue): reopen a finished task for a person's comment merged into a system wake (#21914)
  > - `getCommentSelfAuthorship` in the wake-queue Postgres adapter also returns `includesPersonComment`. This value is true when one of the wake's undelivered comments was written by a person and not by an agent run.
  > - `promoteDeferredWake` reopens a done or cancelled task when `includesPersonComment` is true, also when the wake requester is the system.
  > - The delivered-comment filter is not changed. A comment that a run already received does not reopen the task.
- fix(runner-e2e): keep the supervisor off the heartbeat graph and ship the plugin SDK build (#21860)
  > - Moved `NATIVE_RETAINED_OWNER_WAIT` and `NATIVE_AGENT_DIRECTORY_BUSY_WAIT` to a new leaf module `native-recovery-wait-codes.ts`. `native-controller-recovery.ts` re-exports them, so other importers do not change. `native-runner-checkpoint.ts` imports the leaf. This also removes a checkpoint/controller-recovery import cycle.
  > - Added `tests/runner-e2e/server-startup-import-graph.test.ts`. It uses esbuild to walk the static import graph of `tests/runner-e2e/server.ts` and fails with the full chain if it reaches `@paperclipai/plugin-sdk`.
  > - `runner-full-stack-e2e.yml`: the "Build reusable runner campaign artifacts" job runs `scripts/ensure-plugin-build-deps.mjs` (the same certified helper the cell uses), checks that `shared` and `plugin-sdk` dist and their completion markers exist with a clear `::error::`, and ships both dist trees in the campaign bundle. The cell restore step checks both entry points with a clear `::error::`. The helper's fingerprint receipt lets the cell reuse the shipped output instead of a rebuild.
- fix(recovery): keep a running run visible when a dropped continuation is newer (#21907)
  > - `task-interruption-presentation.ts`: the latest-run candidates skip a run that is `cancelled`, never started (`startedAt` is null), and has error code `recovery_no_longer_needed` or `issue_closed_before_start`. The filter is in each per-arm `limit 1` index scan, so the query shape and cost stay the same.
  > - `recovery/service.ts`: `hasActiveExecutionPath` also counts a `native-status-committer` wake in `queued` or `claimed` with no run for the issue. The native status wake dispatcher owns that wake, so the sweep does not queue a second continuation and does not escalate the task while it waits.
  > - Tests (red on master, green here):
- fix(ui): keep new Recents tasks ready to open from cache (#21904)
  > - Track loaded task IDs for each query client, company, and account.
  > - Check the ten top task references when Recents changes.
  > - Load only new tasks after the page reads finish and the browser is idle.
- fix(ui): align recent task status icons and move fold controls right (#21859)
  > - Remove the left offset from parent task rows.
  > - Put the fold control in a separate right slot.
  > - Center the child guide below the parent status slot.
- fix(recovery): stop an agent configuration write from re-checking every task of the company (#21861)
  > - `run-dispatch-notifications.ts`: split the dispatch queue hints out of `notifyRunDispatch` and add `notifyAgentConfigurationDispatch`. It sends the company-level recovery hints and the dispatch hints, but no task-queue hint.
  > - `issue-recovery-notifications.ts`: add `notifyCompanyRecovery`, which signals only the company-level recovery queues (orphan blockers, active recovery actions, task watchdogs).
  > - `agent-configuration.ts`: a configuration write uses `notifyAgentConfigurationDispatch` instead of `notifyRunDispatch`.
- fix(ui): add a space after composer autocomplete (#21754)
  > - Add one ordinary space in the shared completion path for all seven option types.
  > - Keep final prose spaces through Markdown import with the editor's space entity. Restore ordinary spaces before saving.
  > - Place the cursor after the separator in plain text and in Lexical text elements.
- fix(evals): compare browser zones by clock, wait for the reloaded profile name, bind accounting reads by returned run (#21760)
  > - `internal-routine-audit.ts`: the browser override check compares the UTC offset of the requested and reported zones, now and 182 days later, instead of the zone names. An unknown zone or a different clock still fails setup.
  > - `identity-profile.ts`: after reload, `settledFieldValue` waits up to 15 s for the display name to reach the saved value, then records what the person sees. The grade still fails a name that does not arrive.
  > - `ProfileSettings.tsx`: unedited fields show the session's saved name and image from the first frame. Before, a session restored from the saved read cache rendered an empty name until an effect filled it.
- fix(native): stop a finished warm owner's retained Runner when suspend is refused, and release a suspended Runner whose process left (#21853)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/remote-runner-recovery-diagnostics.ts`: `REMOTE_RECOVERY_PROCESS_CHECK_SCRIPT` exits 48 (`process_runner_id_missing`) for a live process whose command line lacks the runner id. Exit 44 now means only an identity file mismatch.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/sandbox-runner-port-holders.ts`: `retireSandboxRunnerPortHolders` takes `only: { sessionDirectory, runId }` to retire one session's Runner. New `sandboxSessionRunnerExited` and `inspectSandboxSessionRunner` (a read-only `node` script).
- fix(native): release an idle warm owner whose sandbox Runner port a later run took over (#21835)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: new `warmRunnerPortTakenBySuccessor`, used by `warmCloseFailureProvesOwnerGone` after the existing sandbox-termination proof. It holds only when another run has all of these:
  > - When the proof holds, the idle-timer path and the controller-restart path log "warm native session's Runner or sandbox is gone; released its owner" and forget the owner without a checkpoint. Both paths already call `warmCloseFailureProvesOwnerGone`.
  > - `validateNativeRunnerCheckpointOwner` does not change. A checkpoint still cannot land under a live successor.
- fix(auth): report effective Cloud owner access to the board UI (#21817)
  > - `/api/cli-auth/me` uses the trusted Cloud actor's computed `isInstanceAdmin` value for `cloud_tenant` requests.
  > - Session and board-key requests retain fresh stored-role checks. No roles are persisted and no authorization gates or owner settings change.
  > - Regression tests cover Cloud elevation and de-elevation, non-Cloud stored access, and rejection of anonymous/agent callers. Tailscale documentation explains Cloud owner access.
- fix(server): release sandbox leases for runs stopped before runner attach (#21788)
  > - `releaseEnvironmentLeasesForRun` (`run-control.ts`):
  > - `nativeWorkspaceExportPendingForLease` treats a cancelled run with no runner launch evidence as owing no export.
  > - Safety net: `sweepOrphanedActiveLeases` (the existing hint- and deadline-driven orphan-lease work) now retries the run's own release for a due deferral or for a cancelled native run, after the native owner lease has expired. The orphan-lease loader uses the stored `retryAt` as the due time. No new timer or poller.
- fix(workspaces): use project storage for a fresh retry after unowned task storage (#20756)
  > - `server/src/services/plugin-workspaces.ts`: the early `return null` for a previous workspace that no provider owns no longer applies to a fresh attempt. The fresh attempt then uses the project's storage provider, as for a task with no bound workspace.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/plugin-fresh-workspaces.integration.test.ts`: new test "prepares provider storage for a fresh retry that follows a workspace the provider does not own".
- fix(ui): keep the active company on Cases and Status navigation links (#21794)
  > - `ui/src/lib/company-routes.ts`: add `cases` and `status` to `BOARD_ROUTE_ROOTS`, so `applyCompanyPrefix("/cases", "PAP")` returns `/PAP/cases`. Remove the part of the `caseHref` doc comment that described the old behavior.
  > - `ui/src/lib/company-routes.test.ts`: unit test for both roots (red before the fix, green after).
  > - `tests/e2e/cases-read-only.spec.ts`: hold `GET /api/companies` until the Cases link is clicked, then require the URL `/<PREFIX>/cases`. Without the fix the URL is `/cases`, so the test fails every time instead of sometimes.
- fix(native): let the outgoing server checkpoint its idle warm session while the incoming follow-up waits for it (#21791)
  > - `NativeControllerRecoveryWaitingError` carries the owner that the follow-up waits on: the predecessor run and its controller boot. `waitForLiveWarmSessionOwner` and the live-owner wait in `inspectRemoteWarmSessionRecovery` set it.
  > - `deferNativeControllerRecovery` stores that owner in the finalization `failureDetail.waitingForOwner`. The executor passes it through.
  > - `validateNativeRunnerCheckpointOwner` accepts one more successor kind: a parked follow-up waiting on this exact owner. All of these must be true, or the fence still refuses:
- fix(runner): leave an undispatched tool call to the next controller on restart detach (#21786)
  > - `durable-prp-control-plane.ts`: new `SemanticToolHandedOffError`. When the dispatcher rejects with it, the control plane journals no result. The input stays unsettled for the next controller.
  > - `runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: the semantic tool callback goes through `#dispatchSemanticToolInput`. If the call ends as not dispatched after the transport detached for restart, it throws `SemanticToolHandedOffError`. A normal close still journals `semantic_tool_not_dispatched`.
  > - `runnerd-codex-transport-orphaned-tool-at-attach.test.ts`: the test waits for the exact stage instead of the committed input. It runs two cases: the call is in its handler at detach, and the call is held before dispatch across the detach. Both then check the existing attach recovery and the final `completed` result.
- fix(native): release an idle warm session whose Runner is proven gone, and retry a failed idle close (#21782)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.test.ts`: three tests.
- fix(native): keep a warm follow-up that inherited its predecessor's process identity waiting on the same run (#21774)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-controller-recovery.ts` (`deferNativeControllerRecovery`):
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-controller-recovery.integration.test.ts` (real Postgres):
- fix(qa): schedule recovered cleanup promptly and wait for task panels (#21660)
  > - Use one shared first-attempt predicate for the cleanup deadline, sweep selection and ownership report. It now includes recovered workspace cleanup bound to the same run.
  > - Preserve worker spacing, in-flight claims, ownership and saved-file checks, retry delays and attempt limits.
  > - Wait for panel controls after opening the panel. Wait for the selected tab or menu action to finish.
- fix(github): clarify configured review task completion (#21748)
  > - Add shared guidance within the GitHub prompt module for configured completion at 5/5 and finite discussion replies.
  > - Require a complete assessment, confirmed publication, and the current head in the 5/5 guidance.
  > - Preserve explicit CI, merge, and below-5 tracking rules. Keep the shared status arbiter and check thresholds unchanged.
- fix(issues): cover a blocked issue whose own run is queued after its blockers finish (#21770)
  > - `server/src/services/issues.ts`: in `readIssueBlockerAttentionBatch`, when a blocked root has no unresolved blockers, the function now checks the active-issue set that it already built. If the root has its own queued or running run (or an active wake), it returns `covered` with `blockingTreeLive: true`. Otherwise it returns `needs_attention` as before.
  > - The change adds no query. The active-issue set already includes the roots, through their `executionRunId` and their wake requests.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/issue-blocker-attention.test.ts`: two tests.
- fix(server): turn agents' made-up-host Paperclip links into relative routes (#21762)
  > - New `server/src/services/agent-invented-host-links.ts`. It rewrites an absolute link to a relative route only when all of these are true:
  > - Markdown link targets keep their place. Bare links and autolinks become Markdown links, so they stay clickable.
  > - `issueService.addComment` applies it to agent-authored comments, before the redaction and dedupe steps, so a retried identical comment still deduplicates. The run-presentation check still compares the text that the agent wrote. People's comments are not changed.
- fix(runner): keep closing tool-call tags and model control tokens out of OpenCode chat (#21761)
  > - `tool-call-markup.ts`: closing DSML tags (`</｜DSML｜`, `</|DSML|`) count as tool-call markup.
  > - `tool-call-markup.ts`: new `controlTokenStart` finds DeepSeek, ChatML and Llama control tokens (for example `<｜begin▁of▁sentence｜>`, `<|im_start|>`, `<|eot_id|>`) outside code. A new `visibleBeforeModelScaffolding` holds back both kinds while text streams.
  > - Sentinels inside inline code spans are ignored, as fenced code already was.
- fix(queue): keep a message a starting run's first turn took out of the queue (#21747)
  > - `activityService.runsForIssue` returns `firstTurnCommentIds` from the run's `firstTurnQueuedInput` receipt. The field is set only when the receipt names this run, because a retry copies its predecessor's context.
  > - `IssueDetail` adds those ids to the run's start inputs, so the messages are placed in that run and are not inferred as queued.
  > - `RunForIssue` type gets the new optional field.
- fix(comments): keep saved authorization cards from blocking replies (#21744)
  > - Validate generated connection authorization payloads before either insert or update. Use a stable SHA-256 target identity from the existing connection/user idempotency key. Bound its display label.
  > - Read older confirmation custom keys and labels without the new-input length limits. Preserve their exact identities. Keep target types, URLs, document revisions, and resolver checks strict. New requests still use the strict shared schema.
  > - During current and historical comment supersession, keep an invalid saved request pending, report its ID and schema locations, and continue with valid siblings. Database and other unexpected errors still propagate.
- fix(server): wake a delivery task unscoped when it cannot apply settled hints (#21686)
  > - `delivery-work-coordinator.ts`: a settled event goes through `applySettled(hints)`. It tries `onSettled(hints)`. If that throws and hints were given, it tries `onSettled(undefined)` one time. Each failure logs `Delivery settlement could not be applied for <queue>` with `process.emitWarning`. Then `wake()` runs.
  > - Unscoped means `onSettled(undefined)`. Each registered task already defines what that means. For example, scoped deadline queues, dispatch work, muse maintenance and the revocation index mark `*` dirty. Counter-style tasks set their request flag. `chat-action-work` does nothing. The fallback is bounded: it calls `onSettled` at most twice per event, it does not recurse, and it does not schedule another settlement. So it cannot loop.
  > - `work-signals.test.ts`: a new test pins one rule of #21681 for the local path and the remote path. An unhinted `signalDatabaseWork` in the same transaction as a hinted one gives a settlement that is scoped to the hinted set. The owner of `work-signals.ts` confirmed that this is deliberate. The repository does not allow code comments in pull requests, so this sentence and the test document it.
- fix(evals): repair four cohort-15 chat cases and prefix board links in completion feedback (#21682)
  > - `projects.share-project` (`internal-privacy.ts`, `runtime-support.ts`): after the harness follows the agent's link, it waits until the URL settles on the project's canonical route key. This reuses the #21667 approach for agent profiles: `settleAgentProfileRoute` now calls a shared `settleRouteKey`, and `projectRouteKey` follows the app's `projectRouteRef`. The owner's picker selection (`addProjectAccessMember`) now retries as one unit. If the dialog or picker closes, or an option detaches, it reopens them, searches again and selects again. It continues only when the picker shows the person and Add is enabled.
  > - `skills.author-skill` (`integrator-skills.ts`): removed the keyword-regex checklist grader, the waiver regexes, and the reply regexes for "global activation" and "attached to me" claims. The saved-state checks stay: one agent-created writable skill, its current revision authored by the responding agent, the same bytes on the skill and the revision, the Skills/Studio inspection with reload, and assignment scope from saved assignments and audit rows. The checklist check now requires the setup literals `375`, `WCAG` and `AA` as whole tokens in the saved SKILL.md and in the rendered editor. Whether the skill still asks for keyboard-only navigation and visible labels, and whether it waives a check, is left to human review. Oracle version `skill-authoring.v10`.
  > - `inbox-search.show-decisions` (`internal-decision-search.ts`): removed all reply regexes, including the `Riley` requirement and the wording-based separation checks. The grade now uses saved state (pending decisions unchanged, answered history unchanged, each person's own attention queue and resolver) and rendered links to the exact approval, the question's task and the history task. Oracle version `decision-search.v3`.
- fix(recovery): continue a task on its own when its saved files are permanently lost (#21541)
  > - `fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`: the eligibility rule (`interruptionEligibility`, which the fallback and the backlog pass share) also accepts a system-owned source run when a hold of that run has saved-work recovery `exhausted` with a permanent unverifiable-source failure (`UNVERIFIABLE_SOURCE_CODES` × `PERMANENT_SOURCE_RETRY_CLASSES`).
  > - `recovery/assigned-work-sweep.ts`: admission can settle the hold and coalesce the sweep's wake into a pending fresh continuation. The sweep now reports this as `repaired` / `queued` (`continuedFromSavedWork`). Before, it reported `enqueue_refused` / `not_started`.
  > - `legacy-execution-recovery.ts`: the stranded sweep calls `terminalizeLegacyExecution` for the same failed run on every pass. Its restore-hold branch rewrote the hold every time, with a new `automaticRecovery.recordedAt`, a new `updatedAt`, and `continuationDelivery: "invalidated"`. The branch now returns early when the hold already records the same no-replay decision for this run, or when a fresh continuation took the hold over. Finding: a continuation that was already recorded was not clobbered, because the existing `executionReconciliation.runId` guard returned early. But a hold with a `coalesced` delivery state would have been reset, and the churn kept bumping `updatedAt`.
- fix(recovery): continue a native run cut off by a sandbox client timeout (#21736)
  > - `server/src/services/execution-recovery-resolution.ts`: the disposition pass also selects an active watchdog hold with a restart-class cause (`RESTART_CLASS_FAILURE_CODES`) for a terminal native run with no retry pending, unless the safe replacement lane owns that run. It hands the run to `continueFreshInTransaction` and records the fallback's result on the hold, so a run that does not continue is checked again only when due.
  > - `server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`: the fallback treats an active watchdog hold with a restart-class cause as its own. When it continues the task, it settles that hold and unblocks the task, as it does for reconciliation holds.
  > - New red/green test `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-client-timeout-continuation.integration.test.ts`. It drives the real native executor with a provider turn that starts and then fails with the Daytona client-timeout error.
- fix(runner): stop a turn that keeps resending a refused tool call and continue fresh (#21734)
  > - `repeated-tool-failure-guard.ts`: counts refusals per exact call and per run. At 6 refusals of one call, or 20 refusals in the run, it throws `ToolCallLoopError` (`code: "agent_tool_call_loop"`, tool, counts and argument names, never values). The message tells the model the turn stops and gives a short hint.
  > - `paperclip-runner-tool-authority.ts`: `scheduleToolCallLoopContinuation` writes one idempotent native status-wake outbox row (key `agent_tool_call_loop:<runId>`) with `forceFreshSession: true` and the hint as the wake instruction. The outbox dispatcher admits it with `allowRunCoalescing: false`, so it never merges into a deferred comment wake or the active run. An assignee run uses `issue_continuation_needed`. A completion-review run uses `native_completion_review` with its review assignment, which the dispatcher revalidates, so the fresh reviewer session keeps the review.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: the session authority epoch queues that continuation when the guard raises the loop error. The governed-wait lookup finds the wake on the next tool event and ends the provider turn as `yielded` with a `same_agent` continuation, the same way the connection-tools refresh ends a turn.
