If the wake cannot be queued, the refusal still reaches the model and the error is logged.
- fix(issues): count a blocker covered when its wake already created a queued run (#21731)
  > - `server/src/services/issues.ts`: the blocker-attention wake query left-joins `heartbeat_runs` on the wake's `runId`. A queued or deferred wake counts as an active path when its `runId` is empty or when its run is `queued` or `running`.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/issue-blocker-attention.test.ts`: two tests. One covers a child whose wake created a queued run that has not started. One makes sure that a wake whose run already finished does not cover the blocker.
- fix(ui): let Request changes on a plan always take a revision note (#21721)
  > - `TaskChatCompactInteractionCard`: `collectsRejectReason` is true for any plan confirmation (issue_document target with key `plan`). The note label shows "(optional)" when a reason is not required, and the reject button stays enabled with an empty note.
- fix(db): recover prepared statements that a pooler or schema change invalidates (#21718)
  > - `packages/db/src/prepared-queries.ts`:
  > - `packages/db/src/client.ts`, `packages/db/src/index.ts`: a client and its dedicated, liveness, checkpoint, file-storage and listener pools share one state. `preparedStatementStats(db)` returns the prepared, unprepared, demoted and retried counts.
  > - `packages/db/src/prepared-queries-postgres.test.ts`: nine tests against embedded PostgreSQL: restart after a stale plan and after a lost statement (the callback runs once and its later work commits), `repeatable read read only` kept after a restart for driver and ORM transactions, no restart after a later statement, after a tagged first statement, or for an out-of-order await, a savepoint whose first statement fails while the outer transaction continues once, the pooled retry with `.values()`, and the cap.
- fix(restart-chaos): credit a follow-up only to a run that started a provider turn, and post a missed follow-up after the run ends (#21692)
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/wake-evidence.ts`: record `providerTurnStarted` for each run. It is true when the run has a `turn.submitted`, `turn.accepted` or `turn.started` run event.
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/follow-up-delivery.ts`: a run with `providerTurnStarted: false` does not receive its comments. The comments carry to the next run that starts a provider turn. That run is the answering run. Add `undeliveredFollowUpRuns`. Evidence without the field keeps the old behavior.
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/staging-mix-retry.ts`: `runsAfterDone` does not list a run that failed before it could deliver a follow-up as an extra turn.
- fix(chat): park GitHub terminal responses that wait for a newer run or task change (#21705)
  > - `server/src/services/chat-channels/github/terminal-response-work.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/chat-channels/github/terminal-response-work.test.ts` (new, real PostgreSQL), with staging-shaped fixtures: 30 tasks with 4 waiting responses each (codes null 89%, `acknowledgement_unconfirmed` 10%, `acknowledgement_superseded` 1%), and a newer ended run per task that answered a later request:
  > - `server/src/__tests__/chat-channels.integration.test.ts`: `finds owed terminal GitHub work beyond a full page of unrelated request history` now expects the 101 retained requests to carry a wait key in addition to their code.
- fix(heartbeat): settle deferred chat wakes that can never resume instead of retrying every pass (#21678)
  > - `heartbeat/queue.ts`: the stranded-queue sweep no longer touches `updated_at` on chat-inbound wakes. Non-chat saved wakes keep the touch. It moves `updated_at`, which spaces the worker's 30-second deadline for wakes with an execution wait, and a board chat continuation (`ai-personal-connection-recovery` e2e) depends on that spacing. The chat branch now records the outcome of each resume. The log line now has `reason`, `outcome` and `attempts`. Unscoped sweeps skip a wake whose stored next attempt time is in the future.
  > - `durable-chat-wakeup.ts`: add `recordDeferredChatWakeDecline`, `clearDeferredChatWakeRetry` and `deferredChatWakeDeclineForError`.
  > - `chat-channels/inbound/wakeups.ts` (the deferred chat wake owner): for the recovery sweep only, return a decline instead of a silent return or an authority error:
- fix(native): retry a coordinator claim that loses a deadlock instead of failing the run (#21703)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: the coordinator claim transaction runs inside `retryTransientLockContention` (`40P01`, `40001`, `55P03`; 5 attempts, 100 ms × attempt backoff). Each attempt re-reads and re-proves the coordinator, the run, cancellation and lineage, so a retry admits nothing that the first attempt would refuse.
  > - A startup context that a rolled-back attempt opened is finished, and the startup owner's previous context is restored, before the next attempt.
  > - New PostgreSQL test `native-coordinator-claim-deadlock.integration.test.ts`: `retries a claim that lost a deadlock and reaches the provider`.
- fix(native): wait for a live outgoing server before resuming its warm session in a deploy overlap (#21697)
  > - `remote-warm-session-recovery.ts`: new `waitForLiveWarmSessionOwner`. It reads the finished run's finalization owner. While that server is alive inside the warm window, the follow-up defers with the existing `native_controller_recovery_waiting_for_owner` wait. A harness backup that holds the suspended state and was completed after the turn proves that the owner finished its close, so the follow-up continues at once.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: calls that wait before the `suspended` check, so every warm follow-up on another server's session passes through it. The backup check runs only when the owner is alive.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: when a resume has a durable session but no receipt from a harness backup or the admission, it reads the provider artifact receipt recorded on the session's last run. The new Runner then gets the admitted launch root, as a cold continuation already does.
- fix(server): release settled native run process identity at graceful shutdown (#21689)
  > - `releaseOwnedSettledNativeRunProcessesAtShutdown` (`native-local-process-stop.ts`): one read selects at most 500 settled native runs whose `process_host_hash` is this server's PID space and that pass the existing release rules (settled coordinator phase, a checkpoint or provider evidence, no sandbox lease, no pending local lease, a matching `native.process_identity_recorded` event from this host). The server checks that each PID and process group is absent. One statement in one transaction then clears the fields and appends the `native.local_process_stopped` receipt with the original identity. The update checks every rule and the exact recorded identity again, so a changed row is not released.
  > - The transaction sends the same work signals as the per-run release (execution replacement, live-run recovery, retained session cleanup, accounting, waiting instruction copies). It sets a 2 s `statement_timeout`.
  > - `notifyInstructionCopyStopEvidenceForRuns` (`instruction-collection-notifications.ts`): the batch form of `notifyInstructionCopyStopEvidence`, with the same waiting-copy predicate.
- fix(ui): keep a saved task when its plan, run or work-product read answers 404 (#21684)
  > - `ui/src/lib/persisted-query-cache.ts`: a chat read revokes the task on 401 or 403, or on 404 only for the recent comments. The task record keeps revoking on 401, 403 and 404.
  > - `ui/src/lib/saved-task-chat.test.ts`: a 404 for a missing plan, active run or work products keeps the task and its saved chat.
  > - `doc/ui-internals.md`: the read-cache rules state which 404s revoke.
- fix(queue): send a message into a starting run without a cancel or recovery errors (#21661)
  > - `deferredWakeJoinsFirstTurn`: a system or unattributed wake qualifies. Each message must still be the responsible user's own (`firstTurnEligibleComment`). A wake from another user or an agent keeps its own delivery.
  > - Task interruption presentation: find the Interrupt queue by its recorded `queuedCommentInterrupt` intent, not by `requestedByActorType = user`. The hand-off now shows as working for a merged queue too.
  > - Queued-comment read: while a deferred queue carries an Interrupt intent, report an `interrupt_handoff` wait instead of the recovery message.
- fix(evals): grade five cohort-14 chat cases by saved state and clicked links (#21667)
  > - `projects.share-project` (`internal-privacy.ts`): removed the reply regex. The grade now requires (a) a rendered link to the project's configuration page (where Manage access lives) that the harness clicks and that opens the Manage access control, and (b) saved state with no agent-made access grant: Riley is absent from the membership before the owner acts, and no agent `project.access_member_added` or visibility `project.updated` activity exists. The owner step still completes when the link is missing, so later phases keep their evidence.
  > - `teams.install-team` (`external-team-conditional.ts`): removed `namesTeamSkill`. The case contract is "reports conflicts or partial failures truthfully"; truthfulness is meaning, so a person reviews it. The partial outcome is already graded from saved state: the catalog skill is still missing beside the local skill that holds its slug, and the installer recorded a warning. A skill link was not chosen because the blocked catalog skill has no installed page to link, and the contract only asks for links to the installed agents and project. The check is now "a first reply was delivered".
  > - `steering.discard-queued-message` (`runtime-discard.ts`): the later contact request now names a contact person and an attempt-specific phone (a setup change in the harness act step, not the YAML). The grade collects the contact section's distinctive whole lines from the revision that saved it: lines in the section whose heading carries the label, contact name or phone, plus lines that carry one of those markers, excluding lines that also appear elsewhere in that revision. None of those whole lines may remain in the final revision, and the contact name and phone may not appear anywhere in it (substring match on those distinctive literals). A note that quotes the label inside a longer line, and shared lines such as `---`, do not count.
- fix(runner): count returned validation rejections and give a copyable call in repeated-failure corrections (#21666)
  > - `runner-api-client.ts`: Add `runnerApiCallExample`. It builds a copyable call from the operation's catalog schema: the path parameters the model sent, plus a body with the required fields. If no field is required, the body has one free-text field, for example `{"operationId":"PATCH /api/issues/{id}","pathParams":{"id":"<sent id>"},"body":{"title":"<title>"}}`. The "requires body" correction and the "body must be a JSON object, not a JSON-encoded string" correction now include this example in place of the `{...}` placeholder.
  > - `repeated-tool-failure-guard.ts`: Count a result with `ok: false` and HTTP 400/422 as a deterministic failure, in the same way as a thrown 400/422. The first returned rejection passes through unchanged. The second gets a `guidance` note that says the call will not run again. The third and later identical calls are refused without running, and the refusal quotes the response.
  > - Never count transient results: 5xx, 408, 429, 409, 404, and a missing status (timeouts or unknown outcome).
- fix(runner): bound ACPX instructions only where the sandbox delivers them (#21646)
  > - `runtime-sandbox.ts`: `validateProviderPolicy` gets the agent. It applies the 32 KiB bound only to Pi. The type and NUL checks stay for all agents. Cursor, Kimi and Copilot keep their own 32 KiB checks.
  > - `admission-diagnostics.ts`: new closed admission code `ACPX_ADMISSION_INSTRUCTIONS`, and `acpxInstructionsAdmissionError`, which tags the error with that code. The tag wins over the stage code that the host records later.
  > - The instructions bound and NUL rejections in `runtime-sandbox.ts`, `runtime-host.ts` (`boundedInstructions`), `cursor-instructions.ts`, `kimi-instructions.ts` and `copilot-profile.ts` use the new tagged error. The messages contain no instruction text and no provider output.
- fix(github): read only the webhook secrets each delivery step uses (#21651)
  > - Added `githubCredentialRefsFor(refs, keys)` in the GitHub webhook ingress module. It selects the secret refs for named keys.
  > - Staging reads `webhookSecret` first. After the signature holds, it reads `installationId` (only when the event names an installation and is not an `installation` event) and `appId` (it scopes the recovery-receipt denial query).
  > - The replay worker reads `webhookSecret` to re-sign the body. It reads `appId`, `privateKey` and `installationId` only for a recovered delivery that needs a source recheck.
- fix(runner): recover provider-started turns after a controller restart (#21612)
  > - `src/native-session-runtime.ts` (15 added, 4 removed): the two checkpoints in `consumeTurn`, with the sequence of the appended event, and the resumed wait with a consumed disposition marker.
  > - `src/drivers/codex/codex-session-state.ts` (24 added, 1 removed), `codex-session-terminal.ts` (2 added), `codex-session-notifications.ts` (2 added), `codex-harness-session.ts` (24 added, 7 removed), `codex-app-server-driver-impl.ts` (4 added, 1 removed): the snapshot at the event, and the recovery of the driver.
  > - `src/contracts/harness-driver.ts` and `src/contracts/native-session-backend.ts` (1 added each): an optional `boundarySnapshot` on a driver session, and an optional sequence in the snapshot options. Other drivers do not implement it and are not changed.
- fix(runner): settle every recorded tool result and free sessions blocked by a closed turn's pending call (#21490)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-workflow-tools.ts`: workflow tool results name the route `apiOperationId`, like case tools and `call_api`.
  > - `provider_bridge.rs`: add `settle_undeliverable_result`. When the bridge rejects a recorded result for a call it still holds, the call settles with a bounded, non-retryable runner error. Conflicting or unknown results still fail as before.
  > - `provider_backend.rs` (`deliver_semantic_result`): use that settlement, deliver the error to the provider, and record a `semantic_tool_result_undeliverable` diagnostic with the call, operation, reason and the rejected result's digest.
- fix(chat): report an outcome once and create no run when an earlier reply gave it (#21489)
  > - `server/src/services/chat-completion-delivery.ts`: `settleReportedChatCompletions` marks each undispatched completion that an earlier reply reported as delivered, with that reply as its response. It returns true only when nothing else is owed. The outbox `deliver` also checks this before it requests a wake, so no wake and no run exist for an already-reported completion.
  > - `server/src/modules/wake-queue`: a new transaction port, `skipAlreadyReportedChatWake`, runs in the release drain before a deferred wake is promoted. When the earlier reply already reported every outcome of the wake, the wake ends `skipped` ("Already reported by an earlier reply in this conversation") and no run is created. Wakes that carry queued comments or a failed-chat retry are never skipped.
  > - `server/src/services/chat-interaction-outcome-report.ts` (new): the same structural rule for declined tool actions. A conversation agent run that lists its interactions records `chat_interaction.declined_read` for each declined tool action. The decline continuation is settled when the same agent replied in the conversation after the decline, from a run with that receipt, posted after the read. The rule covers only declined tool-action confirmations. Answers, plan reviews and accepted actions keep their own turn.
- fix(runs): say who started a retry run in the issue run read (#21486)
  > - `runsForIssue` (`server/src/services/activity.ts`) reads the wake request of each retry run. It adds `retriedBy` to each row:
  > - The OpenAPI description of `GET /api/issues/{id}/runs` and the chat agent directive tell the agent to use `retriedBy` and not to call a retry automatic unless `retriedBy` says so.
  > - The UI `RunForIssue` type has the new optional field.
- fix(native): wait for a live warm session owner during a deploy overlap (#21627)
  > - `remote-warm-session-recovery.ts`: before live adoption, the run proves that the server process that ran the finished turn is gone. It uses the shared owner proof (`proveOwnerGone`): a PID check in the same process space, or the `controller_instances` lease in another process space. While that server is alive, the run defers with the existing `native_controller_recovery_waiting_for_owner` wait and checks again after 5 s. The wait ends when the server exits or is fenced, or when the finished turn's warm window (idle timeout plus two minutes) ends.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts` and `run-start-handover.ts`: a server that leaves new run starts to a replacement closes its idle warm sessions after 1 s, through the existing idle-timeout close. A session that becomes idle after that closes after 1 s too. This is the same close that shutdown already does, done earlier.
  > - `predecessor-retirement-fallback.ts`: when a retry meets an unretired owner on the same sandbox that its source already asked to replace, it does not ask again. The run fails without another automatic retry, and the task shows a Retry.
- fix(workspaces): settle closed remote workspaces after one confirmed destroy (#21616)
  > - `server/src/services/remote-workspace-lifecycle.ts` (`cleanupRemoteWorkspaceAllocation`):
  > - `server/src/services/execution-workspaces.ts`: the gateway takes `sweepOwned`. The sweep's own cleanup, cleanup-failure and reopen-flag writes set it, and then the gateway does not publish a terminal workspace hint for that workspace. All other gateway callers still publish.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/terminal-workspace-idle.test.ts`: two new tests on real PostgreSQL.
- fix(mcp): preserve Tasks sleep authority and re-enable recovery (#21603)
  > - Read the effective Tasks flag inside the same read-only sleep transaction. Bind that value to the existing pending-task query.
  > - Register the existing Tasks queue notification before the settings transaction writes. The normal commit protocol owns the wake.
  > - Add two real-PostgreSQL regressions to the existing nine-case suite. Extend eight mock transactions with the read projection they now need. Keep their assertions unchanged.
- fix(native): close idle warm sessions in parallel across agents at shutdown (#21597)
  > - `closeIdleWarmNativeSessions` groups sessions by company and agent. Up to four agents close at once, and each agent's sessions close one at a time. The per-session close logic is unchanged.
  > - Idle-sleep retirement (`prepareIdleWarmNativeSessionsForSleep`) keeps a concurrency of one, so it still checks drain authority before each retirement.
  > - `recoverDirectoryRelease` catches `AgentDirectoryOperationBusyError`, keeps the deferred retry time, and returns the pending entry instead of throwing.
- fix(instructions): collect a waiting working copy as soon as its stop proof is written (#21596)
  > - New `notifyInstructionCopyStopEvidence(tx, { companyId, runId })` in `instruction-collection-notifications.ts`.
  > - The collection claim now compares `updated_at` with the value the worker read (millisecond precision). If a stop proof is written between the worker's evidence read and its backoff write, the claim fails and the worker returns the copy as due, so the next pass collects it. Without this fence, the worker could save a 6 h deadline over the proof.
  > - The writes that record stop proof call it in their own transaction:
- fix(restart-chaos): ignore a done the server rejected as incomplete (#21598)
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/follow-up-delivery.ts`: add `rejectedDoneRuns`. It attributes each `native-completion-incomplete` wake to the latest run whose accepted `done` came at or before the wake.
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/wake-evidence.ts` (`completionEvidence`) and `tests/restart-chaos/staging-mix-retry.ts` (`evidenceCompletion`): set a new optional `doneRejected` flag on each completion run.
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/staging-mix.ts`: `completedAtAnchor` and `completionRunIds` ignore a rejected `done`, unless that run posted the DONE comment.
- fix(heartbeat): count an early native hand-off only after its suspend commits (#21572)
  > - `nativeRunsHandedOffEarly` now holds each run's in-flight suspend promise, not only its id.
  > - The shutdown drain awaits that promise (bounded at 30 s). If it fails, returns false or does not finish, the drain runs its own suspend, as it does for a run without an early hand-off.
  > - `runBoundedShutdownStep` and the pre-drain budget pass an `AbortSignal` to each step and abort it on timeout.
- fix(instructions): back off working copies that cannot make progress (#21586)
  > - `instructionCopyRetryAt(waitingSince)` sets the next check to half of the time the copy has waited. The minimum is `INSTRUCTION_COPY_RETRY_MS` (30 s) and the maximum is `INSTRUCTION_COPY_MAX_RETRY_MS` (6 h). An unproven stop measures the wait from the run's `finished_at`. A cleanup measures it from `process_stopped_at`. The deadline stays in the existing `next_attempt_at` column, so a restart keeps it. There is no schema change.
  > - The collection check now reads the copy once, checks the stop evidence, and then claims. For an unproven stop, the claim writes the backoff deadline (and the move from `prepared` to `pending_collection`) in the same UPDATE. Before, the check was two reads plus two signalling transactions.
  > - `tryPatch` skips all work signals when a write only moves deadlines later and keeps the copy in the same queues (collection, cleanup, commit and the stranded-agent predicate). A write that changes queue membership or moves a deadline earlier still signals as before. The write stays in its own transaction, so the existing claim and race tests keep their hook.
- fix(retention): evaluate native retention blockers for many sessions in one statement (#21588)
  > - `SESSION_RETENTION_BLOCKERS_SQL` (new module `native-runtime/native-session-retention-blockers.ts`) is one fixed-text statement, sent through `$client.unsafe`. It takes a JSON list of sessions and returns, for each one, the same blocker reasons as `sessionRetentionBlocker`, in the same order: missing run, live run, incomplete native recovery, recent run (checkpoint mode), unknown, open, executing or recently closed task, pending finalization, live lease. It also returns the session's resume pointer, run, task and lease dependencies, and the latest close time.
  > - `expireCheckpoints` and `expireDurableBackups` evaluate each page of candidates with this statement. They skip blocked candidates and candidates whose session still has a resume pointer. A candidate that is not blocked still enters the existing transaction. That transaction checks the blocker again under row locks before it retires anything.
  > - `reconcileBackup` uses the statement for its dependencies and its deadline. It replaces three dependency reads plus a blocker transaction. The lease references use one index-backed arm for each reference kind (run, backup stamp, task, identity lease).
- fix(recovery): stop re-checking settled native runs that cannot be released (#21590)
  > - `server/src/services/native-local-process-stop.ts`
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/live-recovery-work.ts` (settled kind only)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/recovery.ts` (the settled handler only): returns the outcome. It calls `acknowledgeRemoteStop` only when the run has a non-local lease or is a cancelled `cursor_cloud_api` run. For any other run that function reads two rows and writes nothing.
- fix(recovery): keep the resolved-dependency probes per task whatever the statistics (#21574)
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/work.ts`: the candidate predicate is a named export, `resolvedDependencyCandidate`, and both subqueries end with `offset 0`. PostgreSQL does not pull a subquery with `OFFSET` up into a join, so it stays a correlated subplan. Its index condition then includes the outer task id. The predicate and its results are otherwise unchanged.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/resolved-dependency-candidates.test.ts`: two tests.
- fix(discord): refresh due command registration after task drains (#21573)
  > - Subscribe the command owner to normal drain changes and observed expiry.
  > - Wake it only when current admission allows work and its existing next time is due.
  > - Remove both subscriptions through the normal worker stop callback.
- fix(retention): stop rescanning unchanged pinned workspace snapshots (#21571)
  > - `pinned()` returns the first reference that holds the version (current pointer, importing parent, operation, unfinished child, run, or recovery action) and its ID, not a boolean.
  > - The sweep stores that reference in `metadata.retentionPin` when it pins a version. It writes again only when the reference changes or a deadline must be cleared.
  > - The candidate query leaves out a version with no deadline while its recorded reference still holds. Each pin condition is one SQL builder. `pinned()` and the candidate query use the same builder, so the exclusion can never hold a version that `pinned()` would release.
- fix(recovery): hold the start of queued automatic runs while a company's automatic-run brake is on (#21561)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`: the dispatch pass asks `reserveAutomaticRunStart` after the tried/backoff skip and the claim-attempt budget check, and before the budget increment and the full row read. A held run spends no claim attempt, does no row read, and does not enter the declined-claim backoff. If the brake grants the start (its probe or a half-open release start) but the claim then returns null, throws, or finds the row gone, the pass gives the start back, so another automatic run can use it in the same pass. The dispatcher's person-origin answer is passed as is, and it is `undefined` only when the dispatcher did not check origin (an agent with one queued run). Then the brake's own cached person check decides.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queued-run-line.ts`: `queuedRunDispatchFacts` also returns `personOriginChecked`, so the queue knows when the dispatcher's negative answer is real.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/dispatch-work.ts`: the queued-run dispatch deadline worker adds companies whose next try is due to its scopes, and includes the next try time in its next wake. A held company wakes for its next try without a polling loop. If the brake's bounded wake list had to drop a company, the worker wakes all companies at the earliest dropped time.
- fix(connections): keep machine sign-in secrets and settings in one locked commit (#21566)
  > - `client-credentials-service.ts`: `saveConnection` locks the row, checks that the sign-in method and credential references are unchanged and that the connection is not removed, then writes any new credential and the settings in the same transaction. Test, secret rotation and key staging all use it.
  > - Setup records `tokenEndpointAuthSigningAlgValuesSupported`. Switching from a secret to a key uses `chooseAssertionAlgorithm` on it.
  > - UI: `MachineSignInSetup` takes `applicationId`, and the custom-server flow passes `prefill.applicationId`.
- fix(ui): name a queued turn's hold once, in the turn header (#21564)
  > - `TaskChatRunnerTurn`: a queued turn that has a run and a hold label (agent queue, server hold, or "Waiting to start") no longer renders the current-activity line under the header. Startup preparation before a run exists ("Taking longer than usual…") keeps its slot, as DESIGN.md requires.
  > - `TaskChatThread`: the live tail for other adapters no longer shows the fixed "Waiting to start..." for a queued run. The live run pill above names the wait.
  > - DESIGN.md: a queued turn's hold is named once, in the turn header.
- fix(heartbeat): refresh queued dispatch when task drains end (#21562)
  > - Listen for normal drain changes and observed expiry in the queued-run owner.
  > - Wake that owner only when dispatch is allowed and its reconciliation is pending.
  > - Forward the deadline worker's optional stop callback after its stop fence.
- fix(recovery): ignore the non-assignee comment wake marker in the failed-preparation proof (#21550)
  > - `server/src/services/cancelled-native-startup.ts`: the failed-preparation proof accepts `aiConnectionNonAssigneeCommentWake` when its value is a boolean. The workspace-wait parent proof ignores the same key.
  > - `server/src/services/legacy-preparation-retry-hold.test.ts`: staging-shape tests for a recovered setup failure (failed restore, a workspace that still names its location, the marker, all together), and a Retry that attaches to an earlier refused request and then starts.
- fix(recovery): continue a task whose system retry was refused at claim behind a saved-work hold (#21505)
  > - `fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`, `interruptionEligibility`: the interruption counts as one the system continues when the failed run's cause is system-owned and a later run that the dispatch gate refused before it started is a system recovery run (`recoveryRunSql`, which excludes a person's Retry). A failed run that was itself a person's Retry (`task_retry` or `explicitUserContinuation`) never qualifies this way. The 24-hour recency bound is unchanged. The claim-gate hand-off, the backlog pass and its deadline query all use this one predicate.
  > - `recovery/continuation-ledger.ts`, `recovery/task-automatic-attempts.ts`: one shared rule, `startedOrPendingAutomaticRetry`. An automatic retry that the dispatch gate cancelled before it started (`cancelled`, no `startedAt`, `execution_reconciliation_required`) does not count toward the hourly cap in either ledger.
  > - `recovery/assigned-work-sweep.ts`:
- fix(runtime): verify GitHub bridge health before agent startup (#21537)
  > - Send a bounded, authenticated request from the execution target to the inactive GitHub broker before publishing the launcher environment.
  > - Require the expected inactive response. The probe cannot obtain a GitHub credential because no run is active.
  > - Close a failed HTTP/2 bridge and verify one file-bridge attempt. Keep this recovery in the native GitHub transport owner.
- fix(recovery): pause automatic recovery runs while a company's runs fail before starting (#21443)
  > - New table `automatic_run_brakes` (one row per company, cascade delete with the company; migration `0480`). Migration `0481` builds `heartbeat_runs_company_finished_idx (company_id, finished_at desc) where finished_at is not null` concurrently, so the signal reads the runs that finished in the window directly. It stores the state (closed, open, half_open), the reason with the window numbers, and the compare-and-set fields that two servers share. A company with no row is closed.
  > - New module `server/src/services/recovery/automatic-run-brake.ts`:
  > - Recovery passes defer creating runs while the pause is open. The gate is only where a run is created:
- fix(recovery): check held retained orphans only for candidate leases (#21542)
  > - `releasableRetainedOrphanCondition` and `nextRetainedOrphanDueAt` first apply `retainedOrphanCandidateCondition`. This is a filter with no subqueries and no NULL results: cleanup status, run, provider, lease status, lease policy, and the metadata keys of the three retained orphan shapes. Expired, active and pending-cleanup leases fail it at once.
  > - The held checks (always held and softly held) now run inside `CASE WHEN <retained orphan condition> THEN ... ELSE false END`. PostgreSQL evaluates a CASE in order, so the correlated subqueries run only for candidates. The planner can no longer split the `NOT (a OR b OR ...)` into separate top-level subplans and run them before the candidate predicates.
  > - The two `heartbeat_runs` checks of the always-held part are now one scalar subquery with a primary key lookup. It reads `runner_profile_json->'nativeWorkspaceSync'` once through a fenced lateral, instead of about 12 `#>>` path reads. A scalar subquery cannot become a hashed subplan, so the planner cannot replace it with a full scan of `heartbeat_runs`.
- fix(queue): say a long-queued turn is waiting to start, and name the routine execution that holds it (#21447)
  > - Server: `executionProjectionsForRuns` reads, in one query per batch, whether another open task of the same routine holds the routine's execution. For a never-started queued run that waits on it, the projection sets the cause `routine_execution_in_progress`, the label "Waiting for <task identifier> to finish", and a next action. This check comes before the agent-limit check, because a free slot would not start the run.
  > - UI: a new hook, `useWaitingToStart`, keeps the startup feedback for 30 seconds. After that, a queued turn with no named hold shows "Waiting to start": an hourglass, no spinner, no shimmer. The task chat's runner header, its current-activity tail and the live run pill use it. The hook uses one timer at the threshold and no per-second ticks.
  > - DESIGN.md: the startup-feedback rule now has the 30-second limit and the routine hold.
- fix(heartbeat): keep recovery behind people's work, guarantee a start to tasks people wait on, and admit recovery a few runs at a time (#21427)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queued-run-dispatch-order.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queued-run-line.ts`: the start-history counters use `dispatchHistoryStart` and `dispatchCountersFromStarts`, so dispatch, the place-in-line view and the tests share one rule.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`:
- fix(run-events): watch both late appends for rejection as soon as they start (#21536)
  > - In the test "waits for Stop on the run row and appends after it, and fails without rows after a run deletion", the two `expect(...).rejects` checks are now created immediately after the two appends start. The test awaits them after it releases the deletion.
  > - No product code changed. The product code rejects correctly; the test left one rejection without a handler for a short time.
- fix(secrets): stop credential reads from aborting concurrent budget admissions (#21452)
  > - `secretService` no longer writes `last_resolved_at` / `updated_at` when it resolves a value. It still records the `secret_access_events` row, as before.
  > - `secretService.list` reports `lastResolvedAt` as the later of the stored column (legacy values) and the newest successful access event. It reads one row per secret, backwards on the existing `(secret_id, created_at)` index.
  > - A read without a consumer context (for example a GitHub API credential read) now records a `system` access event, so it still counts as "Last used".
- fix(ci): count a step cancelled as "A task was canceled." as runner loss when the log shows the shutdown (#21514)
  > - `.github/scripts/runner-loss-rerun.mjs`: the cancellation annotation check accepts "The operation was canceled." and "A task was canceled.". The log check still decides. A cancellation with no shutdown or lost-communication line in the log is still a real failure, and its evidence now quotes the actual annotation text.
  > - `.github/scripts/tests/runner-loss-rerun.test.mjs`: a test for "A task was canceled." with and without shutdown evidence.
  > - `doc/DEVELOPING.md`: the runner-loss paragraph names both texts.
- fix(wake-queue): start a person's saved message after a legacy run is continued from saved work (#21518)
  > - `server/src/modules/wake-queue/adapters/postgres.ts`: the release pre-drain check does not ask for legacy reconciliation when the run is superseded by a fresh continuation. This matches the existing native rule in the same function.
  > - `server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.test.ts`: a red/green test for native and legacy runs. A person's comment goes through the real wake path while the task is blocked, the task continues from saved work, and exactly one run must start with the comment.
- fix(recovery): replace a queued run the dispatcher keeps passing over instead of counting it as a pending path (#21502)
  > - New violation `queued_run_stalled` in `recovery/assigned-work-invariant.ts`. A queued run is stalled when all of these are true (read from the database, on the database clock):
  > - A stalled run is not a live run, and it does not let the task's interruption projection ("working") count as a pending path. Its own wake is not counted as a pending wake. A stalled run on a blocked task still counts as live, so the block decides first.
  > - `listSuspects` lets a task through when its only active run is a stalled queued run.
- fix(native): keep a slow but live Runner on retry instead of replacing it (#21462)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts` (`verifyRemoteRunnerOrSettledExit`): with `replaceUnansweredRunner`, the attempt adopts the live Runner, launching nothing and signalling nothing, when:
  > - The baseline is the first successful state read; every successful read is also compared against the controller's acknowledgement. A Runner whose state cannot be read at all during the wait is adopted, not replaced: missing evidence is not proof of a wedge. A Runner whose identity changes during the wait fails closed.
  > - A settled exit during the wait keeps the attempt allowance that admitted the replacement, so it also restores on the extra fourth attempt.
- fix(heartbeat): reach the previous server's live runs first after a release swap (#21450)
  > - Early hand-off at shutdown (`heartbeat/recovery.ts`, `server/src/index.ts`): in deploy-overlap mode, the outgoing server releases the remote controller lease of its live runs at SIGTERM (`recoveryState` `awaiting_runner_reattach`). A run another server already took over is left alone. The incoming server no longer waits for the lease to lapse.
  > - Reattach fast path:
  > - Reattach phase lifecycle (`heartbeat/reattach-phase.ts`): the watch is a worker on the shared delivery coordinator, queue `reattach-phase`.
- fix(runner): poll a live ACPX turn without waiting out the idle read (#21525)
  > - `acpx_provider_backend.rs`: `AcpxCommandExecutor::provider_poll_is_live` returns true only when the session was restored, has no persistence error, does not wait for a next-turn generation, and its state is `turn_starting` or `turn_active` with an active turn, no unconfirmed provider exit, and the run it owns.
  > - `native_provider_backend.rs`: `SelectedExecutor` forwards `provider_poll_is_live` to the ACPX executor.
  > - `tests/acpx_warm_attachment.rs`: new test through `NativeProviderCommandExecutor`. The session is not live before `run.prepare` or after `session.open`, is live after `turn.start`, and a new executor that has not restored its session is not live.
- fix(runner): bound the ACPX receive buffer by bytes, not 512 events (#21524)
  > - `acpx_sidecar_transport.rs`:
  > - `fake-acpx-sidecar.rs`: new `backlog`, `large-backlog` and `count-flood` modes. `flood` now writes 18 MiB of 64 KiB events instead of 513 small events.
  > - `tests/acpx_sidecar_transport.rs`: new tests for a backlog of 2,048 small events, for freeing the byte budget as events drain, and for a flood of tiny events. The existing flood test still checks the error.
- fix(recovery): settle uncertain runner API receipts instead of fencing them forever (#21522)
  > - `runner-api-client.ts`: `executeRunnerApi` sends the receipt key in `X-Paperclip-Runner-Call` with mutations. Reads do not send it.
  > - `paperclip-runner-tool-authority.ts`: `#callApi` passes the receipt key of the reserved mutation to the client.
  > - `middleware/runner-call-outcome.ts` (new): for an agent JWT request with a run id and a valid call key, it records the final HTTP status as a `runner.api_completed` activity row of the run. It records when the handler ends the response, so it also records after the client has gone away. It records one time per request.
- fix(runner): prove a warm Runner's suspension on a slow sandbox host so the next run does not fail (#21472)
  > - `runnerd-codex-transport.ts` (controller-side TypeScript):
  > - `native-runtime/predecessor-retirement.ts`, `heartbeat/run-completion.ts`: the predecessor-retirement fallback records the task's status when the run failed.
  > - `modules/run-dispatch/domain/policy.ts`, `modules/run-dispatch/adapters/postgres.ts`: a continuation of a run that did no provider work, on a task that was already blocked then, is not treated as blocked after recovery. Its dependency check follows the wake it continues. The continuation keeps a person's comment wake.
- fix(secrets): mask secret-bound environment values in run output (#21341)
  > - `secretEnvironmentValues(env, secretKeys)` in `agent-identity-redaction.ts` selects the raw values of keys in `secretKeys` (the keys that came from `secret_ref` or `user_secret_ref` bindings). `secretOutputForms` derives the forms to mask: the full value, the value without outer whitespace, the JSON-escaped form, and each line of a multi-line value (PEM armor lines are not added). It skips a form that has fewer than 8 characters after trimming. This is the same 8-character limit that diagnostic redaction uses for unknown values.
  > - The output redactor keeps these secrets in a separate set, beside the existing agent private-key values. The private-key values are masked everywhere, as before. Environment secrets are masked only in output text. They are not masked in protocol fields: `schema`, `type`, `kind`, `status`, `state`, `outcome`, `stream`, `channel`, `role`, `level`, ids, refs, codes, revisions, versions, digests, timestamps, and names that end in these words (for example `eventType`, `turnTerminalState`, `reportedWorkDisposition`). So a secret that equals a protocol word (for example `completed`) cannot change an event type or a terminal state.
  > - The streaming match now scans left to right. At each position it tries the longest value first. If a longer value could still start at that position, it holds the text until the next chunk, also when a shorter value already matches there. So the tail of a longer secret cannot leak when a shorter secret is its prefix.
- fix(heartbeat): retry a legacy Stop that loses the task-row lock race (#21492)
  > - `run-lifecycle.ts`: when the status write terminalizes a legacy run, it runs through `retryTransientLockContention` with 3 attempts. Each attempt waits at most the 1s task-row `lock_timeout`. Other status writes do not change.
  > - `run-control.ts`: if Stop still gets transient lock contention (`55P03`, `40P01`, `40001`) after all attempts, it throws a 409 conflict with code `run_stop_busy` and the run ID. Before this change it was a 500.
  > - `heartbeat-stop-queued-task.test.ts`: 2 new embedded-Postgres tests. One holds the task row for 1.5s and expects Stop to succeed. One holds the row through all attempts and expects a 409 `run_stop_busy`. It then expects the run to stay `queued` and a second Stop to succeed.
- fix(docker): stop a stale-lockfile build early and keep beta and stable releases buildable (#21333)
  > - `Dockerfile`: a `runner-lockfile` stage copies only the manifests the Runner build installs from, the lockfile and the patches. It runs the frozen, filtered, offline lockfile check, with the arguments of `runnerLockState`. `deps` starts from it. For a stale lockfile it prints the files and `pnpm install --resolution-only --ignore-scripts --no-frozen-lockfile`. For any other failure it prints the output of pnpm and says so.
  > - `scripts/check-release-runner-lockfile.mjs`: exports the Runner inputs of a commit with `git archive` and gives them to `runnerLockState` (`scripts/runner-image-publish.mjs`). Exit 0 for current, 1 for behind, 2 for an error.
  > - `scripts/release.sh` and `scripts/release-lib.sh`: `require_runner_lockfile_current` runs for the `beta` and the `stable` channel, after `--print-version` exits and before any build. It runs in a dry run too. It does not run for `canary` or `nightly`.
- fix(native-runtime): retry a native failure write after a brief lock timeout (#21494)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: The full native failure transaction now runs in the existing `retryTransientLockContention` helper. The helper makes a maximum of 5 attempts with a short backoff. It retries only for 55P03, 40P01 and 40001. Each attempt resets `terminalRunToReport`. A rolled-back attempt has no effect, so a new attempt is safe. Other errors (for example, a lost lease or a post-result failure) do not get a retry.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-failure-write-lock-contention.integration.test.ts`: A new embedded-Postgres test. It keeps a lock on the agent row until one failure-write attempt is cancelled by the lock timeout. Then it makes sure that a later attempt completes the write and that the coordinator releases the lease.
- fix(native): settle a Stop seen at budget admission as that cancellation (#21483)
  > - `reserveRunBudget` now throws the existing `NativeCancellationPendingRecoveryError` for a native run when it finds a recorded Stop. Heartbeat already settles this error through `cancelRunInternal("Recovered durable native run cancellation")`. This is the same path as a Stop that native startup sees when the session opens.
  > - Legacy runs keep the 409 conflict. The incident evidence is for native runs only.
  > - The final dispatch gate does not change. It already treats a recorded Stop as a lost race, not as a failure.
- fix(retry): answer a refused Retry with its refusal instead of accepted (#21497)
  > - `POST /api/issues/:id/retry` runs admission of a plain Retry (not a saved-result finalization, not a connected chat retry) before it answers. It waits at most 5 seconds. If admission takes longer, admission continues after the answer, and the answer stays "accepted" as before.
  > - When admission refused the request for a reason another click cannot change (`heldRetryOffersRetry` is false), the answer is HTTP 200 with `accepted: false` and `refused: { reason, message }`. The message is the same stable text that the task view shows.
  > - The saved request is kept. If its gate clears later (for example, saved-work recovery verifies the files), it still starts by itself.
- fix(native): retry a transient endpoint failure after a same-run bootstrap restart (#21488)
  > - Add `attemptLaunchesFreshBootstrap()` to `native-bootstrap-proof.ts`. It returns true for a first attempt and for a `bootstrap_incomplete` restart. It returns false for `reattach_existing_runner`, `reattach_remote_runner` and `resume_dead_runner`, because these continue an existing Runner or provider session.
  > - In `native-session-executor.ts`, use this helper in place of `!input.restartRecovery` when the executor builds the unclaimed-bootstrap evidence for a startup failure.
  > - Add `native-bootstrap-proof.test.ts` for the helper.
- fix(heartbeat): drain native resume dispatches whose timer already fired (#21479)
  > - `heartbeat.ts`: the fired resume-dispatch body is tracked in a module-level set, `activeNativeResumeDispatches`, shared by all service instances like the other drain sets. `drainActiveRunExecutions()` awaits that set. It still clears only the timers armed before it started. A timer armed during the drain that is already due (for example the immediate dispatch a run arms when its executor detaches) counts as pending work: the drain yields so it fires, then drains what it starts. Timers due later keep their deadline. A first version cleared timers on every pass, and CI showed that this cancelled the self-detach takeover in `heartbeat-native-restart-suspension.test.ts`.
  > - `heartbeatService` exposes `scheduleNativeSessionResumeDispatch` so the regression test can drive it. Recovery already uses it.
  > - `fresh-continuation-fallback.test.ts` uses a plain TRUNCATE again. A future leak fails the test instead of being retried away.
- fix(runner): accept object fields sent as JSON text and name wrong-type rejections (#21485)
  > - Added `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/tool-input-coercion.ts`. `admitToolInput` validates the arguments. If they are invalid, it parses a string in an object-typed field back into its object. It does this only when the field's schema declares `object`, does not allow `string`, the text parses to a plain JSON object, and the whole coerced call validates. It applies to any object-typed field at any depth, not only `continuation`.
  > - The OpenCode MCP bridge and the runner tool bridge use `admitToolInput`. They dispatch the coerced arguments, and they send one `paperclip.runner.tool-input-coerced/v1` diagnostic per accepted coercion. The OpenCode driver already records bridge diagnostics as run events. The diagnostic names the tool and the field paths. It never contains the values. The runner tool bridge has a new optional `onDiagnostic` option.
  > - When parsed text is still invalid, the rejection describes what is wrong inside the parsed object, for example `/options/mode must be string`.
- fix(models): answer an unreachable OpenRouter catalog with 503 instead of a crash report (#21468)
  > - `server/src/services/openrouter-models.ts`: The catalog now maps three failure types to `HttpError(503)` with code `openrouter_catalog_unavailable` and the existing retry message: its own deadline, a classified transport failure (uses `classifyRemoteConnectionError`), and a 408, 429 or 5xx status. A module-local `WeakSet` records only the errors that it creates for these outages. `isOpenRouterCatalogUnavailable()` reads that record.
  > - Other non-OK statuses become a 502 with code `openrouter_catalog_error`. These are still reported. Malformed JSON and an invalid catalog shape are also still reported.
  > - `server/src/middleware/error-handler.ts`: The handler does not send a crash report for an error from `isOpenRouterCatalogUnavailable()`. It still answers 503 with the message and code. The new check is next to the existing MCP connection-failure exclusion.
- fix(recovery): continue a failed continuation again instead of escalating it to the board (#21394)
  > - New `continuation-cause.ts` holds the cause classification: `PERSON_OWNED_FAILURE_CODES` (moved, unchanged), a new short list `PERMANENT_FAILURE_CODES` (low-trust boundary codes, `adapter_engine_unavailable`, `agent_not_invokable`, `agent_not_found`, `agent_interaction_delivery_denied`), `isSystemOwnedFailureCode`, and `isAutomaticContinuationRun` (a retry, scheduled retry, fresh continuation, sweep or recovery run; never a person's Retry). `fresh-continuation-fallback.ts` re-exports what callers use.
  > - `stranded-hold-continuation.ts`: the sweep continues a recent (24 hours) board hold when the failure cause is system-owned, not only for a short allow-list. A reusable sandbox resume failure still needs a transient cause.
  > - `recovery/service.ts`: the stranded reconciler does not escalate an agent-assigned `todo` or `in_progress` task to the board when its latest run was an automatic continuation or recovery run that ended within 24 hours with a system-owned cause. The assigned-work sweep owns that task. A person's Retry, a person-owned cause, a permanent cause, or an older failure escalates as before.
- fix(recovery): continue Runner preparations whose remote workspace was handed on unchanged (#21481)
  > - Add `isReleasedUnchangedRemoteWorkspace` in `server/src/services/remote-workspace-abandoned-preparation.ts`. It is true only when the run's reference is `saved`, and the workspace's unchanged-baseline receipt names this run, the same epoch and the same snapshot, with a release time.
  > - `hasVerifiedWorkspaceRepair` accepts that receipt, next to the copy-back receipt and the abandoned prepared epoch.
  > - Add regression tests for the deploy-interrupted shape (held preparation settles and continues; Retry starts) and for the setup-failure shape (Retry starts). Add a negative test: a preparation that still holds its workspace unsaved keeps its hold.
- fix(accounting): recover closed legacy runs without inventing usage (#21493)
  > - Remove the cost-pending dependency from the existing positive local process-receipt writer. Register accounting work before the receipt update in the same transaction. Recheck current ownership after the notification await.
  > - Let accounting recognize only the exact positive local receipt. Compare its complete identity across spool reads and the locked settlement. Keep incomplete spend unpriced or marked as a lower bound. Retain the existing late-receipt and charge-deduplication paths.
  > - Wire the existing board/company accounting Retry to physical recovery. Check the saved local controller and child through the normal host/birth probes. Recheck the full run and ordered, locked lease set, cleanup, and current local ownership before writing. Do not signal or replay a provider.
- fix(recovery): start an overdue saved-work recovery instead of looping on no pending path (#21478)
  > - `assigned-work-invariant.ts`: new pending intent `saved_work_recovery`. A saved-work recovery that the queue still owes counts as the task's pending path while an attempt runs, while its next attempt is scheduled, or for `SAVED_WORK_RECOVERY_START_BOUND_MS` (10 minutes) after it became due.
  > - `assigned-work-invariant.ts`: new violation `saved_work_recovery_overdue`, used when that bound passes. It comes before the `no_pending_path`, `retry_offered` and blocked checks, so the sweep never sends a wake that the hold refuses.
  > - `assigned-work-sweep.ts`: the sweep loads the fact with the same eligibility predicate the recovery worker uses. For the overdue violation it calls `startSavedWorkRecovery` and records no continuation activity.
- fix(ui): group failed-query Sentry reports by status and query name (#21475)
  > - `ui/src/lib/browser-error-context.ts`: add `queryKindFor`. It keeps only the first query-key part, and only when it is a lowercase word list such as `issues` or `agent-commentary`. Other values become `other`, so an ID, a ref, an email, or a typed value is never sent.
  > - `ui/src/lib/browser-error-context.ts`: add `buildQueryErrorContext`. For an error with an HTTP status, it sets the `query_kind` and `api_status` tags and the fingerprint `["api-error", status, queryKind]`. For an error without a status, it sets only the `query_kind` tag and keeps default stack grouping.
  > - `ui/src/lib/sentry.ts`: add `captureQueryException`. It uses the same serialized queue as `captureBrowserException`. Both functions now share one private capture step.
- fix(daytona): bound the duplex channel close so a stalled kill cannot retire the worker (#21471)
  > - `duplex-command-stream.ts`: `close()` waits for the remote `kill()` for at most `DUPLEX_CHANNEL_CLOSE_KILL_TIMEOUT_MS` (5 seconds). Then it always releases the socket with `disconnect()`. The kill continues in the background. A kill that answers in time keeps the old order: kill, then disconnect, then the acknowledgement.
  > - `duplex-close-deadline.test.ts` (new): regression tests at the session close and at the worker `onDuplexChannelClose` boundary, and a check that the deadline stays well inside the host deadline.
- fix(codex): preserve sandbox certificate trust in Codex shell commands (#20364)
  > - Add `CERTIFICATE_ENVIRONMENT_KEYS` (`SSL_CERT_FILE`, `SSL_CERT_DIR`, `GIT_SSL_CAINFO`, `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`, `DENO_CERT`, `REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE`, `CURL_CA_BUNDLE`) to `packages/paperclip-runner/src/configured-environment.ts`
  > - Native Codex shell policy (`codex-security-config.ts`): always put the certificate names in `include_only`; set inheritance to `"all"` with `ignore_default_excludes=true`, bounded by the explicit allowlist
  > - Pass the certificate names through the app-server environment filter, the ACPX spawn environment filter, the ACPX runtime sandbox `include_only`, the runner platform environment keys, and the Rust process supervisor base key list
- fix(queue): retain chat bindings on resolved card continuations (#20825)
  > - Retain delivered message bindings for resolved chat card responses during queue promotion.
  > - Keep deleted, foreign-task, and self-authored message filters and current chat authorization.
  > - Add a persisted queue regression and Slack/Discord promotion-to-authorization checks, including access revocation.
- fix(diagnostics): report Daytona acquisition state for runs retried as provider outages (#21476)
  > - `collectRunFailureDiagnostics` reads the private acquisition diagnostic for a setup failure with the error code `setup_failed` or `environment_provider_unavailable`. The other conditions do not change: the phase must be `setup`, and the diagnostic must belong to the same run and company.
  > - A new test builds the real wrapper chain (lease failure, then provider failure, then the RPC error that holds the diagnostic). It checks that the fields are reported for the failed run, and that they are not reported for another run or for the execute phase.
- fix(recovery): retain a failed run's sandbox in one lease write (#21474)
  > - `server/src/services/sandbox-stop-and-retain.ts`: `prepareSandboxStopAndRetain` accepts an optional `retention` argument (`leasePolicy` and extra `metadata`). The stop-and-retain update sets these values in the same statement. Other callers do not change.
  > - `server/src/services/legacy-workspace-restore-recovery.ts`: the active-source path passes the repair hold (`retain_on_failure` and `workspaceRestoreRecovery` with `originalLeasePolicy`) to that update. It no longer does a second update on the same row.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/legacy-workspace-restore-lock-contention.test.ts`: a regression test for legacy and native runs. Another session holds the environment row `FOR UPDATE` while the run records its restore failure. The test checks the run's recovery evidence and the lease policy, status, stop intent and repair metadata.
- fix(acpx): admit a Claude tool call only for a tool use its turn announced (#21278)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/runner-tool-bridge.ts`
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/acpx/claude-tool-use-admission.ts` (new): `ClaudeToolUseAdmission`.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/cli/acpx-runtime-sidecar.ts`
- fix(heartbeat): run one dispatch pass per agent, back off refused claims, and enforce the run limit in the claim (#21433)
  > - `server/src/services/agent-start-lock.ts`: replaces the stale-bypass lock with `dispatchForAgent`. One pass runs per agent. Triggers during a pass merge into one follow-up pass that all their callers share. A trigger from inside the agent's own pass does not wait on itself. A pass has a 60 s deadline on a monotonic clock, measured from when the pass started (not from when a waiter was chained). At the deadline the pass stops claiming (an abort signal) and the follow-up may start. A trigger that arrives after the deadline also ends the pass, so a stalled timer cannot keep an agent blocked. Post-pass effects run after the agent is free, as before.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-slot-admission.ts` (new): `assertAgentRunSlotFree` counts the agent's `running` runs and compares them with `maxConcurrentRunsSql()` inside the agent-scoped advisory lock that every claim already takes (`withExternalAdmissionGuard`). It throws `AgentRunSlotsFull` when the agent is full. The count leaves out the run that the claimed run replaces (`retryOfRunId` or `previousRunId`), so a successor that starts while its predecessor finishes is not refused.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`:
- fix(ui): restore chat submission on plain HTTP origins (#20308)
  > - Use `createUuid` for both composer attempt IDs, both request ID fallbacks, and image upload state.
  > - Test five successive sends in task and chat modes without `randomUUID`.
  > - Test upload receipts, retry request identity, the next draft, and first-message creation without that API.
- fix(ui): show each related task once in the Tasks tab (#20315)
  > - Exclude task IDs already shown as subtasks, ancestors, the creation source, or a root blocker from project groups.
  > - Show a creation source that is also an ancestor only in Created from.
  > - Hide empty project groups, including No project.
- fix(ci): keep the typecheck job working on stacked PRs and stop its CLI guidance test timing out (#21451)
  > - `.github/workflows/pr-trusted.yml`: the lane step runs only when `scripts/ci-typecheck-lane.mjs` exists in the checkout. A new step runs the six earlier commands, in their earlier order, when it does not. Only the `typecheck_release_registry` job changed.
  > - `scripts/ci-typecheck-lane.mjs`: the CLI guidance test moved from the parallel checks to the end of the sequential preparation steps, at normal priority.
  > - Tests: `scripts/__tests__/ci-typecheck-lane.test.mjs` and `.github/scripts/tests/ci-plan.test.mjs` assert the two conditions, that the earlier commands appear only in the fallback step, and that the CLI guidance test is a preparation step.
- fix(native): name the recorded turn failure when a native run fails (#21235)
  > - Add `explainNativeTurnFailure()` in `native-run-failure-explanation.ts`. It builds the message only from the diagnostic's closed vocabulary (runner failure code, else a specific Codex error kind). It never uses provider text.
  > - The finalizer and the committed-run projection use this message when `run.error` is empty and the saved diagnostic names a cause. Otherwise they keep `NATIVE_RUN_FAILED_UNEXPLAINED`. The `errorCode` stays `native_run_failed`.
  > - The executor uses the same message before its `Native session <state>` fallback.
- fix(browser-tracing): send API spans only for measured page loads, without timing events (#21441)
  > - `sdk.ts`: the fetch instrumentation runs with `ignoreNetworkEvents: true` (an option of the installed `@opentelemetry/instrumentation-fetch` 0.221.0).
  > - `sdk.ts`: new `measuredRequestsOnly` wraps the tracer provider that only the fetch instrumentation uses. When no `page.load` is open, it returns a non-recording span with an invalid context. Nothing is exported. The W3C propagator sends no `traceparent` for an invalid context, so the server traces that request alone, as for an unsampled tab. The `page.load`, Web Vitals, and backfill spans use the normal tracer and do not change.
  > - Failed idle requests are not kept. The server's own request span records server errors. To keep them, the browser must record every idle request and send its `traceparent` before it knows the status, and that brings back the dangling joins.
- fix(queue): let board users discard agent-authored queued messages (#21458)
  > - Queue responses grant board users discard permission for agent-authored messages. Editing and other human authors' messages retain their existing permissions.
  > - The discard use case honors the actor-specific permission rebuilt under the queue lock. Author agents keep their existing cancellation path.
  > - The board discard route checks private-task access before deletion. The denial for a non-author agent now points to the board action instead of saying that no board action exists.
- fix: Recognize Git mount-boundary absence diagnostics in workspace checks (#21048)
  > - `server/src/services/git-mount-boundary-message.ts`: Retain the shared complete-diagnostic matcher, which rejects partial and appended output.
  > - `server/src/services/managed-repository-checkout.ts`: Retain boundary recognition without changing ordinary-diagnostic behavior or fallback guards.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/workspaces.ts`: Retain boundary recognition and ancestor metadata inspection.
- fix: Wait for the SSH export pipeline to drain before importing the bundle (#20708)
  > - `packages/adapter-utils/src/ssh.ts`: Use Node's pipeline to drain the stream chain, propagate stream errors, and release streams on failure while preserving SSH stderr errors.
  > - `packages/adapter-utils/src/ssh-export-stream.test.ts`: Reproduced truncation and confirmed the fix passes with and without progress. Added failure cleanup cases; their final run and typecheck have not returned, and live SSH fixtures skipped because ssh is unavailable.
- fix: Display Ollama billers with their product names in cost views (#20420)
  > - `ui/src/lib/utils.ts`: Add Ollama and Ollama Cloud names in the shared formatter used by cost views.
  > - `ui/src/lib/utils.provider-display-name.test.ts`: Reproduce the missing names and cover case-insensitive lookup and unchanged fallback behavior. Confirmed failure before the fix; all 20 selected tests and the workspace typecheck passed afterward.
- fix: Complete run cancellation when lifecycle event append fails (#20215)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-control.ts`: Log cancellation event append failures and continue essential cleanup, matching lease-release terminalization.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-control-event-failure.test.ts`: Cover queued and running cancellation with an injected append failure and the real issue-release adapter. Confirmed regression failures before the fix; regression tests, neighboring tests, and final typecheck pass.
- fix: Preserve plugin UI UUID validation and test trailing line terminators (#20058)
  > - `server/src/routes/plugin-ui-static.ts`: Retain the existing guard, PostgreSQL UUID forms, and key fallback.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/plugin-ui-static.test.ts`: Add route tests for four trailing line terminators and accepted PostgreSQL UUID forms. Confirmed the original lookup fails; all 58 focused tests and typecheck pass with the guard.
- fix: Remove the unused OpenCode remote config guard and document host staging (#20045)
  > - `packages/adapters/opencode-local/src/server/runtime-config.ts`: Remove the unused option and contradictory guard while preserving both callers' staging behavior.
  > - `packages/adapters/opencode-local/src/server/runtime-config.test.ts`: Add a regression for isolated staging, generated configuration, cleanup, and operator config preservation. Confirmed failure before the fix; all 50 focused tests pass afterward. Typecheck was started but has not returned a result.
  > - `packages/adapters/opencode-local/README.md`: Document host staging for local and remote execution, remote asset transfer, path replacement, and host cleanup.
- fix: Defer chat milestone result reads and fix regression test transaction typing (#19875)
  > - `server/src/services/chat-run-publications.ts`: Retain publication-dependent guards around both result JSON predicates in the shared milestone query.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/chat-run-milestone-toast.test.ts`: Use the neighboring tests' transaction cast convention. Confirmed both cases fail without the guards and pass with them; all seven selected milestone test files and typecheck passed.
- fix: Allow initial plugin configuration saves and scope completed saves to their original company and plugin (#19847)
  > - `ui/src/pages/PluginSettings.tsx`: Preserve initial saves after successful loading, validation, and pending guards. Capture pluginId and companyId in mutation variables and use them for persistence, cache updates, and invalidation.
  > - `ui/src/pages/PluginSettings.test.tsx`: Retain coverage for initial defaults, saved values, loading failures, and validation.
  > - `ui/src/pages/PluginSettings.save-refetch.test.tsx`: Retain coverage preventing duplicate unchanged saves during refetch.
- fix: Keep tablet task composers above the keyboard and release sizing during pinch zoom (#19725)
  > - `ui/src/components/Layout.tsx`: Retain viewport observation independent of navigation width.
  > - `ui/src/components/Layout.production.tsx`: Apply the same viewport observation to the production layout.
  > - `ui/src/hooks/useMobileVisualViewport.ts`: Clear keyboard state during pinch zoom and handle Safari document panning for classic conversation shells.
- fix(native): preserve pack proof across interrupted reattachment (#21246)
  > - The admission module prepares the claim patch and its recovery source.
  > - The claim saves a versioned reference with the source run, receipt digest, and previous verified state. It adds no copy of provider files or credentials.
  > - A later pending claim can use that reference only if the receipt is unchanged. Normal company, agent, session, Runner, and lease checks still apply.
- fix: restrict chat task sponsors to self or admins (#21218)
  > - Check every submitted sponsor in the configuration transaction. Lock the actor's membership and any required admin role through the save.
  > - Require self-selection unless the actor has a current admin role. Check all target members in the same company.
  > - Keep delegated connection checks, authority notifications, tool updates, and audit attribution.
- fix(recovery): report exact retained sandbox resumes as resumed (#21259)
  > - Return a separate operation receipt after the exact retained allocation passes the existing resume checks.
  > - Pass the receipt through heartbeat preparation and apply it only to the execution target.
  > - Keep the saved lease metadata and workspace unchanged.
- fix(connectors): keep result classification alive during run progress (#20763)
  > - Use READ COMMITTED only for prompt-injection admission. Keep company, project, configuration, agent, run and secret share locks.
  > - Keep classifier settlement at repeatable read. Keep provider calls outside transaction retries.
  > - Test screened results, stored charges and settled holds under run progress. Test four concurrent reads and refusal after a run stops or changes identity.
- fix(connections): answer setup questions with a setup link in ranked results too (#21428)
  > - `server/src/services/connection-intents.ts`: the ranked-matches instruction says to use `connection_request` for data or actions the person asked for, and to answer a setup question with the app's setup-page link instead. It also says not to create a connection or draft unless the person asked for setup.
  > - Test: the ranked instruction includes the setup-page rule.
- fix(runner): answer a live Runner's commands while it replays its backlog after a reconnect (#21442)
  > - `durable-prp-control-plane.ts`: a `command_result` for `session.snapshot`, `session.goal.get`, `runner.drain` or `semantic_tool.result` that reports completed or rejected is applied when it arrives, not after the events queued ahead of it. Failed results, lifecycle, attachment and turn commands keep their place.
  > - `durable-prp-control-plane.ts`: the welcome carries every consecutive pending command (up to 32, within the existing byte bound). A lifecycle command or `run.attach` that is not first is never batched.
  > - `durable-prp-control-plane.ts`: an ordinary event inside a replayed burst is committed at once but checkpointed and acknowledged once per batch (when the queue drains, when a critical event arrives, or after 64 events). An ACK is still sent only after the checkpoint that contains its cursor. The owner fence in `flushCheckpoint` is unchanged. Critical events keep their own checkpoint and ACK.
- fix(runner): advertise flat tool schemas and stop repeated runner-side input rejections (#21398)
  > - Add `providerToolSchemaShapeIssues` (`packages/paperclip-runner/src/contracts/provider-tool-schema-shape.ts`). It reports a root that is not `type: "object"`, has no `properties`, or uses `oneOf`, `anyOf`, `allOf`, `not`, `if`, `then`, `else`, `dependent*` or `$ref`.
  > - Add inventory tests that run this check on every agent-facing tool: the live semantic catalog, the semantic action catalog, the Codex tool specs, `paperclip_finish` and `paperclip_block` (runner), and the server-only tool definitions (server). Before this change, the runner test listed 16 entries for 8 tools: `handoff_case`, `request_human_input`, `read_case`, `update_case`, `create_case`, `read_workflow`, `update_workflow` and `paperclip_finish`.
  > - Flatten these 8 schemas. `handoff_case` is now one object with `to` (optional, `"done"`), and `case` is one object with optional `id` and `create`. Descriptions on the properties state which fields each variant needs.
- fix(ui): save new tasks in the background (#20831)
  > - Add an account-scoped queue around the existing durable submit hook. Keep each request and creation key before closing the composer. Send queued requests in order.
  > - Keep submitted draft settings and files separate from the next draft. Resume keyed requests after a reload. Keep legacy recovery records readable.
  > - Reopen a failed draft when the composer is free. If another draft is open, show a retry action beside it. An earlier receipt cannot clear the later draft or cause late navigation.
- fix(db): release a postgres.js connection's receive buffer once it is fully read (#21432)
  > - `patches/postgres@3.4.9.patch`: in `data()` of `src/connection.js` and `cjs/src/connection.js`, the driver now replaces the receive buffer with an empty buffer when it has handled every message in it. A partly read buffer is not changed. The other hunks of the patch are unchanged. I regenerated the patch with `pnpm patch postgres@3.4.9`, edited the two files, and ran `pnpm patch-commit`. I did not commit the lockfile change from that command.
  > - `packages/db/src/postgres-receive-buffer.test.ts` and `packages/db/src/__fixtures__/postgres-receive-buffer.mjs`: a regression test for ESM and CommonJS on real PostgreSQL.
  > - `packages/db/scripts/benchmark-postgres-receive-buffer.ts`: one command that reports heap, external and rss per connection, before and after large rows.
- fix(native): release a finished session's tool authority so the server heap does not grow per run (#21415)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: new `releaseIdleSessionToolAuthority(scope)`. It removes and revokes the scope's tool-authority epoch when no execution, authority initialization, settle, warm owner, or warm close holds the scope. It is called:
  > - A warm session that stays retained keeps its authority, so the next run in that session still drains the previous run's accounting events as before.
  > - `forgetWarmNativeSession` also calls it, so a warm session that a new session identity supersedes (`retireSupersededWarmNativeSessions`) releases its authority too (review finding).
- fix(environments): stop warm sandboxes once their warm window ends (#21410)
  > - New `server/src/services/warm-sandbox-expiry.ts` owns warm sandbox expiry:
  > - Acquisition (plugin and built-in sandbox paths) records the run's warm window on the lease (`warmSandboxIdleTimeoutMs`).
  > - `nextEnvironmentCleanupAt` includes the earliest warm window end. The environment cleanup worker sleeps until that deadline. It also wakes on every lease release and runs at boot, so a sandbox left running across a restart or deploy is reconciled. There is no interval scan.
- fix(connections): show the setup card unless the person asked to set up the connection (#21400)
  > - `server/src/services/connection-intents.ts`: for a single unconnected tool match, read the latest human message on the task. Give the configure instruction only when its first clause opens with a setup verb and names the matched app ("Connect Notion, then ...", "Can you set up Notion?"). "Connect Slack, then read my Notion" does not count for Notion. Otherwise tell the agent not to create a connection or draft. For a setup question, the agent answers in plain words and links the catalog setup page `/<prefix>/apps/connect?source=<app>`, because `connection_request` ends the turn before any answer text is shown. For a data request, the agent calls `connection_request` to show the setup card. A saved custom connection always uses the card. The check is conservative: a miss never configures anything.
  > - `server/src/services/tool-access/oauth/authorization-start.ts`: the agent handoff link (`authorizationUrl`, also `requiredUserAction.setupPath`) is now a relative app route, not built from the server's redirect origin.
  > - Tests: a red/green service test for the three messages, and the handoff test now requires a relative link.
- fix(heartbeat): let Stop take a task out of the agent's queue (#21407)
  > - `POST /heartbeat-runs/:runId/cancel`: when the run was queued, the route calls the new `returnStoppedWaitingTaskToTodo` after the cancel.
  > - New `server/src/services/heartbeat/stopped-waiting-task.ts`: one guarded update moves an `in_progress` task to `todo` and logs `issue.updated` (reason `stopped_while_waiting`). It changes the task only if the run never started, the task is still assigned to that agent and not to a person, the task lock is free or held by that run, and no other live run exists for the task.
  > - The route keeps its company and run-read checks and still logs `heartbeat.cancelled`. Recovery already stands down for an operator stop, so no new run starts for the task.
- fix(evals): wait for the primary save, select the offered Launch choice, and pace the keyboard reorder (#21396)
  > - `runtime-primary.ts`: wait for the browser's own `PUT /primary-agent/me` that names Morgan, require 2xx, and poll the read-back until Morgan is primary before leaving the page. Look for the "My primary agent" crown only in the header that holds Morgan's avatar. Add `isPrimarySaveRequest`. Oracle `runtime-primary.v6`.
  > - `chat-linked-question.ts` / `internal-tasks.ts`: add `chosenTaskOption`. It finds the one option of the bound saved question that names the Launch task's identifier (a setup literal) and no other candidate's. It uses the option id for the rendered DOM id and the payload's submit label. It does not read option wording. The harness selects that option, checks `aria-checked`, submits, and waits for the `respond` POST. The old text and "Other" paths stay for other cards. The checks that nothing changes before the answer and that only COM-1 changes after it are unchanged.
  > - `chat-catalog-contract.ts` / `internal-tasks.ts`: a chosen option saves no comment, so the flow counted only one human action and failed `catalog-original-human-query`. `tasks.ambiguous-task` now joins the cases that may count a bound UI answer. The case declares `expectedHumanQuestionAnswer` only after the harness answered through the choice, and names the chosen question and option. The flow then verifies the answered card independently: answered by this person, exactly the Launch option and no typed text, one audit event, and one continuation run after it. Oracle `internal.ambiguous-task.v8`.
- fix: name activity actors and explain stopped tasks in plain words (#21406)
  > - `server/src/services/activity.ts`, `server/src/routes/activity.ts`, `packages/shared/src/types/activity.ts`: company and task activity reads add `actorName` (agent name, member name, "Board" for `local-board`, "Paperclip" for the system). Raw ids stay in their existing fields.
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: the existing "no raw IDs or internal codes" rule now names checkout, native run and local-board as internal terms, and tells the agent to name an actor by `actorName`.
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/service.ts`: the notice is now "<agent> can't run right now, so Paperclip stopped this task instead of retrying it. It is still assigned to them.
