To continue, resume or fix the agent, or assign the task to someone else." The manual-pause skip now covers `in_progress` as well as `todo` work with no run.
- fix(chat): do not post a completion report that an earlier reply already gave (#21401)
  > - `server/src/services/chat-completion-delivery.ts`: `recordCompletedHandoffRead` appends a `chat_handoff.completed_read` run event when the conversation agent reads its own handed-off task in the done state (with the task's status version).
  > - `prepareChatCompletionTurn`: a delivery is marked delivered (with the earlier reply as its response) when an earlier reply of the same agent in the conversation was posted after completion, links the task, and comes from a run that read the task at that exact done status. Both conditions are structural (run event and task reference), not reply wording. A reply that links the task without such a read (for example "Morgan is working on it", from a read before completion) does not count.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat.ts`: when every delivery of the completion turn was already reported, the run ends as succeeded before the conversation turn starts, with no model call.
- fix(release): retry Cloud readiness jobs that lost their Spot runner (#21399)
  > - `scripts/cloud-readiness-runner-loss.mjs` (new): classifies a failed readiness attempt with the existing PR classifier (`.github/scripts/runner-loss-rerun.mjs`). It is a runner loss only when every causing job (`failure`, `timed_out`, `startup_failure`) lost its runner. Cancelled siblings are fallout. A run that is still going is cancelled first, then `rerun-failed-jobs` runs on the next poll. The ceiling is readiness attempt 4. Writes go only to `cancel` or `rerun-failed-jobs` of a canonical run, after an identity check.
  > - `scripts/cloud-source-verification.mjs` (canary gate in `release.yml`): on `SourceVerificationFailed`, ask the retry first. The source wait goes from 60 to 90 minutes.
  > - `scripts/watch-release-source.mjs` (master image watcher in `docker.yml`): on a runner loss, keep the image attempt alive and wait for the retried proof. Do not cancel it.
- fix(browser-tracing): end hidden loads, cap timeouts, backfill hard-load API spans, record list view (#21392)
  > - `page-load-recorder.ts`: new outcome `hidden`. A load that starts hidden, or hides before it is ready, ends at the moment the tab hid (same rule `web-vitals` uses for LCP/FCP). For a hard load, the controller reads Chromium `visibility-state` entries, so a hide before tracing started is caught. A redirect while still hidden renames the hidden span instead of starting a second one.
  > - `page-load-recorder.ts`: any end observed after start + 30 s is a `timeout` that ends at exactly start + 30 s. `paperclip.page.suspended=true` marks an end more than 5 s past the deadline or a gap of more than 5 s between frames.
  > - `controller.ts`: a `PerformanceObserver` (`type: resource`, `buffered: true`) keeps only this origin's `/api/` fetch/XHR entries (max 500) until the SDK loads. It does not depend on the 250-entry resource buffer.
- fix(dispatch): start the assigned-work sweep's continuations and count only system recovery for the recovery share (#21391)
  > - `server/src/services/chat-control-recovery-stop.ts`: for an automatic continuation that has no `retryOfRunId`, the gate now walks to the task's latest earlier run (by `issueId`, `nativeIssueId` or the context task id, in the same company) and checks that run. If the task has no earlier run, the result is `clear`. A continuation with `retryOfRunId` is unchanged. A chat close of the previous run still stops the continuation.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`: the recovery-share counter reads which recent recovery-reason starts were person-originated, with the same source-chain query that the dispatcher already uses (`listPersonOriginSourceRunIds`, at most 4 ids). Only system recovery starts reset the share. This matches the in-pass rule (`isSystemRecoveryRun`).
- fix(runner): give agent task and workspace reads the workspace page href (#21384)
  > - `server/src/services/task-workspace-tools.ts`: add `withWorkspacePageHref` and `apiResultMayHaveWorkspace`. They add the page `href` to the workspace in successful agent reads of `GET /api/issues/{id}` (`currentExecutionWorkspace`), `GET /api/issues/{id}/workspace` (`workspace`) and `GET /api/execution-workspaces/{id}`. The function uses the same `href` builder as `get_workspace`. It adds no `href` when the page is off, when the read failed, or when the workspace is in a different company.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/paperclip-runner-tool-authority.ts`: apply the function to the results of the task bridge tools (`get_task`) and `call_api`. The tool authority reads the page setting only for these three operations.
  > - Tests: unit tests for the function, and an integration test through the real tool authority and API. The integration test checks `get_task` and `call_api` with workspace pages on (the `href` is present) and off (no `href`).
- fix(heartbeat): start each claimed run at once so its controller lease cannot lapse unrenewed (#21242)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`: `startNextQueuedRunForAgent` starts each run's execution right after its own claim, not after the whole batch. The concurrency cap, the agent start lock and the run-admission check before each claim are unchanged. If a later claim throws, the runs claimed before it are already executing, not stranded. After merging master, the loop walks master's queued-run dispatcher (#21234: person-initiated runs first, paced recovery delivery): each successful claim is reported to the dispatcher (`dispatcher.claimed`) and then started at once, so master's order and pacing are unchanged. Master's long-queued-run bookkeeping (#21349) is kept too: a claim that returns nothing is still recorded as declined (`noteDeclinedAgedClaim`), and a successful claim still clears that record before the run starts.
  > - `server/src/services/legacy-controller-lease.ts`: `watchLegacyControllerLease` renews immediately when the claim-time lease has less than 50 s left (older than one renewal interval). It uses the same conditional renewal statement. That statement extends only a lease that this boot still holds on a running row. The deadline is unchanged.
  > - Tests: red/green tests for both changes. A test also proves that a late watch on a revoked, reaped or lapsed lease still loses it and does not change the row.
- fix(evals): state the product's handoff authority in the handoff-same-task workflow fixture (#21383)
  > - Replace one sentence in the fixture's workflow document text. New text: "Ordinary task authority applies to the handoff: the assignee may hand off its own primary task unless another run owns that task."
  > - No grader, run bound or oracle change.
- fix(retry): move a recorded continuation forward before its delivery (#21379)
  > - `admitTaskRetry` in `server/src/services/task-retry.ts` does not return `alreadyMovedOn` when `undeliveredContinuation` finds a recorded, undelivered continuation for the task. The check runs under the task row lock.
  > - A new test in `fresh-continuation-retry-delivery.test.ts` covers the three record kinds: `fresh_isolated_workspace`, `writer_ended` and native.
  > - The presentation code is unchanged. The interface does not offer the action in this state yet.
- fix(recovery): do not continue from a latest run read while it was still finishing (#21365)
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/service.ts`: after the live-path check, the stranded sweep skips a non-review task whose latest-run snapshot was not terminal. Review tasks keep their participant-run check, which already does this.
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/service.ts`: an optional test hook, `afterStrandedLatestRunRead`, runs after the sweep reads the latest run, alongside the existing `beforeStaleLockClear` and `beforeOrphanedRunTerminalWrite` hooks.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/heartbeat-process-recovery.test.ts`: a regression test finishes the run between the two reads and expects no continuation.
- fix(evals): accept the case owner's direct handoff from chat in handoff-same-task (#21372)
  > - Replace `normal-primary-execution-authority` with `owner-execution-authority`. It finds the run that saved the handoff comment. That run must be a Morgan run that started after the person's request, not the stopped run. It must be the single answering chat run, or a Morgan primary-task run woken by a comment that the chat run saved. If the handoff records an owning execution, it must be that same run.
  > - Add `every-run-has-a-handoff-role`: one stopped run, one chat answer, the recording run, and one Riley review. Any other run fails.
  > - Run bounds: 3 to 4 runs, `cancelled: 1`, `succeeded: 2..3`. Oracle version `case-handoff.structural.v3`. The descriptor JSON matches.
- fix(recovery): deliver a recorded continuation as the person's Retry run, keeping its isolation (#21378)
  > - `fresh-continuation-retry-fold.ts`:
  > - `heartbeat/queue.ts` (wake admission, issue transaction): a non-system, non-delivery wake with no execution blocker takes the pending continuation. The run is inserted as before, then delivered as the continuation. The fold after a delivery wake now applies to every fresh-continuation kind. No change to ranking or to the claim loop.
  > - `execution-recovery-resolution.ts`: the delivery pass folds a saved Retry for every fresh-continuation kind. The superseded-run fold keys on `recoveryActionId`.
- fix(native-runtime): a remote Runner reattachment continues a turn the Runner can recover (#21362)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: `resumeInterruptedTurn` is now also true for a `reattach_remote_runner` restart-recovery claim. The runtime still continues the turn only when the Runner itself sends `turn.failed provider_turn_lost_on_restore` with `recoverable: true` for the checkpointed active turn.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: one paragraph describes this behaviour, and says that a lost turn that still ends the run keeps `native_provider_terminal_failed`.
  > - Tests:
- fix(runner): ask once more for a text answer when a turn that did tool work ends empty twice (#21359)
  > - `OpenCodeServerDriver`: when a turn already did tool work and the correction comes back empty, the driver sends one more request. That request asks for a short plain-text answer first and the completion call after it. The text does not depend on tool-call delivery, so a chat reply arrives even if the host drops the call again. An execution task still gets its completion call.
  > - A turn makes at most two correction requests. A turn without tool work keeps the existing single correction.
  > - Only tool work that the active turn reports counts. A late tool update from an earlier turn does not earn an extra request.
- fix(native): relaunch a remote Runner that exited for a fresh bootstrap (#21374)
  > - `remote-runner-recovery-diagnostics.ts`: new `exitedRunnerRequestedFreshBootstrap` (diagnostic) and `runnerStateRequestsFreshBootstrap` (saved state). They accept lifecycle `recoverable_failure` with `lease_expired_requires_bootstrap`, `transport_reconnect_grace_exceeded` or `transport_reconnect_deadline_exceeded`. These are the three codes the Rust Runner records after `shutdown_preserving_cleanup`. The state check also requires no pending terminal delivery and no warm transition.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts` (`verifyRemoteRunnerOrSettledExit`): when the exact Runner process is proven gone and its diagnostic names one of these exits, the server reads the saved state again. If the state has the same six identity fields and still names that exit, the reattachment launches a Runner from it instead of failing. The outbox does not need to be empty and a turn may be active: the new Runner replays from the same cursor. The attempt bound is the one the reattachment already admits (3, or 4 for an unanswered-Runner replacement claim). Other exits are unchanged.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts` (host preparation in `createRunnerdBackend`): a resumed sandbox for a reattachment that did not adopt a process re-reads the Runner state. When it still has the same six identity fields and names the fresh-bootstrap exit, preparation keeps it in place, as for an adopted live Runner. It no longer fails with `runner_harness_state_mismatch: resumed_not_suspended` (found by Greptile).
- fix(presentation): a provider failure the system is still recovering shows as working, not as needing Retry (#21369)
  > - `server/src/services/task-interruption-presentation.ts`:
  > - No change to recovery decision code, the fallback, or the Retry path. No new record or marker. The coordinator row already records "still deciding".
- fix(evals): grade full cohort 11 cases on saved state and make the interaction DOM id unique (#21366)
  > - `ui/.../TaskChatInteractionCard.tsx`: only the timeline item has the `interaction-<id>` page anchor. The composer takeover card has no id. Every variant has `data-interaction-id`.
  > - Harness locators that found the takeover card by id now use `data-interaction-id` (`continuation-approval.ts`, `runner.spec.ts`, `external-secret-proposal.ts`, `external-company-secret.ts`, `external-team-configure.ts`, and their fixtures).
  > - `integrator-case-close.ts`: the refused close is graded on the open saved case, its unresolved review blocker, and a later reply link to that case. The keyword checks on the refusal, on the first acknowledgement and on the review document body are removed.
- fix(retry): continue from recorded work when saved files cannot be recovered (#21363)
  > - `savedFilesUnrecoverable` and `unrecoverableSavedFilesFailure` (`system-recovery-state.ts`) are the one rule for saved files that no recovery can apply. The rule is true for a spent recovery whose failure is a permanent source refusal: `source_unavailable`, `allocation_ownership_ambiguous` or `historical_source_missing`. It is false for `source_binding_invalid`, because a new recovery generation can still verify that source. It is false for `archive_unsafe` and `snapshot_verification_failed`, because a fresh attempt still imports earlier saved snapshots and would fail before the agent starts. It is also false after a fresh attempt is recorded.
  > - Admission (`explicit-native-continuation.ts`) no longer parks a Retry of an accepted result in `saved_work_recovery_*` when every live recovery of the run is unrecoverable. The Retry takes the existing fresh-workspace path (`fresh_isolated_workspace`). That path keeps all stop proofs, leaves the source sandbox and workspace untouched, and starts a fresh session.
  > - When such a Retry starts, the task gets one system comment. The comment says that Paperclip could not recover the unsaved files, why, and that the task continues from its recorded work (comments, documents, attachments and pushed commits). The settled hold records the same text.
- fix(runner): record an OpenCode call's serving host when the call ends (#21358)
  > - `opencode-server-driver.ts`: the provider proxy keeps the host it reads for each call and records it when that call's response ends. It no longer clears the recorded host when a request starts. A call that ends without a readable host (skipped, non-2xx, redirect, or failed) records no host.
  > - `opencode-server-driver.test.ts`: a new red/green test completes one call. Then it starts a second call that has already sent its first chunk with another host, and keeps it open. The test checks that the turn's usage names the first call's host. Then it ends the second call and checks that the next turn names the second call's host.
  > - `doc/run-log-events.md`: the "OpenRouter serving host" section now says the host is recorded when a call ends, and why.
- fix(recovery): restart a remote bootstrap again after it waited for a busy agent directory (#21370)
  > - Restart recovery accepts a new remote proof, `remote_bootstrap_restart_deferred_before_launch`. It applies when all of these are true:
  > - The run then goes through the same resumption admission as the other settled remote retries (agent invokable, task not paused, dependencies ready, no competing run).
  > - Added red/green integration tests built from the staging shape: the run restarts again (generation 3), and a runner identity, a provider event, or a checkpoint saved during the wait keeps it held.
- fix(evals): grade the company-secret binding confirmation from saved state (#21368)
  > - In `external-company-secret.ts`, `human-attributed-and-confirmed-after-authorization` no longer checks the reply text. It requires:
  > - The observation now always collects the source conversation's saved comments (before, it did so only on the follow-up and card paths), so every path can bind the reply to its run and time.
  > - The truthfulness pattern for the proposal phase and the other wording patterns are dropped from this check. The credential-in-chat guard elsewhere in the case is unchanged.
- fix(recovery): continue a native terminal failure whose provider is never proven stopped (#21364)
  > - `native-safe-replacement.ts`: an unverified-ownership decision now waits only until 5 minutes after the run's terminal time (`finished_at`, else the finalization's last update), compared with the database `now()`. After that, the pass records `replacementDenied` and the existing watchdog hold, with a plain-language next action. The expiry is checked again inside the locked transaction, so two servers cannot both act, and a successor that a concurrent pass committed first still wins.
  > - The deadline applies only to `native_provider_terminal_failed` runs whose finalization has no lease owner. Other failure codes and person-owned causes are unchanged.
  > - The bound reuses `RECOVERY_CONTINUATION_MAX_HOLD_MS` (5 minutes) from `recovery-continuation-pacing.ts`, as `NATIVE_OWNERSHIP_PROOF_BOUND_MS`.
- fix(runner): treat tool-call markup in OpenCode text as an unexecuted call (#21361)
  > - Added `tool-call-markup.ts` in the OpenCode driver. It detects leaked tool-call markup in one linear scan: DSML and DeepSeek call-token sentinels anywhere, and `<function_calls>` / `<tool_call>` wrappers only at a line start with a call body. It ignores fenced code blocks.
  > - The driver holds markup (and a trailing fragment that can still become markup) out of streamed progress text.
  > - A completed text part that contains markup is not a final-answer candidate. The driver emits the `opencode_tool_call_markup_discarded` diagnostic and records the position, so earlier text cannot become the reply either.
- fix(runner): stop a model from resending the same rejected tool call (#21360)
  > - Add `RepeatedToolFailureGuard` (`server/src/services/native-runtime/repeated-tool-failure-guard.ts`). It keys each failure by tool name and a hash of the canonical arguments.
  > - Count only failures that must repeat for identical arguments: `400`/`422` refusals and schema errors. Other errors, such as `503`, pass through unchanged and the call still runs.
  > - On the second identical failure, keep the original error text and add the names of the arguments that arrived, a note that a missing argument did not arrive, and that Paperclip will not run this exact call again.
- fix(recovery): settle an older run's saved-work hold, fold a waiting Retry into the isolated continuation, and plain recovery wording (#21348)
  > - Older-run holds (`fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`, `modules/run-dispatch/adapters/postgres.ts`):
  > - A person's Retry and the isolated continuation (`heartbeat/queue.ts`, `execution-recovery-resolution.ts`, new `fresh-continuation-retry-fold.ts`):
  > - Plain wording (server and UI together):
- fix(recovery): enqueue a fresh interruption's continuation at once and record why a folded wake did not start (#21354)
  > - `deliverReconciledExecutions` now sorts pending continuations into two classes by when the source run ended. "Fresh" means that the source run ended within `RECOVERY_BACKLOG_AGE_MS` (30 minutes), which is the same value the dispatcher uses to tell fresh recovery from backlog.
  > - A fresh continuation is enqueued without the queue-depth room check. It is limited only by the per-agent limit (one fresh continuation per agent per pass) and the per-company cap per pass. Among fresh records, the newest interruption goes first. A record whose wake was refused moves behind records that were not attempted yet.
  > - Backlog continuations keep their earlier rules. These are the queue-depth room check, the bypass after the 5-minute hold bound, oldest held first, and one per agent per pass.
- fix(presentation): a task with a continuation waiting to start shows as continuing, not as needing Retry (#21353)
  > - `taskInterruptionPresentations` in `server/src/services/task-interruption-presentation.ts`: a primary fresh-continuation record with `continuationDelivery: "pending"` reads as "continuing" with no time limit. Before, this applied only for 15 minutes (`RECOVERY_CONTINUATION_DELIVERY_BOUND_MS`). The state is the existing `working` state that the code already uses for a recorded continuation; no new state is added. The UI shows the agent's turn as queued and shows no Retry button.
  > - A refused delivery attempt (`continuationDeliveryAttemptedAt`) does not end the state. The record stays `pending` and the delivery pass tries again on the next pass, so the system still owns the next step.
  > - The select no longer reads the hold time columns, because the projection does not use them now.
- fix(evals): grade the company-secret setup reply by its rendered link (#21350)
  > - Add `external-secret-setup-link.ts`: the shared rendered-link check. It finds a visible link in the reply's rendered message to this company's `/company/settings/secrets`. It clicks the link, requires the case's entry control to be visible, and then returns to the conversation. The personal-secret check (`external-personal-secret-link.ts`) now uses it, with no change in behavior.
  > - Add `external-company-secret-link.ts`. The company entry is **New secret** on the company **Secrets** tab, which is selected if needed. The company link takes no query.
  > - `external-company-secret.ts`:
- fix(recovery): bound automatic attempts per task, one deadline per interruption, and batch the invariant report's reads (#21347)
  > - `issue-tree-control.ts`: a new batched pause-hold read. For a set of tasks it uses at most two statements: one to find any active hold, then one recursive ancestor read. It has the same depth bound and cycle guard as the per-issue gate. `assigned-work-sweep.ts` uses it, and reads the org chart once per batch to judge each assignee.
  > - New `recovery/task-automatic-attempts.ts`: one per-task ledger of automatic attempts in the last hour (sweep continuations plus the scheduler's `transient_failure` retries). The sweep counts both for its cap and its spacing (`assigned-work-sweep.ts`). The scheduler's transient retry lane (`heartbeat/retries.ts`) reads the same ledger. Once the sweep has continued a task in the hour, a transient retry that would exceed the cap of six is reported as exhausted. If the sweep never continued a task, the scheduler keeps its own chain bound.
  > - `native-finalization-reconciler.ts` (`claimNativeSessionResumptions`): the compare-and-set also requires the other owner's lease to have lapsed by `clock_timestamp()`.
- fix(dispatch): bound how long a queued run waits and keep a person's interrupted Retry person-first (#21349)
  > - `queued-run-dispatch-order.ts`: a ready run that has waited for one hour (`QUEUED_RUN_AGING_MS`) moves up to at least the work tier.
  > - `queued-run-dispatch-order.ts`: every fourth start (`OLDEST_DISPATCH_SHARE`) goes to the longest-waiting ready run that has aged, whatever its class or priority. The dispatcher tracks this share inside one pass.
  > - `queue.ts`: the dispatcher reads the agent's recent starts to know how many starts passed over the oldest run. One query now serves both the recovery share and the oldest-run share.
- fix(evals): grade the personal-secret setup reply by its rendered link (#21344)
  > - Add `tests/runner-e2e/chat-catalog-domains/external-personal-secret-link.ts`. It finds a visible link in the reply's rendered message (`#comment-<id>`) whose target is this company's `/company/settings/secrets`. An optional `definition` query must name the required definition. Other origins, companies, paths, credentials, fragments and query keys are refused. It clicks the link and requires the person's own-value entry to be visible. A link that names the definition opens that definition's detail sheet, so the check requires **Set my value** in the sheet, which must show `PERSONAL_PUBLISHING_KEY`. Otherwise it selects **My secrets** if that tab is not active and requires a visible **Set value** button on the row bound to the required definition (`my-secret-<definitionId>`). It then returns to the conversation and records the proof as `personal-secret-reply-link.json`.
  > - In `external-personal-secret.ts`, the setup reply is no longer checked against wording patterns. It must still be the agent's own comment from the bound run. The grade uses the recorded link proof, which must belong to a reply of the initial run (a turn can post several messages). The reply link is now followed before the human-entry phase.
  > - The confirmation and question paths are unchanged. When a question or confirmation card already carries the handoff, the reply link is not checked, so an optional link cannot fail those paths.
- fix(recovery): a queued continuation is not shown blocked, recent temporary failures continue, and notices say what a person can do (#21336)
  > - **Queued continuation is not shown blocked** (`explicit-native-continuation.ts`, `recovery/assigned-work-invariant.ts`, `recovery/assigned-work-sweep.ts`):
  > - **Recent temporary failures are continued** (new `recovery/stranded-hold-continuation.ts`, `recovery/assigned-work-sweep.ts`, `fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`, `recovery/stranded-notice.ts`):
  > - **Plain recovery notices** (`recovery/service.ts`, `recovery/stranded-notice.ts`, `recovery/successful-run-handoff.ts`): see the wording table below.
- fix(native): finish the run from a provider turn a timed-out start left running (#21338)
  > - `native-session-runtime.ts`: a terminal that the recovered session newly settled now counts as the adopted provider terminal when the saved checkpoint had no active turn. The runtime consumes that terminal instead of starting a fresh turn.
  > - New `runnerd-abandoned-turn-start-reattach.test.ts` with the real runnerd and the fake Codex app server. Each attempt runs in a fresh module graph, as each attempt runs in its own executor process in production. The first attempt times out on turn.start against an unreachable remote Runner and leaves it alive.
- fix(runner): keep configured secret values out of OpenCode output, storage and model requests (#21330)
  > - **Secret names cross to the runner.** `configuredEnvironmentProjection` takes the controller's secret key names and adds `PAPERCLIP_SECRET_ENV_KEYS`. This marker holds only the names of configured entries that came from secrets. It travels with the configured projection across the TypeScript and Rust runner boundaries (`configured-environment.ts`, `configured_environment.rs`). The server passes `secretKeys` in `runtime-execution.ts`.
  > - **Command output is filtered before OpenCode reads it.** For a launch that has configured secrets, the runner sets OpenCode's `shell` to a small POSIX wrapper (`drivers/opencode/output-custody.ts`). The wrapper runs the shell OpenCode would use anyway. It sends stdout and stderr through an `awk` filter that replaces exact secret values. OpenCode never receives the raw bytes, so its streamed previews, its database and its model requests stay clean. The wrapper keeps the exit status. It does not wait for background processes. The values come from the environment that OpenCode already holds and are never written to disk.
  > - **Other tool results are rewritten before they are saved.** A local OpenCode plugin rewrites completed non-shell results (file reads, searches, MCP results) in `tool.execute.after`. OpenCode runs that hook before it stores or returns the result. The wrapper refuses to run any command until the plugin has started for the current launch (per-launch nonce). So no command can write a secret to a file that a later read returns without filtering.
- fix(native): let a same-run reattachment take over its own retained remote Runner cleanup (#21328)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/native-session-runtime.ts`: `executeNativeSession` takes a new option `retainedRunnerReattachment`. With a remote cleanup scope, admission retires quarantine entries that belong to the same run in the same remote scope. Removal stops later close retries. A close attempt that is still in flight stays an admission owner until it settles, so the old and new controllers do not overlap. Other runs, other sandboxes and local backends keep the gate.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: sets `retainedRunnerReattachment` only for a `reattach_remote_runner` recovery of the same run.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: records the takeover.
- fix(recovery): a run that fails before the agent is asked anything continues by itself on a fresh computer (#21297)
  > - New typed cause `native_failed_before_provider_turn` (`server/src/services/native-runtime/failed-before-provider-turn.ts`). The executor raises it only when the launch gate fails, the run was not cancelled, and the Runner turn ledger proves that no turn command was issued. The proof is taken at the failure point, before the session closes. In all other cases the original error is rethrown and today's path stays.
  > - The executor quarantines the failed run's session root, so the successor starts in a new root.
  > - `heartbeat/predecessor-retirement-fallback.ts` accepts the new cause: it records the proof on `runnerProfileJson.failedBeforeProviderTurn`, marks the run's reusable sandbox leases for replacement, and schedules the successor in the existing `interrupted_preparation` lane (same cap of 12 and the same backoff).
- fix(recovery): repair stale pending recovery states and tell the fresh run what the cut-off run already did (#21257)
  > - Invariant bounds (`server/src/services/recovery/assigned-work-invariant.ts`, `server/src/services/recovery/assigned-work-sweep.ts`):
  > - A continuation whose delivery is stuck is repaired, not just no longer counted (`server/src/services/recovery-continuation-bounds.ts`, `execution-recovery-resolution.ts`, `fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`):
  > - Opening note (`server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`):
- fix(native): keep a reattached Runner alive when its provider recovery deadline ends (#21321)
  > - Add `NativeProviderRecoveryDeadlineError` (same message as before) and use it for the provider recovery deadline in the native session runtime.
  > - In the Codex driver, a recovery aborted by this deadline detaches the controller (no `turn.stop`, no suspend, no signal). A Stop and every other abort still close and stop the Runner.
  > - On the server, `nativeRecoveryCommandUnanswered` recognizes the deadline. The failure records `adoptedRunnerUnanswered`, so the next claim carries `replaceUnansweredRunner`. That attempt fences the old Runner lineage, launches a fresh Runner from the saved journal and resumes the same provider thread (the #21272 path). It does not adopt the slow Runner again for another 900 s.
- fix(runner): wait for a slow Codex turn start while it reports progress (#21329)
  > - `runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: the default wait for an accepted turn's `turn/started` is now 90 s (measured max 76 s), not 30 s. An explicit `turnStartTimeoutMs` keeps its old bound.
  > - The wait restarts its window each time the runner commits a provider notice, or a runner diagnostic that names the accepted turn. The total wait stops at 180 s (option `turnStartProgressCeilingMs`, so tests can lower it). A provider relaunch after the accepted `turn.start` does not extend it.
  > - The adoption check (runner snapshot proves the accepted turn live) now runs before each window end, not only before the first one. A Runner exit and a runner failure of the pending turn still fail at once.
- fix(native-runtime): deliver assigned gateway tools to every native harness through the PRP relay (#21274)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-resume.ts`: the relay marker in the tool contract fingerprint is unconditional.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: the text about the tool contract fingerprint now states the rule, the tool names and the cutover.
- fix(recovery): deliver every pending continuation within a bounded time (#21324)
  > - `deliverReconciledExecutions` in `server/src/services/execution-recovery-resolution.ts`: a continuation held for 5 minutes or more is read even when its agent has a queued recovery run. Before, only the full-queue check let it through.
  > - Each page of the pass now reads at most one candidate per agent (`row_number() over (partition by agent)`), so one agent's backlog cannot fill the page budget and hide other agents' continuations.
  > - A continuation counts against the company's limit only when its wake puts a run in the queue. A continuation that was invalidated or replaced by a newer run does not use its agent's turn.
- fix(environments): continue on a new sandbox when an idle runner sandbox never starts (#21323)
  > - `reusable-sandbox-resume-replacement.ts`: `reusableSandboxReplacementIsSafe` becomes `reusableSandboxReplacementPlan`. It returns `retire` (same rules as before), `keep`, or `null`. It returns `keep` for a runner lease without a verified harness backup only when the caller passes `providerCannotStart`. All other conditions still apply: idle lease, same workspace, clean workspace, committed snapshot, this run is the writer, and no access refusal.
  > - Add `keepReplacedReusableSandboxLease`. After the replacement lease is recorded, one guarded write marks the old lease with `reusableSandboxKeptAfterReplacement`. The lease keeps its status and its sandbox. Nothing destroys it.
  > - `environment-runtime.ts`: set `providerCannotStart` only when the idle start deadline was extended and the run then used all of it on timeouts. Lease selection skips a lease with the keep marker, so later runs do not spend time on it and obsolete-lease cleanup does not destroy it.
- fix(native): give a Runner whose first unanswered read came on the last attempt one replacement attempt (#21322)
  > - `nativeUnansweredRunnerReplacementDisposition` (executor): when attempt 3 fails because its adopted live Runner left a recovery read unanswered, and that attempt was not itself a replacement, the failure stays `retryable_failure` (`native_session_interrupted`, retry in 30 s) instead of `native_session_retry_exhausted`.
  > - `claimNativeRestartRecoveries`: a failed remote retry at attempt 3 with `adoptedRunnerUnanswered` is not treated as an exhausted budget. The claim is `reattach_remote_runner` at provider attempt 4 with `replaceUnansweredRunner`.
  > - `nativeAdmittedProviderAttemptLimit` (executor admission gate): a `reattach_remote_runner` claim with `replaceUnansweredRunner` is admitted up to provider attempt 4. Every other claim keeps the limit of 3.
- fix(heartbeat): rank a person's joined comment first and refill free run slots (#21277)
  > - `queuedRunDispatcherForAgent` also reads person wakes whose `runId` is a queued run of the agent (by `run_id IN (queued run ids)`, so an earlier saved comment that a run adopted is included). A run with such a wake ranks as person-initiated and is never counted as system recovery.
  > - `listUndispatchedQueuedRuns` now returns a queued run when its agent has fewer `running` plus `scheduled_retry` runs than its `maxConcurrentRuns`, and its task has no `running` or `scheduled_retry` run. The re-drive backstop and the ownership I7 check use this query.
  > - The query counts each agent's live runs once, in one grouped subquery that is joined to the queued runs.
- fix(evals): admit the in-flight chat completion follow-up on a settled source (#21313)
  > - Add `chatCatalogSourceCompletionFollowUp` to `tests/runner-e2e/chat-catalog-run-policy.ts`. It identifies the lead's `chat_task_completed` run on the source whose every completion update names separate work in the run ledger.
  > - In the `source-settled-with-active-work` check, admit at most one such follow-up while it is `queued`, `running` or `succeeded`. Accept a source `conversationState` of `active` only while that follow-up is in flight.
  > - Keep the other rules strict. The original answer must exist and must have succeeded. Any other active, failed or extra source run still fails. The declared run-count bound still applies.
- fix(recovery): keep the sandbox that holds abandoned unsaved files out of replacement and cleanup (#21304)
  > - New `server/src/services/remote-workspace-abandoned-files-hold.ts` holds the single predicate for "this lease holds abandoned files". It is one SQL fragment (`abandonedWorkspaceFilesHeldSql`) plus a TypeScript wrapper (`abandonedWorkspaceFilesHeldOnLease`). A lease is held while a workspace in its company has an abandoned-epoch receipt whose `location.providerLeaseId` equals the lease's provider lease id, and that receipt has no `released` object. A receipt written by the current release (no new fields) counts as unreleased.
  > - Replace and park. A failed resume of a held sandbox still lets the run replace it, so the task continues at once on a new sandbox restored from the last committed save. `retireReplacedReusableSandboxLease` parks the old lease as `retained` with the `abandonedWorkspaceFilesParked` marker (failure reason `abandoned_workspace_files_held`) instead of `pending_cleanup`. `reusableLeaseCanBeResumed` and `reusableLeaseCanBeCleanedUp` skip a parked lease, so later runs never select it again. The check is a metadata read with no query, so the run path does no extra work.
  > - Every path that can destroy a sandbox now honours the hold:
- fix(docker): keep local build output out of the Runner image's build context (#21312)
  > - `.dockerignore`: two rules.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/runner-image-identity.test.ts`: one new test. For each package among the identity's inputs it collects the `outDir` of every tsconfig a script compiles with `tsc -p`, the `target` directory beside every Cargo manifest a script names, and the `outDir` of every Vite configuration. It requires that the last `.dockerignore` rule matching each directory is an exclusion. Today that is eight directories. A new output directory inside the inputs fails the test until it has a rule.
  > - No image build reads that directory from the host. The Runner stages, the `build` stage of the application image and `docker/daytona-runner` all compile the kernel themselves (`tsc -p`, not incremental), and the eval kernel is a development dependency of the Runner only.
- fix(retry): offer Retry only when admission can take it, and start held saved-work Retries (#21315)
  > - `hasVerifiedPreparationLog` (`verified-preparation-log.ts`) now accepts a log made only of host notices that the host writes before it hands the turn to an adapter: the mentioned-skills line (first record only, checked against the saved skill selection) and the workspace freshness and session-reset notices. Every record must have the same attempt, sequential `seq`, `stdout`, and the excerpt and watermark must match. Any other line still keeps the run held.
  > - `verifiedUnchangedRestoredSnapshot` (`remote-workspace-snapshot-reuse-receipt.ts`) proves that a failed preparation's saved state is the snapshot it restored. It needs `executionStage: preparing`, `providerWorkStarted: false`, exactly one succeeded restore of that snapshot at that epoch and lease, and no snapshot save by the run. `verifiedWorkspaceRepairReceipt` uses it next to the existing reuse proof.
  > - `savedWorkOffersRetry` (`workspace-recovery-receipt.ts`) is the one rule for Retry on a saved-work hold. Retry is offered when the saved work is recovered and verified, or when recovery is exhausted in a state that only a Retry restarts (a new recovery generation, or the fresh-session fallback after `source_unavailable`). Retry is not offered while recovery re-arms by itself (a stop recheck or a newer capture policy), or while a copyback owner's stop is unconfirmed. The blocker uses this rule for the top-level `canRetry` and for `workspaceRecovery.canRetry`, so the two parts cannot contradict.
- fix(native): continue again when a deploy interrupts an automatic continuation (#21314)
  > - `restart-interrupted-turn.ts`: `lostControllerToRestart` finds restart evidence on the run: a `native.recovery.transition` with `restartKind: "graceful"` (shutdown), or a restart claim (`reattach_remote_runner`, `reattach_existing_runner`, `resume_dead_runner`, `bootstrap_incomplete`) without the `same_run_retry` trigger.
  > - Both `automatic_retry_used` refusals (a person's Retry that the hook requested, and a system retry of owed work) now continue when the run has restart evidence. A deploy interruption spends no budget, so a task that only deploys interrupt never runs out. Each continuation needs a new deploy during its run, so deploys bound it. A loss without restart evidence keeps the one-time rule. All other gates are unchanged: proven Runner exit, unknown-turn record, the issue still assigned and open, idempotency per run, and the Retry's admission gates.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md` describes the rule.
- fix(runner): withhold Claude's self-scheduling tools from Paperclip ACPX sessions (#21276)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/acpx/permission-policy.ts`: new constant `CLAUDE_SELF_SCHEDULING_TOOLS` with the four tool names.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/acpx/runtime-sandbox.ts`: the Claude `settings.json` now always has `permissions.deny` with the four tools, in each permission mode. The `allow` rules do not change.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/acpx/environment.ts`: `createSanitizedAcpxSpawnInput` sets `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_CRON=1` for each Claude launch. The host cannot override it.
- fix(recovery): a chat turn waiting on the person is not stranded work (#21302)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-completion.ts`: run completion reads the run with `unsafeFullResultJson` before it merges the native result. It no longer writes the read projection's reduced copy back.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-response-wake-wait.ts`: `hasCommittedNativeConversationTurnWait` checks the run's committed finalization decision (`conversation_turn_finished`) through `native_run_finalizations` and `status_decisions` for the conversation's own agent.
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/service.ts`: the sweep's finished-chat-turn skip also uses that durable check, so runs already saved with the reduced copy are recognized.
- fix(environments): verify workspaces on run leases and release settled tasks (#21289)
  > - Acquire the logical run lease before provider workspace verification and reuse it for preparation and execution.
  > - Preserve the existing session and workspace choices. Recompute workspace-dependent inputs only after a definite refusal; acquire credentials and managed runtime inputs once.
  > - Save the exact refusal and lease before cleanup. Use a workspace-scoped creation key only for the resulting fresh attempt.
- fix(queue): another agent's comment no longer hides a pending approval (#21303)
  > - `server/src/routes/issues.ts`: `findQueuedCommentWake` returns a later message queue only when it holds a message from a person. A queue with only agent comments yields to a pending saved response. With no saved response it is still returned, as before.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/issue-queued-comments-routes.test.ts`: the eval ordering (a fresh-session approval, then another agent's comment) keeps the approval as the queue, and a later person message in that queue still takes the view.
- fix(heartbeat): hold the new owner's run while the reassigned owner's run settles (#21301)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`: the `predecessor_settling_in_process` hold in `claimQueuedRun` also holds for another agent's native run on the same task when that run is terminal but its executor is still active in this process. A live run of another agent (for example a reviewer) does not hold it.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`: `notifyOtherAgentsQueuedOnSettledIssue` sends a queued-run dispatch signal when a native executor settles and another agent has a queued run on the same task. It does nothing when no run waits.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat.ts`: the executor calls it after its cleanup, next to the existing same-agent `startNextQueuedRunForAgent`.
- fix(runs): a person's Stop wins over lease-release teardown (#21300)
  > - `server/src/services/acknowledged-native-stop.ts`: `hasRecordedUserStopRequest` and `recordedUserStopRequestSql` recognize a person Stop that the cancel route recorded (`cancelledByActorType: "user"`, a user id, and a requested or acknowledged execution cancellation or a startup cancellation fence).
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-lifecycle.ts`: `terminalizeRunOnLeaseRelease` re-reads the run before it decides. A recorded person Stop now gives `cancelled`. The `interrupted` write is fenced on "no person Stop recorded", so a Stop that lands between the read and the write makes the write fail. The function then reads again and writes `cancelled`.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/heartbeat-run-lease-release-terminalization.test.ts`: two orderings (Stop recorded before the teardown read; Stop recorded after the read, with the stale snapshot) and a control (an agent-only request still reports `interrupted`).
- fix(test): remove the Pi fault calibration file race and halve its runtime (#21286)
  > - Root cause A: the fixture child wrote `child.json` and `exit.json` with `writeFileSync`. That call creates the file and then writes it. The test polls only `exists()` and then parses. A reader could see the created but empty file. Now the fixture writes `<name>.tmp` and renames it into place, so the file appears only with complete content. This also fixes the same race on `exit.json`.
  > - Root cause B: a profile of one calibration showed that about 1.0 s of 1.28 s was spent in `inspect()`. Each `inspect()` call reads and SHA-256 hashes the runner executable and the distribution Node executable. Both were copies of the 123 MB Node binary. Each calibration calls `inspect()` five times, and the suite runs four calibrations, so the suite hashed about 5 GB. Polling sleeps were only 0.09 s. The synthetic runner root now is a copy of `/bin/sh` that runs a short script. It is still a distinct, pinned ELF, and `/proc/<pid>/exe` still resolves to it. The script keeps the same launch shape (direct path, or `/proc/self/fd/3` or `/proc/self/fd/7` with the executable descriptor opened only for the wrapper child). It keeps the same SIGTERM cascade. The distribution Node, the wrapper and the Pi child are unchanged.
  > - The outer `spawnSync` timeout is now a named constant (45 s) with its margin in a comment. The Vitest test timeout derives from it (+5 s), so a slow Python run reports its own output instead of a Vitest timeout.
- fix(runner): quiesce the Core-dialed route once the Runner commits its suspend (#21245)
  > - `RunnerExecutionDriver.controlPlaneRegistration` results can now include an optional `quiesce()`. Core calls it when it has committed the Runner's exit. The route then stops liveness and reconnect, and it keeps the established receive/ACK path.
  > - `DurablePrpControlPlane.onRunnerExitCommitted(listener)` is new. The authority calls its listeners after it saves a completed `runner.suspend` or `runner.shutdown` result and before it sends the acknowledgement. The Runner exits when it gets that acknowledgement, so a route quiesced here never sees the exit as a loss.
  > - `awaitRunnerSuspensionBarrier` calls a new `onExitCommitted` callback once, when its suspend command is `completed`. It also calls the callback when it reuses a completed receipt and the durable state is still `suspended`. A rejected, failed or unanswered suspend never calls it.
- fix(retry): start a held Retry once a stopped preparation is proven stopped (#21295)
  > - `readFailedPreparationSettlement` (`failed-preparation-settlement.ts`) accepts one trailing host lifecycle event: the no-replay settlement event that execution recovery appends. The event must have the exact payload shape and must name a resolved `legacy_execution_requires_reconciliation` hold whose evidence binds the same run. Any other event still fails the proof.
  > - `failedNativePreparation` (`cancelled-native-startup.ts`) asks for a verified workspace repair only when the run holds prepared files: a recorded remote workspace, a workspace restore failure, or required workspace recovery. A run with no recorded remote workspace and a stopped sandbox has nothing to repair. The lease termination receipt, the bootstrap evidence and the settled-event proof are still required.
  > - New regression test `legacy-preparation-retry-hold.test.ts` uses the exact shape of the staging run.
- fix(verify): report stale SDK public input pins locally, before CI (#21283)
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.mjs`: add `staleSdkPublicInputsInWorkingTree(root)`. It applies the existing drift guard to the working tree (tracked and untracked files that git does not ignore). Add `isSdkPublicInput(file)` and the shared failure message `staleSdkPublicInputsMessage(stale)`. The committed-tree guard keeps its behavior and now uses a shared comparison.
  > - `scripts/local-verify.mjs`: when the diff touches a pinned input, the registry or `report-lock.json`, `pnpm verify` checks the pins first. If they are stale, it exits 1 and prints the files and the refresh command. The check reads files only. It does not write the registry.
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-reviewed-fixture.ts`: the error `Reviewed SDK reference source changed` now names the file and gives the refresh command.
- fix(retry): release a Retry held on a resolved decision (#21267)
  > - `hasPendingContinuationDecision` (`explicit-native-continuation.ts`) is now the single check for a decision that holds a person's continuation. Admission and the task projection both use it.
  > - For a Retry, a pending interaction that was created before the failed source run was created does not hold the Retry. That turn was already admitted while the decision was open. Decisions that the failed run created (for example a plan review) still hold the Retry.
  > - `releaseDecisionHeldExecution` (`deferred-execution-wait.ts`) removes the saved `decision_pending` wait from deferred receipts on the task and hints each receipt to the deferred-receipt worker. It runs in the resolution transaction of every interaction resolution path (through `notifyChatConfirmationResolution`) and in question answers.
- fix(tool-gateway): advertise only the MCP capabilities the gateway token may list (#19279)
  > - server/src/routes/tool-gateway.ts: `initialize` reads the session that `initializeNamedGatewayProtocol` already returns, and advertises each capability only when the token may list it.
  > - server/src/__tests__/tool-gateway.test.ts: a new test sends `initialize` with five tokens (tools only, tools and prompts, tools and resources, no list action, and no action list) and checks the exact capabilities. It also checks that the tools-only token gets 403 `gateway_token_action_denied` on `resources/list`.
- fix(native-runtime): a follow-up that arrives as a warm session closes continues the task (#21279)
  > - `claimWarmNativeInstructionCopy` now also waits when an idle close is in flight for the session scope (`closingWarmNativeSessions`). The wait uses the same deadline as the existing release wait (`warmSessionReleaseWait`), so it adds no new wait and nothing before `turn.start` on the normal path.
  > - `validateNativeRunnerCheckpointOwner` accepts the finished owner's final checkpoint (accepted result, no new work) when `issues.executionRunId` points at a successor run that is `queued` or `scheduled_retry` for the same company and task and has no `native_run_finalizations` row.
  > - Tests: a server test for a follow-up that arrives while the idle timeout closes the warm session; four new scenarios (`queued_successor`, `scheduled_retry_successor` × `runner.suspend`/`turn.stop`) and four new negatives (`queued_wrong_issue`, `queued_new_work` × both commands) in the early-retirement checkpoint matrix.
- fix(ci): re-run cancelled runs that lost a runner, and let runner-loss evidence win (#21291)
  > - `ci-runner-loss-rerun.yml`: the job also runs when the PR run concluded `cancelled`. The trigger type, `permissions`, the repository, path, event and attempt-1 conditions do not change.
  > - `runner-loss-rerun.mjs`, `eligibleRun`: accepts `completed/cancelled` as well as `completed/failure`.
  > - `runner-loss-rerun.mjs`, `decide()`: classifies jobs that concluded `cancelled` as well as `failure`. It still skips the `ci / verify` and `ci / e2e` aggregates, and a failed or cancelled routing gate still blocks the re-run.
- fix(secrets): return 422 for secret proposals missing a required field (#15348)
  > - Check `name` and `justification` in `createSecret` before `trim`, and return 422 when either is missing.
  > - Check `justification` in `createBinding` the same way.
  > - Keep the existing empty-value and malformed-value checks and the optional `key` field unchanged.
- fix(environments): let same-run recovery create a replacement once its sandbox is proven stopped (#21223)
  > - `server/src/services/sandbox-creation-operations.ts`: when `createOwnedSandbox` finds the key held by an `attached` operation whose lease is not active, it checks the same stop proof that same-run recovery requires before it acquires: `hasRemoteTerminationReceipt(resolveRemoteTerminationLease(lease))`, for the sandbox that this operation created (`creationProviderLeaseId`, or the lease's `providerLeaseId`).
  > - Migration `0475_sandbox_creation_owner_unresolved.sql` recreates `sandbox_creation_operations_owner_idx` under the same name as a partial unique index (`state <> 'resolved'`). The drop and the create are in one migration transaction. The Drizzle schema matches.
  > - The conflict lookup in `createOwnedSandbox` ignores resolved rows. An active attached lease is still replayed. Pending and `needs_reconciliation` rows still refuse. `releaseLease` does not change.
- fix(recovery): retry a failed Retry run's sandbox resume failure when the sweep reaches it first (#21124)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/retries.ts`: add `failedPreparationOwnsBoundedRetry(run)`. It is the persisted form of the run-completion decision: a failed legacy run with no native session, bootstrap evidence that provider work did not start, and a transient preparation code. A restore failure counts only when its owner marked it retryable. Codes that an accepted interaction retries itself are excluded.
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/service.ts`: when the latest run is such a run, the sweep does not escalate it as non-retryable. It goes to `scheduleRecoveryRetry`, which is the run's own bounded scheduler. The scheduler reuses a successor that already exists, so the executor and the sweep create one successor. The task escalates when the scheduler reports exhaustion or a refused continuation. When the scheduler declines the retry for another reason (for example a permanent refusal), the task escalates once as a non-retryable failure, as before. The #21108 settling skip still runs first.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat.ts`: pass the predicate to the recovery service.
- fix(runtime): record a plugin task's stop receipt when its stop is proven (#21237)
  > - `server/src/services/environment-task-runtime.ts`
  > - `server/src/services/remote-execution-termination.ts`
  > - `server/src/services/environment-run-orchestrator.ts`: same-run plugin recovery uses `remoteExecutionHasReleased`. The same-run resume check after it requires released receipts, so its behavior does not change.
- fix(ui): keep long agent names inside task columns (#21155)
  > - Limit agent and human identity width to the containing task column.
  > - Add a production-component Storybook example for long names, names without spaces, and short names.
  > - Add browser layout checks for both identity columns, avatar size, full text, adjacent values, and row height in both themes at 640px and 1200px.
- fix(native): replace a reattached Runner that left its recovery read unanswered (#21272)
  > - `nativeRecoveryCommandUnanswered` (executor) finds a `native_session_recovery_failed` whose Runner did not answer a recovery command (`PRP command <type> timed out` or `runnerd exited while waiting for <type>`). Identity, authority, integrity and incompatibility refusals do not match.
  > - When such a failure ends a `reattach_remote_runner` attempt on a sandbox target, the failure detail records `adoptedRunnerUnanswered: true`.
  > - `claimNativeRestartRecoveries` copies it to the next claim as `replaceUnansweredRunner: true`, in the same way as `exitedBeforeSessionOpen`.
- fix(recovery): offer Try again on a model-unavailable failure and recheck the model first (#21244)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/model-rejected-retry.ts`: `rejectedModelRetryRefusal` reads the model the failed run sent (`runnerProfileJson.nativeExecutionInput.provider.model`) and the model a new run would use (the task's model override for its assignee, else the agent's model). If they are the same, it returns a 409 refusal with code `native_provider_model_still_unavailable`, the setting to change (`agent_model` or `task_model_override`) and the rejected model.
  > - `server/src/routes/agents.ts`: the exact failed-run retry (`POST /agents/:id/wakeup`, `reason: retry_failed_run`) applies this check before it queues anything.
  > - `ui/src/components/task-chat/adjacent-errors.ts`, `TaskChatThreadView.tsx`: one `isRetryableFailureMarker` rule, now including "Model unavailable".
- fix(heartbeat): draining/yielding servers claim no queued run, and closed tasks get no system continuation (#21258)
  > - `claimQueuedRun` (`server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`) refreshes and checks the run-admission gate first. When admission is closed (draining or yielding), it leaves the run queued and returns. The dispatch passes already did this check; now every start path does.
  > - `claimQueuedRun` re-reads the task for a system continuation (`issue_recovery_action_restored`, `issue_continuation_needed`, `issue_assignment_recovery`, blocker-stall wake). If the task is `done` or `cancelled`, it cancels the run as `issue_closed_before_start`, skips its wake, and releases the task. Other wake reasons, including a person's message, are not affected.
  > - That cancel never consumes a person's message. Admission can attach deferred comments to a queued system continuation without changing its wake reason. The cancel locks the run's wake and the run (the order admission's coalescing uses), reads the run's current comment ids, and if any is a person's comment that no run of this agent has received yet (read-only use of `deliveredQueuedCommentIds`, which includes the history-read rule), the run is not cancelled: it is claimed as that person's follow-up, which may reopen the task through the normal path. A comment attached concurrently is either seen under the lock or, after the cancel commits, saved behind the cancelled run by admission.
- fix(presentation): offer Retry only when no automatic continuation or scheduled retry is pending (#21264, includes #21200)
- fix(recovery): continue twice-interrupted tasks and waiting follow-ups after a release swap (#21250)
  > - Admission (`server/src/services/execution-workspace-policy.ts`, `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`, `server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`): `isUnrunnableWorktreeCombo` accepts `freshIsolatedContinuation`. The admission preflight sets it from the new `isFreshIsolatedContinuationWake`, which returns at once (no query) unless the wake context carries `freshContinuationIsolation`. This does not touch the dispatch ranking.
  > - Follow-up promotion (`server/src/modules/wake-queue/adapters/postgres.ts`): the stranded-queue pass and the release path skip the native finalization gate and the reconciliation incident for a run that the fallback superseded (`agentRunSupersededByFreshContinuation`).
  > - Saved-input choice (`server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`): a deferred user wake counts as saved input only when it has no comment ids, or still has a message that the agent has not received (`undeliveredLegacyUserCommentIds`, called, not changed). Otherwise the fallback records its own continuation.
- fix(runner): prove ownership right before starting a turn in a recovered session (#21260)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/native-session-runtime.ts`:
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/native-session-runtime.test.ts`: new tests "proves it still owns a recovered session right before starting a turn in it" (startup turn) and "proves it still owns a recovered session right before a restart continuation" at startup and when the lost turn's terminal arrives (`resumeInterruptedTurn`).
  > - Startup turn on a recovered session: new checkpoint, covered by the first test.
- fix(heartbeat): deliver a queued comment again unless the reading run cites it as handled (#21243)
  > - `server/src/services/queued-comment-delivery.ts`: the history-read delivery rule now also requires the reading run's `run.result.accepted` event to cite `comment:<id>` (top-level `evidence[].ref` or a criterion's `evidenceRefs`, prefix match so an annotated ref counts). The read revision, succeeded-run and finished-after-revision rules are unchanged. All consumers (release drain, stale-continuation save, ownership reconciler, first-turn queued input, remote-Stop resume) use this one rule.
  > - New `readByEarlierTurnCommentIds`, `commentsArrivedDuringRun` and `taskHistoryHandlingGuidance`.
  > - `get_task_history` (both tool authorities) returns `arrivedDuringThisTurn` (the `comment:<id>` refs of comments other authors posted or edited after the run started) and a `handling` instruction to cite each applied comment in `paperclip_finish` / `paperclip_block` evidence. The comment list itself is unchanged. A failure to compute the list returns no guidance, so the comment stays owed.
- fix(native-runtime): a timed-out step records its real cause instead of leaving the run unrecoverable (#21262)
  > - Add `redactNativeFailureError` in `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-failure-redaction.ts`. It redacts the message and stack in place when it can. When the message is read-only, it returns a new `Error` with the redacted message, the same `name` and the redacted stack.
  > - Use it in the startup failure handler of `executePaperclipNativeSession` in place of the direct assignment.
  > - Add unit tests for a real `TimeoutError`, a read-only `AbortError` that holds a secret, and an ordinary typed error.
- fix: expose reconciliation wake reason in diagnostics (#18236)
  > - Add `execution_reconciliation_required` to the safe wake-diagnostic reason allowlist.
  > - Add focused projection coverage for the known reason and unknown-reason redaction.
  > - Extend the existing diagnostics-route regression matrix.
- fix(claude-local): fail Opus 5.5 ACP runs early when the bundled Claude Code is too old (#13927)
  > - `acp.ts`: before launch, compare the bundled `claudeCodeVersion` with `minimumClaudeCliVersionForModel()`. Return `claude_cli_version_incompatible` with the CLI lane fields.
  > - `cli-capabilities.ts`: `readBundledClaudeCodeVersion()` resolves the SDK through the bridge, like `build-provider-pack.mjs`.
  > - Match the `jp.` and `au.` Bedrock prefixes. Opus 5.5 has both profiles.
- fix: strip systemd service-manager env vars from spawned agent processes (#11584)
  > - In `runChildProcess` (`packages/adapter-utils/src/server-utils.ts`), strip `NOTIFY_SOCKET`, `LISTEN_FDS`, `LISTEN_PID`, and `LISTEN_FDNAMES` from the environment.
  > - The strip happens after the `process.env` + `opts.env` merge, in the same block that already strips the Claude Code nesting-guard variables, so it also covers callers whose `opts.env` is built as `{ ...process.env, ...env }` and would otherwise re-introduce the leak.
  > - This is a single chokepoint fix. It covers every local adapter that goes through `runChildProcess`. Remote/sandbox execution targets do not use `runChildProcess` and are unaffected.
- fix(heartbeat): start the new owner after reassignment away from a queued creator run (#21256)
  > - `server/src/routes/issues.ts`: on reassignment, when no live run holds the task's lock, also release the outgoing assignee's never-started `issue_assigned` run for that task. This uses the existing `issue_reassigned` stop from #21030. The run is read through a new `heartbeat.getQueuedAssignmentRunForIssue` accessor in `heartbeat/run-state.ts`; route test mocks of the heartbeat service gain that method.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`: when the queue gate withholds a queued run as `issue_assignee_changed`, it now releases the task and promotes the new owner's deferred wake. The promoted run starts after the claim lock is released (`deferredPostCommitEffects`), like the neighboring cancellation branches. A failed promotion is logged and left to recovery.
  > - `server/src/modules/run-dispatch/adapters/postgres.ts`: the same cancellation hints each deferred wake of the task to the deferred-receipt worker in the same transaction, so recovery runs even if the process stops before promotion.
- fix(projects): say where a person restores an archived project (#21254)
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: `PATCH /api/projects/{id}` now says that archiving sets `archivedAt`, hides the project from the sidebar and selectors, and keeps the project, its tasks and their history. Restoring sets `archivedAt` to null. A person unarchives on `/<prefix>/projects/<projectId>/configuration` with the Unarchive button, not on the company Status page.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/project-archive-restore-guidance.test.ts`: the agent API search for the eval's query and for `unarchive restore project` returns this guidance.
- fix(routines): keep webhook secrets out of chat and link the reveal page (#21253)
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: `POST /api/routines/{id}/triggers` and `POST /api/routine-triggers/{id}/rotate-secret` now describe the secret handling. For a signed or bearer trigger, the URL and signing mode can be shared. With `signingMode: none` the URL alone starts the routine, so it is treated as a credential too. The secret value, or a header that contains it, is never written in a chat reply, comment, document or task. The agent links `/<prefix>/routines/<routineId>/triggers`. For a trigger still awaiting setup, the link is `/<prefix>/routines/<routineId>/triggers?triggerSetup=<triggerId>` and the person chooses Generate new key. Otherwise the person chooses Edit webhook, then Replace key. These are the same controls the human webhook setup uses.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/routine-webhook-secret-guidance.test.ts`: the agent API search for both operations returns this guidance.
- fix(recovery): keep a draining server's lease alive and end a crashed server's runs in about 2 minutes (#21238)
  > - Lease renewal on its own connection (`packages/db/src/client.ts`, `packages/db/src/index.ts`, `server/src/services/controller-instance-lease.ts`):
  > - A crashed controller's runs (`server/src/services/recovery/ownership-registry.ts`, `server/src/index.ts`, `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-restart-recovery.ts`):
  > - Only `stopped` (written by the boot's own graceful stop) and `fenced` (a newer epoch fenced it after its lease expired plus 30 s grace) count. `live` and `grace` never count, so a controller that is alive and renewing is never treated as gone. `expired` alone does not count either.
- fix(runner): name missing tool fields and what the call included (#21252)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/tool-input-error.ts`: for missing top-level required fields, the error now says `missing required fields: idempotencyKey, caseType, title, kickoff.`, `This call included only: workflow_document_text.`, and a placeholder example built from the schema, such as `{"idempotencyKey":"<idempotencyKey>",...,"kickoff":{"toAgentId":"<toAgentId>","phase":"<phase>","instructions":"<instructions>"}}`. Other validation errors follow after `Also:`. Field-description guidance is unchanged.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/opencode/mcp-bridge.ts` and `runner-tool-bridge.ts`: pass the call arguments to the error builder. Only argument names are read; values are never echoed.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/tool-input-error.test.ts`: a test on the real `create_case` schema and a test that other errors still follow the missing fields.
- fix(evals): grade full cohort 9 chat cases on saved state and served links (#21249)
  > - `routines.create-daily-brief` (oracle `integrator.create-daily-brief.v2`): the saved schedule check (one enabled `0 9 * * 1-5` trigger in America/Chicago, the browser person's timezone) already grades the timezone. The reply check now requires a rendered link to the saved routine or the Launch project, where the work lands. The keyword list for the timezone is removed. The UTC negative stays (and now also catches "9:00 AM UTC"), and so does the "future summaries already ran" negative.
  > - `routines.run-now` (oracle `routine-invocation.v5`): when the agent's invocation has no idempotency key, the harness skips the replay probe and records `replayProbe: skipped-no-idempotency-key`. The check passes with an "Unverified" detail, and still fails on a second invocation, an unrecorded probe, or a skipped probe when a key exists.
  > - `companies.configure-decision-model` (oracle `configure-decision-model.v2`): the wording regex is removed for this case. The act now follows the reply's rendered links, in order, to the page that shows the Decision model control (the same method as `companies.fast-responses`). The case requires the service to stay off after the reply and that link. `prompt-injection-guard` is not changed.
- fix(feedback): keep run ids and dates intact when redacting phone numbers (#21251)
  > - `server/src/services/feedback-redaction.ts`: the phone rule now runs only on the text between public identifiers. UUIDs and ISO-8601 dates and timestamps are copied through unchanged. Other rules are unchanged.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/feedback-redaction.test.ts`: a regression test with the `-4314-9924-` UUID, an all-digit UUID, a date and a timestamp, next to a real phone number that must stay redacted.
- fix(runner): re-bind any settled ACPX checkpoint across a Runner upgrade (#21220)
  > - Added `settled_checkpoint_blockers`. A settled checkpoint is not closed, has a provider identity, has no active turn, has no unconfirmed provider exit, and has no undelivered event except a `session.resumed` notice. `run.attach` now admits with this function. The warm-attach readiness probe also uses it, and still waits for a pending `session.resumed` notice.
  > - Restore re-binds any settled checkpoint to the current launch profile digest, whichever run saved it and whatever its lifecycle (`session_open`, `prepared` or `suspended`). Candidate admission, a changed launch shape and pending runtime requests still block the re-bind.
  > - `rebinds_interrupted_turn` keeps only its distinct case: the same run's `turn_starting` or `turn_active` turn, under the lost-turn recovery preconditions from #20112. The settled rule now covers its `session_open` case.
- fix(recovery): pace recovery continuations and start person-initiated work first (#21234)
  > - New module `server/src/services/heartbeat/queued-run-dispatch-order.ts` decides the order in which an agent's queued runs start. Person-initiated runs start first: `task_retry`, external chat messages, and any wake that a user requested. Other work comes next, together with recovery runs for interruptions that ended in the last 30 minutes. Old-backlog recovery runs come last. Inside each class, the existing order applies: readiness, then priority, then age. Runs whose dependencies are not ready stay last.
  > - Recovery runs get a bounded share. When the agent started 3 other runs after the oldest queued recovery run arrived and none of them was recovery work, the next slot goes to a recovery run. The share applies across the whole batch when one dispatch fills many slots. Thus people cannot starve recovery.
  > - `startNextQueuedRunForAgent` in `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts` uses the new order. It changes only the ranking. It does not change `claimQueuedRun`, the admission preflight, or the promoter.
- fix(startup): report ready without waiting for backlog first passes (#21247)
  > - `server/src/index.ts`: These first passes now start in the background as tracked scheduler work and do not hold readiness: tool reviews, gateway sessions, delivery workers, GitHub connection workers, the terminal workspace sweep, environment lease cleanup (scheduler-disabled path) and scheduled database backups. Shutdown and idle sleep still wait for them through `trackHeartbeatSchedulerWork`, and each queue keeps its retry schedule.
  > - `server/src/index.ts`: The restart backup after an idle wake (a `pg_dump` whose time grows with the database) holds idle eligibility (`markIdleStartupComplete`), as before. It no longer holds readiness.
  > - `server/src/index.ts`: Every remaining await on the boot path is a named `startupTimeline.precondition(...)`: `listen`, `runtime_control_operations`, `execution_policy`, `managed_environments`, `worktree_run_execution`, `scheduling_suppression`, `native_recovery`, `external_adapters` and `cloud_configuration`. These preconditions do not change.
- fix(native): count a parent run's read of a finished child as delivery of its result (#21227)
  > - `native-child-completion-delivery.ts`:
  > - `paperclip-runner-tool-authority.ts`: `get_task_context` records the finished child tasks it returns. A failed write is logged and leaves the result owed.
  > - `routes/issues.ts`: `GET /issues/:id`, `GET /issues/:id/documents` and `GET /issues/:id/documents/:key` record the read when the request has all of these:
- fix(native): wait for a dated provider limit reset and keep it out of Sentry (#19775)
  > - Keep current master’s nearest-calendar-date rule. An expired reset stays expired. It does not move a year into the future.
  > - Read full or short month names and optional explicit years. Reject invalid dates, clocks, month names, time zones, partial year-as-clock matches, and dates more than 45 days ahead.
  > - Exclude a failed native `native_provider_usage_limit` from `unexpected` Sentry reporting only with a valid typed `quota_exhausted` or `rate_limit` failure, outside setup and without a process signal. Keep the task failure and cooldown. Keep other failures and `all` mode reportable.
- fix(recovery): continue only recent interruptions from the held backlog, newest first, paced (#21222)
  > - `continueHeldExecutions` and `heldExecutionsAwaitingFreshContinuation` (server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.ts):
  > - The rule I chose, and why: 24 hours and interruption codes only. A person who is waiting for a task notices within a day. A run that failed for configuration or provider reasons fails again the same way, so a person must change something and press Retry. The 7-day rule of the assigned-work sweep is too wide for this pass: 76 of the 176 continued tasks were from 1 to 7 days old.
  > - The live path does not change. Fresh interruptions come through restart recovery and the disposition pass (`recovery_refused`), and through continuation wakes. They are not paced and have no age rule.
- fix(recovery): continue a task blocked behind its workspace's ended writer once the hold releases (#21221)
  > - Add `continueTasksAfterWorkspaceWriterReleased` (`server/src/services/recovery/released-writer-escalation.ts`). It selects board-owned `stranded_assigned_issue` escalations whose latest run failed setup with the unfinished-writer error before any provider work, by the current assignee. In one transaction it checks that the block belongs to the escalation, that no newer run exists for the task, that dependencies allow work, and that the workspace's writer hold is now free. Then it resolves the escalation with a named outcome, returns the task to `todo`, records one plain activity notice, and records a pending wake.
  > - A later status decision that only kept the Blocked status it found (from `blocked` to `blocked`) does not own the block. A decision that chose the block still keeps the task blocked.
  > - Run the pass in `reconcileActiveRecoveryActions`, next to the legacy rehome pass, and include its next wake retry time in the pass deadline.
- fix(recovery): settle a run's late exhaustion hold into its recorded fresh continuation (#21217)
  > - `server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`: when a continuation is already recorded for the run, the decision settles the run's other owned reconciliation holds (blocking, not a human gate) into it: resolved, `restored`, system-owned, the person-facing text, `coalescedIntoRecoveryActionId` and the primary's delivery state. A task those holds blocked returns to `todo`. The continuation delivery worker is notified.
  > - `server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.test.ts`: regression test for a late exhaustion hold.
- fix(evals): grade recovery on fresh continuation, accept in-turn queued-comment reads, verify the saved service model (#21210)
  > - `runtime-infrastructure.ts` (`recovery.explain-stall`, `recovery.source-action`, oracle `infrastructure-recovery.v6`): after three real Runner losses, the setup waits for one system-owned fresh-continuation record of the failed run (`?inspectSourceRunId`), resolved as `restored`, `replay: "none"`, the failed run marked superseded, and one run with `retryOfRunId` = the failed run that waits for its brief in its own workspace. The chat question is asked while it runs. The harness then releases the brief, and the grade requires the continuation to succeed, save the brief's reference as the latest revision and complete the original task. No person-owned hold and no blocked task at the reply. Three runs: failed 1, succeeded 2. The reply must still come from a run that read the recovery record (now also the continuation run) and link the original task. Setup timing is saved to `runtime-fresh-continuation.json`.
  > - `runtime-infrastructure-retry.ts` (`recovery.retry-exhausted`, oracle `retry-exhausted.v6`): an infrastructure loss no longer offers Retry. The case uses a failure only the person can resolve (`native_provider_model_rejected`, a `PERSON_OWNED_FAILURE_CODES` member) and keeps the Retry-under-budget-hold journey unchanged. The setup proves the task waits blocked with no fresh continuation and no successor run for 45 s.
