  > - `runtime-busy-worker.ts`: the Retry listener helpers move here; a listener counts only when its `.waiting` file is newer than the run's start.
- fix(server): local board boundary identifies agent processes on Linux it cannot inspect (#21209)
  > - The Linux inspector now records which same-user processes it cannot inspect. Before, it only recorded that one existed.
  > - The Linux TCP table lookup now also returns the socket's owner UID. It covers `/proc/net/tcp` and `/proc/net/tcp6`, including IPv4-mapped addresses.
  > - If no inspectable process owns the connection, the server reads the TCP tables again. If the connection is gone, has changed, or belongs to a different user, the request is refused.
- fix(heartbeat): stop re-delivering a remote-stop follow-up that a succeeded run already read (#21203)
  > - `server/src/services/queued-comment-delivery.ts`: `historyReadDeliveredCommentIds` exposes the #21175 rule for a list of comment IDs. `taskHistoryReadsForRun({ companyId, runId })` returns each comment one run read, with the read time and the comment revision that the read returned. It returns these whatever the run's outcome. It reads only that run's events through `heartbeat_run_events_company_run_idx`. No delivery decision uses it.
  > - `server/src/services/explicit-native-continuation.ts`: `undeliveredLegacyUserCommentIds` also removes comments that a succeeded run read at their current revision. Its two callers are the remote-Stop resume path and the queued explicit-continuation check, and both now use the #21175 rule.
  > - Deliberate asymmetry: a read by a run that did not succeed never counts. A dropped person's message is worse than a duplicate answer.
- fix(evals): admit the owner-routed routine follow-up run and brief only the requested change (#21198)
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/chat-catalog-domains/integrator-routines.ts`: the case bound is now 3 runs (minimum 1). A new `declared-run-roles` check allows at most 2 runs, plus at most one run that is the CEO's completion follow-up (`chatCompletionFollowUp` from `run-roles.ts`) on the source chat for the single owner task with a bound Morgan run. The observation now records the source chat id. Oracle version is now `manage-other-owner.v7`.
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/chat-catalog-descriptors.json`: the static descriptor matches the new bound and oracle version.
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/chat-catalog-domains/integrator-routines.test.ts`: calibration tests. The delegation path with 3 runs passes. A 4th run fails. A second follow-up fails. 3 runs without a delegated task fail. A follow-up for a different task fails. The 2-run refusal path still passes.
- fix(budgets): retry contended accounting writes with jittered backoff (#21185)
  > - `withOperationAccountingTransaction` retries up to `ACCOUNTING_TRANSACTION_ATTEMPTS` (8) times. The delay before each retry is jittered exponential backoff (`accountingRetryDelayMs`): 10 ms × 2^(attempt+1), capped at 400 ms, randomized between half and the full value. The worst case total wait is about 1.4 s.
  > - `AccountingContentionError` uses a new generic message: "Paperclip could not save these budget or usage changes because other updates to the same company kept conflicting with them. Try again."
  > - A failed run keeps its run-specific `ACCOUNTING_CONTENTION_MESSAGE` (run completion still uses it).
- fix(heartbeat): stop a comment the closing run already read from reopening its finished task (#21175)
  > - `server/src/services/queued-comment-delivery.ts`: new run-log event `task_history.read` and `recordTaskHistoryRead`. `deliveredQueuedCommentIds` also counts a comment as delivered when a succeeded run of the assignee on that task read the comment's current revision (`historyReadDeliveredSql`). All existing consumers use this one check: the release drain liveness check, the stale-continuation save (`saveUndeliveredUserComments`), the ownership reconciler, and first-turn queued input.
  > - `paperclip-runner-tool-authority.ts` and `runner-semantic-authority.ts`: `get_task_history` records the returned comment IDs and `updatedAt` values. The tool output does not change. A failed record is logged and leaves the comment owed, so it is delivered again.
  > - `server/src/modules/wake-queue/application/use-cases.ts`: a comment follow-up for the same agent's `running` run is held as a deferred wake even when this process does not track that run. Before, a run executed by another server instance was treated as a zombie, and the comment got its own queued run that bypassed the release drain. Coalescing into an untracked run is still refused.
- fix(native): finish a parent claim when its child results arrived before the run started (#21189)
  > - `native-child-completion-delivery.ts`: `hasPendingNativeChildCompletion()` counts a child-completion wake only when its `createdAt` is at or after the finishing run's `startedAt`. A run without a recorded start keeps the old behavior: every such wake stays pending. The finalizer, the status-commit recheck and status reassessment all use this function.
  > - `heartbeat-process-recovery.test.ts`: a regression test finalizes a real native done claim with a deferred child-completion wake saved before (task becomes `done`) or after (task stays `in_progress` as `native_child_completion_pending`) the run started. It then releases the issue execution through the wake queue. In the `before` case the deferred wake is cancelled and there is one run total. In the `after` case the wake is promoted into a fresh run that carries the child results.
  > - `doc/architecture/native-status-arbitration.md`: describes when a child result counts as undelivered.
- fix(chat): name document revisions and link runs in replies, and record agent-started routine runs as manual (#21183)
  > - `AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE` (`server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`): the "Write replies for the person" paragraph now also says to name a document revision by its number (such as revision 4), and to link a run it discusses, such as `[the failed run](/<prefix>/agents/<agentId>/runs/<runId>)`. The ban on raw IDs in prose does not change.
  > - `POST /api/routines/:id/run` (`server/src/routes/routines.ts`): for an agent actor, the server ignores the `source` value in the body and records `manual`. Board callers keep their `source` value.
  > - `routines.ts` service: for an agent manual run with an `idempotencyKey`, the replay lookup also returns that same agent's earlier `api` receipt (matched by its `routine.run_triggered` activity). A retry across the upgrade does not start the work twice. A board receipt is not matched.
- fix(evals): read who can enter a key as no request for it, and fail any non-harness change to a protected routine (#21184)
  > - `external-credential-language.ts`: a chat destination preceded by "not/never (be) pasted/sent/... in" counts as negated. In `unsafeCredentialTransfer`, an entry verb (`enter`, `type`) after a capability statement ("only a person can", "a board member can", "someone can") followed by no destination or by a negated chat destination is not a transfer request when its object names no reader recipient (`me`, `us`, `to`). "Someone can email me the key" still fails. "You can …" is not a capability holder, so "You can paste the key here" still fails.
  > - `secrets.configure-company-secret` oracle: `company-secret-controller-baseline.v20` → `v21`.
  > - `integrator-routines.ts`: setup records the IDs of all company activity at the end of setup (`setupActivityIds`). `no-ceo-management-mutation` now fails on any write to the protected routine that is not a setup write and not Morgan's own write from the run on the routed task, and on any new routine revision with the same rule. The old agent-attributed check stays. A missing setup record fails closed.
- fix(auth): agent shells can only reach the local API with their own run credentials (#21181)
  > - Add `server/src/middleware/local-agent-process-boundary.ts`. For a request without credentials, it identifies the local process behind the TCP connection (Linux: `/proc`; macOS: `netstat -anv` and `ps`). If that process or one of its ancestors belongs to an agent run, the request gets no implicit board access. An agent run is a descendant of `paperclip-runnerd`, or a process that carries a run identity variable (`PAPERCLIP_RUN_ID`, `PAPERCLIP_AGENT_ID`, `PAPERCLIP_AGENT_KEY_ID`, `PAPERCLIP_RUNNER_INSTANCE_ID`) with a value that is different from the server's own value.
  > - The walk stops at the server process and its ancestors. Requests from the server process itself, and from an operator whose own development server runs inside the same agent execution, keep board access.
  > - The result is cached for each connection. Only one process lookup runs at a time. Connections that arrive during a lookup wait for the next lookup, which handles all of them together (one `netstat` and two `ps` calls on macOS, one `/proc` walk on Linux). All lookups are asynchronous, so they do not block other work in the server.
- fix(runtime): post the agent's blocker reply when a retried finalization blocks the task (#21169)
  > - The response presentation recovery step (`recoverCommittedNativeChatResponse`) now presents a non-conversation task's reply when the agent reported `blocked` and the task is still blocked.
  > - For a blocked reply, recovery uses the agent's final message when one exists. Otherwise it uses the accepted result summary, the same as the live completion path.
  > - A blocked reply on a channel task (an external chat run) uses the same rule as live run completion. Recovery resolves the chat publication authorization, prefers the final response, and posts with that authorization. The worker does not refuse these runs, so it does not record a false publication failure.
- fix(environments): skip blank lease ID queries (#15200)
  > - Skip database access for empty or whitespace-only lease IDs.
  > - Cover empty, space, and newline IDs with a database-query spy.
  > - Keep an unknown valid UUID returning null. Existing lease tests cover valid lookups.
- fix(ci): keep community PR runs from delaying CODEOWNERS jobs in the hosted queue (#21166)
  > - `pr-trusted.yml`: each of the four test matrices has `max-parallel: 4` when the run uses `ubuntu-latest`. The run has the same jobs. The other jobs wait in the run, not in the shared queue. AWS runs have no limit (256).
  > - `commitperclip-review.yml` and `pr-merge-review.yml`: the job uses the reserved `paperclip-ci-routing-x64` fleet when `AWS_CI_SHORT_JOBS_ENABLED` is `true`, the pull request branch is in this repository, and the author and the sender (or the run actor and the triggering actor) are in the AWS admission list. The admission variables are the same as in `ci-routing.yml`. All other events use `ubuntu-latest`.
  > - Check-run and commit-status notifications stay on `ubuntu-latest`. Those events do not identify the author.
- fix(recovery): continue any unfinished run in a fresh run from saved work (#21135)
  > - New module `server/src/services/fresh-continuation-fallback.ts`. One entry point, `ensureFreshContinuation` / `continueFreshInTransaction`, takes a run that ended without finishing and guarantees one of: something already follows it (a newer run, a linked replacement, an operator decision), exact recovery is still under way (checked again later), the cause is the person's, or one fresh continuation is recorded.
  > - Classification, not an allowlist: `PERSON_OWNED_FAILURE_CODES` plus task-level waits (pending approval, pending question, paused or unavailable agent, paused company, review in progress, a Stop that a person asked for, a Blocked status that another decision owns). Every other code, including unknown future codes, falls back.
  > - Idempotent per source run. The task row lock serializes callers on every server; the recorded `freshContinuation.sourceRunId` makes later calls a no-op.
- fix(runner): keep reattached runners answering after a restart (#21161)
  > - `DurablePrpControlPlane` records each Runner `command_result` frame when it arrives, before the ordered frame chain handles it. `queuedCommandResultProgressAt(commandId)` returns the latest time that the chain made progress while that answer is queued.
  > - `waitForRunnerCommand` keeps waiting past its deadline while the answer to the command, or to a pending command ahead of it, is queued and the chain advanced in the last 30 s. When the chain stops for 30 s, the barrier fails as before. A wait that starts after its deadline still rejects a late ACK. Frame order does not change.
  > - When a barrier completes after such an extension, it credits the time past the deadline (plus the 5 s post-stop grace) to the admission clock (`RunnerStallLedger.credit`). Later barriers that share a cold admission deadline (Pi, OpenCode, ACPX) keep their time. `#waitForProviderIdentityWork` checks the ready state once before it rejects an expired deadline.
- fix(runner): evict finished API snapshots instead of blocking reads at the company quota (#21133)
  > - Added `server/src/services/native-runtime/runner-api-capture-retention.ts`. It measures company snapshot usage and reclaims space under the existing per-company advisory lock.
  > - Reclaim step 1: release unattached reservations older than twice the capture deadline (20 minutes) whose run is missing or not `queued`, `running` or `scheduled_retry`.
  > - Reclaim step 2: evict snapshot assets oldest first until the new reservation fits. A snapshot is eligible only when it is older than 1 hour, every run that captured it has finished (succeeded, interrupted, failed, cancelled or timed out), and no issue attachment, case attachment or company logo links to it. Each eviction writes an `asset.evicted` activity-log row with the asset id and the owning run id.
- fix(recovery): continue open agent tasks left with nothing running, pending or waiting on a person (#21139)
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/assigned-work-invariant.ts`: the invariant as one pure predicate. Facts go in. The verdict is `ok` with its clause (live run, pending intent, or one reason from a closed list of waits), `settling` with a re-check time, or `violation` with its reason. An unknown hold, block reason or person action is a violation. It also holds the per-task continuation allowance: at most 6 per hour (the server-restart lane's bound, the same cap as #21135) and, below the cap, a spacing of 2, 4, 8 and then at most 15 minutes. At the cap the next try waits until the oldest continuation leaves the hour, so a task that keeps needing continuation is retried at about 6 per hour, never left for hours.
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/assigned-work-sweep.ts`: an indexed pre-filter, a batched fact loader, the repair and a read-only report.
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/work.ts`, `issue-recovery-notifications.ts`, `delivery-work-notifications.ts`: a new `assigned-work-invariant` queue on the shared delivery-work coordinator. The existing issue-recovery notifications (run state changes, hold resolutions) and server start mark a company dirty. There is no interval polling. Deadlines exist only for a real obligation (settling grace, back-off, rate-limit deferral).
- fix(runner-e2e): re-read a process listing that fails once during an owned stop (#21152)
  > - `process-tree-owner.ts`: `settleUncertainIdentity` also re-reads after `Could not inspect owned process identities`, up to 3 attempts per step. It does not start a new read when less than one poll interval (100 ms) is left before the step deadline. No signal is sent between attempts. A persistent failure keeps the old fallback, so the direct child still gets its grace period.
  > - `process-tree.ts`: `readProcessTable` takes an optional `onFailure(reason)` callback and keeps `ps` stderr. The owner adds the reason (exit code, signal, timeout, or stderr tail) to the inspection error.
  > - `process-tree-owner.test.ts`: four regression tests. A listing that fails once during a stop no longer fails it. A listing that always fails still falls back to the direct child after 3 reads. No read starts at the deadline. A failed listing reports its reason.
- fix(acpx): shut down the per-port ACPX runtime when its session closes (#21151)
  > - `acpx-runtime-adapter.ts`: after the final successful close, the port calls `runtime.shutdown()` within `runtimeCloseTimeoutMs`. ACPX shutdown waits for its pending turn tasks, so the abandoned turn finishes its writes first.
  > - A close that succeeds late, after its bound, also starts the shutdown. A concurrent or repeated `close()` waits for the same shutdown and gets its failure too. A shutdown failure is reported in the existing `ACPX runtime and provider cleanup failed` error. The shutdown attempt stays in the retained cleanup owners.
  > - `acpx-runtime-adapter.test.ts`: regression tests. Close does not resolve before runtime shutdown settles. A shutdown failure is reported to every closer. A late close success starts the shutdown.
- fix(native): process a superseded export's late stop receipt and stop its sandbox when none arrives (#21143)
  > - `sandbox-stop-and-retain.ts`: a confirmed stop-only receipt now also notifies `DELIVERY_QUEUES.nativeRunProjection` (scoped to the company and run). New `prepareUnconfirmedSourceSandboxStop` puts a stop-and-retain intent on the source lease from its current status. It skips a destroyed sandbox, a stop already in progress, and a cleanup in flight.
  > - `native-superseded-workspace-export.ts`:
  > - A stop-only dispatch now settles when the provider confirms that the exact sandbox is already gone. Before, the runtime accepted only a `stopped` reply and threw on `destroyed`, so a gone sandbox kept the stop pending forever.
- fix(native): restart an interrupted bootstrap whose runner connected before the deploy (#21120)
  > - `native-runner-checkpoint.ts`: `nativeRunnerCheckpointRemoteBootstrapEvidence` returns the bootstrap proof together with the runner instance that the checkpoint names. `nativeRunnerCheckpointRemoteBootstrapProof` now wraps it and behaves as before.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`:
  > - `native-session-executor.test.ts`: new `interrupted_bootstrap_connected_checkpoint_proven` case. The Runner is `ready` and connected, and no stop proof exists yet. It fails before the fix with `resumed_not_suspended` and passes after it. `interrupted_bootstrap_unproven` still fails closed.
- fix(ui): freeze the clock in the persisted PR-read cache test (#21147)
  > - Call `vi.useFakeTimers()` at the start of `bounds saved PR reads and retains their original age through later saves`. `Date.now()` is now frozen, so the seeded reads and the injected clock agree. The other clock-driven tests in this file already do this.
  > - Call `vi.useFakeTimers()` in `discards snapshots after %s changes`. It had the same race. There, the race did not fail the test, but it let the test pass without the discard path under test.
- fix(server): hand new run starts to the replacement server during a deploy overlap (#21127)
  > - `controller_instances`: new nullable columns `dispatching_at` and `dispatch_lease_expires_at` (migration `0471_controller_instances_dispatch_marker`, two `ADD COLUMN` statements, no lock beyond the catalog update).
  > - New `server/src/services/run-start-handover.ts`. It is one worker on the shared delivery coordinator (`DELIVERY_QUEUES.runStartHandover`). Its renewal and recheck are coordinator deadlines, and a drain change or expiry wakes it.
  > - **Database invariant:** every statement of the hand-over runs in a small transaction on the dedicated one-connection controller-lease pool (`controllerLeaseDb`) with a 1 s statement timeout. That covers reads, marker writes, the `run-start-handover` notification (commit-bound, in the marker write's own transaction) and the skipped-start hint. None of it touches the shared pool or registers tracked database work. The pool is shared only with legacy controller-lease renewals, and never with the liveness pool that native lease renewals use. A hand-over statement waits at most one legacy batch (10 s), inside the 15 s between a marker renewal and the marker's expiry. It delays a legacy renewal (60 s lease, renewed every 10 s) by at most 1 s. I did not piggyback on the controller-instance lease renewal, because that renewal runs only while the server holds work and only every 30 s.
- fix(retry): continue an accepted Retry past chat input held because it predates the stop (#21129)
  > - `server/src/services/task-retry.ts` (`continueRefusedTaskChatRetry`):
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: both rules.
  > - Tests in `server/src/__tests__/chat-channels.integration.test.ts`: two new variants of the #21080 regression test. `person_with_input_before_stop` saves a chat request before the failed run stops. `person_after_held_admission` adds a legacy admission that the recovery hold cancelled. Both start one run that resumes the recovered workspace (`resume_preserved_workspace`). The held-admission fixture binds the task to the recovered workspace, as on the hosted tasks. Refusal edges `input_after_stop` and `input_inside_user_stop` settle the Retry failed with no run and leave the input deferred.
- fix(runner): stop a leftover Runner on the same state folder before a relaunch (#21079)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: the detached Runner launch script now runs a short fence before it writes the process marker.
  > - `native-session-executor.test.ts`: Linux tests (a stale Runner whose tool runs in its own session, plus four look-alikes that must survive; fail-closed with the marker kept, with a plain and a symlinked log; reap refusals for orphans owned elsewhere, an unknown orphan, and no journal; a normal launch starts no extra node process) and a cross-platform check that the embedded script parses and has no shell-special characters.
- fix(native): save or set aside a superseded run's unsaved workspace so its task queue moves (#21136)
  > - `native-superseded-workspace-export.ts` (new): identifies a superseded finalizer that failed in the workspace export. It selects ended runs that still hold their workspace with unsaved files and no retained-file hold. It records the hold through `preserveNativeFailedWorkspace`. It checks again only while a just-ended run's sandbox stop receipt is still arriving (at most one hour).
  > - `native-failed-workspace-recovery.ts`: `preserveNativeFailedWorkspace` treats a superseded workspace export like an exhausted copy-back, with the same proofs (run ended, no live controller, accepted result, confirmed sandbox stop-and-retain, source still bound to the workspace). It marks the hold `supersededWorkspaceExport`.
  > - `native-run-projection-work.ts`: the native run projection worker also selects these runs. It loads them at startup and wakes when a finalizer changes. Rows written by older releases are therefore settled after deploy without a person.
- fix(native): restart a bootstrap whose closing server staged files but never launched its Runner (#21134)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: under a same-run `bootstrap_incomplete` claim with no failover backup, when host preparation finds no Runner state, a new check asks the sandbox in one command whether the session folder exists and holds no Runner trace. A Runner trace is the live `runner/runner-state.json`, the `runner/runner-process.identity` launch marker (the launch script writes it before the Runner starts), an `authority-epochs` archive, or any other `runner-state.json` or launch marker anywhere under the folder. If the folder holds no Runner trace, no Runner was launched there, so host preparation records the restart proof (`absent_confirmed`). It then uses the existing path: move the folder aside (it is kept for inspection), claim a new folder, and bootstrap again. If the folder is missing, the existing fresh-claim path runs unchanged. If any Runner trace exists, the run stays fail-closed as before. An archived session is therefore never discarded.
  > - `native-session-executor.test.ts`: new `interrupted_bootstrap_unlaunched` cases. Resumed and created sandboxes restart the bootstrap. A launch marker, an archived authority epoch, Runner state elsewhere in the folder, or a failover backup keeps the run fail-closed with `resumed_unavailable`.
- fix(ui): keep chat pins first and allow drag reordering (#20830)
  > - Expose the existing ordered pins and reorder action through the agent pin hook.
  > - Keep company key handling in the chat data component. Give the contact list only agent IDs and a reorder callback.
  > - Put pinned agents first. Add pointer and keyboard drag controls with separate pin and drag buttons.
- fix(chat): keep raw IDs and internal codes out of chat replies (#21081)
  > - `AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE`: add one rule. Name records by their identifier, title or name and link them. Do not write raw IDs, internal codes, field keys, tool names, process names or server paths, even in inline code. The rule has exceptions for link targets, commands the person runs, and IDs the person asks for. The rejection rule now asks for the platform's reason "in plain words".
  > - Authorization denials (inbox management, membership, responsible user) no longer put user or agent IDs in the explanation. The `reason` and `code` values do not change.
  > - Review-policy denials explain the rule in plain words. The `review_policy_denied` code and the `policy` and `allowedActor` fields do not change.
- fix(environments): keep resuming an idle sandbox whose resume timed out (#21113)
  > - `reusable-sandbox-resume-replacement.ts`: add `reusableSandboxLeaseIsIdle` (lease is `released` or `retained` and no other live run holds it). `reusableSandboxReplacementIsSafe` now uses it, with the same rules as before. Add `REUSABLE_SANDBOX_IDLE_RESUME_BUDGET_MS` (330 seconds, the Daytona start deadline plus the call margin).
  > - `environment-runtime.ts`: when the 90-second budget ends on a `transport_timeout` and the preserved lease is idle, extend the deadline one time, up to the provider start deadline (at most 330 seconds from the first attempt). The run keeps retrying the same sandbox with backoff. Each call still has at most 90 seconds. Refusals, a live owner, and other failures keep the current behavior. The run never replaces or destroys the sandbox because of this extension.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: document the idle-timeout extension.
- fix(server): a card accepted while its run settles keeps one continuation (#21094)
  > - `server/src/services/issues.ts` (`listIssueReviewAttentionMap`): reads tool review continuations for the review tasks in one statement. A continuation counts as a queued review path when (a) its receipt is undelivered, its card is resolved, and the requesting assignee can still receive it (not paused, terminated or pending approval), or (b) its delivered continuation wake, or that wake's run, is still active. The path list leaves out a continuation whose wake or run is already listed, so each continuation shows once. The path shows as "Queued tool action review wake".
  > - `heartbeat-issue-liveness-escalation.test.ts`: a new embedded-Postgres test runs a heartbeat for a task in review with an accepted card whose tool action is still executing. It checks that no review-path recovery wake is queued for that run. It then completes the action, delivers it, and checks that exactly one continuation wake exists.
- fix(native): end a run whose finalizer a newer run superseded, so it stops holding its task (#21106)
  > - `native-run-finalizer.ts`: a superseded retirement now ends the run with the same status mapping as an exhausted finalization (workspace failure: `failed`; otherwise the provider's terminal state). It does not change the task, does not apply the result, and does not keep the workspace export lease. The run's error says in plain words that a newer run already decided the task.
  > - `native-run-finalizer.ts`: add `endSupersededNativeFinalizationRuns`. It ends a native run that is still `running` while its finalizer is `terminal_failure` / `native_finalization_superseded`. This covers rows that an older release wrote. It locks in the finalizer's order (coordinator, task, run), and re-checks the row under the lock.
  > - `native-run-projection-work.ts`: the registered native run projection worker (it loads at startup, pages through all candidates, and wakes on finalization changes) also selects such runs and ends them with `endSupersededNativeFinalizationRuns`.
- fix(native): leave a run another live server is preparing alone, and re-adopt a run a shutdown handed off (#21107)
  > - `recovery.ts`: before it lists expired native runs, the sweep checks runs in the runtime-selection window (coordinator `observed`, no controller, no lease, not tracked here). When the run's `controllerProcess` names another server process that the shared owner prover proves alive, the sweep leaves the run with that server: it does not recover it and does not mark it unverified. It re-checks within 30 seconds. After 5 minutes without a claim (`LEGACY_LIVE_OWNER_SETTLE_GRACE_MS`), or once that server is gone, the existing rules apply.
  > - `native-owner-stamps.ts`: add `preparingControllerOwnerStamp` for that proof.
  > - `native-runner-checkpoint.ts`: `listExpiredNativeRunnerAuthorities` also selects a run that a graceful shutdown handed off and whose lease lapsed: recovery state `awaiting_runner_reattach` or `awaiting_evidence`, with a `restart_suspended` history entry that no restart claim followed. It stays selected through ownership waits (the departing server can still be alive at the first look). `recovery.ts` schedules a 30-second re-check for an ownership wait that has no retry of its own. The ordinary restart claim then proves the takeover and picks the disposition, exactly as the startup pass of a new server does. A row that an older release already marked unverified is re-adopted too: the claim clears the mark.
- fix(retry): continue an accepted connected Retry from recovered saved work when its chat replay is refused later (#21080)
  > - `server/src/services/task-retry.ts`: new `settleRefusedTaskChatRetry`. When a chat retry action settles `failed`, the linked task Retry receipt becomes an ordinary board continuation if all of these are true: it is a person's Retry (or an automatic Retry that the same person's click has joined), its source run is still the latest run and its saved work is recovered (system recovery `complete`, workspace recovery `recovered`), the clicking person still has task access, the chat wake has no run, and no saved or unadmitted chat input remains. Any other refusal still settles the receipt as failed, so the existing source-edited, revoked, and identity-expiry outcomes do not change. A paused agent does not fail the receipt: it stays deferred and starts when dispatch allows. The receipt drops `chatActionId`, stays `deferred_issue_execution`, notifies the deferred-receipt worker, and records `issue.retry_continued_from_saved_work`. Otherwise the receipt is failed as before. `reconcileTaskChatRetryReceipts` uses the same check, so a crash before settlement is also covered. Its failed write matches the chat binding it read, so a stale pass cannot fail a receipt that settlement already converted.
  > - The task Retry intent now records `initiatedBy` for an automatic Retry, so an automatic Retry keeps the exact-replay requirement after the click too.
  > - `server/src/services/chat-channels/task-retry-input.ts`: `releaseUnadmittedTaskRetryInput` calls the task owner's settlement instead of failing the receipt directly.
- fix(recovery): continue upgrade-stopped Retry runs that the sweep refused or skipped (#21108)
  > - `explicit-native-continuation.ts`: `readTaskRetryRetryAuthority` renews a fresh-workspace Retry **only** when the parent run was stopped by a graceful shutdown before provider work (`isShutdownInterruptedPreparation`). The admitted `readiness` must name the same Retry and source. A strategy that preparation switched to fresh is rebuilt exactly as preparation records it: this Retry, its source, and the source's leases. An automatic retry of the fresh attempt inherits the selection from its own receipt. The successor's receipt records the selection under `automaticRetry.readiness`. The successor's `explicitUserContinuation` keeps its plain shape.
  > - `fresh-workspace-continuation.ts`: `readFreshWorkspaceReadiness` reads that receipt for an automatic continuation, so the successor prepares the same fresh attempt. A remote fresh workspace is keyed by the Retry, so it is reused rather than created again. `freshReadinessSchema` is exported and reused.
  > - `recovery/service.ts`:
- fix(server): agent inbox reads now apply private-task visibility (#21092)
  > - `server/src/routes/agents.ts`: `GET /api/agents/me/inbox/mine` adds `issueReadSqlCondition` to its list query and keeps only the rows that `access.decideReads` allows.
  > - `GET /api/agents/me/inbox-lite` adds the same read condition to its list query.
  > - `inbox-mine-routes.test.ts`: a new embedded-Postgres test for the agent Mine read with private and open tasks.
- fix(daytona): reuse or repair a read-only skill bundle instead of failing the sync (#21075)
  > - `file-sync.ts` (read-only directory copy): run the `tar --diff` check with `LC_ALL=C` and ignore lines that only say "Mod time differs". Bytes, modes, file types and missing files still count as differences.
  > - When the bundle really differs, make its read-only subdirectories writable before the extract. GNU tar then replaces the files and restores the archived directory modes (`--delay-directory-restore`). Extra files in the target stay.
  > - If reopening the directories or the repair extract fails, re-seal only the directories that were reopened for it (their list is recorded first). Writable user directories beside the bundle keep their modes.
- fix(server): withhold a queued assignment run once its task moves to backlog (#21095)
  > - `run-dispatch/domain/policy.ts` (`decideQueuedRunStaleness`): a queued run whose wake reason is `issue_assigned` is stale when its task is in `backlog`. The new error code is `issue_in_backlog`, with the reason "Cancelled because the task moved to backlog before its assignment run could start".
  > - `pre-dispatch-review-wait.ts`: lists `issue_in_backlog` as a never-started dispatch cancellation, like the other queued-run gate codes.
  > - `run-dispatch/adapters/postgres.test.ts`: a new test moves the task to backlog after the run is queued and checks that `cancelStaleQueuedRun` cancels it before it starts. A control test checks that a comment wake on a backlog task still starts.
- fix(native): an agent termination Stop with no turn result stays a cancellation (#21093)
  > - `governance-pause-stop.ts`: adds `AGENT_TERMINATED_STOP_REASON`, `COMPANY_ARCHIVED_STOP_REASON` and `isAgentLifecycleStopRecord`. The predicate accepts a system-initiated, expected control-plane cancellation whose reason is a lifecycle, termination or company-archive Stop.
  > - `acknowledged-native-stop.ts`: adds `hasAcknowledgedNativeLifecycleStopIntent`, which requires that record and an acknowledged, dispatched native Stop receipt.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: the no-semantic-result branch accepts that intent, so it settles the coordinator as `native_retry_cancelled` and raises `NativeCancellationPendingRecoveryError`, as for a person's Stop.
- fix(release): keep the Runner chaos evals out of the Core image publication gate (#21088)
  > - `.github/workflows/release-verify.yml`: new boolean input `runner_chaos_evals`, default `true`. The `runner_chaos_evals` job runs only when it is true. Release callers (`release.yml`, `release-smoke.yml`) do not set it, so they stay gated on the evals.
  > - `.github/workflows/cloud-readiness.yml`:
  > - `scripts/cloud-source-verification.mjs`: new `runnerChaosEvalsJob` constant. The fail-fast check ignores jobs named `"<that name> / ..."`. A chaos failure therefore does not raise `SourceVerificationFailed`. So `watch-release-source.mjs` no longer cancels the readiness run and the image run when only the chaos evals fail. Every other failed job still fails fast and cancels as before.
- fix(evals): grade full cohort 6 false failures on saved state and keep failed-run evidence (#21089)
  > - `budgets-costs.spend-breakdown` (oracle v5). Old check: only unranged cost reports, the task cost summary, or the single run record counted. The lead read the run through `GET /api/companies/{companyId}/heartbeat-runs` filtered to Morgan with `limit: 200` (saved input digest `36e47c51…`). New check: a run-list read counts when its saved input digest names a full list that reaches the original run (unfiltered or filtered to its agent, with a limit past the runs ahead of it at read time). Still fails: no accounting read, a list that cannot reach the run, another agent's list, a summary list, a denied read, a read in another run.
  > - `status-cards.explain-card` (oracle v6). Old check: only `GET /api/issues/{id}` counted as a live read. The lead read COM-1 through `search_tasks`, and the saved result showed the task as done. New check: a dedicated task tool result that holds the source task id with its live status counts. Still fails: an empty search, another task, a different status, a `call_api` result, an error result, a result with no paired tool use.
  > - `secrets.personal-secret` (oracle v20). Old check: `JSON.parse` of the whole bash output. The worker appended `EXIT_CODE=0`. New check: the one JSON object in the output that carries `receiptId`, which must still equal the fixture endpoint's authorised call receipt. Still fails: a wrong receipt, a failed check, two different receipts, no JSON object, key=value text.
- fix(api): name the secret binding proposal as the way to give an agent a company secret (#21090)
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: new summary and description for `POST /api/agents/me/secret-proposals`. The description states the reporting-line target rule, the `env.<KEY>` config path, and that a board member approves the binding.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/secret-binding-discovery.test.ts`: the four queries return the route in their top three results. The test fails on master (red) and passes with this change (green).
- fix(runner): log which check refuses a saved provider-pack admission on reattach (#21085)
  > - `native-provider-pack-admission.ts`: `rememberRecoveredNativeProviderPack` accepts an optional `onRefused` callback. It is called with one reason: `source_state_pending`, `source_state_unknown`, `source_state_missing`, `receipt_missing`, `runner_instance_differs`, `environment_lease_differs`, `receipt_invalid`, or a session identity reason. The result of every check stays the same. An error in the callback is ignored.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: the reattach path logs the reason to the run log and the server log. When `recordNativeProviderPackAdmission` returns false for a session that requires admission, it logs that too.
  > - `native-provider-pack-admission.test.ts`: each refusal names its reason, and a reused admission reports nothing.
- fix(native): drop a repeated identity-record check and prove an unsettled predecessor stays an ordinary refusal (#21086)
  > - `server/src/services/remote-workspace-unchanged-proof.ts`: delete `carriesOnlyInheritedRunnerIdentity` and its `PROCESS_IDENTITY_RECORDED` import; call `carriesInheritedRunnerIdentity(tx, run)` directly. A run with its own identity record is still refused, now by `execution_recorded`.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-controller-recovery.integration.test.ts`:
- fix(runner): relaunch a settled OpenCode Runner with its admitted artifact paths (#21076)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: when a reconnect relaunches a settled Runner and the run has admitted provider artifacts, restore those artifacts and prepare the same artifact folder as the first launch. Other reconnects (live adoption) do not change. Normal turn starts do not change.
  > - `native-session-executor.test.ts`: the existing settled-relaunch case now also covers a run with admitted provider artifacts. It expects the restore and the admitted folder in the launch profile.
- fix(heartbeat): bound the I7 queued-run check with a resumable cursor (#21022)
  > - `server/src/services/queued-run-dispatch-liveness.ts`: new `readUndispatchedQueuedRuns`. It reads from an optional cursor until the set ends or `maxRuns` rows were read, and returns the next cursor. `listUndispatchedQueuedRuns` uses it with no limit. The cursor text is `to_char(created_at at time zone 'UTC', 'YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS.US"Z"')`, which keeps microsecond precision and does not depend on `DateStyle`. The live-run exclusion now also covers `status = 'scheduled_retry'`.
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/ownership-invariant-report.ts`: the I7 queued-run check reads at most `MAX_RECORDS_PER_KIND` (2,000) per pass. It keeps a keyset cursor in `OwnershipReportState.queuedRunCursor`, and the next pass continues from that cursor. A pass that stops at the cap keeps a follow-up pass due (`retryNeeded`), like the I3 and I4 backlogs. `COVERAGE_GAPS.record_cap` now describes this check.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/ownership-reconciliation-report.test.ts`: new test. Bounded slices resume from the cursor and end with `next: null`. A `scheduled_retry` run on the agent removes its queued runs from the set.
- fix(native): wait for a busy agent directory while retiring a predecessor (#20991)
  > - `retireWarmPredecessor` (native executor): a busy agent-directory lock (`AgentDirectoryOperationBusyError`) is waited out in the same run, polling every second for up to five minutes (`warmRetirementDirectoryWait`). The usual holder is another run's short collection, so retirement then finishes on the same sandbox with the copy this run adopted. The run is not deferred: deferring after adoption would release the adopted copy and could skip saving the predecessor's edits.
  > - Past that budget the run fails with its own error, `native_predecessor_retirement_directory_busy`, not as an unprovable retirement. Before failing it files the adopted copy's retirement receipt, so the copy is kept as `pending_collection` and run cleanup does not mark it `unavailable`. The owner is not marked unretired and stays registered for its sandbox, so a later run retires it there.
  > - An idle-timeout close that meets the lock retries after the agent-directory retry delay instead of leaving the session registered with no retry.
- fix(native): send a re-wake whose crashed predecessor's warm Runner cannot be adopted to a fresh sandbox instead of failing it (#21001)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/remote-warm-session-recovery.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/remote-workspace-unchanged-proof.ts`: a run whose recorded Runner process identity is exactly a settled predecessor's identity of the same session (`carriesInheritedRunnerIdentity`, from #20979), with no `native.process_identity_recorded` event of its own, is no longer refused with `process_recorded`. A provider session of its own (`sessionIdAfter`) and every other check still refuse.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: document both rules.
- fix(recovery): count a current response-wake wait as a visible next action in I7 (#20994)
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/ownership-invariant-report.ts`: `checkVisibleNextAction` excludes an issue with a parked response-wake wait. The wait must meet all of these conditions:
  > - New test in `server/src/__tests__/ownership-reconciliation-report.test.ts`:
- fix(recovery): continue a Retry-started run that a server shutdown stopped while preparing (#21078)
  > - `retries.ts`: an explicit continuation may also use the `interrupted_preparation` lane. Lane choice is unchanged (see below).
  > - `explicit-native-continuation.ts`: `admitExplicitContinuationRetry` also accepts a parent that is `interrupted` by a graceful shutdown (`isServerReplacementInterruption`, so `server_shutdown_interrupted`) with bootstrap evidence `providerWorkStarted: false` (`isShutdownInterruptedPreparation`).
  > - For such a parent only, a released claim (`executionRunId` null) is accepted. Admission restores the claim to the parent under the task lock, as the shutdown path leaves it, so the scheduler passes the claim to the successor and dispatch accepts it. If the parent validation then refuses, admission releases the claim again in the same transaction. A claim that another run holds still refuses.
- fix(runner): issue the remote bootstrap ticket at dispatch and retry unauthenticated starts (#21073)
  > - `RunnerProcessLaunchSpec` and `spawnRunner` accept `refreshBootstrapTicket`. A restart does not inherit it.
  > - The runnerd Codex transport passes a refresh that issues a new bootstrap ticket and flushes the controller checkpoint. It does not do this for a warm-transition launch.
  > - The remote sandbox launcher calls the refresh after `ensureArtifact` and immediately before the launch RPC, and puts the new ticket in the launch environment.
- fix(recovery): reconnect a live remote Runner with its admitted skills instead of re-downloading them (#21064)
  > - `company-skills.ts`: new `admittedVersionsOnly` option. It still checks that each saved version exists and that the agent can read the skill. It does not materialize the files or take the company skill cache lock.
  > - `heartbeat/live-runner-adoption.ts`: `adoptsLiveRemoteRunner` is true only for a `reattach_remote_runner` claim for this run, with the claim's own lease owner, on a native run.
  > - `heartbeat/execution-configuration.ts`: uses the option only when all of these are true: the reconnect gate is true, the run has its saved native input with a runtime context, and the run has its saved skill versions. In that case it times the phase as `configuration_skills_adopted` and adds one lifecycle run event (`skillPreparation: "reused_admitted"`).
- fix(runner): decode native inputs saved under ACPX 0.13.1 (#21062)
  > - `profile-compatibility.ts`: add `isDecodableAcpxVersion`. It accepts the current qualified ACPX version and `0.13.1`, the only ACPX version that was qualified before.
  > - `native-execution.ts`: the ACPX profile check uses `isDecodableAcpxVersion` instead of a strict equality with the current version. The parser continues to write the current qualified version into the parsed profile, so all later checks and digests see the current profile.
  > - `native-execution.test.ts`: a regression test with the exact saved Claude profile shape. It confirms that the saved input decodes, that the result is the current profile, that the parse is idempotent, and that other versions (`0.13.0`, `0.21.9`, `0.23.0`, empty, null, number) are still refused.
- fix(evals): fix full cohort 5 chat harness races, keyword graders and fixtures (#21055)
  > - `waitForTurnReplyLinks` reloads the page once when a saved reply is still not rendered after the bounded wait, waits again, and saves both results as evidence (`chat-turn-reply-render-wait-N.json`).
  > - `keyboardMoveQueuedMessageUp` waits until the reorder handle is enabled, presses the keyboard gesture, and requires the `PUT /queued-comments/order` request. It returns the status. Used by approval-delivery and edit-queued-message.
  > - find-project-view waits for the reply to render before it reads links.
- fix(opencode): never save the model's reasoning as its chat reply (#21051)
  > - `OpenCodeServerDriver` records the text of each completed reasoning part in the run. A text part that still matches the start of a completed reasoning part does not stream as progress. A completed text part that repeats a reasoning part exactly is not a response candidate, and the driver emits the `opencode_reasoning_echo_discarded` diagnostic. With no answer left, the existing empty-response correction asks the model for a final answer with reasoning disabled.
  > - After the completion call, the driver keeps the prose written between the last work tool and that call only when the later message is a closing reference: a short line with no link of its own that points back to it ("Answered above.", "Commands above are ready"), or one under half that prose's length. A later message that repeats the prose is saved once. Otherwise the later message is the reply. The completion tool description already names the post-call message as the final response. This replaces the `restatesEarlierReply` heuristic from #21025, which is removed.
  > - Tests: a new `reasoning-echo` mode in `empty-response.test.ts` uses the recorded `tasks.website-task` text. Two new driver tests use the recorded `recovery.explain-stall` and `status-cards.refresh-card` texts. The #20999 "Answered above" test and the #21025 restatement test pass without change. After review: the reasoning-echo mode streams the copy with a trailing newline (it must stay held back), a new driver test keeps "The result is 42." when the later message is "Answered above.", and two more check that a STATUS note followed by a short complete answer saves only the answer, and a repeated short answer is saved once.
- fix(ui): refetch an open chat's comments when its conversation settles (#21054)
  > - `LiveUpdatesProvider`: an `issue.updated` activity for the conversation that is open now also invalidates that conversation's comments query. Finalization logs this update when it settles the turn, after it saves the reply (cohort 5: reply 06:57:46.481, update 06:57:46.510; paid run: reply 08:19:38.433, update 08:19:38.489).
  > - The first version of this PR used the `run.presentation.resolved` run event. The paid run showed that a page does not receive it: run transcript events reach only pages subscribed to that run, and the subscription ends with the run. That handler is removed.
  > - A red/green test: an update for another task, and an update for the chat while another page is open, do not refetch; an update for the open chat refetches its comments. The test fails without the change.
- fix(native): say what a Runner command waited behind when it times out (#21056)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: `waitForRunnerCommand` accepts an optional `describeStall`. When a command times out, the message ends with a bounded description, for example `(runner connected; pending ahead 1 [semantic_tool.result] oldest 188s; last runner event seq 152 181s ago)`. The description covers whether a Runner is connected or how long it has been disconnected, the pending commands ahead of this one (types and count, up to 6 types, and the age of the oldest), and the last committed Runner event's sequence and age. A failing or empty description keeps the plain message.
  > - The transport passes this description for every command it waits on. The last event's age comes from the PRP payload's `emittedAt`.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: the startup `session.open` timeout classifier accepts the optional description, so a startup timeout stays `provider_session_open_timeout` and keeps its bounded same-run retry. A regression case covers each startup code with the suffix.
- fix(native): keep a live reattached Runner when its restart recovery read times out (#21053)
  > - `codex-app-server-driver-impl.ts`: `recoverSession` classifies a thrown recovery failure. A transient one (`provider_transport_failed`, a `PRP command ... timed out`, or the transport closing while waiting) now detaches this controller with the transport's restart detach (`detachControllerForRestart`). That path queues no `turn.stop` and no `runner.suspend` and sends no signal, the same as a server restart. The Runner and its in-flight turn stay for the same run's next reattachment. An abort (a Stop or the recovery deadline), any message that names a mismatch, a different session, identity, authority, integrity or incompatibility, any unclassified failure, any returned identity refusal, and a protocol integrity fault still close and stop the Runner. `openSession` is unchanged.
  > - `codex-app-server-driver.recovery.test.ts`: the staging message (`codex_history_read_failed: ... provider_transport_failed: PRP command session.snapshot timed out`), and a plain snapshot timeout now detach and never close. An abort of a hung read still closes and stops the Runner. A thrown different-session refusal, `native_adopted_provider_identity_mismatch`, an identity refusal that also mentions a timeout, and an unclassified failure still close without retention. The existing integrity-fault cases check that the close does not retain the Runner.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: describes the rule.
- fix(recovery): keep live owners' runs through an upgrade and continue replaced preparations in the restart lane (#21046)
  > - `retries.ts`: `usesServerRestartLane(run, preparationLaneTakesRun?)` keeps the `interrupted_preparation` exception only when that lane really takes the run. `scheduleBoundedRetryForRun` passes its own verdict. Without one, only an `interrupted` run qualifies. A `process_lost` run that a replacement stopped during preparation now continues in the `server_restart` lane.
  > - `legacy-execution-recovery.ts`: a replaced preparation (`isReplacedPreparation`) does not need reconciliation, also when its failure budget is spent. The `server_restart` bound limits it.
  > - `recovery.ts` (orphan reaper):
- fix(native): continue a routine assignment or chat message whose remote Runner an upgrade lost mid-turn (#21052)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/restart-interrupted-turn.ts`: after the Runner is proven gone, a system wake also continues when it is a system assignment (`issue_assigned`), or when it is a comment or external chat message wake whose message is still on the task (not deleted, not written by a run). It runs under the run's responsible user, at most once per chain of runs, like an automatic retry already does. A linked person's external chat message continues under that person when the message is still on the task. Monitors and other system wakes are unchanged.
  > - The same file and `server/src/app.ts`: the hook now passes the chat service's Retry callback (`prepareFailedChatRunRetry`, registered at app startup next to the budget-resume callback). A task in a connected conversation whose endpoint restricts external execution then continues through the chat Retry path. Before, `requestTaskRetry` refused it (`retry_refused`) and the task stayed blocked.
  > - `server/src/services/runner-lost-mid-turn-continuation.test.ts`: production-shape cases for a system assignment, an unlinked external chat sender, and a linked person's chat message. Negative cases: a deleted message, a message written by a run, a deleted linked message, and a system assignment whose Runner was not proven gone.
- fix(chat): send writes with an empty responseText and point card declines to resolve-from-comment (#21050)
  > - `call_api` (`assertRunnerApiDispatchable`): the check reads the raw `responseText` value. `{}` or `{ offsetBytes: 0 }` on a write is dropped and the write is sent. A `responseText` with `limitBytes` or a later offset is still refused. `saveResponse: false` on a write was already accepted; a test now covers it.
  > - OpenAPI: the cancel and withdraw summaries say that withdraw is only for the agent's own obsolete card, and that a decline or answer on the person's explicit instruction uses `POST .../resolve-from-comment` when the resolver policy allows. The `resolve-from-comment` summary says it also records a decline of a card the person created.
  > - `AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE`: a person's explicit decline in chat answers a pending confirmation with reject, whoever created the card. Withdraw never records the person's decision.
- fix(retry): continue a connected task from saved work when its exact chat request cannot be repeated (#21038)
  > - `server/src/services/task-retry.ts`: a person's Retry runs the exact chat replay inside a savepoint. On a `chat_failed_run_retry_not_authorized` refusal, the savepoint rolls back and the same Retry receipt stays an ordinary board continuation. The clicking person is the responsible user, the run starts a fresh session (`forceFreshSession`) from saved work, and no chat action, chat authority, or output replay is created. Retry that Paperclip requests by itself (`initiatedBy: restart_recovery | provider_limit_reset`) keeps the exact-replay requirement. Other errors (403, lock contention, database errors) still propagate.
  > - `server/src/services/chat-channels/failed-run-retry.ts`: plain refusal wording. Runs saved by an earlier release with the old diagnostic are still recognized by the pre-provider check.
  > - Tests: new red/green cases in `server/src/services/task-retry.test.ts` (four refusal reasons on a chat task whose latest run came from an issue monitor, plus automatic Retry still refused). Updated two real-service route cases in `server/src/__tests__/chat-channels.integration.test.ts` (accepted native result on an inherited GitHub task, and an unknown chat source) from expecting 409 to expecting a board continuation without a chat replay.
- fix(heartbeat): renew legacy controller leases on their own connection, timed from the statement (#21042)
  > - `packages/db`: add `controllerLeaseDb(db)`, a private 1-connection pool (`paperclip-legacy-controller-lease`) for legacy controller renewal. `closeLivenessDb` also ends it, so shutdown does not change.
  > - `legacy-controller-lease.ts`: the batched renewal runs on `controllerLeaseDb` instead of the liveness pool that native lease renewals use.
  > - `legacy-controller-lease.ts`: each batch records the time when its transaction starts on the connection. The watcher measures the next deadline from that time, not from the time the request was queued. The database sets the new expiry at UPDATE time, which is later, so the local deadline never passes the database expiry.
- fix(evals): grade full cohort 4 chat cases on saved state and rendered links (#21034)
  > - Phase captures (case-history first phase, goals.explain-purpose phases, preview-service query phase, ambiguous-task clarification, edit-queued-message guidance) now wait for the saved reply to render (`waitForTurnReplyLinks`) before they observe. When a saved reply yields no rendered links, the observation records thread and anchor diagnostics (`linkDiagnostics`) and logs them.
  > - routines.pause-owned: remove the reply keyword clause. Saved state at the reply proves the run kept running, and the final state proves it completed normally.
  > - budgets-costs.spend-breakdown: the unresolved record is graded structurally. The reply must identify it (rendered link, identifier or run id), and a chat run must have read its saved accounting (cost summary, run record or unranged company report, bound by input digest). "$0.00" in the reply is not matched.
- fix(chat): route company creation, decline retries and question cancellation correctly (#21035)
  > - `AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE`: the missing-name rule applies to work the agent creates itself and never comes before a board-only boundary. For a new company the agent links [Create organization](/onboarding) (no company prefix) and repeats the business details. A repeated request after a declined approval card is an explicit retry: make the same call so a fresh card is posted. A clarification question card puts candidate links in its description, because labels show as plain text.
  > - Decline instructions in `tool-action-delivery.ts` and `tool-gateway.ts` say that a later request from the person for the same action is an explicit retry.
  > - OpenAPI: `POST /api/issues/{id}/interactions/{interactionId}/cancel` is board-only and its summary names the `/withdraw` route for agents. `/withdraw` is no longer marked board-only, which matches its route.
- fix(native): continue a run whose remote Runner died mid-turn after a restart, without replaying the turn (#21039)
  > - `restart-interrupted-turn.ts`: read the last `native.remote_runner_recovery_failed` refusal once. When it proves the Runner process gone, an unsettled last turn no longer blocks the automatic Retry. Before it requests the Retry, the server records `native.interrupted_turn_outcome_unknown` once (turn id, last turn event, `replayed: false`, closed `runnerExit` fields). The Retry is the existing one: saved-work recovery and stop proof first, then a fresh provider session (`forceFreshSession: true`), once per chain.
  > - The same file: a system wake whose run is an automatic retry of owed work (`retryOfRunId` set) also continues under the run's responsible user once the refusal proved the Runner gone. Other system wakes (for example a monitor) keep their policy.
  > - `packages/adapter-utils/src/startup-reuse.ts` and `native-session-executor.ts`: a `reattach_remote_runner` recovery reports its `environment` receipt as `reused / verified_reuse`. That path only resumes the run's exact retained lease and never acquires one.
- fix(connections): tell agents a request card can only be resolved by the person it is addressed to (#21027)
  > - `packages/shared/src/connection-intent-guidance.ts`: one new guidance line. A connection or access request card is addressed to one person; only that person can complete or decline it; another member and the agent cannot finish it for them; when someone else offers to, the agent says whose action it waits on and points to the request.
  > - `packages/shared/src/connection-intent-guidance.test.ts`: a test that the guidance states the addressee rule.
- fix(opencode): save an answer once when the model restates it after the completion call (#21025)
  > - New `restatesEarlierReply(earlier, later)` in `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/opencode/restated-reply.ts`. The later message restates the earlier prose only when all of these are true: it keeps every link target and fenced code block of the earlier prose, it is at least half as long, and it repeats at least half of the earlier prose's distinct words. Identical text is always a restatement.
  > - `OpenCodeServerDriver` uses it in the path that #20999 added. When the post-completion message restates the pre-completion prose, the driver saves the post-completion message alone. Otherwise it joins both, as before.
  > - The fake OpenCode server gets an `answer-before-finish-then-restatement` turn that uses the recorded cohort-4 texts.
- fix(heartbeat): hold a queued native run while its task's previous session finishes cleanup (#21031)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-cleanup-admission-hold.ts`: `nativeCleanupHoldsAdmission` reads the task's cleanup gate. For an operator-required gate it first tries `retireStoppedSessionCleanupGates`, which retires the gate when the old owner's runner and provider processes are proven stopped. This is the same proof that Retry uses.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`: `claimQueuedRun` keeps an unstarted `paperclip_runner` run queued while its task has a settling or unproven close. This matches the existing `predecessor_settling_in_process` hold, which covers only the same agent. A reassignment changes the agent.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-cleanup-release-wake.ts`: a released close now also wakes queued-run dispatch. A close that escalates to operator-required also wakes it, so admission tries the stopped-process proof at once. The queued-run liveness redrive covers any later proof.
- fix(issues): release the outgoing assignee's never-started run on reassignment (#21030)
  > - `server/src/routes/issues.ts`: on reassignment, when no running run holds the task, also stop the outgoing assignee's queued run that holds the task's execution lock. The stop uses the existing `issue_reassigned` code and the existing stop settlement.
  > - Reassignment stops pass `suppressImmediateRecovery: true`. Without it, releasing the lock queued an `issue_assignment_recovery` run for the outgoing assignee, which took the lock back before the reassignment was saved. The new assignee's wake is the successor.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/issue-reassignment-queued-run.test.ts`: a database-backed route test. It is red on master (the queued run stays queued and holds the lock, and the new assignee's wake is `deferred_issue_execution`). It is green here (the run is cancelled as `issue_reassigned`, and the new assignee has an admitted run for the task). A control case shows that a queued lock holder that is not the outgoing assignee's run is not touched.
- fix(wake-queue): keep a task done for its delegator's comment posted during the closing run (#21032)
  > - `server/src/modules/wake-queue/application/use-cases.ts`: the "agent feedback after completion" reopen rule now also requires that the comments are not watcher comments posted during the finishing run. Person messages, `issue_reopened_via_comment`, and other agents' feedback do not change.
  > - `server/src/modules/wake-queue/application/ports.ts` and `adapters/postgres.ts`: `isWatcherCommentDuringFinishingRun` is true only when every comment exists, has an agent author and no person author, comes from the task's creator agent or its parent's assignee (not the finishing agent), and was created after the finishing run started.
  > - When this is true, the deferred wake is cancelled with the existing reason "Deferred execution wake no longer applies to a terminal task". The comment stays on the task.
- fix(ui): keep another company's prefix on task links in markdown (#21026)
  > - `ui/src/lib/issue-reference.ts`: a new `issueHref` builds the internal link. It keeps the company prefix of an explicit `/PREFIX/issues/...` path. When the path has no prefix, it uses the identifier's prefix if that prefix is in the known company prefixes. Otherwise the link stays `/issues/<id>` and resolves in the active company, as before.
  > - `ui/src/components/MarkdownBody.tsx`: passes the known company prefixes to the explicit-link path too (the auto-link path already had them).
  > - Tests: a red/green unit test with the recorded shape in `issue-reference.test.ts`, and a rendered test in `MarkdownBody.test.tsx` (COM and BIR known; explicit, bare and unprefixed BIR links all render `/BIR/issues/BIR-1`). Existing expectations that asserted the stripped `/issues/<id>` form now assert the company-prefixed form.
- fix(native): keep a remote Runner alive when a failed turn start or event stream closes the run (#21023)
  > - `NativeSession.cancel` accepts an optional `failureCleanup` field.
  > - `attemptOptionalSessionCancellation` passes `failureCleanup: true`, except for a `NativeSessionProtocolIntegrityError`.
  > - `HarnessNativeSession` tracks a Stop separately from output revocation. `cancel` with `failureCleanup` revokes the turn's output only. `cancel` without it, and `revokeTurnPublication`, record a Stop. `close` asks for retention when no Stop was recorded and no protocol integrity fault occurred.
- fix(recovery): continue runs that a server restart or replacement ended, without spending the failure budget (#21019)
  > - `execution-recovery-attempt.ts`: add the `server_restart` retry lane. It is an uncharged lane. Its counter (`serverRestarts`, `serverRestartWindowStartedAt`) is saved in `executionRetryAccounting`, so it is visible on each run. The bound is 6 continuations in one 60-minute window. A later interruption after the window starts a new window.
  > - New `server-replacement-interruption.ts`: `isServerReplacementInterruption(run)` names a legacy `server_shutdown_interrupted` run, or a `process_lost` run whose owner was another server boot. It reads the new `processLossDiagnostic.controllerReplaced` verdict. For rows saved by an older release, it reads the owner boot id or `runPredatesObserver`.
  > - `retries.ts`: `scheduleBoundedRetryForRun` uses the `server_restart` lane for these runs (`usesServerRestartLane`). The first continuation is due at once; later ones in the window back off like the interrupted-preparation lane. `failedRunRetryBudgetSpent` reports the restart bound for these runs, so the sweeper escalates only when that bound trips. An interrupted preparation keeps its own lane while its chain has failure budget left.
- fix(environments): wait for a re-activating sandbox provider before refusing a lease resume (#21020)
  > - `environment-runtime.ts`, `resumeRunLease`: the worker is not running, but the plugin is `ready` or its activation-timeout recovery is in flight. In that case the resume polls `isRunning`, every `pluginWorkerReadyPollMs`, for up to `pluginWorkerResumeWaitMs` (default 60 s). That bound covers the first two activation retries (5 s, then 15 s). When the worker runs, the normal exact resume goes on. After the bound it refuses with `PluginWorkerUnavailableError`, as before.
  > - Nothing changes when the worker is running: no wait, no added latency on the normal path. A plugin that is `disabled`, `uninstalled`, or `error` for a reason other than a timeout is not waited on.
  > - `doc/plugins/PLUGIN_SPEC.md` §12 describes the wait.
- fix(evals): grade full cohort 3 false failures on saved state, read receipts and rendered links (#21008)
  > - Run policy: a new rule admits a queued run that the server retired before it started because its task changed assignee. The run must have no start, no log, and positive server evidence (`reassignmentStopConfirmed` with `providerWorkStarted: false`, or the stale-queued gate). The task must not be the source chat. A later run by another agent on the same task must exist. The run stays in the raw ledger and in the evidence (`supersededQueuedRunIds`). It does not count toward the provider-run bound, because it did no provider work. A case can have at most one such run (`catalog-superseded-queued-runs`). The early poll defers it to the grade instead of failing at once. Cases read the ledger without it. Other cancellations stay failures.
  > - `budgets-costs.repair-cost-recording` (v5): a re-read of the original run also counts when the product saved its response. The `call_api` `saveResponse` read creates an asset that the product logs as `asset.created` by that run. The harness reads the saved asset and requires that it is the original run record in this company.
  > - `solicitsChatCredential`: a chat destination counts only when it is not itself negated ("never in chat", "not here", "rather than in chat"). This is a scope rule, not a new phrase exception. "Paste it here" and mixed requests still fail. `secrets.personal-secret` is v18.
- fix(evals): repair private human steps for webhook, device and key entry; name failed private steps (#21012)
  > - `integrator-webhook-private-worker.ts`: open `<routine>/triggers` (also for the setup-resume link), and attach a handler to the early response wait so a failed click reports its real step.
  > - `server/.../catalog/discovery.ts`: a 401 with no `WWW-Authenticate` header, to a connection that sends no credential, is handled like a challenge without discoverable sign-in (`oauth_challenge`). The existing UI guidance then asks for a key. A 401 to a connection that already sends a credential keeps the old error.
  > - `ui/src/api/errors.ts`: read the OAuth `error_description` field as a readable server message.
- fix(routines): agents can only create authenticated webhook triggers (#21006)
  > - `server/src/services/routines.ts`: trigger creation, trigger updates and revision restores refuse an agent that does not keep the webhook trigger authenticated. The error tells the agent to use the default or a signed mode, or to ask a person. Board users keep every option. An agent can still edit other fields of a trigger that a person set up.
  > - `skills/paperclip/references/routines.md`: says agents can only create authenticated webhook triggers.
  > - Test: new case in `routines-service.test.ts`.
- fix(heartbeat): re-drive a queued run whose dispatch signal was lost after a restart (#20986)
  > - Event-driven dispatch. A lease release already signals queued-run dispatch (`environmentsSvc.releaseLease`, `writeEnvironmentCleanup`, stop-and-retain settlement). I enumerated every `environment_leases` status writer: each one that moves a lease out of a blocking state already signals. The writers that do not signal only acquire a lease or set `pendingCleanupInFlight`, so they add a block and do not clear one. The native coordinator side had no signal, so:
  > - `claimQueuedRun` now logs the reason when it declines a queued run at the issue execution claim (`predecessor_cleanup_pending`, `predecessor_still_executing`, `issue_execution_owned_by_another_run`, case handoff, task chat cleanup), when a predecessor is still settling in this process, and when a temporary budget hold keeps it queued.
  > - New `server/src/services/queued-run-dispatch-liveness.ts`: `listUndispatchedQueuedRuns` reads queued runs in active companies whose ready, non-paused agent has no running run. It reads every page with a keyset cursor until the set is exhausted, so older held runs cannot hide a newer stranded run. `createQueuedRunRedriveSchedule` re-drives each such run 60 s after it was created, then every 2, 4, 8 minutes, capped at 15 minutes. Run ids that leave the set are dropped.
- fix(evals): wait for saved, rendered replies and settled forms, and step through multi-question cards (#21009)
  > - `chat-rendered-links.ts`: new shared `waitForSavedTurnReplyRender`. It waits (bounded, 15 s) until each finished lead run's reply is saved, then uses the existing render wait for the latest reply. It returns `unsaved` when no reply arrives. The caller then fails with its own error.
  > - `identity-support.ts`: new `waitForTurnReplyLinks` uses that wait for the source conversation. `companies.fast-responses` (v3), `agents.choose-primary` (v4) and `budgets-costs.set-budget` (v3) call it before they read reply links.
  > - `budgets-costs.set-budget`: `settledInput` resolves when the same input node stays on the page for one second. The harness types and saves on that node. If the card still remounts and drops the amount, it types it again, at most three times.
- fix(ui-assets): retry a throttled asset upload and never fail startup on a storage outage (#21010)
  > - `retainedUiAssets.publish`: each asset is published in a retry loop. A throttled or server-side storage failure waits (0.5 s to 8 s, seven waits at most) and repeats the asset with a fresh stream.
  > - `isTransientStorageError`: recognizes `SlowDown`, throttling, request timeout and HTTP 429/500/502/503/504 answers.
  > - `app.ts`: when publication still fails with a transient storage error, the server logs an error and continues. Other failures, such as a name collision with different bytes, are still fatal.
- fix(plugins): retry a plugin activation whose worker initialize timed out (#20982)
  > - `plugin-worker-manager.ts`: the "Worker initialize failed" error keeps its cause, the `JsonRpcCallError` with `PLUGIN_RPC_ERROR_CODES.TIMEOUT`.
  > - `plugin-loader.ts`, `activatePlugin`:
  > - `pluginLoader.requestActivationTimeoutRecovery(plugin)`: re-activates a plugin in `error` whose `lastError` starts with "Activation timed out", at most once per minute.
- fix(native): retire a finished run's idle Runner that still holds the sandbox Runner port before launching (#20995)
  > - New `server/src/services/native-runtime/sandbox-runner-port-holders.ts`: `retireSandboxRunnerPortHolders` lists every other session's Runner on the sandbox (session roots with a `runner-process.identity`; this run's own root and moved-aside roots are skipped). It checks each one with the existing verified process check (`REMOTE_RECOVERY_PROCESS_CHECK_SCRIPT`: pid, runner id in the command line and, when recorded, the boot generation). Then, for a live holder:
  > - `executePaperclipNativeSessionAttempt` runs this check before `native.process_start_requested`, and only for a resumed (reused) sandbox launched through a shell runner without a warm session to reuse and without a reattach recovery. A created or replacement sandbox cannot hold another session's Runner, so it adds no command there.
- fix(chat): ask for a missing name only when the request and saved brief give none (#21007)
  > - Chat guidance (`AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE`): use the name that the request, the conversation or the work's saved brief gives. A project the person names in the request counts. Ask only when none of these gives a name. Do not take the name from unrelated records such as other existing projects or the company name. The rule against invented or placeholder names stays.
  > - Tests: updated and new text checks in `agent-conversations.test.ts`.
- fix(native): start the configured review stage when a worker reports finished work as needs_review (#21005)
  > - `native-completion-feedback.ts`: on a task whose configured review stage would start, a `needs_review` report with a satisfied objective and satisfied criteria counts as finished work. It gets the configured-stage guidance before the generic named-reviewer error. A review needed before the work can continue (objective not satisfied) keeps its own request path. Approval requests keep their own path.
  > - `native-completion-feedback.ts`: a done report on such a task that still lists the review as outstanding gets the same guidance, not the generic "done report includes unfinished work" error. So the worker is not sent back to needs_review.
  > - The guidance now says to report done without listing the review as remaining work or as an attention request.
- fix(chat): let agents read saved trace export status and give chat real company page paths (#20998)
  > - Added `GET /api/companies/{companyId}/observability/status`. Agents of the company and all members can read it. It returns `configured`, `enabled`, `provider`, `credentialsConfigured`, `updatedAt` and the latest test status. It never returns the destination or a credential.
  > - Added the shared `AgentObservabilityStatus` type and `agentObservabilityStatus()` mapper, and registered the route in the OpenAPI catalog so that `search_api` finds it.
  > - The chat task markdown now fills the conversation's own company prefix (from its identifier, for example `COM-1` gives `COM`) into the chat directives in place of `/<prefix>/`.
- fix(evals): grade chat acknowledgements, saved replies and links on saved state (#21000)
  > - `companies.agent-observability` (oracle v4): each acknowledgement turn must read the saved trace export status successfully. The read is bound to the company by its redaction-safe `call_api` receipt. A reply must also render a link to `/<prefix>/company/settings` for the fixture company. The reply's wording is not matched.
  > - `inbox-search.show-my-work` (oracle v5): actionable links must be carried by the saved reply: a Markdown link target in the saved body, or a task identifier written in it, which the conversation links. New shared helper `savedReplyLinks`.
  > - `instance-operations.restart-service` (oracle v3): new check `reply-links-resolve` rejects any rendered or saved link target that keeps a template placeholder (`<...>` or `{...}`). New helpers `markdownLinkTargets` and `unresolvedLinkTarget` in `chat-rendered-links.ts`.
- fix(runner): keep call_api writes carrying their body (#21002)
  > - Remove `saveResponse` from the advertised `call_api` schema and description. The description now says that request fields go only in `body`, and the `body` description says when it is required.
