  > - Add a separate read-only `save_api_response` tool (`operationId`, `pathParams`, `query`). It accepts only GET operation IDs, has no body field, and uses the existing `saveResponse` export path with the same access, sensitivity, budget and integrity rules.
  > - Refuse a write before dispatch when its body is missing: PATCH with a required body (as before), and POST or PUT whose JSON request schema names required fields. The error names the operation, the allowed fields, the received arguments, the shape to resend, and that `query`, `responseText` and `saveResponse` never carry request fields.
- fix(heartbeat): deliver a person's answer that waited behind a turn that closed the task (#20996)
  > - `wake-queue/application/use-cases.ts`: before cancelling a deferred assignee wake on a done task, check whether it carries a person's owed answer. If it does, reopen the task to `in_progress` and promote the wake. An answer continues the assignee's own execution, so the existing continuation status gate applies unchanged.
  > - `wake-queue/adapters/postgres.ts`: add `owedInteractionAnswer`. It applies only to an undelivered response that a person resolved, that is not a tool action, and that was saved at or before the task's `completedAt`. It returns `blocked` when unresolved blockers forbid `in_progress`. `reopenIssue` takes an optional target status.
  > - The blocked case cancels the answer wake with an explicit reason and does not reopen the task, so the release step does not fail. A regression test reproduces the earlier throw (fix for the Greptile P1).
- fix(opencode): keep the answer written before the completion call in the saved reply (#20999)
  > - `OpenCodeServerDriver` settles the final response from all eligible prose parts when it selects a later message after the completion call and the model also wrote prose after its last work tool and before that call. It joins the parts in order with a blank line. Other selection paths do not change.
  > - The chat transcript adapter removes the latest progress messages when they compose the saved response exactly, so the text does not show twice.
  > - Added a fake-server fixture and a driver test for the "answer, then a line that refers back" turn. Updated the existing post-tool test: the saved reply now keeps the earlier prose too, because structure cannot tell commentary from an answer.
- fix(native): prove an unconnected bootstrap from the sandbox's own Runner state on a volume-free server (#20992)
  > - `inspectOwnUnpreparedBootstrap` now runs for any `bootstrap_incomplete` claim of this run. When no proof was recorded, it records one only if the sandbox Runner state proves it never connected (`remoteRunnerStateNeverConnected`: `lastConnectionProtocolVersion: null`, `lastControllerCommandSeq: 0`, `reconnectCount: 0`, empty outbox and processed commands, no pending terminal delivery). The exact Runner process must also be stopped or proven absent by the existing `stopStaleRemoteRunnerBeforeBootstrap`, which uses the Runner state's own identity. All earlier conditions from #20972 still apply: same run, Runner instance and session, a lifecycle other than `suspended`, no failover backup and no archived rotation snapshot.
- fix(agents): keep a managed instructions bundle managed when a restarted volume-free server saves another setting (#20997)
  > - `server/src/services/agent-instructions.ts` (`syncInstructionsBundleConfigFromFilePath`): when the saved bundle is managed and the effective config keeps the saved `instructionsFilePath`, keep it managed with the root that `deriveBundleState` already resolves (the current server's managed root) and the saved entry file. A different `instructionsFilePath` is classified as before.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/agent-volume-free-config-patch.test.ts`: route tests with embedded Postgres for a Codex agent and a Claude agent: a one-setting change after a fresh-home restart, the same change on the original server, and a real switch to an external path, which is still refused.
  > - `doc/DEVELOPING.md`: describe how a volume-free server keeps a managed bundle across a new home.
- fix(ephemeral): start a volume-free server over harness backups a volume-free server left unsaved (#20990)
  > - `assertEphemeralServerData`: when the only reason is `native_harness_backups_require_migration` and the filesystem carries the volume-free owner marker (`volumeFreeControllerStateOwned`), the gate removes that blocker, logs a warning, and lets the server start.
  > - A filesystem without the marker is refused as before. All other blockers are unchanged.
  > - `readEphemeralServerDataEligibility` is unchanged, so the migration and enrollment callers still see the blocker.
- fix(retry): carry the original wake to every re-queued run (#20989)
  > - `server/src/services/execution-continuation.ts`: new `requeuedWakeReason`.
  > - This one point covers every successor that links with `retry_of_run_id`: bounded transient retries, `interrupted_preparation` (#20863), workspace and AI-connection waits, missing-comment repair (`run-lifecycle.ts`), native capacity retries, safe replacement, and the board Retry.
  > - Tests:
- fix(evals): grade approval delivery on the saved answer, not on which queued wake is listed (#20981)
  > - `runtime-approval-queue.ts`: add `approvalResponseListing`. After the discards, accept the answer listed alone and locked, or only the chat lead's relay listed. Reject any person message or an editable answer.
  > - Keep the edit attempt through the queue route for both listings. Require it to be refused and the saved answer to stay unchanged. Check the locked card in the UI when the answer is listed.
  > - Add `classifyApprovalSuccessors`. Grade delivery on saved run state: exactly one later worker turn bound to the answer, with the question ID and Acorn in its native input. Allow at most one other turn that carries only the lead's relay. Reject any other turn.
- fix(native): link a person-owned agent blocker to the agent's open question whatever owner kind the model reports (#20969)
  > - `evidence-classifier.ts`: new `nativeBlockerOwnerKind` and an optional `ownerKind` on the blocker assessment (`agent`, `user`, `system`, `external`, legacy `board`, or `unspecified`). A `user` owner is now board-owned, like `board`: a person resolves it, so it is not bound to the reporting agent.
  > - `status-arbiter.ts`: both agent-reported blocker decisions (`task_wide_blocker_bound`, `current_track_blocker_waiting`) carry `blockerOwnerKind`.
  > - `blocked-question-answer.ts` and the status committer: `questionsAwaitedByAgentBlock` records the agent's own pending, undismissed questions when the reported owner is one a person resolves: `user`, `external`, legacy `board`, or no recognized kind. `agent` and `system` owners and dependency blocks (`durable_dependency_blocker_bound`) stay excluded. A decision without `blockerOwnerKind` keeps the previous board-bound rule. All other guards are unchanged: asked by this agent, answered by a person, decision still current, `statusVersion` match, guarded checkout.
- fix(native): restart a follow-up the closing server cut before it attached, instead of reattaching to its predecessor's Runner (#20979)
  > - `native-restart-recovery.ts` (`claimNativeRestartRecoveries`): add the `remoteFollowUpNeverAttached` proof. It is true only when all of these are true:
  > - `verifyRemoteRunnerReattachment` is unchanged. It still refuses a Runner that names a different run.
  > - `heartbeat/queue.ts` (`startNextQueuedRunForAgent`): check run admission again before each claim. A run that a scan reaches after shutdown closed admission stays queued for the next server.
- fix(native): restart a bootstrap whose Runner exited before writing provider state on a resumed sandbox (#20972)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: when the full harness check fails during host preparation, `inspectOwnUnpreparedBootstrap` reads `runner-state.json` alone. It returns `not_suspended` for this run only when all of these are true: this run has a proven bootstrap restart (`bootstrap_incomplete` recovery plus the stop proof), no failover backup exists, no archived rotation snapshot is in use, and the Runner state names this run, this Runner instance and this session with a lifecycle other than `suspended`. Otherwise the result stays `unavailable`, as before. The existing restart path then moves the session root aside (it is kept, not deleted) and claims a new one.
  > - The proof of an own bootstrap restart was recorded only when the server still had the run's local Runner controller root. A volume-free server, or a root that held no identity, skipped that step, so the first version of this change did not fire live (wave `w-20261112T002811-s6402`). `inspectOwnUnpreparedBootstrap` now also establishes that proof itself from durable sandbox evidence: the run's `bootstrap_incomplete` recovery claim, a Runner state of this run, Runner instance and session that never connected (`lastConnectionProtocolVersion: null`, `lastControllerCommandSeq: 0`, `reconnectCount: 0`, empty outbox and processed commands, no pending terminal delivery), and the same verified stop or proven absence of that exact Runner process (`stopStaleRemoteRunnerBeforeBootstrap`). Any other result keeps `unavailable`.
  > - Chaos coverage: `--runner-bind-fault` in `tests/restart-chaos` gives every server of the wave `PAPERCLIP_TEST_RUNNER_BIND_FAULT=first_launch`. With `NODE_ENV=test` only, the remote Runner launcher starts a short-lived listener on the Runner's listen port before a Runner's first launch on that server, so the Runner exits with a real `runner_ingress_bind_conflict`. Documented in `tests/restart-chaos/README.md`.
- fix(evals): form repair-cost-recording's saved-usage debt and grade it by saved state (#20895)
  > - New `tests/runner-e2e/chat-accounting-fault.ts`, following `chat-archive-storage-fault.ts`: a temporary trigger on the case-owned database refuses cost-event writes for the one prerequisite task. The product's own accounting transaction rolls back, so the finished run keeps its saved usage pending and the product's automatic retries keep failing. Refused writes are counted in a rollback-proof sequence. It is allowed only when this case is the single selected execution, and it never writes product rows.
  > - Setup: the case creates the prerequisite task unassigned, arms the trigger, assigns the task, and waits until Costs shows the genuine `retryable` row. The trigger is closed on a setup failure and at cleanup.
  > - Act: the trigger is dropped right before the person clicks "Retry cost recording". Automatic recovery can record it first; the saved accounting read afterwards classifies either result (an unpriced event still shows in data quality, so it counts as unresolved).
- fix(evals): gate steering approval-delivery and steer-active on real product state (#20899)
  > - `runtime-approval-queue.ts`: gate the question on the parked turn (`pendingInputYield`: succeeded, yielded, `response_wake` keyed to the question). An ordinary human message then wakes a real second Morgan turn that waits at the task gate. Ordinary comments and the bound answer queue behind that turn. The run count goes from 3 to 4.
  > - `runtime-approval-queue.ts`: remove the reply keyword checks (`acornNamingDirection` and the "I have answered" regex). Grade exactly one saved answer path instead: the human response control, or an attributed canonical response.
  > - `runtime-active-steering.ts`: accept either definitive capability. Unsupported branch: no enabled Steer control, a visible Interrupt control, a steer attempt with the person's session refused with `409 steering_unsupported`, no `issue.queued_comment_steered` activity, the exact message still queued, and one continuation that consumes it and registers a rendered blue `website.html`.
- fix(evals): name unmet completion-gate readiness fields and grade the gate read, not reply words (#20887)
  > - `internal-task-completion.ts`: `unmetPriorDeliveryReview` lists each unmet readiness field. The poll uses it for `accept` and `timeoutDetail`.
  > - Setup and grader accept one or more succeeded Morgan runs (a product continuation is valid). Any failed, cancelled or timed-out worker run is an unmet readiness condition, so setup stops before the paid CEO turn. The delivery must come from one of these runs.
  > - The `truthful-outstanding-review` reply regex check is replaced by `checked-actual-completion-gate`: the CEO's chat run read the task (`GET /api/issues/{id}` or `get_task`, matched by record-bound receipt) and replied. The saved pending stage, unchanged policy and absence of a forced Done still prove the outcome.
- fix(workspaces): retry a workspace_finalize record write that loses a deadlock (#20971)
  > - New `server/src/services/transient-lock-contention.ts`: `postgresErrorCode`, `isTransientLockContention` and `retryTransientLockContention` (5 attempts, 100 ms × attempt backoff, only 55P03, 40P01 and 40001).
  > - `workspaceOperationService.createRecorder().recordOperation` wraps its `workspace_finalize` state writes (the running-row insert and the terminal update) in that retry. Each attempt is a new transaction, or a new savepoint when the service runs inside a caller's transaction.
  > - `native-provider-pack-admission.ts` uses the shared helper. Its behavior and its exported `nativeProviderPackAdmissionFailureCode` name do not change.
- fix(runner): never start an OpenCode session without its Paperclip tools (#20964)
  > - `opencode-server-driver.ts`: while a session opens (fresh, resumed or recovered), the driver reads OpenCode's `GET /mcp`. If the `paperclip` server is not `connected`, it calls `POST /mcp/paperclip/connect` up to two times. If the server is still not connected, it throws `paperclip_tools_unavailable`. The existing startup retry restarts OpenCode for this error (three attempts in total). After that, the session fails visibly.
  > - The check runs in parallel with session creation or resume. The added work on a healthy start is one local HTTP request.
  > - `fake-opencode-server.mjs`: the fixture now serves `GET /mcp` and `POST /mcp/paperclip/connect`. It connects to the real bridge with the configured token, and it can simulate failed connects.
- fix(retry): re-deliver the failed run's wake when the board retries it (#20970)
  > - `buildExecutionContinuation` adds `trigger.retriedReason` when the current wake is a Retry (`task_retry` or `retry_failed_run`). The value is the source run's own wake reason. A Retry of a Retry inherits the first non-retry reason from the source run's saved continuation.
  > - `ExecutionContinuationEnvelope.trigger` gets the optional `retriedReason` field.
  > - `renderPaperclipWakePrompt` adds `- retried wake: <reason> (this Retry re-delivers the wake the failed run did not finish; handle it as that wake)` to the wake summary for fresh and resumed sessions.
- fix(ephemeral): start a restarted volume-free server over the Runner controller files it wrote itself (#20952)
  > - Add `server/src/services/native-runtime/volume-free-controller-state.ts`. `claimVolumeFreeControllerState(true)` writes `.volume-free-owner.json` atomically (temporary file, fsync, rename, directory fsync) when the state directory is absent or empty. It returns `owned` for a directory that has the marker and `unclaimed` for a directory with other files. `claimVolumeFreeControllerState(false)` removes the marker.
  > - `server/src/index.ts` calls the claim right after the volume-free configuration check. This is before any controller or recovery can write. A server started without volume-free mode releases the claim, because it may write files that exist only on its disk. An `unclaimed` result logs a warning.
  > - `hasLocalNativeControllerObligations` ignores the marker entries. It returns no obligation for a claimed directory. It now delegates to `findLocalNativeControllerObligation`, which returns the run, task and file (or `database_checkpoint`).
- fix(runner): keep a connected adopted Runner through a failed liveness probe (#20965)
  > - `#watchAdoptedRunner`: on an `isAlive()` rejection, the monitor records when verification was first lost. It does not fail the transport while the control plane has an authenticated Runner connection and less than `runnerReconnectGraceMs` has passed. A successful check resets the clock.
  > - Unchanged: an `isAlive()` result of `false` fails the run at once. A rejection with no Runner connection fails it as before. A transport with no reconnect grace (local adoption) fails it as before. The close path still uses `isAlive()` directly.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md` describes the rule.
- fix(native): start the configured review stage when a native worker reports done (#20882)
  > - `server/src/services/issue-execution-policy.ts`: add `completionReviewStageEntry`. It applies the full policy transition that the route applies for `status: "done"` and returns `starts` (with the stage patch), `none` or `rejected`.
  > - `native-run-finalizer.ts`: a done decision on a task whose policy starts a stage becomes in_review with reason `execution_stage_review_required`. When the policy refuses the transition, the decision is in_progress with reason `execution_stage_transition_rejected` and a recovery note. It is never done. Deliverable checks still run first.
  > - `status-decision-committer.ts`: for a stage-entry decision, apply the stale-owner check, start the stage from the locked task in the same status update (this also clears a monitor that the new assignee cannot use), and wake an agent participant with `execution_review_requested` or `execution_approval_requested`, as the route does. If the policy changed, the saved decision is stale and finalization retries.
- fix(recovery): retry lost-lease preparations instead of holding them for reconciliation (#20936)
  > - `server/src/services/cancelled-native-startup.ts`: `isInterruptedNativePreparationCandidate` accepts a failed `controller_lease_lost` run with the bootstrap certificate (`providerWorkStarted: false`), the same as a certified `process_lost` run. The error label alone still grants nothing. This lets the existing `legacy_interrupted_preparation_hold` settlement, terminal-time proof, status receipt, and `interrupted_preparation` retry lane apply to these runs.
  > - `server/src/modules/wake-queue/*`: the release snapshot carries `retryableBeforeProviderWork` (bootstrap certificate plus a `transient_infra` continuation classification). When an automatic continuation retry failed for such a cause, release returns `released` instead of blocking. The stranded-task sweep then retries with its existing backoff and 3-attempt cap, and blocks only after that budget is spent. Non-retryable causes, uninvokable agents, and failures after provider work still block at once.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: documents both rules.
- fix(evals): name each team member's readiness and grade configure-team structurally (#20879)
  > - Cherry-pick of #20878: the OpenCode probe classifies a provider 401 as `opencode_hello_probe_auth_required`.
  > - The setup blocker now lists each member as `name (expected missing account | expected ready | other member): status [non-info checks]`.
  > - Grader: remove the member-name substring check and the "team is ready" regex. Identification is the rendered link to the missing member's own page, which the case already required. The followup is graded by a non-empty reply from its own succeeded run, not by the words "ready/configured/verified/account". Fresh per-member probes, preserved working configuration and revisions, and the attributed repair stay unchanged.
- fix(evals): give choose-capable-target a saveable computer and grade it structurally (#20881)
  > - `runtime-capability-choice.ts`: setup saves an SSH computer on a reserved `.invalid` host (no key, never probed) beside the local fixture computer. Production capabilities advertise Claude Code on both. The `enableEnvironments` experimental setting is still set only through `PATCH /api/instance/settings/experimental` and restored in cleanup.
  > - Grader: removed the reply regex and keyword checks. New checks: agent gets 403 on capabilities and probe; the reply renders a link to `/<prefix>/company/settings/instance/environments` (or a target's edit page); the reply has no board-only SSH settings (host, user, path); targets (including status and metadata) and the worker are unchanged; exactly one chat turn and no tasks. `verifyResult` clicks the rendered link and checks that the page shows both computers and loads the same capabilities.
  > - `execution-environments.yaml`: context changed from "one target supports the harness and another does not" to support versus health, with the reason. Recorded in `chat-full-catalog-testops-manifest.json` `changedFields`, pinned in the manifest test, and the inventory digest is updated.
- fix(native): stop or release unclosable idle warm Runners on environment delete, and say why a close failed (#20957)
  > - `closeIdleWarmNativeSessions` now returns `failures: [{ runId, issueId, environmentId, providerLeaseId, reason }]`. The reason is the close error with its code, its settlement record and its cause. It also logs `idle warm native sessions did not close` with that list. The idle-timeout close logs its error too.
  > - `closeWarmNativeSessionsForEnvironment` takes `forceStopUnsettled` and `db`. The environment delete route sets both. When the graceful close fails, Core retires the owner as #20844 does: one confirmed Launcher `stop`, the credential snapshot, `complete`, then instruction collection. A failed forced stop is added to the reason.
  > - When retirement still fails and the owner's sandbox lease has a `destroyed` termination receipt, Core releases the owner. It does not contact the sandbox. It logs `warm native session's sandbox was already destroyed; released its owner` with the close error.
- fix(evals): grade case facts by the saved fact, bind case kickoffs to the CEO run, stop block-on-design on a missing blocker (#20948)
  > - `cases.update-case-facts` (oracle v8): grade the saved correction structurally, with no word lists. The browser fact differs from the case's own setup value, and no field keeps the setup's stale literal "not yet confirmed", in the saved case and in the reviewer's handoff context. A person reads whether the wording says mobile Safari.
  > - `cases.create-case` (oracle case-create.v2): bind the kickoff and its documents to the CEO chat run that was active when the kickoff was saved, not to `sourceRunId`. Admit one CEO run on the primary task woken by Morgan's own case handoff back to the CEO, and add a check that binds it to that handoff. Run bound is now 2 to 3.
  > - Shared run policy (`chat-catalog-run-policy.ts`, `chat-catalog-contract.ts`): new optional `maximumLeadCaseHandbackRuns` for `source-settled-with-active-work`. The default is 0, so other cases grade exactly as before. No other oracle version changes.
- fix(native): keep the agent's own unanswered question open after a turn ends without a report (#20934)
  > - New `server/src/services/native-runtime/own-question-wait.ts`:
  > - `finalizeNativeRun` (`native-run-finalizer.ts`): when the proposed decision is `done`, the task is not an Agent Chat conversation, the report cites only the agent's final message, and the agent's own question is still pending, the decision becomes `in_review` with reason `own_question_unanswered` and releases the checkout. The question stays pending, so the person's answer continues the task through the existing answer path.
  > - Dismissal (`POST /issues/:id/interactions/:interactionId/dismiss`, `dismissInteraction`): new `enqueueOwnQuestionDismissalWake` runs inside the dismissal transaction, only when the card moves from pending to dismissed. When the task is `in_review` on an applied `own_question_unanswered` decision of the assignee's run, the dismissed card is the assignee's own question, and no other pending, undismissed question of that agent remains, it saves a durable wake intent (`issue_question_dismissed`, idempotency key per question) in the existing native status wake outbox (`enqueueWake`, now exported). The outbox dispatcher delivers and retries it, so the agent continues without the answer. Dismissal of any other request keeps the no-wake rule.
- fix(chat): keep the person's brief as a document on a new case's primary task (#20954)
  > - `CASES_CHAT_DIRECTIVE` (rendered only while Cases is enabled): one sentence. When the person opens a case from a brief, save the brief as a document on the primary task with `initialDocuments` on `POST /api/companies/{companyId}/case-handoffs`. Case fields do not replace the brief.
  > - `case-history-api-discovery.test.ts`: a regression test for the new guidance.
- fix(environments): resume a warm sandbox that its finished predecessor is still releasing (#20953)
  > - `server/src/services/environment-runtime.ts`: add `waitForSettlingReusableLease`. Before the plugin and built-in sandbox paths select a reusable lease, acquisition polls (250 ms) for up to 20 s while a matching `reuse_by_environment` lease is `active` under a different run that is `succeeded`, `failed`, `cancelled` or `timed_out` and finished in the last two minutes. It then continues with the normal selection, which resumes the released lease.
  > - A running predecessor causes no wait (unchanged: the new run gets a new sandbox). A predecessor that ended more than two minutes ago and never released causes no wait.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/environment-runtime.test.ts`: two red/green cases (succeeded and failed predecessor release the lease 400 ms after the successor starts acquiring) and one case for an old unreleased lease (no wait, new sandbox, old lease untouched).
- fix(native): continue the interrupted turn after a relaunch that failed before its runner existed (#20944)
  > - `scheduled-resume-continuation.ts`: when the decision refuses only for `local_stop_unverified` on a local target, `scheduledResumeContinuationAllowed` calls the existing `recordUnidentifiedLocalRunnerStop` (the same proof explicit continuations use). It requires that the newest event is this host's launch request, that no process carries `--run-id <runId>`, and that no runner state for the run changed after the launch request. On success it records `native.local_process_stopped` (`basis: unidentified_runner_absent`) in the claim transaction and decides again. All other checks (exact checkpoint, recoverable, session established, checkpoint exists, no replacement, admitted attempt) still apply first.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: logs the refusal reason when a scheduled `exact_checkpoint_resume` will not continue the interrupted turn.
  > - `native-scheduled-resume-continuation.integration.test.ts`: two cases. A failed relaunch after a verified stop now continues the turn (`resumeInterruptedTurn: true`, attempt 2). A failed relaunch while a process with `--run-id <runId>` is alive still refuses. The fixture now advances `nextEventSeq` after inserting events directly.
- fix(runner): reject an out-of-order controller command instead of exiting (#20946)
  > - `runner-core/src/durable/state.rs`: a new command whose `controllerSeq` is not `lastControllerCommandSeq + 1` (behind or a gap) now yields `CommandDisposition::Reject` with `status: "rejected"` and `{ code: "controller_sequence_mismatch", message, expectedControllerSeq, receivedControllerSeq }`. The command is not executed or journaled and the last sequence is unchanged; the process keeps running. Replay of an already-processed command and the compacted-history rejection are unchanged.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: a failure carrying `controller_sequence_mismatch` is classified as `native_controller_journal_behind_runner`, a permanent (terminal) failure with a Retry action that says no provider work began and that Retry reconciles the journal from the saved checkpoint or stops with a precise error.
  > - Tests:
- fix(chat): save a requested start order as a first-class blocker (#20947)
  > - `AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE`: one paragraph. When the person says an existing task must not start until another is done or approved, add the prerequisite to the waiting task's `blockedByIssueIds` with `PATCH /api/issues/{id}`, and keep existing blockers. A comment or a promise does not stop the work. Link both tasks.
  > - `agent-conversations.test.ts`: a regression test for the new guidance, in the directive and in the rendered chat prompt.
- fix(runner): keep remote recovery and launch through a short plugin RPC stall (#20945)
  > - `remote-runner-recovery-probe.ts`: adds `isPluginRpcTimeout` (an error, or its cause chain, with `PLUGIN_RPC_ERROR_CODES.TIMEOUT`). It also adds a per-recovery `RemoteRunnerPluginStall` with a 3-minute budget (`REMOTE_RUNNER_PLUGIN_STALL_BUDGET_MS`). The caller's recovery deadline caps it. After the first plugin RPC timeout in a recovery, its read-only probes use the stall deadline instead of the 30-second deadline. Signals are never retried. Other transport failures keep the 30-second budget.
  > - `verifyRemoteRunnerReattachment` and `verifyRemoteRunnerOrSettledExit` share one stall budget across the saved-state read, the identity-marker read, the process check and the post-exit settled-state read (`readRemoteRunnerState`).
  > - `waitForRemoteRunnerProcessIdentity`: a plugin RPC timeout extends the 20-second identity wait to the stall budget.
- fix(ui): align sidebar avatars and navigation icons (#20926)
  > - Use 16px agent avatars for pinned and recent conversation rows.
  > - Give `SidebarNavItem` a fixed 16px square icon slot with centered contents and left-aligned text. The existing token scale supplies the size.
  > - Add light and dark Storybook examples with mixed icons and long labels. The browser assertions compare slot sizes, label positions, and glyph bounds.
- fix(heartbeat): renew every legacy controller lease in one shared statement (#20924)
  > - `server/src/services/legacy-controller-lease.ts`: All controllers of one process renew through one queue per database. One flush is in flight at a time. Requests that arrive during a flush share the next flush, so a renewal waits behind at most one other statement. A flush is one transaction on the private lease pool: `statement_timeout` and one statement that renews all requested rows.
  > - The statement locks the requested rows with `for update skip locked`. A row that another transaction holds is reported busy, and only that run retries after 250 ms. It does not stall the other runs in the batch. A row that is not this boot's live legacy lease is reported rejected, as before.
  > - Renewal no longer emits the `active-execution-recovery` notification. A renewal only moves the deadline later. Recovery already has a deadline at the earlier expiry and rereads the moved deadline then. This removes one recovery pass, `pg_current_xact_id` and `pg_notify` per renewal.
- fix(dispatch): let the answer to the agent's own question continue the task it blocked on (#20931)
  > - Status committer (`status-decision-committer.ts`): when it applies an agent-reported, board-owned blocker (`current_track_blocker_waiting` or `task_wide_blocker_bound`), it records the agent's own pending, undismissed ordinary questions on the task as `awaitingInteractionIds` in the decision JSON, in the same transaction under the issue lock (`questionsAwaitedByAgentBlock`).
  > - New `agentBlockingQuestionState` (`server/src/services/blocked-question-answer.ts`). It reads only server-owned rows. It returns the verified `lastStatusDecisionId` and `statusVersion` when: the answered interaction was created by this agent on this task and resolved by a person; the task is Blocked and still assigned to the agent; the task's `lastStatusDecisionId` is an applied Blocked decision with one of the two reason codes, made by a run of the same agent, whose `awaitingInteractionIds` contains this interaction; and the task's `statusVersion` still equals that decision's `projectedStatusVersion` (nothing changed the status since).
  > - `decideQueuedRunStaleness` (`server/src/modules/run-dispatch/domain/policy.ts`): the Blocked exemption for an answer continuation also accepts this fact (`isAgentBlockingQuestionAnswer`). The postgres adapter loads it under the issue lock.
- fix(recovery): release a handed-off run's saved-files hold for the person it handed the task to (#20941)
  > - `server/src/services/execution-blocker.ts`: `restoreHoldReleasedForCurrentOwnerPredicate` now also releases a recovered file-preservation hold in one more case. The task must be assigned to a person, and the stopped run's `resultJson.humanSelfHandoff` must name that task and that person. The existing proof is still required: the completed-recovery receipt bound to the action, and a copyback rather than a snapshot-only save. Until the files are saved, the hold stays.
  > - `server/src/services/workspace-recovery-admission.test.ts`: two embedded-Postgres tests.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: one sentence on the restart case.
- fix(runner): keep a connected remote Runner through a stalled plugin supervision probe (#20940)
  > - `createRemoteRunnerProcessLauncher` (`server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`): when the health probe throws (RPC timeout or transport error) and the Runner holds an authenticated connection, the monitor waits one poll interval and probes again. It does not reject the process completion. The unknown period has a bound: the remote runner reconnect grace (7 minutes). When the Runner is not connected, the monitor keeps the old behavior: it rejects after the 30-second probe budget, without exit proof.
  > - Probe results that positively answer (exit 45 "process gone", or identity and generation failures) keep their exact current handling.
  > - `RunnerProcessLaunchSpec` (`packages/paperclip-runner/src/control-plane/durable-prp-control-plane.ts`) has a new optional `isRunnerConnected` callback. `spawnRunner` passes it to the process owner and keeps it on restart.
- fix(native): launch a warm follow-up from its predecessor's saved journal, and restart a Runner that exited before session.open (#20928)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/warm-controller-journal.ts` (new):
  > - Defence in depth for the server side of the same failure (`native_session_recovery_failed: runnerd exited while waiting for session.open`, then `runner_remote_process_identity_unavailable` on every same-run retry): a Runner proven to have exited while this attempt's opening command (`session.open`, or the recovery probe) was still unanswered is a failed fresh start, not a live Runner to reattach.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: calls the refresh right after the configuration-changed retirement of a warm owner, and the reconciliation in `restoreRemoteSuspendedRunnerCheckpoint` with the runner state it already reads (no added remote read, no wait before `turn.start`).
- fix(native): park a response_wake with no admitted response instead of self-waking (#20935)
  > - `status-arbiter.ts`: a `yielded` / `response_wake` result with no admitted response source and no blocking remaining work now commits `response_wake_waiting_for_input`, keeps the task `in_progress`, and has no effects. Policy version is `phase6-v14`. Board, external chat, plan, governance, dependency, pause and unfinished-work branches are unchanged and still come first.
  > - `native-runtime/native-response-wake-wait.ts` (new) and `recovery/service.ts`: recovery treats that applied decision as a durable wait while it is the issue's latest decision, the issue is `in_progress` with the same agent assignee, and no user comment arrived after the run started. This applies to the stranded-work sweep and to the paused-agent passive-wait check.
  > - `execution-projection.ts`: a committed run with this reason shows the phase `waiting_for_answer`, label "Waiting for a reply", and a next action that tells the user to comment or change the status. This shows only while that decision is still the issue's latest decision and the issue is `in_progress`; an older run shows "Completed".
- fix(native): keep remote runnerd exit evidence and report it on failed recovery (#20907)
  > - `REMOTE_RUNNER_CHILD_LAUNCH_SCRIPT` sends runnerd's stderr to `diagnostics/runnerd.process-stderr.log` (mode 0600, in the 0700 directory) instead of `/dev/null`.
  > - `remote-runner-recovery-diagnostics.ts` has a read-only `REMOTE_RUNNER_EXIT_EVIDENCE_SCRIPT`. It prints a 2 KiB tail of `runnerd.stderr.log` and of `runnerd.process-stderr.log`. It reads the session diagnostics directory first, then the shared runtime-root directory used by external Runners (Dot, Muse, grok_bot). It never reads through a symlink. Each file keeps its own share of the 4 KiB budget.
  > - When recovery's process verification finds `process_not_running`, the diagnostic adds these fields:
- fix(issues): let a native run hand its own task to a person without cancelling itself (#20884)
  > - `server/src/routes/issues.ts`: the signed current-run human self-handoff exemption now applies to native runs. All other checks stay: exact run, agent, company and task, `agent_jwt` source, human recipient. For a native run the route first records the hand-off on the run (`resultJson.humanSelfHandoff`, new `native-human-self-handoff.ts`). Board reassignment, agent keys, and handoffs to an agent or to nobody still stop the outgoing run.
  > - `current-wake-comments.ts`: the terminal fence accepts a run whose recorded hand-off matches the person who now owns the task. Without the record (for example a board reassignment), or after a later reassignment, it still refuses.
  > - `native-restart-recovery.ts`: after a restart, a handed-off run is not resumed. It passes the execution-lock check and is then settled by the existing ownership gate (`task_ownership_or_status_changed`) without changing the person's task, instead of staying `running`.
- fix(authz): send agents denied a cost-recording route to the Costs page (#20922)
  > - `server/src/routes/authz.ts`: `COMPANY_COSTS_PAGE` (`/<prefix>/activity/costs`); `/companies/:id/costs/*` denials use it. Budgets and budget incidents keep `COMPANY_BUDGETS_PAGE`.
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: the retry route's description says a board user retries with Retry cost recording on the run's row on the Costs page.
  > - `board-access-denial.test.ts`: regression row for the retry route (red before the change, green after).
- fix(native): keep an unreachable remote Runner alive when a failed run closes (#20923)
  > - `runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: `close(reason, { retainUnsettledRemoteRunner })` accepts a new option. When the option is set, the Runner is remote (a process launcher or an adopted Runner), the close is not a `stop_cleanup`, the Runner is still alive and suspension is not proven, the close does not stop the Runner. It skips the `waitForProcess` kill, the adopted-runner `SIGKILL` and `forceKill`. It still releases the controller route, writes the unsettled checkpoint and throws `NativeSessionCloseUnrecoverableError`, so the session stays quarantined and is never reused. The settlement diagnostic records `runnerRetained: true`, and a new diagnostic records `remote runner left alive unsettled for same-run reattachment`.
  > - `HarnessNativeSession.close` asks for retention only when the session was not explicitly cancelled. After `cancel()` (Stop), it asks for the old behavior, so Stop still stops the Runner.
  > - `CodexHarnessSession.close` passes the option to the transport. `HarnessSession.close` and `CodexAppServerTransport.close` accept the optional field. Callers that do not set it keep the old behavior.
- fix(execution): keep resumed native runs alive past a stale finalization deadline (#20920)
  > - `execution-control-reconciliation.ts`: when a running native run has no accepted result and its finalization owner holds a live lease or has a scheduled same-run resume, the reconciler clears the stale run deadline and does not fail the run.
  > - `execution-control-reconciliation.ts`: when the live owner's own settlement deadline is due, the reconciler moves the deadline to the lease expiry. It surfaces the failure only after that lease lapses.
  > - `execution-control-deadline.ts`: adds the cause constant and a plain message. The reconciler writes this message to the run error, the run next action, and the recovery action.
- fix(runner): keep the OpenCode MCP bridge on keep-alive so OpenCode keeps its Paperclip tools (#20921)
  > - `mcp-bridge.ts`: removed `response.setHeader("Connection", "close")`. The bridge uses Node's default keep-alive again, as before #20886.
  > - The per-call delivery diagnostics from #20886 stay, so a recurrence of the stalled parallel call still shows socket reuse, idle time and `written` in the run log.
  > - `mcp-bridge-delivery.test.ts`: the pooled-client test now asserts that the bridge does not close connections, that a later call reuses a socket, and that each call still gets a correct delivery record.
- fix(server): defer a run that meets a busy agent directory instead of failing it (#20829)
  > - `server/src/services/agent-directory-working-copies.ts`: The busy lock now throws `AgentDirectoryOperationBusyError`, still a 409 with the same message. Lock behavior is unchanged from `master`: a fail-fast `pg_try_advisory_xact_lock` held for the life of the holder's transaction.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/agent-directory-busy.ts`: `prepareWithAgentDirectoryDeferral` wraps only instruction preparation and turns the busy error into `AgentDirectoryPreparationDeferral` ("Another run of this agent is still using its files"). A busy lock met while collecting files after a turn is not wrapped and keeps its existing path.
  > - **Before native admission** (a brand-new run, such as the first run of a task): `server/src/services/heartbeat/retries.ts` `finalizeAgentDirectoryBusyDeferral` cancels the run as an expected control-plane wait (`providerWorkStarted: false`) and schedules an `agent_directory_busy` retry 5–15 seconds later. The task keeps its execution lock on the retry. The lane spends no failure retries (`execution-recovery-attempt.ts`), its count is the retry run's `scheduledRetryAttempt`, and it stops after 30 attempts. `pre-dispatch-execution-evidence.ts` classifies this wait like the existing workspace and AI-connection waits, so retry scheduling does not ask for reconciliation.
- fix(upgrade-readiness): report restartable runs and a handoff verdict that blocks only on lost work (#20910)
  > - `summarizeUpgradeReadiness` returns an additive `handoff` object: `version`, `ready`, `recoverableRuns`, `restartableRuns`, `blockingRuns`, `pendingWakes`, `unsavedWakes`.
  > - `handoff.ready` is true when the instance is not draining, the census is complete, and `handoff.blockingRuns` and `handoff.unsavedWakes` are 0. Saved pending wakes do not change it.
  > - The wake queue tracks each public wake admission until its wake request and queued run commit. `unsavedWakes` is the count of admissions before that commit. Without a valid count, every pending wake is unsaved.
- fix(secrets): let an approved binding fill a blank agent config value (#20908)
  > - `secret-proposals.ts`: `applyBindingApproval` treats `undefined`, `""` and `{ type: "plain", value: "" }` as an unset key for both `env.<KEY>` and other config paths.
  > - `secret-proposals-routes.test.ts`: regression tests for an empty string and an empty plain value. The existing test that a non-empty value conflicts still passes.
- fix(recovery): let Retry and the person's answer continue a held task with a pending decision (#20872)
  > - `admitExplicitNativeContinuation` (`server/src/services/explicit-native-continuation.ts`): a task Retry is no longer held by a plain pending question (`ask_user_questions`) or connection request (`connection_intent`). Any interaction with approval semantics still holds Retry: a plan review (`issue_document` target), a governed tool action, a secret proposal, a connection authorization, and other confirmations. A pending approval still holds every continuation. A new message supersedes a plain question, as on master, and waits for a decision with approval semantics.
  > - `admitExplicitNativeContinuation`: the person's own resolution of an interaction (`mutation: "interaction"`, answered/accepted/rejected, resolved by the same user) is explicit user input. It continues the held task like a new message: a fresh session, the hold resolved as `explicitUserContinuation` with the `interactionId`, and `issue.execution_recovery_settled` activity. It never replays the failed run. Governed tool-action confirmations are excluded. The existing `readQueuedInteractionResponse` reads the answer.
  > - `heartbeat/queue.ts`: both admission calls pass the wake payload to the admission.
- fix(cases): say a Cases-disabled denial is final and name the Experimental setting (#20892)
  > - Rebased on #20894, which already gave the case routes a `cases_disabled` code, a final remediation and the hidden-settings check. This PR moves that remediation into one helper used by every case and workflow path, and adds: no substitute task or project before the person chooses, and the offer of an ordinary task.
  > - New `casesDisabledError()` in `server/src/services/cases-disabled.ts`: same 403 message, plus `code: "cases_disabled"` and a remediation. The remediation says the denial is final, forbids a substitute task or project before the person chooses, links `[Experimental settings](/<prefix>/company/settings/instance/experimental)`, and offers an ordinary task.
  > - Native tool results carry only the error message, so the native case tools, workflow tools and runner tool authority inline the remediation in the message (`casesDisabledError({ inlineRemediation: true })`).
- fix(costs): mark unpriced usage in the agent, model and project cost reports (#20889)
  > - `costService.byAgent`, `byAgentModel` and `byProject` return `unpricedEventCount` (same filter as the summary: unpriced and not subscription_included).
  > - Shared types `CostByAgent`, `CostByAgentModel` and `CostByProject` add `unpricedEventCount`.
  > - The OpenAPI catalog entries for the cost reports that count unpriced usage now say that costCents counts priced usage only, that unpriced usage must be reported as unpriced (never $0.00), and that /costs/data-quality lists those runs.
- fix(evals): serve case-facts its own evidence; accept block-on-design's blocked-interaction reply (#20891)
  > - `startRunBriefGate` takes an optional `briefText`. The default text does not change for the other cases.
  > - `cases.update-case-facts` serves "no further reproduction evidence has been received yet; the affected device is still unconfirmed" after release (oracle v7).
  > - `task-coordination.block-on-design` (oracle v8):
- fix(opencode): ask for the empty-response correction with reasoning off (#20890)
  > - `OpenCodeServerDriver` sends the empty-response correction with the `paperclip-no-reasoning` variant. Normal turns keep their own reasoning mode.
  > - `empty-response.test.ts` starts the `reply` and `empty-again` cases with reasoning on. It checks that the first prompt uses `paperclip-default` and the correction uses `paperclip-no-reasoning`.
- fix(authz): name the Experimental settings page when an agent is denied an instance experiment (#20894)
  > - `server/src/routes/authz.ts`: the board-only denial map links Experimental settings for `/instance/settings/experimental`. It does not link it when `instance.experimental` is hidden.
  > - `server/src/routes/cases.ts`: the Cases gate keeps the "Cases are disabled" message. It adds `code: cases_disabled` and a final remediation that names the same page (same hidden-page guard).
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: the GET and PATCH catalog entries for experimental settings name the page. They say that agent credentials cannot read or change these settings. They give Cases (`enableCases`) as an example.
- fix(chat): name goal links, missing-name questions and component reference pages in chat guidance (#20888)
  > - Chat guidance: goal link format `/<prefix>/goals/<goalId>`, and a rule to explain a task's purpose from its saved goal chain (GET /api/goals/{id}, follow parentId), link every record, and say so when the task has no goal.
  > - Chat guidance: ask for a missing name (for example the product name on a landing page) with `request_human_input` (`questions`, one required free-text question) before building or delegating; never use a placeholder name.
  > - Chat guidance: when the person asks to see a component or chat state, link the design guide (question card states) or the named `/ux-lab/...` pages instead of creating a real card or a mock, and say they are synthetic fixtures.
- fix(chat): deliver the person's time zone to chat agents (#20898)
  > - Database: add the nullable `time_zone` column to `user_workspace_preferences` (migration `0468`). This is an instance-wide personal preference by user, as AGENTS.md section 5 permits.
  > - Shared: `WorkspacePreference` has `timeZone`. `updateWorkspacePreferenceSchema` accepts an optional IANA `timeZone` (max 64 characters, checked with `Intl.DateTimeFormat`). `coordinatorWorkspaceEnabled` is now optional. The request must contain at least one field.
  > - Server: the preference service returns `timeZone` and updates only the fields in the request. The activity log entry stays the same. The OpenAPI entry uses the shared schema.
- fix(evals): start the owned dev instance with a supported run command (#20885)
  > - Remove `--yes` from the owned `paperclipai run` command. `run` has no such flag. It onboards only when the config is missing, and `worktree init` writes the config.
  > - Link the installed `node_modules` of every workspace package into the copied checkout. Before, the fixture linked only six packages, so `run` could not compile the copied plugin SDK.
  > - Write the owned CLI output to a private `0600` log under the attempt temp root, outside the owned root that cleanup deletes.
- fix(authz): name the environment settings page when an agent is denied an environment route (#20880)
  > - `server/src/routes/authz.ts`: board-only denials on `/companies/:id/environments...`, `/environments/...` and `/environment-leases/...` now link `[Open environment settings](/<prefix>/company/settings/instance/environments)` and add one sentence that says execution environments are board-only throughout. The link is left out when the operator hides `instance.environments`. `/companies/:id/project-environments` (agent-readable) is not matched.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/board-access-denial.test.ts`: regression tests for the list, capabilities, probe and lease routes, the scope sentence, the project-environments exclusion, and the hidden-settings case.
- fix(opencode): report a provider 401 from the hello probe as missing authentication (#20878)
  > - `parse.ts`: add `openCodeErrorStatusCodes`, which reads the HTTP status from OpenCode error events.
  > - `test.ts`: treat a 401 status on the hello probe as `opencode_hello_probe_auth_required`, in addition to the existing wording match.
  > - `test.remote.test.ts`: regression tests with the two recorded OpenRouter 401 outputs, and a negative control (500 and 429 stay generic failures).
- fix(runner): answer each OpenCode MCP tool call on its own connection (#20886)
  > - `mcp-bridge.ts`: every bridge response sends `Connection: close`. The MCP client cannot reuse a pooled keep-alive socket for a later request. A loopback connect per request costs much less than the failure mode.
  > - `mcp-bridge.ts`: each `tools/call` records one closed delivery diagnostic. This includes rejected calls (unsupported tool, invalid input, duplicate identity), which record the outcome `error` (`paperclip.opencode.tool-delivery-diagnostic/v1`) when its response closes. The fields are the call id SHA-256, the socket request index, the socket idle ms, the handler ms, the outcome (`result`, `error` or `unanswered`) and `written` (`writableFinished`). It holds no tool names, inputs or results. The limit is 256 records per bridge.
  > - `opencode-proxy-finish-diagnostics.ts`: the proxy forwards these records to the run log as `warning` frames after session binding. It validates the exact key set and value types and drops anything else.
- fix(recovery): retry a system-woken run once when it lost its runner before any turn (#20873)
  > - the run failed with one of the existing reattachment-loss codes (unchanged gate);
  > - a positive durable record proves that no turn was ever issued: the run's last `native.remote_runner_recovery_failed` event carries `remoteRunnerRecovery` with `stage: process_verification`, `reason: process_not_running` and `providerTurnIssued: false`;
  > - the run also recorded no turn event and no tool event;
- fix(native): retry a throttled post-turn checkpoint for real; wait out storage throttling while saving the provider session (#20871)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: when a close fails because its checkpoint failed (and the control-route release did not fail), the transport keeps the checkpoint and the rest of the close. The next `close()` runs the checkpoint again and then finishes the close. All other close failures (for example a missing suspension proof) stay memoized as before.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-harness-backup-storage.ts`: `persistNativeHarnessBackup` runs inside the existing storage outage wait (`waitOutStorageOutages`, purpose "saving the provider session"). A storage-unavailable failure (outage, throttle, rejected credentials) that outlasts one request's retries is retried with the bounded exponential storage wait (2 s doubling to 30 s, 5 minutes in total). Each request still uses the jittered per-request throttle backoff from #20165.
  > - Restart chaos: storage fault `ST8` answers the next 9 to 12 (seeded) harness-backup `PUT`s with `503 SlowDown`. The storage audit reports it when it never changed a request. The README documents it (for `--env daytona --storage s3`).
- fix(native): an unretirable predecessor sends the next attempt to a fresh sandbox (#20863)
  > - `native-runtime/predecessor-retirement.ts`: a typed pre-provider error `NativePredecessorRetirementUnprovenError` (`native_predecessor_retirement_unproven`) and the `replaceBeforeReuse` lease marker.
  > - Native executor: every path that retires a predecessor for a new run (instruction-copy claim, instruction-directory reservation, session reset, configuration change) throws the typed error when the close fails. The owner stays registered for its own sandbox, so no run on that sandbox launches over it. A run on a different sandbox forgets that owner instead of replaying its cached rejected close. The executor does not settle this error as a provider failure.
  > - A prior-owner fence that refuses a terminal owner on a resumed sandbox (`prior_owner_terminal_state_indeterminate`) also throws the typed error.
- fix(heartbeat): retry every pre-provider lease acquisition failure and rest it on Retry (#20857)
  > - a lease-acquisition failure that is not a create timeout: the plugin RPC deadline (`-32003`) and an unfinished provisional creation recorded for this run;
  > - a transient database error while the lease is recorded;
  > - the exhausted state: a system-owned recovery action and Retry instead of a board-owned `stranded_assigned_issue`, for every `environment_provider_unavailable` cause (including #20828's);
- fix(workspaces): verify existing worktrees before fresh retries (#20876)
  > - Check the prior task binding and writer state before asking a storage provider to verify a checkout.
  > - Extend the existing prepare operation with an optional verification mode. Providers opt in through their manifest.
  > - Return through normal workspace reuse after verification. Keep the fresh retry fallback when verification cannot establish checkout.
- fix(native): retry a remote provider startup failure once its runner exit is proven (#20874)
  > - Runner transport (`runnerd-codex-transport.ts`): new `runnerExitConfirmed()`. It is true only after a completed close settled the runner (provider stopped, runner suspended) and the runner process exit was observed.
  > - Codex driver (`codex-app-server-driver-impl.ts`): after it closes the transport for a failed session open, it sets `runnerExitConfirmed` on the error when the transport proves the exit.
  > - Executor (`native-session-executor.ts`):
- fix(projects): do not offer a single project storage option (#20875)
  > - `projectEnvironmentOptions`: when the configured default is unavailable and only one storage group exists, the first environment of that group becomes `defaultEnvironmentId`, and `error` is null. When two or more groups exist, an unavailable default still produces the error. The server does not guess.
  > - Project create uses the same default, so an API request without `environmentId` now succeeds in the single-group case.
  > - `NewProjectDialog` shows the selector only when `groups.length > 1`.
- fix(db): preserve legacy project reads during rolling upgrades (#20860)
  > - Add migration 0467 to restore the legacy nullable UUID column and its original foreign key. Leave the published 0466 SQL unchanged.
  > - Apply only the exact SHA-bound 0466/0467 pair in one transaction. Lock the project table, retain nonempty values in a temporary table, execute both real SQL files, restore values, and record both real journal entries before commit.
  > - Apply the additive 0467 repair normally when 0466 was already committed.
- fix(heartbeat): keep an agent's explicit resume comment that a stale continuation dropped (#20856)
  > - `deferUndeliveredCommentsOfStaleRun` finds, per owed comment, the comment's own `issue.comment_added` activity record: written by its author (another agent, not the assignee) with `resumeIntent: true`. Merged runs, whose wake is rewritten as comments join, therefore keep each comment's own intent.
  > - `saveUndeliveredUserComments` also admits those comments (`explicitResumeCommentIds`). When only such comments remain, the deferred receipt records the agent as requester and carries `resumeIntent`/`followUpRequested` in its payload and wake context. The release drain's existing rule for an agent's explicit resume on a `done` task then reopens and delivers it. Person-comment behaviour is unchanged.
  > - `doc/run-log-events.md` describes the rule.
- fix(runner): keep orphan-leaving tests apart from the verifying lineage reap (#20849)
  > - Root cause: the reap's orphan rule covers every process of this user on the host. The reaping test and the orphan-leaving tests run in the same test process at the same time. The test's own processes are always attributed through the recorded group and session, so the leftover was another test's orphan.
  > - `process_lineage::host_orphans` (test-only): a gate. A test that can leave an orphan of this user holds `may_orphan()` until the orphan is gone or a zombie. A test whose reap must verify holds `no_foreign_orphans()`. Holders of `may_orphan()` never wait for each other, so a test can hold several without a deadlock. `wait_until_group_gone()` waits until a killed group has exited, because a signal takes effect after `kill` returns.
  > - `reaping_stops_a_dead_runners_provider_and_its_escaped_tool_job` holds `no_foreign_orphans()`.
- fix(native): retire a superseded warm owner instead of failing the new run (#20844)
  > - `closeWarmNativeSession` (server native executor): during retirement for a different normalized session, accept an unrecoverable close when the old owner is contained. A plugin task owner must show its `executionStopped: true` receipt and a completed handoff. Without a task owner, the Runner's own durable suspension (`settlement.runnerSuspended`) is the proof. Unsettled Core tool callbacks of the discarded conversation no longer block the reset.
  > - With a plugin task owner, any close error qualifies once the Launcher receipt exists. This includes a failed `stop` that `releaseRunnerProcessOwnership` rethrows, and "Controller journal drain timed out": the discarded session's journal lives in its own state root. Owners without a task keep the narrower rule. `--matrix` now rejects session faults.
  > - `createPluginRunnerTaskOwner`: add `retireSupersededTask()`. When close left the receipt unproven, the new run sends one confirmed `stop` (not resent after a confirmed stop), takes the credential snapshot, and calls `complete`. It never reads the old state.
- fix(native): do not claim a server restart for a held same-run retry (#20832)
  > - Restart recovery records `trigger: "same_run_retry"` in the recovery action evidence when the held run was a saved `retryable_failure`. It reads this from the coordinator, so a deferred check or a check after a real restart records the same trigger.
  > - `executionRecoverySettlementNote` says "Automatic retry of this run stopped" for such a hold. It does not name a cause, because the classifier can also stop it for `execution_recovery_budget_exhausted`. Holds without the field keep the old text.
  > - Tests: one integration case with the staging signature and one unit case for the note.
- fix(native): start a fresh session when an accepted run's provider state was not saved (#20811)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-provider-state-unsaved.ts` (new): `markAcceptedRunProviderStateUnsaved` adds `nativeProviderStateUnsaved: { code: "native_provider_state_incompatible", reason: "harness_backup_not_saved" }` to the run's `runnerProfileJson`. One conditional `UPDATE` writes it only when the run's coordinator holds a result with `schema_status = 'accepted'`. `clearRunProviderStateUnsaved` removes it.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: the remote close checkpoint (`checkpointRemoteRunner`) now wraps the existing body. When the checkpoint throws in a sandbox without a reusable lease, it calls the marker, writes one run-log line, and throws the original error. A marker or log failure cannot replace that error. If a later checkpoint of the same run saves, it clears the mark.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-resume.ts`: `rebindNativeSessionCheckpoint` returns `null` for a source with the mark, as it already does for `errorCode = native_provider_state_incompatible`. `findNativeSessionResumeRun` projects the new key.
- fix(native): retry a contended provider-pack admission write (#20850)
  > - `recordNativeProviderPackAdmission` retries a write that waits for its claim when it fails with `55P03`, `40P01` or `40001`, up to 5 attempts with 100–400 ms back-off. Each attempt is a new transaction with the same checks (claim, run, epoch, receipt). A stale claim still returns `false`. Other errors still throw. A write that does not wait for its claim keeps its `NOWAIT` refusal.
  > - New `nativeProviderPackAdmissionFailureCode` reads the SQLSTATE from the error or its cause.
  > - The executor's run-log line now ends with `(cause=<SQLSTATE or native_* code>)`, so a lost admission is diagnosable. It carries no paths or secrets.
- fix(native): let a finish through after one stale-result refusal; rebuild a missing instruction bundle (#20833)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/terminal-call-freshness.ts` and `runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: when the Runner refuses a terminal call with `terminal_call_before_results`, it records the refused call id with the returned-but-unseen call ids it named. A later terminal call ignores those calls only when the refused call's `tool.execution.completed` (the refusal reached the provider) is committed before the later call starts. A terminal call queued before the refusal reached the provider, a call that was pending at the refusal, and a call made after it are judged as before. The record is in memory, so after a Runner restart the agent can get one more refusal.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/runtime-context.ts`: new `rehydratePinnedInstructionAsset`. When the admitted instruction bundle is not present on this host, it rebuilds it from the agent's instruction revision whose content has the admitted digest (single-file bundles), or from the current instructions when they still produce the admitted digest. It never substitutes different content. When nothing matches, it leaves the context unchanged.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: `createRunnerdBackendWithinSessionClaim` calls it before the backend starts. When the bundle is present this is two local file reads.
- fix(auth): preserve credential sign-in during server overlap (#20839)
  > - Register issuer as an optional internal account field with client input and response output disabled.
  > - Set `local:credential` in the trusted before-create hook. Preserve any nonempty issuer supplied by internal code.
  > - Leave unconfigured providers without an issuer on the new NULL-issuer behavior. No social provider is configured by this module.
- fix(daytona): retry a sandbox create that timed out before any agent work (#20828)
  > - Daytona: one fresh acquisition is now capped at 180 s. A create that has not returned after 90 s is looked up once by its creation name. A sandbox in `started` is adopted, and setup continues. A sandbox in `pending_build`, `building_snapshot`, or `pulling_snapshot` keeps waiting inside the cap. Any other state ends the attempt with the existing cleanup envelope and an acquisition diagnostic whose elapsed time reached the expired budget.
  > - Daytona: a late SDK failure no longer runs the compensating delete for a sandbox that was adopted.
  > - Host: `readTransientEnvironmentProviderFailure` reads the validated acquisition diagnostic. An acquisition whose first failure was its own deadline is transient. The run fails with `environment_provider_unavailable`, category `timeout`, and uses the bounded bootstrap retry (30 s, then 60 s). An earlier setup failure stays `setup_failed`. Message text is never parsed.
- fix(native): apply a saved accepted result on Retry instead of a new turn (#20819)
  > - `preserved-result-finalization.ts`: the eligibility checks move into one read (`readPreservedResult`). They are unchanged: an accepted, succeeded result of this run, a completed saved-work recovery with a verified receipt, no Stop, no newer run, no current execution, and no applied generation. A new read-only `canResumePreservedResultFinalization` uses the same read without locks. `resumePreservedResultFinalization` keeps the finalizer's lock order (coordinator first) and takes the issue and run rows with NOWAIT, so it cannot deadlock with the finalizer, owner-stop confirmation or Retry dispatch; a busy row is retried briefly, and the last attempt waits for its locks. It also writes an activity entry, `issue.saved_result_finalization_resumed`, when it applies a result.
  > - `explicit-native-continuation.ts`: Retry admission holds an accepted Retry (`evaluate`) of a run whose saved files are verified and whose accepted result is still held, with the wait `finalizing_saved_result`. It holds while a saved-work hold of that run is still `scheduled`, `running` or `awaiting_evidence` and the result is otherwise applicable (no Stop, newer run, other owner or current execution), or once `canResumePreservedResultFinalization` is true. In every other case admission is unchanged.
  > - `heartbeat/run-control.ts` (`resumeTaskRetry`): after dispatch, if admission held the Retry with `finalizing_saved_result`, it calls `resumePreservedResultFinalization`. That coalesces the Retry into the source run with `strategy: finalize_result` and re-arms finalization. This is one key read after admission; the resumption runs only for a held Retry. The recovery worker already calls the same function after each saved hold, so either path finishes the work.
- fix(projects): make workspace storage the project's only placement choice (#20835)
  > - **Remove the project run environment.** Migration `0466` drops `projects.environment_id`. Run placement no longer reads the project. Runs use the agent default, then the instance default, then local execution. The low-trust task sandbox and the enforced sandbox policies keep their precedence.
  > - **Keep the #20651 recovery and binding guards.** A recovered run still uses its admitted environment lease (source `run`). Company-binding checks before admission and during lease creation stay.
  > - **Keep the creation choice.** **Available in** in New Project still selects storage. It still shows only when the environments need more than one storage owner. That can only occur when a storage plugin is installed. `POST /projects` with `environmentId` selects the storage of that environment and does not save the environment.
- fix(native): let a saved governed wait settle on its own same-run resume (#20838)
  > - `server/src/services/execution-blocker.ts`: `getExecutionBlocker` takes `resumingNativeRunId`. For that run it skips only the active action whose wake policy is `resume_native_run` for the same run and whose evidence names that run. Workspace-restore holds and all other actions still block.
  > - `server/src/modules/run-dispatch/adapters/postgres.ts`: the dispatch staleness decision passes the native run id.
  > - `server/src/modules/run-dispatch/adapters/postgres.test.ts`: the failing case and two controls (an action for another run, an action without a resume policy still cancel).
- fix(config): detect the tailnet bind host only when the bind reads it, one time per process (#20843)
  > - `server/src/config.ts`: `loadConfig()` calls `detectTailnetBindHost()` only when the bind configuration reads the address. With a configured bind mode, that is `tailnet` only. Without one, it is a host that `inferBindModeFromHost` cannot resolve to loopback or all interfaces without the address.
  > - `server/src/config.ts`: the result of `tailscale ip -4` is kept for the life of the process, also when the command fails. `PAPERCLIP_TAILNET_BIND_HOST` is not kept: each call reads it first, as before.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/config-tailnet-bind-detection.test.ts` (new): regression tests that count `tailscale` starts.
- fix(queue): show messages a booting run's first turn takes as sent (#20822)
  > - `firstTurnQueuedCommentIds` (server, `first-turn-queued-input.ts`) returns the messages in a deferred queue that the target run's first turn already sealed, or will take while it is still starting. It uses the same eligibility rule as `claimFirstTurnQueuedInput`. It reads only lean columns, so the queue poll does not transfer run snapshots or large wake payloads.
  > - The queue read path and the queue mutation path both put these ids in a new optional `firstTurnCommentIds` field on `IssueQueuedCommentQueue`.
  > - The task view puts each of those messages in the conversation as input of that run, at its submission time, and not in the steering pane.
- fix(chat): do not wake the assignee for a decision its own run recorded (#20842)
  > - `server/src/services/chat-channels/interactions/continuations.ts`: when the current interaction was resolved by the assignee's own run from a comment (`resolvedByAgentId` is the assignee, `resolvedByRunId` is set, no `resolvedByUserId`, and `result.commentId` is set; only resolve-from-comment writes that), the worker marks the action `processed` with `interaction_wakeup_owned_by_responding_run` and creates no wake. Actions saved before the deploy take the same path.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/chat-interaction-publications.test.ts`: real resolve-from-comment flow (no wake) and the board-accept control (one wake).
  > - Reviewed-chat attestation and every authorization check are unchanged.
- fix(runner): keep Codex planning read-only on Daytona without network namespaces (#20820)
  > - `createIsolatedCodexAppServerArgs`: when `PAPERCLIP_RUNNER_EXTERNAL_SANDBOX=1`, the `paperclip-runner-workspace-read-only` profile is now `{":root"="read"}` (plus the brokered `GH_CONFIG_DIR` write that planning already had). Its network still follows `PAPERCLIP_RUNNER_NETWORK_ACCESS`. It has no deny entries, so Codex 0.160.0 treats it as full-disk read and skips its filesystem helper, which always unshares the network. Local and SSH planning keep the narrower profile unchanged.
  > - The Codex session context now records `rootAccess` `read` (planning) or `write` (default) in an external sandbox, instead of always `none`.
  > - The Runner still sends `permissions: "paperclip-runner-workspace-read-only"` and the plan collaboration mode on `thread/start` and `turn/start` for planning. A new Rust test pins that for an externally sandboxed read-only launch (no `sandbox: danger-full-access`, no `externalSandbox` `sandboxPolicy`).
- fix(runner-e2e): resync the SDK lockfile pins to master's current lock (#20834)
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.json`: regenerated with `node tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.mjs $(git rev-parse HEAD) --write`. Only two values change:
  > - No other pin changes. The lockfile and `report-lock.json` do not change.
- fix(ui): refresh dependent tasks when a blocker changes (#20836)
  > - `LiveUpdatesProvider`: when activity changes a task's status, retry or recovery fields, invalidate every cached `issues.detail` query whose `blockedBy` or `blocks` summaries contain that task. The match uses the UUID or the identifier, because authenticated events can omit the identifier.
  > - Add a regression test for this in `LiveUpdatesProvider.test.ts`.
- fix(runner-e2e): resync SDK public lock pins and make the lockfile bot keep them current (#20623)
  > - Root cause: #20615 changed `report-lock.json` from output `2360d5e9…` to `b3208306…` and changed the `pnpm-lock.yaml` blob from `851e65af…` to `6d825f97…`. The registry kept the old values. `refresh-lockfile.yml` commits only the lockfiles and the report-lock proof, and it merges without CI.
  > - `source-sdk-public-inputs.json`: regenerated with `node tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.mjs <HEAD> --write`. Only `inputs["pnpm-lock.yaml"]` and `lockSha256` change. The compiler, parser and Zod pins do not change.
  > - `source-sdk-public-inputs.mjs`: the sync now copies `lockSha256` from the committed `report-lock.json` `outputSha256`. It refuses when the committed lock is not that policy's reviewed input or output. This is the same rule that `prepareReviewedSdkReference` applies. Only the lockfile bot can write that policy, so a contributor cannot choose `lockSha256`.
- fix(native): continue a governed-wait follow-up in a fresh session when its saved provider state is gone (#20809)
  > - `server/src/services/sandbox-disk-full-fault.ts` (new, test-only): `sandboxDiskFullFaultTarget` wraps the target of a final save only when the harness armed that save. The capture commands then exit 1 with the exact message the in-sandbox script writes on ENOSPC, and the provider download fails with `No space left on device`. The gate follows `@paperclipai/shared/crash-points`: `PAPERCLIP_DISK_FULL_FAULT_CONTROL` is read once at import and ignored under `NODE_ENV=production`. Disarmed, the hook is one comparison and returns the save's own target.
  > - `server/src/services/remote-workspaces.ts`: the capture call in `saveRemoteWorkspace` passes its target through the hook. No other product change. `workspace-recovery.ts` is not touched.
  > - `tests/restart-chaos/disk-full-faults.ts` (new): the DF1 kind, the control-file seam (atomic writes), the rest evidence (`activeRecoveryAction.evidence.systemRecovery.failure.cause.label`), the `disk_full_fault` T check, and the DF1 R3 rule (one expected Retry that must finish the task).
- fix(build): report compiler signals in server typecheck layers (#20603)
  > - Print a stderr diagnostic before the existing early return for a compiler signal.
  > - Include the layer, signal, compiler command, and working directory.
  > - Add real subprocess regressions for SIGTERM in below and SIGKILL in cycle.
- fix: upgrade Better Auth to 1.7.16 with account migration (#18906)
  > - Pin Better Auth to 1.7.16. The repository refresh bot owns the committed Core lockfile.
  > - Make the old issuer column nullable. Keep existing issuer values.
  > - Replace the issuer index with a unique provider/account index.
- fix(slack): require mentions only for channel roots (#20821)
  > - Rename the control to **Require at-mention in channels**. Explain the thread and DM rules in the help text and operator documentation.
  > - Keep the mention policy in the Slack module. Use the thread root and message IDs to identify a reply.
  > - Call that policy from durable admission, wake authorization, and queued run start. These shared paths must enforce the same setting when a delivery resumes. Other providers keep their current behavior.
- fix(native): end provider startup rejections with Retry instead of an ownership hold (#20808)
  > - Server (`native-session-executor.ts`): new permanent code `native_provider_startup_rejected`.
  > - Runner: planning runs keep Codex's `paperclip-runner-workspace-read-only` profile, also in an external sandbox. Codex 0.160.0's plan collaboration mode is a prompt ("Plan Mode (Conversational)") and does not block writes or commands, so it cannot replace that enforcement.
  > - Ask mode: an ask task builds `executionMode: "default"`. Its launch has `readOnly: false`, the external sandbox profile, and no collaboration mode. Ask mode restricts only Paperclip mutation tools, not the Codex sandbox.
- fix(queue): keep tool action outcomes out of the steering queue (#20582)
  > - Add `isToolActionOutcomeReceipt` in `server/src/services/queued-interaction-response.ts`. It identifies an interaction receipt wake that carries a tool action (`toolAction` identity or `toolActionRequestIds`).
  > - `findQueuedCommentWake` in `server/src/routes/issues.ts` skips these wakes, so `GET /issues/:id/queued-comments` does not list them. The payload projection adds only the tool action's `actionRequestId`, `toolName` and `toolActionRequestIds`, never its result text.
  > - The locked by-id queue lookup (steer, interrupt, discard) rejects these wakes with `queued_comment_not_pending`, so a stale client cannot interrupt a turn for a tool result.
- fix(ui): omit task IDs from parent breadcrumbs (#20804)
  > - Remove the parent identifier from the task page breadcrumb data.
  > - Remove the unused viewport dependency from the breadcrumb effect after review.
  > - Show the Private label at all widths for a private parent.
- fix(recovery): wake a parent whose refused completion waited on dependencies that later clear (#20803)
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/service.ts` (`wasBlockedByAssigneeAgent`): a native block with reason `durable_dependency_blocker_bound` is not a self-block. The dependency owns its wake. Other native blocks and agent blocks keep the self-block rule.
  > - The same function counts a later succeeded `workspace_finalize` with `owningService: workspace_recovery` and no workspace as the blocker settling, which matches the readiness rule from #20728.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/heartbeat-issue-liveness-escalation.test.ts`: regression tests for the refused-completion shape (one wake, no second wake), the recovery finalize without a workspace, and a safety case for a different native block reason.
- fix: preserve exact Markdown in native document writes (#20605)
