  > - Use a separate untrimmed required-string helper for the document body. Keep the existing requiredString callback signature and all other field normalization.
  > - Add real tool-handler, database, and authenticated HTTP tests for create, update, read-back, and replay. Cover leading spaces, hard breaks, blank lines, final newlines, no final newline, Unicode, and mixed line endings.
  > - Keep blank and invalid bodies rejected. Document whitespace preservation and the existing REST escaped-line-break exception.
- fix(native): start a cold server from active database checkpoints (#20792)
  > - Let an absent local marker use the existing full checkpoint, run, session, workspace and owner checks.
  > - Keep validation of every present marker and the final file reread that detects a change during inspection.
  > - Add a cold-home variant of the existing real PostgreSQL active-checkpoint regression. Its live source owner has a different boot ID from the inspecting process.
- fix(native): retry a follow-up whose own server stopped serving before its remote runner (#20786)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts` (`nativeProviderRecoveryEvidence`): add one narrow case before the generic checks. When all of these are true, return `bootstrap_retry` (with `checkpointExists: true` and `providerSessionEstablished: false`):
  > - `native-restart-recovery.ts`: export the existing `carriesSettledPredecessorSession` proof, so that the executor uses the same proof that restart recovery uses for rebound sessions.
  > - `native-controller-recovery.integration.test.ts`: add a regression test. It also shows that the verdict stays `ambiguous_state` for a different failure code, for an unknown provider session, for a checkpoint with its own turn, and for a run with a provider event.
- fix(native): ask once to re-check Paperclip before resting blocked on its own stall (#20779)
  > - `nativeCompletionFeedback` now rejects a `blocked` report one time per run when all of these are true: the blocker owner kind is `system`, the owner name contains "Paperclip", the reasonCode is `external_service_unavailable`, the run is still running, and it owns the task.
  > - The correction tells the agent: Paperclip answered this report, so it is reachable now. Re-read the current state before writing again. Repeat a needed write with the same `idempotencyKey`. Report blocked only if a fresh Paperclip call fails now.
  > - The second identical report in the same run is accepted as before, so a persistent real failure still rests blocked.
- fix(native): resend a throttled harness backup upload instead of losing it (#20772)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-harness-backup-storage.ts`: pass the encrypted scratch file as `sourceFile` to `putFile`.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-provider-artifacts.ts`: the same for the provider-artifact bundle.
  > - `native-harness-backup-throttle.integration.test.ts` (new, focused file): a real S3 client against a local endpoint that throttles the first `PUT` of each object once. Cases for a Codex backup, and for an OpenCode backup with provider artifacts. Each case also throttles the first read-back of each object once. Each case checks that the backup is current and restores the provider session bytes.
- fix(recovery): release a parent's Retry once a done child's recovered files are saved (#20728)
  > - `server/src/services/issues.ts` (`pendingFinalizeBlockerCondition`): the blocker task's latest workspace operation is settled when a succeeded recovery finalize (`owningService: workspace_recovery`) from the same company, task, and run started at or after that operation finished. The operation must be finished (`succeeded`, `failed`, or `skipped`). The recovery finalize can have no workspace (all rows written so far) or this workspace.
  > - The rule is scoped to the blocker task and its run. It does not change the workspace-wide `finalized_at`, so other tasks that share the workspace keep their own barrier.
  > - `server/src/services/workspace-recovery-dependency-finalize.test.ts`: new regression tests that run the real remote "Recover saved files" path, make the child `done`, and block a parent that shares the child's workspace.
- fix(workspaces): stop re-saving a retained sandbox whose disk is still full (#20758)
  > - `workspace-recovery.ts`: a save that fails with `remote_workspace_sandbox_disk_full` after a confirmed cache cleanup (or with nothing to clean up) now fails the generation with `export_interrupted`, retry class `user_action_required`, and cause `{ label: "sandbox_disk_full", code: "ENOSPC" }`. That class is not retried automatically, and it is not a permanent-source class, so recovery never falls back to the last save and never drops the run's unsaved files. The next sweep hands the hold to the board.
  > - A cleanup whose outcome is unknown (lost acknowledgement, interrupted journal) keeps the existing scheduled retry, because its journal resumes on the next attempt and can still free space.
  > - `retained-provider-cache.ts`: `reclaimInactiveNativeProviderCache` returns `{ changed, confirmed }` instead of a boolean, so the caller can tell "nothing to delete" from "outcome unknown". It has one caller.
- fix(native): name a resume that finds no saved Runner state, so Retry starts a fresh session (#20757)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/contracts/harness-driver.ts`: add `NativeRunnerResumeStateUnavailableError` (same message as before) and `isRunnerResumeStateUnavailable`.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: throw the typed error at the three branches where the control-plane state is absent. The branch with no PRP identity and the quarantine branch are unchanged.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/codex/codex-app-server-driver-impl.ts`: classify the typed error as `provider_state_incompatible` when the saved session has no turn or runtime request in flight. Extract the in-flight test that the moved-root check already used, without changing it.
- fix(workspaces): hand on a proven-unchanged preparation epoch instead of abandoning it (#20736)
  > - `recovery/remote-workspace-unsaved-files-settlement.ts`: before it abandons a preparation-only epoch, the settle calls the existing `releaseUnchangedRemoteWorkspaceWriterById` (lock the workspace, check the writer, then `releaseUnchangedRemoteWorkspaceWriter`). That releases the writer at its saved version (dirty false, writer cleared, location and root kept, baseline marked released, run reference set to `saved`) only when `proveRemoteWorkspaceUnchanged` holds. The proof requires all of these durable records:
  > - If the proof fails for any reason, the settle abandons the epoch exactly as before. The existing settle preconditions (run terminal, still `preparing`, no finalization, no work-started event, no save in flight) are unchanged and run first.
  > - `packages/shared/src/crash-point-registry.ts` and `remote-workspaces.ts`: add the crash point `remote_workspace.prepare.after_publish_before_provision`, so the chaos harness can kill exactly at this boundary. It counts only a preparation that published an unchanged baseline (a restore of a saved version), and it is a no-op unless a test arms it. `tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.json` is refreshed because the registry file is a pinned SDK input.
- fix(native): hash runner artifacts and run archive tar off the event loop (#20722)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/off-loop-file-work.ts` (new):
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`:
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/runner-binary.ts`: the default daemon lookup (`resolvePackagedRunnerBinary`, used by the local and remote resolvers) classifies the daemon with `runnerBinaryFileTarget`. It reads the 64 KiB header, not the whole file.
- fix(runner): refuse a moved-root same-session checkpoint by name (#20723)
  > - `codex-app-server-driver-impl.ts`: the moved-root decline from #20712 now carries `failureClass: provider_state_incompatible`. The runtime already maps that class:
  > - The server already treats `native_provider_state_incompatible` as a permanent failure (no automatic retry, board action text "Retry the task to continue in a fresh provider session with the same workspace"). `rebindNativeSessionCheckpoint` returns no checkpoint after that code, so a board Retry starts a fresh session in the same workspace. No server change is needed.
  > - A moved-root checkpoint with an active turn or a pending runtime request now reports `provider_side_effect_pending`. The runtime never replaces it under any policy, and the run ends with the permanent code `native_provider_side_effect_pending` for an operator to reconcile. Before, it threw the working-directory refusal, which recovery recorded as the retryable `native_session_interrupted`.
- fix(native): offer Retry at once when the Runner refuses a turn start on a closed session (#20729)
  > - Runner (`durable/mod.rs`, `acpx_provider_backend.rs`): new closed class `provider_session_closed`. The Runner sets it on these refusals:
  > - Server (`native-session-executor.ts`): a `turn.start` failure with that class maps to the new permanent code `native_provider_session_closed`. The server reads the class from the authenticated command result, and from the command-failure text if the error was rewrapped. `nativeSessionFailureDisposition` treats the code as permanent, so the first refusal ends the run as `terminal_failure` with a board owner and a plain next action.
  > - Docs: `packages/paperclip-runner/docs/durable-recovery.md` states the class and the older-Runner behavior.
- fix(recovery): give due exhausted holds their own fair lane in the recovery worker (#20725)
  > - `server/src/services/workspace-recovery.ts`: `reconcileRetainedWorkspaces` draws candidates from lanes. Each lane is a page that its own cursor advances: unfinished saves; for the worker, exhausted holds whose re-arm or fallback is due; and the obligation pass. Before, the obligation pass ran only when no unfinished save was selectable (the gate at `const unfinished = rows.length > 0; if (!rows.length) { ... }`).
  > - Each lane with work takes one free slot before any lane takes a second. The lane that goes first rotates on each run, so with one free slot (a long local export holds the other) each lane still gets it in one run of three. Actions of a run that one lane took join that run's job without another slot.
  > - The due lane wraps at its tail. A hold that becomes due by time alone (a lease expiry or a re-arm backoff) is reached after the obligation pass completed with no committed change.
- fix(native): a same-boot stall never leaves a run waiting on itself (#20721)
  > - `claimNativeRestartRecoveries`: when the recorded controller is this exact process (same boot ID and PID):
  > - `heartbeat.ts`: when the executor ends with `NativeControllerDetachedForRestartError`, there was no shutdown or hot-restart handoff, and this process's own lease lapsed (`ownNativeExecutionLeaseLapsed`), heartbeat schedules restart recovery for the run when the executor exits. Recovery then reattaches the remote runner whose turn is still running, restarts an unstarted bootstrap, or fails the run with a working Retry.
  > - `claimNativeRestartRecoveries`: a self-owned lease that is still live, with no driver here, now returns a `retryAt` at its expiry, so the wait is bounded.
- fix(runner): do not count stopped time against a turn start or an ACPX sidecar request (#20720)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: `#startTurn` runs under `#withAdmissionClock`. The turn-start deadline, the held-tool-call wait, the start-event wait, the adoption check time and the `session.snapshot` wait all read `#waitNow()`, so they share one stopped-time credit.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/runner/crates/runner-core/src/acpx_sidecar_transport.rs`: `receive_response` blocks in 1 s slices. `credit_stopped_time` treats a slice overshoot beyond 2 s as a stop. It moves the deadline by the overshoot, and to at least 5 s after the thaw, so a stop in the last slice still leaves the sidecar time to answer. Total credit per request is capped at 15 min.
  > - The control-plane clock (`RunnerStallLedger`) now uses the same rule: a stop is credited in full plus a 5 s grace, capped at 15 min per admission.
- fix(native): restart in place a Runner that only settled an earlier attempt's suspend (#20724)
  > - `runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: when the transport opens its controller, it records the number of commands already in the journal. These commands come from earlier attempts.
  > - When a Runner exits with code 0 during startup, the transport reads the local Runner state once. It restarts the Runner in place, once per attempt, with a bounded 30 s grace through the existing `#recoverRunnerProcess`, only when all of these are true:
  > - While that one restart runs, the startup waits for the replacement. The startup does not fail on the old process.
- fix(runner): replace a follow-up session whose remote workspace root moved (#20712)
  > - `claimNativeRestartRecoveries`: when the recorded controller is this exact process (same boot ID and PID):
  > - `heartbeat/shutdown-handoff.ts`: add `isRunHandedOffForShutdown`. Heartbeat recovery passes it to the claim.
  > - `suspendedByCleanDrain` now uses the shared `handedToNextControllerSinceClaim` helper. Behavior is the same.
- fix(runner): accept the acknowledgement of an indeterminate suspend replay (#20714)
  > - Runner (`runner.rs`): a new `wire_command_status` gives the status that goes on the wire. `wait_for_terminal_result_ack` accepts the acknowledgement when its status is the wire status (`failed`) or the journal status (`indeterminate`). Command id, type, and controller sequence must still match exactly.
  > - Controller (`durable-prp-control-plane.ts`): for a result with `execution_indeterminate`, the `command_result_ack` names `indeterminate`. Runners that shipped before this change accept only that status. The controller keeps the terminal-lifecycle fence for an indeterminate suspend, so it does not send the next command to a Runner that is exiting. The command stays `failed` in the controller journal, as before.
  > - Runner Rust test `indeterminate_suspend_replay_accepts_the_acknowledged_wire_status`: it seeds a journaled suspend, starts the Runner against a fake controller that acknowledges `failed` and then `indeterminate`, and checks that the Runner finishes as `suspended` with no fence. Before the fix it failed with the production message.
- fix(ui): keep the GitHub star prompt off tasks that need a Retry (#20713)
  > - `GitHubStarPrompt` waits while the location is one task (`/:prefix/issues/:ref`) or one agent chat (`/:prefix/chats/:agent`). Task list views (`issues/all`, `issues/done`, and so on) do not count as one task.
  > - `GitHubStarPrompt` waits while a task Retry (`data-testid="task-execution-retry"`) or a task status banner (`role="status"` with `aria-label="Task status"`) is on screen. It uses the same DOM observer that already waits for other dialogs.
  > - If the prompt is open and the user goes back to a task or chat page, or a task Retry or status banner appears, the prompt closes.
- fix(workspaces): restore a saved workspace into one copy of sandbox disk (#20695)
  > - `workspaceArchive` restore has a `consumeArchive` mode. It creates the directories and symlinks first, then writes the file bodies from the last to the first. After it verifies each file against the manifest hash, it truncates the archive behind that file. The archive and the tree together need the workspace size plus the largest file. The default mode, which the server uses for local restores, does not change.
  > - The sandbox restore script uses that mode on a private rename of the uploaded archive. A later upload to the same path cannot change the file that is being consumed. The script removes the consumed file when it stops.
  > - The helper's own staging is now inside the root-specific `<root>.restoring-*` folder, not in a shared `.workspace-restore-*` sibling.
- fix(server): record a long event-loop block with the deltas that name its cause (#20709)
  > - `server/src/event-loop-stall-monitor.ts` (new): one unref'd 1.5 s timeout, re-armed after each check, measures how late it runs. (Server startup creates no interval timers, so it is not a `setInterval`.) When a check is 2 s or more late, it logs once with these fields:
  > - `server/src/index.ts`: starts the monitor when the listener binds. It stops the monitor at the start of shutdown, and when a failed startup closes the listener. It writes the line with the existing `logger.warn`.
  > - This is a local log line only. It is not the Telemetry path and not the Observability (OpenTelemetry) path. No data leaves the process.
- fix(runner): do not count time the control plane was stopped against a Runner command wait (#20704)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: add `RunnerStallLedger`, the clock of one startup or recovery admission. `now()` is wall time minus the stopped time observed since the admission began. Its poll loops and a 1 s ticker share one last-observation time, so each stop is credited once, wherever the process was when it stopped (a poll loop, a checkpoint flush, a spawn). Only the part of a gap beyond 5 s is credited.
  > - `#start` and `#resume` run under one ledger (`#withAdmissionClock`). A nested startup keeps the outer ledger. The ticker stops when the admission ends.
  > - During an admission, `waitForRunnerCommand`, the provider-identity wait, the cold admission deadline, the recovery admission deadline, and the default 30 s command deadline all use this clock.
- fix(native): restart a bootstrap whose Runner suspended before the provider opened (#20691)
  > - During host preparation, a Runner state that is suspended with no provider session identity returns the new incomplete reason `provider_unopened`, but only when all of these hold:
  > - The created-lease and resumed-lease restart branches share one check (`restartableBootstrapState`) and accept `provider_unopened` exactly like `not_suspended`. They move the old session aside and claim a new, untouched session root.
  > - Every other case still throws `provider_identity_incomplete`: another run's state, a restart without the proof, a normal resume, and close checkpointing.
- fix(chat): never batch messages of two chat conversations into one wake (#20697)
  > - New `server/src/modules/wake-queue/adapters/chat-conversation-batch.ts`: reads the inbound chat links (`chat_message_links`, direction `inbound`) of queued messages and finds messages of a conversation other than the expected one.
  > - Deferred-wake lookup (`findExistingDeferredWake`): when the incoming wake has a conversation, a saved wake qualifies only if none of its queued messages is an inbound message of another conversation. This is one more predicate in the same query (no new round trip). It uses the existing `chat_message_links_company_comment_idx` index.
  > - Coalescing into the active run (`coalesceIntoActiveExecutionRun`): returns no merge when the merged batch would hold messages of a conversation other than the one it records. The wake is then saved behind the run, which is the existing fallback.
- fix(heartbeat): bind queued card-answer interrupts to the delivered answer (#20698)
  > - `server/src/services/run-identity.ts`: the shared receipt binding (consumed `coalesced` receipt for the same company, agent and run; same task; `user` request from the marker's actor) runs first, as before. Then:
  > - `server/src/__tests__/heartbeat-queued-run-claim-isolation.test.ts`: a successor of a card-answer interrupt is claimed, runs, and has the clicking user as responsible user. Two negative cases: the run delivers a different answer, and another user resolved the answer. Both stay rejected.
- fix(skills): release a skill cache lock whose holder's deployment was replaced (#20688)
  > - `server/src/services/runtime-skill-cache.ts`: `runtimeSkillCacheLockHolderExited` builds a local owner stamp from the recorded controller (boot ID, PID, process space, start time, and the Railway deployment and volume when recorded). It returns true only for the `gone` verdict of `proveOwnerGone`.
  > - Same-space PID rules do not change. A dead PID is gone, a live PID with no start time is unprovable, and this server's own boot is alive.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/runtime-skill-cache.test.ts`: a new test for a holder in another process space whose deployment the platform replaced.
- fix(native): wait out an ACP usage limit's reported reset, then Retry the task (#20692)
  > - `provider-quota-reset.ts`: parse calendar resets such as "resets Nov 12, 6pm (UTC)", "resets Jul 17th at 4:30 PM (America/Chicago)" and "Resets on August 2, 9am (UTC)". A past date rolls into next year. An impossible date (Nov 31) is refused. Clock-only parsing is unchanged. This also fixes the agent-level `provider_limit_wait` reset and the legacy `provider_quota` reset for these messages.
  > - New `native-runtime/provider-limit-resume.ts`:
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: for a terminal usage limit, the hold and run failure detail say when the limit resets and whether Paperclip retries then, instead of "ambiguous session state". The evidence records `providerLimitResetAt`.
- fix(heartbeat): retry a legacy run whose lease this process lost before dispatch (#20694)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/recovery.ts`: `restartInterruptedBeforeDispatch` no longer requires the lease to come from another boot. The other clauses stay: legacy runtime, a stamped controller boot id, and `preparing` with no process id or group in the state committed by the revocation. Locally tracked runs and runs with a live lease are still skipped before this point.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/heartbeat-process-recovery.test.ts`: two cases in the hosted shape (assignment wake and due-monitor wake, `paperclip_runner`, prepared remote workspace, same-boot expired lease, `preparing`). They expect bootstrap evidence, a scheduled retry, no recovery hold after a second sweep, and no adapter call. A control case keeps a same-boot `dispatching` run held for reconciliation.
- fix(native): read settled harness state again after a dropped sandbox connection (#20690)
  > - The settled-state capture (inspect plus `syncOut` of each persistence directory into a pending directory) is now one step that can repeat. The step writes nothing durable, so it is safe to repeat.
  > - A dropped, reset or refused sandbox connection repeats the step, at most 3 attempts, with 1 s and 2 s delays. Each repeat clears the pending directory and inspects the live state again, so the copy always matches the inspected identity.
  > - Any other failure fails at once, as before: a state mismatch, a missing directory, an unsafe archive, or a TLS failure.
- fix(recovery): finish the passive recovery pass before restarting it (#20680)
  > - `workspace-recovery-work.ts`: a committed change marks the passive pass for restart (`restart`). It no longer resets the cursor. While a restart is pending, an unfinished pass that made progress continues on the next run.
  > - `workspace-recovery.ts`: when the passive cursor passes the last candidate and a restart is pending, the same run starts a fresh pass from the first candidate, so a new change is not delayed by another run interval. Without a pending restart the pass records `complete` and does not wrap, so paused input does not keep the worker active.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: document that a pass is never restarted midway.
- fix(cases): hold direct case status writes to terminal handoff authority (#20686)
  > - `server/src/services/case-handoffs.ts`: add `assertCaseStatusWriteAuthority`. For an agent actor, on a case that has a primary task, a status change into or out of `done`/`cancelled` must pass the same check as the terminal handoff (`authorizeTerminalTask`: the agent is the primary task's assignee). A change to `done` then returns 422 and names the terminal handoff. Board actors, non-terminal status changes and fact edits do not change.
  > - `server/src/routes/cases.ts`: call the check inside the case write transaction in `PATCH /api/cases/:id` and in the keyed upsert branch of `POST /api/companies/:companyId/cases`.
  > - The terminal ownership denial now names the owning agent and the primary task, and asks the caller to contact that agent on the task. The message keeps its old first sentence.
- fix(evals): wait for rendered replies, bound runs by role and grade chat outcomes structurally (#20685)
  > - `chat-rendered-links.ts`: `collectConversationLinks` waits (at most 10s, once per reply per page) for the latest saved lead reply to render when its conversation is open. A page that does not show the conversation does not wait. `observeInternal` and the flow conversation read use it.
  > - `budgets-costs.set-budget`: waits for the rendered reply before it reads anchors. It fills the budget only after the form shows the saved cap, and checks that the typed value stays.
  > - New `chat-catalog-domains/run-roles.ts`: identifies a run by role (a worker woken by a comment that a named run saved on the task; the CEO's chat completion follow-up).
- fix(runner): resend an ACPX turn whose start was cut off before the prompt left (#20675)
  > - New file contract in `protocol/provider-schemas/acpx-sidecar.schema.json`: `turnDispatchJournalFile` (`paperclip-turn-dispatch.jsonl`) and `turnDispatchJournalRecord` (`received` / `dispatched`). The generator emits the file name and schema into TypeScript and Rust, so the pack contract digest covers them.
  > - Sidecar (`turn-dispatch-journal.ts`, `codex-runtime-adapter.ts`, `runtime-host.ts`, `acpx-runtime-sidecar.ts`): when the runtime port starts a turn, before any provider work, it truncates the journal and writes `received`. ACPX calls `onAcpMessage("outbound")` synchronously before it writes a message to the provider. On the turn's first `session/prompt` request the port appends `dispatched` and fsyncs it there, so the record exists before the bytes leave. A failed dispatch write aborts the prompt write. A failed `received` write removes the file and runs the turn unjournaled.
  > - Runner (`acpx_turn_dispatch.rs`, `acpx_provider_backend.rs`): the journal is read only after the provider lifetime fence proves that the sidecar and provider are gone. A lone complete `received` record for the turn proves the prompt never left.
- fix(chat): consume a saved chat wake whose message a completed run answered (#20689)
  > - `server/src/modules/wake-queue/adapters/postgres.ts`: a saved wake whose reason is `External chat message received` and whose payload mutation is `chat_message_received` is treated as message-only. If a completed run already received all of its messages, the release consumes it, the same as a comment wake. If any message is still unread, it is promoted as before.
  > - `server/src/services/chat-inbound-wakeup-publications.ts`: a chat receipt consumed for that reason does not publish "This follow-up was not started". Other declines keep that notice.
  > - `server/src/services/queued-comment-delivery.ts`: constants for the chat message wake reason and the delivered-message reason.
- fix(native): deliver the retry after a failed native turn, once per chain (#20681)
  > - `server/src/services/chat-control-recovery-stop.ts`: the native-parent proof accepts the `schedule_retry` effect, as well as `enqueue_continuation`, when the effect targets the wake intent. Other effect kinds (for example `notify_owner`) are not changed. The other checks (applied decision, delivered effect, same company, task and agent) do not change.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/status-arbiter.ts`: new input `failedTurnContinuationUsed`. When it is true, a failed run is decided as `native_run_failed_continuation_exhausted`: the task is blocked with a board blocker and no wake. New constants `NATIVE_RUN_FAILED_RETRY_CAUSE` and `NATIVE_RUN_FAILED_CONTINUATION_EXHAUSTED_ACTION`.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-failed-turn-continuation.ts` (new): `isFailedTurnContinuationRun` reads the run's dispatcher-set `nativeStatusWakeIntentId` and proves from the intent row (company, agent, task, committer actor, `retryCause`) that the run is the automatic continuation of a failed turn. A copied or unknown id does not count.
- fix(native): wait out a run-event append before saving provider-pack admission (#20682)
  > - `recordNativeProviderPackAdmission` sets `lock_timeout = 1000ms` for every call, not only `waitForClaim` calls.
  > - The run-row lock is now `FOR UPDATE` without `NOWAIT`, so it waits for a concurrent append within that bound.
  > - The coordinator (`native_run_finalizations`) lock keeps `NOWAIT` when the caller does not wait for the claim. The existing fence tests still pass.
- fix(workspaces): stop retrying a copy-back that hit a full sandbox disk (#20678)
  > - `recordRetryableFailure` treats a workspace failure whose cause starts with `remote_workspace_sandbox_disk_full:` as an exhausted budget on the first attempt. The code stays `native_workspace_sync_out_retry_exhausted`, so the existing export hold, saved-work recovery, and export Retry apply unchanged.
  > - The run error for that case says that the sandbox disk is full and that Paperclip saves the files again without repeating the agent's work. It no longer says "after 3 attempts".
  > - Other copy-back failures keep their bounded retry.
- fix(storage): never promise a storage retry no storage wait owns (#20676)
  > - `storage-unavailable.ts`: add the held messages (outage and rejected credentials). They say Paperclip is not retrying automatically and offer Retry or a message. Add `storageUnavailableHeldMessage()` and `presentStorageFailureMessage()`.
  > - `run-completion.ts` (`failRun`): compute the error code first. Use the storage retry promise only when the code is `storage_unavailable`. Under any other code (for example a native recovery hold), use the held message. No storage wait is created for such a run, because it could only be refused.
  > - `run-completion.ts` (`failRun` and `failRunSetup`): replace `markStorageWaitExhausted` with `settleStorageWaitMessage`. It keeps the promise only when a retry is scheduled. It writes the exhausted text when the budget is spent, and the held text when the wait was refused, its write failed, or a case handoff took over.
- fix(native): give a relaunched settled runner its admitted provider pack (#20683)
  > - `createRunnerdBackend` (native session executor): when a persisted-runner reattachment finds no live runner to adopt, select the saved provider-pack authority (`reattach: true`), so `expectedProviderPackManifest` is the admitted pack.
  > - `prepareRemoteRunnerAssets` no longer returns early for that relaunch. It verifies the installed pack as an exact identity and sets the active pack root, the same path as a reattach without a persisted artifact.
  > - A live adopted runner is unchanged: it still skips pack selection.
- fix(native): settle a restart-orphaned write_document from its transactional receipt (#20674)
  > - `PaperclipRunnerToolAuthority.recover()` now settles `write_document` through its transactional receipt (`#recoverTransactionalWrite`). It runs the same receipt-guarded mutation as an ordinary call. If the receipt exists, it returns the saved result and writes nothing. If no receipt exists, it applies the write once.
  > - A definite refusal that rolls the transaction back (a 4xx such as a stale `baseRevisionId`, a reused idempotency key, invalid input) settles as a tool error. Any other failure leaves the call unsettled, as before.
  > - The set of operations with this proof is explicit: `TRANSACTIONAL_RECEIPT_OPERATIONS` contains only `write_document`. Other tools keep their current recovery rules.
- fix(native): retire a finished remote owner whose gone Runner left a delivery unacknowledged (#20677)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts` (`readSettledRunnerdProjection`): when the prior run's sandbox leases ended on another sandbox (`remoteRunnerGone`, the existing proof), unfinished `run.attach`, `semantic_tool.result`, `session.snapshot` and `session.goal.get` commands no longer hold the retirement. These join the lifecycle commands that the rule already excused.
  > - In the same case, a journaled `semantic_tool.result` answers its `semantic_tool.input`. The controller journals the answer after the tool ran. Only its delivery was outstanding.
  > - No other proof changes. The prior run must still be terminal, with a committed result, a released coordinator lease, no recovery state, a stopped or fenced controller, a delivered `run.terminal`, and no live work after it. Quarantine copies must pass the same checks.
- fix(native): read the scheduled-resume evidence the resume reconciler claimed (#20656)
  > - `native-finalization-reconciler.ts`: when it claims a same-run resume, the reconciler appends one `same_run_resume` entry to `recovery_history`. The append is bounded like other history writes, and it runs in the same update that clears the failure. The entry copies `recoveryMode`, `recoverable`, `providerSessionEstablished`, `checkpointExists`, `successorRunId` and `replacementDenied` from the saved failure, with the controller boot and provider attempt.
  > - `scheduled-resume-continuation.ts`: the evidence for the third proof now comes from either of two durable records:
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: passes the coordinator's last history entry and the controller boot to the decision.
- fix(native): continue a task whose quarantined completed turn's holds settle (#20662)
  > - `attachPreservedRemoteWorkspaceSnapshots` no longer throws for an unverifiable source. Each of the five refusal shapes now skips the source and logs `remote_workspace_recovery_receipt_invalid` with a reason: `receipt_without_reference`, `reference_unreadable`, `receipt_on_unsaved_reference`, `receipt_unverified`, and `receipt_snapshot_missing` (was `remote_workspace_recovery_snapshot_missing`).
  > - `retainRemoteRecoveryAttachments` no longer throws for a retained recovered copy that it cannot verify. It does not publish that copy again. The files stay in the workspace.
  > - The writer check (`remote_workspace_recovery_writer_changed`), the workspace access check, and snapshot read and digest failures still refuse.
- fix(workspaces): skip unverifiable recovery sources instead of failing Retry (#20666)
  > - `attachPreservedRemoteWorkspaceSnapshots` no longer throws for an unverifiable source. Each of the five refusal shapes now skips the source and logs `remote_workspace_recovery_receipt_invalid` with a reason: `receipt_without_reference`, `reference_unreadable`, `receipt_on_unsaved_reference`, `receipt_unverified`, and `receipt_snapshot_missing` (was `remote_workspace_recovery_snapshot_missing`).
  > - `retainRemoteRecoveryAttachments` no longer throws for a retained recovered copy that it cannot verify. It does not publish that copy again. The files stay in the workspace.
  > - The writer check (`remote_workspace_recovery_writer_changed`), the workspace access check, and snapshot read and digest failures still refuse.
- fix(native): let a restart claim take back its lapsed lease at budget admission (#20650)
  > - `native-execution-lease.ts`: two new helpers. Both use the #20625 own-claim rule. Neither takes a lock out of order.
  > - `budget-reservations.ts`:
  > - `heartbeat-native-restart-suspension.test.ts`: the test "still settles a recovery whose claim merely expired" encoded "expired means not the owner". #20474 and #20625 changed that rule for an untouched own claim. The test now has two cases:
- fix(links): retain PR status across page reloads (#20654)
  > - Add link-preview queries to the existing user and company persistence rules.
  > - Keep at most 100 previews across accessible companies. Use the existing 24-hour retention, build boundary, and sign-out cleanup.
  > - Keep the original read time and freshness when restoring previews. Other query types keep their existing restore behavior.
- fix(agents): return to the index after termination is accepted (#20667)
  > - Call the existing termination callback after any successful termination response, including `terminating` and `cleaning_up`.
  > - Cover both action menus, roster refresh, failed requests, and unsaved changes that appear while a request is pending.
- fix: preserve compatible historical Codex WEB evidence (#20665)
  > - Accept the exact prior Codex profile hash only when the complete current profile has the reviewed successor hash and the provider is Codex.
  > - Keep the current entry and excluded-path checks. Keep facts, controller, archive, skill, and ownership checks.
  > - Add a frozen prior policy fixture with its literal hash. Test old receipt reads, both credential files, unknown policies, changed authority, and current writes.
- fix: recognize provider-owned Runner workspace storage (#20663)
  > - Share one metadata predicate between startup eligibility and offline workspace migration selection.
  > - Validate a complete saved owner against a same-company paperclip_runner agent and a plugin environment with matching nonempty keys. Per-run environment overrides remain supported.
  > - Keep ordinary local workspaces and invalid ownership bindings in the migration set.
- fix(heartbeat): release task Retries held by a drain as soon as it ends (#20640)
  > - `server/src/services/scoped-deadline-work.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/deferred-receipt-work.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat.ts`: passes `subscribeRefresh` that listens to task-drain changes and expiry. Before this change, a deploy drain left the worker waiting for its 30-second re-check, behind the scan's reconstruction delay.
- fix(recovery): release a lapsed owner of a finished coordinator so stop-proof holds re-arm (#20661)
  > - `workspace-recovery-stop-proof.ts`: add `lapsedFinishedCoordinatorOwner` and `releaseLapsedCoordinatorOwner`. A lease owner on a finished coordinator (committed, terminal failure, applied) whose lease lapsed, or that has no expiry, does not block `sourceRunProvablyOver`. The stop-proof re-arm clears that owner with a compare-and-swap in its locked transaction, and records `releasedLapsedOwner` in the marker and the activity.
  > - `workspace-recovery-capture-policy.ts`: the capture-policy re-arm uses the same rule, because it shares the same check and discovery clause.
  > - `workspace-recovery-fallback-discovery.ts`: the discovery clause still waits for the abandoned-owner settlement for a stale owner of an unfinished coordinator, but it no longer excludes a hold whose finished coordinator has a lapsed owner. A live lease still defers the re-arm to its expiry.
- fix(ui): allow queued message deletion before queue acknowledgement (#20553)
  > - Keep trash enabled for a saved message with discard permission when the displayed queue ID is absent.
  > - Use the existing author-only cancellation route when queue identity is pending. Keep the revision-checked route when queue identity is present.
  > - Keep failed cancellations visible. Refresh the queue after cancellation and remove successful deletions from comment caches.
- fix(native): keep crediting proven unstarted restarts past the generation limit (#20647)
  > - `restartResumeKeepsAttempt` now returns `{ keepsAttempt, noProgressRestarts }`. A new helper judges the interrupted attempt as `provider_not_started`, `provider_progressed` or `charged`, with the same proofs as before.
  > - `noProgressRestarts` is 0 when the interrupted attempt is proven to have made provider progress. Otherwise it is the previous claim's count plus one. A clean-drain claim carries the previous count.
  > - Before generation 12 the decision does not change.
- fix(workspaces): release the Blocked status of a sibling-settled no-replay hold (#20648)
  > - `workspace-recovery-fallback.ts`: new shared `holdOwnsBlockedStatus`, used by `continueFromLastSavedWorkspace` and `settleSiblingRecoveredHold`. A settled no-replay hold that owns the Blocked status returns the task to `todo`. A newer decision's Blocked status is kept. An export hold's status stays with its own result finalization.
  > - `workspace-recovery-source.ts`: `workspaceRecoverySourceEligible` excludes a resolved hold that carries `siblingWorkspaceRecovery`.
  > - `workspace-recovery.ts`: `reconcileRetainedWorkspaces` runs several holds of one run one after another in that run's job, instead of skipping the later ones.
- fix(native): never leave a cleanup-gated task without Retry or an owner (#20639)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: add `provenStoppedOwnNativeSession`. It returns proof only for a local run with a pid-bound `native.local_process_stopped` record (no later launch) and with every recorded provider process gone. An ACPX session open names no provider PID; its sidecar counts only when it shares the runner's process group, which the stop record proves empty.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: add `retireStoppedOwnSessionCleanupGate`. With that proof, it retires the run's own operator-required gate through the existing `completeTerminatedLocalNativeSessionCleanup` boundary and records `native.session_cleanup_gate_retired`.
  > - `heartbeat.ts`: the native recovery preamble calls it right after it records the local process stop, before admission. The recovery decision itself does not change.
- fix(native): continue a turn lost on restore in a proven same-run resume (#20645)
  > - New `server/src/services/native-runtime/scheduled-resume-continuation.ts`. It decides whether a same-run scheduled resume may continue a lost turn. Each of the three proofs comes from a durable record:
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: computes that decision in the coordinator claim. It passes `resumeInterruptedTurn` when either the restart claim is `resume_dead_runner` (unchanged) or the decision allows it.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: describes the rule and the refused cases.
- fix(tool-gateway): let agents create gateway tokens without receiving the value (#20613)
  > - Agent create: `POST /api/tool-gateway/gateways/:gatewayId/tokens` from an agent with delegated authority returns 201 with token metadata only (`id`, `tokenPrefix`, `expiresAt`, `pendingReveal: true`), plus `tokensPath`, a `reveal_credential` `requiredUserAction` for the responsible person, and a `nextStep` instruction. The saved row has no usable value: its stored hash is of a value that is generated and then discarded. Gateway authentication also rejects `pendingReveal` rows.
  > - Person reveal: new person-only `POST /api/tool-gateway/gateway-tokens/:tokenId/reveal`. It generates the value, stores its hash, clears `pendingReveal` and returns the value once. A conditional update makes a second or concurrent reveal return 409. Only the token's responsible person can reveal it (403 `gateway_token_reveal_requires_responsible_person`). Agent callers get 403 `gateway_token_requires_person`. A token revoked before reveal cannot be revealed.
  > - Why a reveal that generates the value: no stored plaintext existed to reveal, and storing one (even encrypted) for later display adds a secret at rest. Generating the value at reveal time keeps the existing one-time display and stores only a hash.
- fix(native): settle tool callbacks and holds that a server restart orphaned (#20628)
  > - Root cause: `DurablePrpControlPlane.#recoverPendingSemanticInputsOnce` (`packages/paperclip-runner/src/control-plane/durable-prp-control-plane.ts`) keeps an input pending when `recoverSemanticToolInput` returns null. `PaperclipRunnerToolAuthority.recover` returns null for every tool without a durable receipt. Nothing else could settle the input, so `semanticToolResultsSettled()` stayed false and `#closeOnce` in `runnerd-codex-transport.ts` threw `NativeSessionCloseUnrecoverableError`.
  > - The control plane now passes `admission` to `recoverSemanticToolInput`: `this_controller` when this instance dispatched the callback live, `earlier_controller` when it restored the input from an earlier controller's journal. The field is not written to the result command or the journal. A live dispatch leaves this record when its result is journaled.
  > - New `packages/paperclip-runner/src/control-plane/orphaned-tool-callback.ts`:
- fix(workspaces): settle a resolved no-replay hold once a sibling saved its files (#20641)
  > - `workspace-recovery-fallback.ts` (`settleSiblingRecoveredHold`): a resolved no-replay hold (`isRetainedFileHold`) can now settle when a verified receipt of a sibling action saved its run's files. Settling removes `automaticRecovery`, so the hold stops blocking the task. It starts no turn.
  > - `workspace-recovery-fallback-discovery.ts`: the worker discovers such a resolved hold when a sibling of the same run is recovered.
  > - New test in `workspace-recovery-capture-policy.test.ts` with the exact staging shape of both holds: board owner, `active_run_watchdog`, system recovery policy version 1 without a capture policy version, stage `restoring_files`, `export_interrupted` with cause `provider_error`, 6/6 attempts, `historicalWorkspaceSource` and `workspaceRestoreRecovery` lease ids.
- fix(native): settle warm follow-ups that a restart left behind (#20631)
  > - `inspectRemoteWarmSessionRecovery` returns `null` when this run's sandbox is `created` or `replacement`. Such a sandbox holds no Runner to adopt. The caller then uses ordinary admission.
  > - `inspectFinishedPerTurnRunnerdOwner` also retires a **warm** owner on a remote sandbox runner when this run's sandbox is `created` or `replacement`. It uses all the existing proofs: terminal run, committed result, released coordinator lease, stopped or fenced controller, settled journal, and quarantine copies. Local warm Runners and resumed sandboxes keep the live path.
  > - `restoreRemoteSuspendedRunnerCheckpoint` accepts a suspended Runner whose only open fence is its own undelivered `runner.suspend` receipt. The fence must name `runner.suspend` with lifecycle `suspended`, it must be the last controller command, and the restored journal must not name that command differently. A provider cleanup, unsent events, a turn's terminal fence or a warm transition still refuse with `runner_not_settled`. A later authorized Runner process reconciles the receipt, as the Runner already does.
- fix(recovery): bootstrap a remote follow-up that a restart cut before its Runner launched (#20642)
  > - `native-restart-recovery.ts`, no controller checkpoint for the run (`remote_runner_never_received_commands`): a follow-up with `sessionIdBefore` qualifies when it carries only its continued-session checkpoint, and a predecessor's settled Runner identity (`carriesInheritedRunnerIdentity`) no longer disqualifies it. A recorded process identity, a Runner-sent event, a provider event or a pending request still keeps the run held.
  > - `native-restart-recovery.ts`, controller checkpoint exists: the existing Postgres proof (`nativeRunnerCheckpointRemoteBootstrapProof`) now also runs when the run carries only a rebound checkpoint whose provider session a settled earlier run of the same session saved (`carriesSettledPredecessorSession`). The copied provider session then does not count as this run's provider evidence. The controller checkpoint stays the authority: any turn command or turn event keeps the run held.
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: describe the rule.
- fix(native): take back a lapsed lease held under the run's own restart claim (#20625)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-execution-lease.ts`: the "untouched" part of `nativeExecutionLeaseHeldCondition` now also accepts the run's own local restart-recovery claim. All of these must be true:
  > - `native-execution-lease-reacquire.test.ts`: new cases for each claim kind, a requested claim, failure settlement under the claim, and eight fenced variants.
  > - `doc/plans/2026-11-09-explicit-native-lease-claim-tokens.md`: Step 1 lists the new rule.
- fix(realtime): limit a shared live-event read set to 100 events and 250 ms (#20632)
  > - `live-event-delivery.ts` replaces a shared read set after 100 events or 250 ms. Before, the limit was one second with no event limit.
  > - `live-event-delivery.test.ts` pins the exact limits. One set serves events 2 to 101 and event 102 opens a new set. Access is revoked during that set, and event 102 is not delivered. A set is kept at 249 ms and replaced at 250 ms.
  > - `doc/live-transcript-delivery.md` states the limit and that queued output from before a revocation can still be sent up to that limit.
- fix(native): restore a warm backup when its source sandbox is idle (#20627)
  > - `native-retired-backup-source.ts`: an idle warm source is a third retired backup source. It must be `released` or `retained` with policy `reuse_by_environment`, its own run must be terminal and not the current run, and it must have no cleanup in flight, no restore hold or pending shutdown settlement, and no pending native workspace export. The backup stamp, single-owner chain, and later-provider-work checks still apply.
  > - Before the restore, the idle source is claimed with a compare-and-swap on the checked row. The claim moves its `reusableSandboxLease` scope into a `reusableSandboxLeaseSuperseded` receipt. Reuse never resumes that older state again, and the next acquisition treats it as obsolete and destroys it. If a run resumed or changed the source first, the claim matches no row and the backup is not restored.
  > - `environments.ts`: a cross-run reuse handoff now requires that the source row has no superseded receipt. The supersede claim and the handoff compete on the same row, so an acquisition that selected the source before the claim cannot resume it afterward.
- fix(runner): report monitor notes length limits (#20604)
  > - Use one local value for the published notes limit and its description. The server test checks agreement with the persisted shared validator. Keep the standalone Runner package boundary.
  > - Read the numeric maximum from the authorized JSON Schema validation error. Keep submitted values private and preserve the 512-byte feedback bound.
  > - Test the published schema and real bridge dispatch path at 500 and 501 characters. Test saved contents and rejection without monitor changes with real PostgreSQL.
- fix(environments): retry and safely replace a reusable sandbox that will not resume (#20626)
  > - Add `server/src/services/reusable-sandbox-resume-replacement.ts`:
  > - `environment-runtime.ts`: cap the resume deadline at the budget, treat a state transition as transient, and call the replacement policy before failing. A replacement records `sandboxLeaseAcquisition: { outcome: "replacement", reason: "resume_failed" }`; workspace preparation then restores the saved snapshot.
  > - `remote-workspace-transport.ts`: add the closed failure label `sandbox_state_transition`, so a strict failure names the cause instead of `provider_error`.
- fix(release): fetch draft-notes PR summaries in batches (#20602)
  > - `scripts/draft-stable-notes.sh`:
  > - `scripts/draft-stable-notes.test.mjs`:
  > - `package.json`: adds the test file to `test:release-registry`. These tests did not run in CI before.
- fix(runner): keep redacted display events inside their schema bounds (#20607)
  > - `acpx_event_payload.rs`: new `fit_redacted_display_to_length_bounds`.
  > - Both places that redact rich display events call it before their post-redaction schema check:
  > - A new `tests/acpx_rich_events.rs` test decodes a 60,000-character `plan.proposed` (Claude ExitPlanMode) with `token=abc`, and a 65,536-character tool output, through `decode_acpx_event`.
- fix(runner): stop relaunching a runner that keeps exiting after restore (#20608)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/runnerd-codex-transport.ts`:
  > - `runnerd-codex-transport.test.ts`:
- fix(ui): show why a queued message edit was not saved (#20583)
  > - `TaskChatComposer` shows a save failure for a queued message edit in its existing `role="alert"` line (`data-testid="task-chat-queued-edit-error"`). The error clears when the edit ends or a new save starts.
  > - `api/errors.ts` has copy for `queued_comment_revision_conflict`, `queued_comment_stale_queue`, `queued_comment_stale_target` and `queued_comment_not_pending`.
  > - The existing queue refresh after a 409 updates the revision, so a second save works.
- fix(evals): grade 16 chat cases by saved state instead of reply keywords (#20600)
  > - `activity.audit-change`: attribution is graded by structure. The reply names the recorded editor, names no other company agent (new setup literal `otherActors`), and states only real transitions. Sentence grammar is no longer matched.
  > - `app-permissions.ask-before-action`: each continuation reply must exist and must link no app record other than the reviewed connection. It no longer must name the app.
  > - `budgets-costs.spend-breakdown`: the reply must point at the record with unresolved usage (task link, identifier or run id) and must not give it a zero or free amount. The words "subscription" and "incomplete" are no longer required.
- fix(runner-e2e): refresh SDK public input pins after lockfile refresh (#20618)
  > - `inputs["pnpm-lock.yaml"]` is now the git blob of master's lockfile (`6d825f97…`). I generated it with `node tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.mjs <HEAD> --write`.
  > - `lockSha256` is now `b3208306…`. This is the `outputSha256` in `tests/runner-e2e/report-lock.json` after #20615, and the sha256 of the committed lockfile. `source-sdk-reviewed-fixture.ts` requires these two values to be equal.
  > - No other values change.
- fix(daytona): force a stuck stop before starting a retained sandbox (#20609)
  > - `packages/plugins/sandbox-providers/daytona/src/plugin.ts`: `waitOutSandboxTransition` tracks how long the sandbox stays `stopping`. After `STUCK_STOPPING_FORCE_AFTER_MS` (30 s) it calls `sandbox.stop(seconds, true)` once, then keeps polling. A refused force stop keeps the old wait within the same deadline.
  > - The wait is a `lease.wait_transition` span. The force stop is a `lease.force_stop` child span.
  > - `lease-tracing.ts`: the closed `paperclip.sandbox.startup.state` label also records `starting`, `stopping`, `restoring`, `archiving` and `resizing`. Before, these were all `other`.
- fix(runtime): omit Codex refresh handoffs from backups (#20614)
  > - Exclude `auth-refresh.json` and `auth-refresh.json.tmp` from the shared Codex checkpoint profile, covering native and ACPX homes.
  > - Extend real checkpoint-copy coverage to ACPX Codex, both handoff filenames, and native Codex. Keep the live files and session history intact.
- fix(workspaces): save remote workspaces that contain outside symlinks (#20569)
  > - `remote-workspace-snapshot-archive.ts`: capture records an absolute or escaping symlink in the manifest `excluded` list and omits it, instead of failing the save. It never follows a link. Confined links are kept. Restore validation is unchanged and still refuses an archive that names an outside link.
  > - Added `REMOTE_WORKSPACE_CAPTURE_POLICY_VERSION = 2`. Each new system recovery generation records `capturePolicyVersion`. Generations without the field count as version 1.
  > - New `workspace-recovery-capture-policy.ts`: an exhausted save whose last failure was in `preserve_files` (`export_interrupted`) under an older capture policy gets one new bounded generation. Restore refusals (`archive_unsafe`) are not re-armed, because restore validation is unchanged. It shares the run-over guard (`sourceRunProvablyOver`) with the stop-proof re-arm. It writes a `capturePolicyRecovery` marker and `issue.workspace_recovery_rearmed` activity with reason `capture_policy_changed`. The new generation has the current version, so the rule cannot repeat for it.
- fix(evals): count a pending question card as the turn's response where the case allows it (#20597)
  > - New `chat-catalog-domains/turn-response-card.ts`: `turnResponseCards()` returns pending cards from runs whose `presentationDecision.reasonCodes` contains `interaction_owns_conversation_response`. A card must be created by the lead, be human-answerable (a text question, two or more choices, or an ordinary confirmation) and not carry `toolAction`, `secretProposal` or `connectionAuthorization`.
  > - `settlePrivilegedChat` treats such a card as a settled turn. The privileged graders still require their written guidance, so `developer-labs.component-reference` now fails at grading instead of timing out.
  > - `goals.align-existing-work` reads the card from the run woken by the foreign-goal request as that phase's answer. A leaked foreign text, or a card that does not refuse, still fails. Oracle `paperclip.chat-catalog.internal.goals-align.v2`.
- fix(ui): let chat review requests open the document they ask about (#20590)
  > - `CompactTarget` in `TaskChatCompactInteractionCard.tsx` gives an `issue_document` target on the same chat the href `#document-<key>`. Plan targets, targets with an explicit `href`, attachments and documents on other tasks keep their current behavior.
  > - Added a `TaskChatThread` test: a pending draft review request in the takeover links `#document-draft`.
- fix(runtime): persist managed Runner credential refreshes across execution targets (#20580)
  > - Handle `auth.credentials.updated` for managed native Codex, ACPX Codex, and Grok subscriptions across local, SSH, sandbox, and supported plugin execution. Check the company, agent, run, session, Runner, and provider before writeback.
  > - Share grant, secret, version-selector, session-epoch, and provider freshness checks across targets. Explicit reconnects and revoked grants win. Writeback does not need an invocation's temporary home.
  > - Rebind the transport's credential handler to each warm invocation's current exporter. Handle idle and trailing close events. Fence detached controllers and closing-handle reuse.
- fix(native): validate active controller durability during eligibility (#20601)
  > - Require the exact external-persistence marker and a complete decoded encrypted checkpoint for active runs. Match run, agent, task, session, Runner and workspace identities. For warm reuse, also permit an older marker only when its full older checkpoint matches that marker and its exact earlier successful run has a committed accepted result in the same company, agent, task, session, Runner and workspace. Keep the current live writer mandatory. Read the marker again after validation.
  > - Accept that proof only with its fenced live writer and task binding, or the existing exact saved recovery snapshot. The saved-snapshot path still requires the full active journal.
  > - Keep newer remote workspace authority strict. A malformed reference cannot fall back to an older prepared reference. Conflicting plugin task authority remains blocked.
- fix(runner): accept request_human_input payload version forms models send (#20588)
  > - `payload.version` in the tool schema accepts `1` or `"1"` and is optional. Its description says Paperclip stores version 1.
  > - A questions card no longer requires `payload.version`. It still requires `questionSet` or `questions`.
  > - The payload description no longer tells the model to send `version:1`.
- fix(native): Retry re-finalizes a saved result that ran out of finalization retries (#20578)
  > - `native-run-finalizer.ts` (`recordRetryableFailure`): when the budget runs out on an accepted result of a provider-succeeded run, and the cause is contention (`status_cas_exhausted` or `side_effect_planning_failed`), the coordinator stays `retryable_failure`. It tries again after 2 minutes, then after 10 minutes, each with a fresh budget. Each delay is recorded once in `recoveryHistory` (`automatic_finalization_backoff`). After both delays, the old terminal path runs. Deterministic causes (for example `native_finalization_invalid`) and workspace-scope failures are unchanged. The existing deadline worker (`native-result-commit-work`) runs the retry, so no new timer or poll is added.
  > - New `native-runtime/exhausted-finalization-retry.ts`:
  > - `task-interruption-presentation.ts`: the run read adds `nativeFinalizationRetry` from that predicate. A succeeded run with this flag shows `state: "failed"`, `canRetry: true`. It also adds `nativeFinalizationRetryPending` (`savedResultRetryPendingCondition()`: coordinator not committed or terminal, with an `exhausted_finalization_retry` entry). While it is true the task shows `state: "working"` with `recoveryStep: "finishing_recovery"`, so the user sees the Retry took effect. Done or cancelled tasks, user gates, and ordinary runs are unchanged.
- fix(workspaces): run private tasks parked in a personal project (#20589)
  > - `assertTaskWorkspaceExecutionAccess` marks the task's own project as a container when it is not also the selected workspace or source project.
  > - `assertTaskWorkspaceSourceProjectAccess` accepts a `deny_project_private` decision for such a container only when it is a personal "My private tasks" project with no project workspaces. Task read is already authorized earlier in the same check.
  > - Shared private projects, personal projects with workspaces, workspace and source projects, and low-trust boundary denials are not changed.
- fix(search): apply task privacy to artifact results (#20595)
  > - `company-search.ts`: artifact counts and artifact rows apply `options.issueReadCondition`.
  > - `routes/companies.ts`: `GET /companies/:companyId/artifacts` passes the caller's `issueReadSqlCondition` to the artifacts service.
- fix(tool-gateway): make gateway client token creation person-only (#20581)
  > - `POST /api/tool-gateway/gateways/:gatewayId/tokens` returns 403 `gateway_token_requires_person` to agent callers before it creates a token. The message is final. It gives the Tokens page path (`/<prefix>/apps/gateways/<gatewayId>/tokens`) and tells the agent not to ask for or repeat token values. The response also has `tokensPath`.
  > - Board (person) callers are not changed.
  > - The API catalog entry for the route now says that it is person-only.
- fix(recovery): re-queue native runs that fail on a transient database fault before provider work (#20561)
  > - `run-failure-cause.ts`: new durable record `resultJson.nativePreDispatchFailure = { version: 1, providerWorkStarted: false }`, and `isNativePreDispatchTransientFailure`. The predicate needs a native failed run, a generic code (`adapter_failed`/`setup_failed`), the record, and a transient database `failureCause` (`40001`, `40P01`, `55P03`, `57P01`-`57P03`, `53300`, `08xxx`, postgres.js connection codes).
  > - `run-completion.ts`: `failRun` and `failRunSetup` write the record for a native run only when all of these are true:
  > - `run-completion.ts`: a recorded transient failure closes the unclaimed coordinator as `terminal_failure` (`native_pre_dispatch_failure`), because `hasPendingRunCleanup` otherwise holds the successor on it. It then calls the existing `scheduleBoundedRetryForRun` with `retryReason: "interrupted_preparation"`. An answered-interaction continuation keeps its own retry lane. The storage-outage lane is unchanged.
- fix(native): settle cleanup-gate refusals after a completed turn without a dead-end Retry (#20572)
  > - Add `server/src/services/native-runtime/completed-turn-cleanup.ts`. It holds one predicate for "a cleanup-gate refusal after this run's own provider turn completed" and the plain user wording. It reads only the saved `errorCode` and `runnerProfileJson.sessionCheckpoint`.
  > - `task-interruption-presentation.ts`: the projection never sets `canRetry: true` for a cleanup-quarantined run, which matches `routes/agents.ts` and `canRetryStoppedRun`. A quarantine after a completed turn projects as `resolved` only while the task waits on unresolved blocker tasks, so the task shows that wait. Without such a wait, the missing task disposition stays visible. Other quarantined runs show a plain settling message without Retry. The list query computes the completed-turn fact in SQL.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: the refusal keeps its permanent, named code, its hold and its ownership, and does not replay provider work. The next action uses plain wording, and names the saved work after a completed turn.
- fix(evals): name stale SDK pins and fix four chat-eval harness defects (#20579)
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.mjs`: add `staleSdkPublicInputs(root)`. It compares the registry with `git ls-tree HEAD` for every pinned input (the roots and build inputs that the pins file lists), and returns each changed, unpinned or removed file.
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/source-sdk-public-inputs.test.mjs`: a test fails when the committed pins do not match the committed tree. The message names the files and gives the `--write` command. The Runner support suite runs it for every `packages/**` change. A synthetic test covers changed, unpinned and removed inputs.
  > - `tests/runner-e2e/chat-catalog-domains/runtime-handoffs.ts` and `identity-profile.ts`: call the existing `waitForRenderedReply` before they read rendered links.
- fix(storage): abort a snapshot upload a restart cut off once its save attempt is superseded (#20542)
  > - The S3 storage provider and the storage service get `abortIncompleteUploads(objectKey)`. It lists the multipart uploads under that exact key (`ListMultipartUploads` with the key as the prefix), skips other keys that only share the prefix, and aborts each one. `NoSuchUpload` counts as already done. The local-disk provider has no multipart uploads and does nothing.
  > - Save publication: the save journals every attempt's object key in `metadata.uploads` before any remote I/O. When one attempt moves the snapshot from `pending` to `uploaded`, no other attempt can win any more. The winning process now aborts the incomplete uploads of every other journaled attempt key. Each abort is best effort and bounded to 5 s. A snapshot with one attempt sends no extra request.
  > - A losing uploader that is still live: if its upload fails after another attempt has published the snapshot, it now returns the published snapshot (after it verifies it) instead of the error. So an abort from the winner never fails a save that is still running in another process.
- fix(native): preserve parked Runners across controller restarts (#20586)
  > - Detach confirmed released entries during controller shutdown. Keep busy, unreleased, and close-marked entries on the existing cleanup path.
  > - Share the detach operation with idle expiry and cached-owner handoff.
  > - Record that a local handle has started detaching. Later cleanup cannot fall back to stopping its Runner after a partial detach failure.
- fix(recovery): read a retained Runner from its own allocation and continue unverifiable no-replay holds from the last save (#20567)
  > - `assertNativeRetainedSandboxBinding` now returns the directory that the retained lease recorded. It accepts a different target directory only when both are canonical sibling allocations of the same execution workspace under the sandbox's `.paperclip-workspaces` (`<workspaceId>` or `<workspaceId>-<epoch>-<leaseId>`). Same sandbox (`providerLeaseId`), environment, run, issue and committed owner are still required. Other directories stay refused.
  > - `restoreRemoteSuspendedRunnerCheckpoint` reads the Runner state from that recorded directory. A moved source must be suspended and settled. A Runner left in the old allocation is never adopted live.
  > - The retained-source fallback (`continueFromLastSavedWorkspace`) and its discovery now also cover the native no-replay hold (`native_continuation_requires_reconciliation`, active or resolved with outcome `blocked`, replay blocked for the same run). Rows saved by earlier releases are discovered without migration.
- fix(ui): show chat activity immediately during admission (#20559)
  > - Use the default activity presentation when a queued turn has no specific server hold.
  > - Retain maintenance, agent setup, server restart, previous-run, provider, and preparation labels.
  > - Omit the elapsed-time suffix until a start time is known. This prevents a frozen Working for 0s header before a run exists.
- fix: stop prompts from encouraging subtasks (#20362)
  > - Align accepted-plan prompts and remove direct child-creation advice from runtime and error text.
  > - Make task tool descriptions neutral. Keep their fields, defaults, and handlers.
  > - Update the main skill, onboarding, CEO instructions, company templates, specialist workflows, and matching guides.
- fix(runner): record pending calls at sidecar shutdown (#20272)
  > - Emit `sidecar_shutdown_requested` through the existing diagnostic stream and stderr before shutdown can remove waiters.
  > - Record pending tool, input, permission and completion-normalization counts. Include at most 16 oldest tool calls with elapsed time and a SHA-256 call ID. Exclude arguments, results, operation names and raw call IDs.
  > - Add real sidecar process tests for EOF and both shutdown signals. Check successful resolution, cancelled calls, pending-call rejection, provider cleanup and sidecar exit with code 0 and no signal before fallback cleanup.
- fix(native): release a restart claim that lapsed during a failed preparation (#20529)
  > - `native-execution-lease.ts`: new `releaseLapsedNativeRestartClaim`. It is one conditional UPDATE that releases the claim only when the run is `running`, the coordinator is `observed` with no result, and it still names this exact `leaseOwner`, `controllerGeneration` and `attempt` with an expired lease. A successor claim, a result, or a live lease leaves the row unchanged.
  > - `heartbeat.ts`: when failed-workspace preservation defers on a restart-claim execution, the failure path tries that release. If it succeeds, the execution schedules a restart-recovery pass for the run (`scheduleNativeSessionResumeDispatch(..., "restart")`) after it leaves `activeRunExecutions`. Recovery then reclaims the run, or blocks it with a named reason, right away. The path also logs the redacted cause, the claim kind and whether the claim lapsed. Before, this return was silent.
  > - `preserveNativeFailedWorkspace` is unchanged. It still refuses an expired claim (`refuses a stale expired recovery claim`).
- fix(native): give run finalization its own retry budget (#20555)
  > - `native-run-finalizer.ts`: `recordRetryableFailure` counts failed finalizations of the saved result in `heartbeat_runs.result_json.finalizationFailureCount`. It ends retries after 3 failures. It no longer reads `coordinator.attempt`, which counts provider attempts. The count goes on the run because each claim clears the coordinator's `failure_detail`. Workspace-scope failures keep their existing `workspaceFinalizeAttempt` budget.
  > - New regression test `native-finalization-retry-budget.test.ts`: a coordinator saved at `attempt: 3` with no count stays `retryable_failure` after the first failure. It goes to `terminal_failure` only after the third failure.
- fix(ui): restore pinned sidebar links above More (#20369)
  > - Move the existing mixed pin list into the main navigation after Inbox.
  > - Remove the separate Pinned section and heading.
  > - Restore placement checks for expanded and collapsed rendering.
- fix(recovery): start a Retry after a restart cut off a relaunch whose earlier Runners were proven stopped (#20537)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: the `native.process_start_requested` payload adds `requestedAt`. `unidentifiedLocalRunnerProvenAbsent` takes `earlierRunnersStoppedBefore` and accepts this run's saved state only when the Runner journal and lineage entries predate it (`runnerStateUnchangedSince`).
  > - `native-local-process-stop.ts`: `recordUnidentifiedLocalRunnerStop` reads the last two process events and passes the request time only after a stop receipt (or no earlier launch).
  > - `explicit-native-continuation.test.ts`: new embedded-Postgres case. A native run with a proven-stopped Runner, saved Runner state, and a later unidentified relaunch: the Retry is admitted and the stop is recorded. It stays blocked (`process_identity_missing`) when the Runner journal or a lineage entry changed after the request, when the request has no `requestedAt` (previous release), when the earlier Runner has no stop receipt, when a whole-second file time is within 2 s of the request, or when a `--run-id` Runner process is alive. A whole-second time 3.5 s before the request is admitted.
- fix(runner): explain task scope on missing tool inputs (#19774)
  > - Add UUID and active-task guidance to `get_task`, `comment_on_task`, and the three explicit-task document tools.
  > - Return bounded, distinct descriptions for up to three direct missing required fields. Keep ordinary validation errors unchanged.
  > - Share this formatter between the Runner and OpenCode MCP bridges.
- fix(ui): show parent tasks in responsive breadcrumbs (#20366)
  > - Add the immediate parent from the existing authorized ancestor response to the global task path.
  > - Make parent and current titles shrink. Apply a 48-character display limit without changing stored titles.
  > - Keep the current task number visible on phones. Keep private parent labels noninteractive.
- fix(recovery): keep a remote restart claim's lease through slow preparation (#20540)
  > - `heartbeat/recovery.ts`: start the preparation lease renewal for every remote restart claim (`scope.remote`), not only `reattach_remote_runner`.
  > - `native-runtime/native-execution-lease.ts`:
  > - Tests: one recovery case and three lease-renewal cases (below).
- fix(scripts): build the plugin SDK in the agent worktree preflight (#20473)
  > - `scripts/agent-preflight.mjs`: add a `plugin-sdk` step after install. It runs `scripts/ensure-plugin-build-deps.mjs`. That script builds `@paperclipai/shared` and `@paperclipai/plugin-sdk`, and it skips them when their content fingerprints match the last build. The step reports `built` or `skipped`. It fails when the builder fails or when a completion marker is missing after the build.
  > - The SDK build does not use the shared cross-worktree cache. Both packages compile in about 1.6 s with the native compiler, so a cache clone does not save time.
  > - `scripts/__tests__/agent-preflight.test.mjs`: new tests. They use the real builder script with a stand-in compiler. They cover a fresh build, a skip when sources do not change, a rebuild after a source change, a rebuild when only the SDK `dist` is missing, a failed build, and missing outputs. The file is added to `test:release-registry` next to `agent-build-cache.test.mjs`.
- fix(native): accept harness diagnostics in a remote session-opened bootstrap proof (#20543)
  > - `native-bootstrap-proof.ts`: add `harness.diagnostic` to `SESSION_OPEN_EVENT_TYPES`, which `runnerdStateHasNoProviderTurn` uses. The proof still requires every command to be `run.prepare`, `session.open` or runner maintenance (`session.goal.get` and similar), every committed event to be accounted for in order, and no adopted state or warm transition. A turn command or any turn event still fails it. The local opened-session proof already accepted `harness.diagnostic` through `PRE_TURN_EVENT_TYPES`.
  > - `native-runner-restart-recovery.integration.test.ts`: a case built from the observed record (goal read plus two diagnostics). It is classified `bootstrap_incomplete` with `remote_session_opened_without_turn`, and the executor's restored-checkpoint proof agrees. A diagnostic followed by `turn.started` stays blocked.
- fix(heartbeat): leave a preparing run to the next controller after a graceful drain (#20514)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: add `watchNativeRestartDetach(runId)`. It records the run's restart-detach marker when the execution starts. It reports `true` only when a later restart detach for that run happens. A marker left by an earlier detach does not count.
  > - `heartbeat.ts`: the execution creates the watcher at start. In the inner failure path (before `runCompletion.failRun`) and the setup failure path (before `runCompletion.failRunSetup`), if a restart detached the run and no provider dispatch started, the execution logs the handoff and stops without settling. It uses the same teardown flag (`nativeSessionResumeScheduled`) as `NativeControllerDetachedForRestartError`.
  > - `heartbeat-native-restart-suspension.test.ts`: add a red-before/green-after regression. A recovery execution is drained during preparation and then checks the real run-log writer authority. With the drain, the run stays `running` with `awaiting_runner_reattach`, and the next controller resumes it. Without the drain (the lease only expired), the run still fails `adapter_failed`.
- fix(heartbeat): show a storage outage wait as live status, then record it once (#20460)
  > - `storage/storage-unavailable.ts`:
  > - `heartbeat/storage-wait-notice.ts` (new):
  > - `heartbeat.ts`: installs the notice next to `endStorageOutageWaitOn` (one line).
- fix(run-log): answer 404 for an object run log that was never written (#20539)
  > - `server/src/services/object-run-log-store.ts`: a read of a log with no head (never written or deleted) throws `notFound("Run log not found")` (HTTP 404) instead of a plain `Error`.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/agent-live-run-routes.test.ts`: route tests drive the real object run-log store behind the route. A never-written log answers 404. A store read failure still answers 500. A board user of another company gets 404 and the store is not read.
  > - `server/src/services/object-run-log-store.test.ts`: the never-written read test now expects the 404 error.
- fix(recovery): retry a preparation killed by a restart in the uncharged lane (#20534)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/recovery.ts`: the `process_lost` write sets `controllerLeaseExpiresAt` to now when `restartInterruptedBeforeDispatch && legacyControllerGone`.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/heartbeat-process-recovery.test.ts`:
  > - `doc/execution-semantics.md`: one additive sentence saying the lane also covers a hard kill once the killed controller is proven gone.
- fix(run-log): read a resumed run's own log while the store still hides its new head (#20523)
  > - `server/src/services/object-run-log-store.ts`: `read()` reads the head from the store as before. When the store says the head does not exist and this process has a live writer for that handle, it uses the writer's head. The writer published that head, so it proves the log exists. A handle without a live writer still fails at once with "Object run log not found".
  > - `server/src/services/object-byte-journal.ts`: `recordVerifiedSourcePrefix` reads the head again to confirm ownership before it publishes the optional source-prefix proof. When that head reads as missing, it now throws `ObjectJournalSourcePrefixProofUnavailableError` (the existing "proof is optional" result). The store then continues without the proof, and the next resume scans the prefix again. A visible head with a different etag, a closed head or a deleted head still fails as `ObjectJournalConflictError`. Later appends still publish the head with a conditional write against the writer's etag, so another writer is still detected.
  > - `server/src/services/object-run-log-store.test.ts`: a test hides the successor's just-published `head.json` for the full resume sequence that `nativeRunLogPrefixCursor` runs against the store (`read`, then `recordVerifiedSourcePrefix`).
