Before this change, `read` fails with "Object run log not found"; with only the read fix, the proof step fails with "The output journal is owned by another writer". After both, the read returns records 1–3, the proof is marked unavailable, and a later append and read return records 1–4. A second test checks that a read of a log that was never written still fails at once.
- fix(native): make Retry work after a turn start cut off once Codex recorded the turn (#20516)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: when the runner-state fence refuses a run before launch (`prior_owner_*` or `legacy_prior_owner_*`), the executor records the server-authored event `native.prior_owner_fence_refused` with the closed refusal `reason`, then fails the run as before. A failed write leaves the run held (the safe side).
  > - `native-local-process-stop.ts`: `nativeRunNeverLaunched` accepts a failed run that has this event (and still no launch, identity, `adapter.invoke` or runner-sourced event). It no longer reads the error text.
  > - `explicit-native-continuation.ts`: when the task's in-process cleanup quarantine is operator-required, a Retry no longer returns `cleanup_quarantined` at once. Like a new message, it must first pass `verifyStoppedNativeSessionForContinuation`, which releases the quarantine and records `native.stopped_conversation_verified`. A native Retry always starts a fresh conversation (`forceFreshSession`), so it never resumes the interrupted turn. If the proof fails, Retry still returns `cleanup_quarantined`.
- fix(recovery): settle a drained run's hold once its controller is proven gone (#20496)
  > - `legacy-controller-lease.ts`: new `provenGoneLegacyControllerBootId(db, companyId, runId)`. It returns the boot id of a legacy run's unexpired controller lease when the shared prover proves that controller's recorded process gone, else null.
  > - `execution-recovery-resolution.ts`: `settleUnrecoverableExecutions` computes that boot id before its transaction. When the live lease's boot id matches, it expires that lease and settles the hold instead of waiting.
  > - New `execution-recovery-dead-controller.test.ts` (embedded Postgres): a drained run whose controller process has exited is settled at once, its lease is expired, and `isInterruptedNativePreparation` turns true (continuation eligible) while the original lease would still run for about 55 s. A lease held by the current process still waits and keeps its expiry.
- fix(accounting): close a remote-Runner turn completed across a session restore as a lower bound (#20524)
  > - **Receiver (`createNativeUsageReceiver`).** It now accepts `session.resumed` for turn-reporting providers (ACPX, OpenCode) while a turn is open. It journals the restore as a receipt like any other producer event, so turn accounting marks the open turn restored. A replacement controller rebuilds the same state from that journal.
  > - **Restores never fail delivery.** A restore from another run or source, or one with no open turn, is ignored, not rejected. An unacknowledged lifecycle event would otherwise replay without end.
  > - **Session-authority filter.** `acceptAccountingEvent` now uses a new `isNativeUsageDeliveryEvent`: the usage events plus `session.resumed`. A restore for another run is ignored there too.
- fix(runner): relaunch a provider whose startup only timed out without a reply (#20521)
  > - The deadline that passes is the runner's own 30 s request deadline. When a startup request (`initialize`, `thread/start`/`thread/resume`, `thread/read`) reaches that deadline with the provider still running and silent, the runner sets `noReply: true` on the durable `initialization_failed` startup record.
  > - Not `noReply`: a provider error response, a provider that exited before it replied, or closed stdout.
  > - `retire_orphaned_startup_attempt` retires an `initialization_failed` fence only when `noReply` is set and the recorded process and its process group are both gone (`ESRCH`). This is the same proof as `spawned` in #19784. A rejection, an exit, a live process or group, and spawn or admission failures stay fenced.
- fix(recovery): treat a retired prepared remote epoch as nothing to recover (#20491)
  > - New `server/src/services/remote-workspace-abandoned-preparation.ts`: `isAbandonedPreparedRemoteWorkspace` (the strict rule) and `abandonedPreparedRemoteWorkspaceExists` (its SQL pre-filter).
  > - `workspace-recovery-receipt.ts`: `hasVerifiedWorkspaceRepair` also accepts the rule.
  > - `workspace-recovery-source.ts`: a retired prepared source is `source_unavailable` / `use_alternative_strategy`.
- fix(runner): settle a saved governed wait whose parked turn was lost on restore (#20519)
  > - `native-session-runtime.ts`: add `governedWaitTerminalProven(event)`. A failed terminal proves the stop only when `isNativeRestartInterruption(error)` is true (code `provider_turn_lost_on_restore`, `recoverable: true`). Other terminals keep the old rule (`providerTerminalObserved !== false`).
  > - The governed-wait accounting drain and the saved-replay check both use that helper.
  > - Tests: two new variants of the #20494 test (a lost terminal after the restart, and a lost terminal already saved before the next recovery). Two new rejection cases: a non-restart failed terminal and a non-recoverable restart loss still fail with `governed_wait_provider_terminal_unproven`.
- fix(accounting): close a local-Runner turn completed across a session restore as a lower bound (#20517)
  > - `createNativeTurnAccounting` observes `session.resumed` and marks every open turn as restored. A terminal event for a restored turn without a complete report marks it lost. The snapshot then closes it as a lower bound (`accountingUsageLost`), through the existing lost-on-restore path. A new `turn.started` clears the mark.
  > - A lost terminal turn keeps its identity in the snapshot, so the executor's terminal-turn match certifies the final checkpoint as complete. This came from review.
  > - The native session executor includes `session.resumed` when it rebuilds turn state from the run's event log after a controller restart.
- fix(runner): classify Claude's rejected selected model as an unavailable model (#20513)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/acpx/provider-failure.ts`: for the `claude` agent, an AIR `request` session failure whose title is Claude Code's fixed "There's an issue with the selected model (…). It may not exist or you may not have access to it." is classified `model_unavailable` (`requires_action`). The native runtime already marks such a failed turn `native_provider_model_rejected`, and the server already projects that as `choose_model` with `rejectedModel`.
  > - Other AIR categories, other agents and other request titles stay `unknown`.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: a `native_provider_model_rejected` failure after the provider session opened now gets a choose-model next action ("The provider rejected the selected model. Choose a supported model for this agent, then retry the task. Automatic retries are stopped."). Before, only a rejection before the session opened got that action; the Claude case fell through to "Inspect the original provider failure and explicitly resolve the ambiguous session state; do not open a replacement provider session." Recovery mode, blocking and retry behavior are unchanged; only the action text changes.
- fix(run-log): wait for a just-written journal head instead of failing the claim (#20509)
  > - `server/src/services/object-byte-journal.ts`: `claim()` reads the head and claims from it. When the head read as missing and the create fails as a conflict, it waits with backoff (250 ms doubling to 4 s, up to 60 s) and claims again from the head it then reads. A conflict on a head it could read, or a deletion tombstone, still fails at once.
  > - `server/src/services/object-byte-journal.test.ts`: a new test hides a just-written head for three reads. Before this change the second claim fails with "The output journal is owned by another writer". After it, the claim gets the next generation and keeps the committed bytes.
- fix(native): keep another server's orphan sweep off a lease its owner took back (#20508)
  > - `setRunStatusFromLive` (`server/src/services/heartbeat/run-lifecycle.ts`) has a new option `onlyWhileNativeLeaseLapsed`. It locks the coordinator row first. This is the same order that `lockNativeExecutionLeaseForReacquire` uses. The UPDATE then requires that no executor lease on the run is live.
  > - `markNativeOwnershipUnverified` (`server/src/services/heartbeat/recovery.ts`) takes `leaseLapsed`. The orphan sweep in `recoverActiveExecutions` sets it when it read the lease as lapsed or unset. Other callers do not change. They keep the same call shape.
  > - A new regression test, red before the fix: `server/src/__tests__/heartbeat-native-orphan-sweep-lease-race.test.ts`. It also checks that the sweep still marks a lapsed run whose owner did not come back, and that the late owner stays fenced out.
- fix(native): resume a run whose turn.start a restart cut off before Codex created the turn (#20507)
  > - Runner (`codex_provider.rs`, `provider_backend.rs`): when the restored provider state has an ambiguous turn start, no active turn before the loss, and the resumed thread has no active turn, the runner reads the newest turn of the thread (`thread/turns/list`, limit 1, metadata only, the same request `read_thread` already sends). If there is no turn, or the newest turn is one Paperclip already settled, Codex never created the turn. The runner clears the ambiguity, emits a `harness.diagnostic` with code `ambiguous_turn_start_not_created`, and restores an idle session. The old `turn.start` stays `execution_indeterminate` and is never executed again. The same-run resume then starts the run's turn once, through the existing "recovered session without an active turn" path.
  > - Server (`native-local-process-stop.ts`): `nativeRunNeverLaunched` also accepts a failed native run whose error is the runner-state fence refusal `runner_state_identity_mismatch: prior_owner_*` (or `legacy_prior_owner_*`). The executor throws it only while it migrates another run's saved state, before it requests a launch. The existing evidence check still applies: any launch request, identity record, `adapter.invoke` or runner event keeps the run held. Other runner-state errors (for example `authority_indeterminate`) stay held.
  > - Fake Codex (`fake-codex-app-server.rs`): keeps the thread's turn history in a `<state-file>.turns` sidecar, so `thread/turns/list` returns history like real Codex (newest first, honours `limit`). New `--hang-turn-start <file>` holds `turn/start` "before" or "after" the thread records the turn, to model a kill in flight.
- fix(accounting): keep budget admission and usage handoff alive under run-row updates (#20484)
  > - `reserveRunBudget` runs at READ COMMITTED. All mutable reads follow the shared admission fences, the agent `FOR SHARE` lock and the run-row `FOR UPDATE`. Exposure stays one SQL statement (hold-to-ledger atomic). The `budget_reservations_run_idx` unique index and the run lock still prevent a second hold.
  > - The final dispatch gate's accounting phase (`dispatchResolvedInteractionIfCurrent` through `withIssueThenRunLocks` in `run-dispatch/adapters/postgres.ts`) runs at READ COMMITTED. Its ownership phase already read the same staleness facts at READ COMMITTED under the same issue and run locks. Retry promotion and stale-run cancellation keep their repeatable-read snapshot. The first L1 proof wave found this writer exhausting `40001` once the other three were fixed.
  > - `createRunUsageRecorder` runs its receipt-source handoff and its capture-failure fence at READ COMMITTED. Both lock the run row first, the same as `persistUsageReceipt` after #20385.
- fix(runner): settle ACPX turns when the cancel reply is lost (#20498)
  > - `AcpxSidecarTransport::request_turn_cancel`: sends `turn.cancel` and waits only to a caller deadline. On a missing reply it keeps the transport usable and retains the request ID, so a late reply is skipped in both the response and the event-poll paths. A rejected reply and transport errors keep their existing fail-closed result.
  > - `AcpxProviderSession::interrupt_turn`: waits at most 5 s (or the shorter configured request bound) for the cancel reply. On a missing reply it retires the runtime from warm reuse and returns. The provider's turn terminal still arrives through polling, and `turn.stop` still reaps a provider that never stops.
  > - `AcpxProviderSession::deliver_tool_result`: a board Stop can land while a semantic tool runs. The session's own `turn.cancel` then retires the call in the sidecar before its result arrives, and the turn terminal may still be buffered. The sidecar rejects the result as stale (`ACPX_TOOL_CALL_STALE`). When this session sent `turn.cancel` for that turn, the Runner now keeps the durable result, like a result for an already closed turn. Every other rejection still fails closed. (`AcpxSidecarTransport::request_tool_resolve` reports whether a rejection was the stale-call case.)
- fix(native): classify a Codex API-key unknown model as a model rejection (#20488)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-provider-failure.ts`: the Codex observation recognizes the API-key terminal shape (`codexErrorInfo` `httpConnectionFailed` or `responseStreamDisconnected` with `httpStatusCode: 404`, and the message prefix for the configured model only). It classifies it as `native_provider_model_rejected` with a new closed diagnostic `NATIVE_MODEL_NOT_FOUND_DIAGNOSTIC` (`codex`, `model_not_found`, `404`, `api_key`). No provider text or model name is stored in the diagnostic. A recoverable turn, another model's 404, or another status is not classified.
  > - `readNativeModelRejectionDiagnostic` accepts both closed diagnostics, so run failure diagnostics show it.
  > - `TaskInterruptionPresentation.rejectedModel` (shared type): with `userAction: "choose_model"`, the task query carries the model id from the run's native execution input. Only a `native_provider_model_rejected` run discloses it.
- fix(recovery): retire the ledger row of an abandoned workspace save (#20502)
  > - New `server/src/services/recovery/remote-snapshot-ledger-settlement.ts`:
  > - `settleAbandonedRemoteSnapshotSave` now also retires the save's pending ledger row in the same transaction, when the conditions above already hold.
  > - `ownership-registry.ts`: `remote_snapshot_ledger` now has `settle: settleAbandonedRemoteSnapshotLedger`.
- fix(native): take back an executor lease that lapsed only because the host slept (#20474)
  > - `native-execution-lease.ts`: add `nativeExecutionLeaseHeldCondition` and `lockNativeExecutionLeaseForReacquire`. A lease is held when it is live. For a local run, it is also held when it lapsed and is untouched. "Untouched" means all of these are true:
  > - The caller's WHERE already matches the owner token, attempt and controller boot/pid/start.
  > - The lock helper locks the coordinator row. If the lease lapsed, it also takes a share lock on the run row. The predicate then reads the latest committed run, and an in-flight run write cannot slip past it.
- fix(workspaces): name a failed workspace setup command on the task page (#20478)
  > - `WorkspaceCommandExitError` now carries the setup step (`workspace_provision`, `workspace_seed`, `workspace_runtime_provision`), the configured command and the command output.
  > - New `server/src/services/workspace-setup-command-failure.ts`: it builds a bounded, redacted `setupCommandFailure` record (step, command up to 200 characters, exit code, last 300 characters of output). It also recognizes the older message format, so runs recorded before this change still get a name.
  > - `run-completion.ts` adds `setupCommandFailure` to the setup failure `resultJson`. The error code (`setup_failed`) and all retry and recovery fields stay the same.
- fix(native): let Retry continue after a run that failed before native selection (#20482)
  > - `native-local-process-stop.ts`: `nativeRunRefusedBeforeLaunch` (renamed `nativeRunNeverLaunched`) now also proves "never launched" for a settled native run in this state:
  > - `explicit-native-continuation.test.ts`: the guard "failed for another reason without a process record" now separates real pre-selection failures from the shapes that must stay held.
- fix(native): re-attach an answer run whose run.attach a restart cut off (#20477)
  > - `runnerd-codex-transport.ts`: the same-run resume waits on the recovered `run.attach` as before. If that wait fails, the new `cutOffRunAttachmentPayload` checks the journal. It queues one new `run.attach` with the same payload, and waits on it, only when all of these are true:
  > - `provider_backend.rs` (Runner): `retire_orphaned_startup_attempt` also treats a `prepared` state with a saved thread and a nonzero process generation as restorable. `attach_run` already documents this state as the settled checkpoint that `run.attach` restores. The other fence rules do not change. The fence is retired only before the spawn, or when the recorded process and its group have exited.
  > - Tests:
- fix(runner): settle a saved governed wait whose shutdown drain left sequence gaps (#20494)
  > - `isGovernedDrainRetainedEvent` (new, in `native-session-runtime.ts`) names the facts that the governed-wait drain persists: usage, the turn terminal, the interrupt acknowledgement, cancelled or expired runtime requests, and a failed or cancelled `run.terminal`, all for the parked turn. The drain now uses it in place of the same inline condition. Drain behavior does not change.
  > - Governed-wait recovery replay accepts a `sourceSeq` gap only when:
  > - The run, normalized session and source instance identity checks do not change. Any mismatch still fails with `native_governed_wait_replay_mismatch`.
- fix(heartbeat): name the cause of generic adapter failures and keep SQL text off the board (#20483)
  > - Runner: move the #20385 event-commit cause extraction into `safeErrorCauseCode` (`packages/paperclip-runner/src/control-plane/safe-error-cause.ts`) and export it. The Runner's own diagnostics use the same function, unchanged in behavior.
  > - Shared: add the `failureCause` contract (`packages/shared/src/run-failure-cause.ts`): one `[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]` token of at most 80 characters, `normalizeRunFailureCause`, `readRunFailureCause`, and `withoutSqlStatementText`.
  > - Server: add `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-failure-cause.ts`. Settlement records `resultJson.failureCause` only when the code is generic (`adapter_failed`, `setup_failed`). `errorCode` stays the same.
- fix(heartbeat): resume legacy runs a graceful shutdown stopped before dispatch (#20461)
  > - New `server/src/services/legacy-shutdown-before-dispatch.ts`: one SQL proof that a legacy run interrupted by graceful shutdown never reached the adapter. The proof requires: `executionStage = 'preparing'` with a controller boot ID, no `budgetDispatch.handedOffAt` handoff receipt, no process identity, no execution-start event, and no native or workspace-recovery obligation.
  > - `terminalizeLegacyExecution` applies that proof in the same transaction that commits the shutdown interruption, under the run lock. A matching run gets `executionRecovery: { kind: "bootstrap", providerWorkStarted: false }`. The existing process-loss retry then schedules it in the interrupted-preparation lane (no delay, no failure budget spent), and no hold is created.
  > - `executionBlockerPredicate` ignores a `legacy_execution_requires_reconciliation` hold whose source run carries the same proof. This releases holds that an older server saved for the same case, so Retry starts a run (upgrade-safe for old rows).
- fix(storage): wait out expired or rotated S3 credentials and re-resolve the key (#20456)
  > - `storage-unavailable.ts`: `ExpiredToken`, `ExpiredTokenException`, `TokenRefreshRequired`, `InvalidToken`, `InvalidAccessKeyId`, `InvalidClientTokenId` and `SignatureDoesNotMatch` answered by the store with 400/403 count as storage unavailable. `AccessDenied` and a credential chain that cannot load any key (`CredentialsProviderError`) stay permanent. New credential-specific run message; the exhausted message also names credentials.
  > - `s3-credential-refresh.ts` (new): S3 client middleware that calls the client's credential provider with `forceRefresh` after a rejection, once per credential generation (requests that signed with an already-replaced key do not refresh again), then rethrows so the existing storage wait owns the retry.
  > - `s3-provider.ts`: installs the middleware on both S3 clients.
- fix(runner): retain Claude GitHub broker settings through the Rust sidecar (#20463)
  > - Retain the broker URL and both broker capabilities when Claude has a nonempty managed launcher directory.
  > - Keep raw GitHub tokens, unrelated secrets, empty launcher configurations, and sibling ACPX providers outside that exception.
  > - Exercise the regression through the public sidecar initialize protocol in the existing Rust integration suite. Document the restart requirement.
- fix(workspaces): read saved workspace files in place instead of extracting the whole archive (#20442)
  > - `openWorkspaceSnapshotRanges` (`remote-workspace-snapshot-storage.ts`):
  > - New `saved-workspace-archive.ts`:
  > - `remote-workspace-files.ts`: with the new `openSavedWorkspaceArchive` option, saved stat, list, read and download use the verified manifest, not the extracted-directory helper.
- fix(workspaces): recognize committed imports in linked source scope (#20444)
  > - Let the existing imported-source owner match optional exact linked physical roots in addition to its existing nested-child paths.
  > - Require each added linked root to have a finished successful same-company journal, an exact committed or superseded snapshot binding, and the same manifest digest.
  > - Include those proven physical roots in the online scope and its source fingerprint checks.
- fix(workspaces): protect migration sources and add guarded recovery service (#20443)
  > - Count saved migration markers and unfinished migration journals as Git expiration consumers. Keep failed migration retention holds as consumers too.
  > - Expose `supersedeExpiredRemoteWorkspaceMigrationCohort` as an internal service API for a privileged operator with a real database handle and reviewed company/cohort inputs. It is called directly by the PostgreSQL/Git test and reviewed one-time administrative qualification. It adds no CLI mode or HTTP route. The existing CLI still refuses a damaged cohort; this PR does not claim automatic CLI recovery.
  > - Require an exact unpublished cohort of up to eight members. It requires completed expiration and an unexpired restore recipe for each missing member. The other members must retain their saved source fingerprints.
- fix(workspaces): verify archived repository intents before publication (#20441)
  > - Separate strict repository request metadata from filesystem absence proof.
  > - Admit only strict candidates backed by a canonical archived Git recipe to private materialization.
  > - Verify target absence and safe parents before a new upload and after authenticated archive restore.
- fix(ui): keep the persisted cache's final state when it cancels reads (#20431)
  > - `ui/src/lib/persisted-query-cache.ts`: `clearAccountData` and the denied-task eviction cancel with `{ revert: false, silent: true }`. A silent cancel does not dispatch a later error state and does not call `QueryCache.onError`. The cancelled fetch promise still rejects with `CancelledError`, as before.
  > - `ui/src/lib/query-cancellation.test.ts`: two regression tests use the real `createAppQueryClient` and `startPersistedQueryCache`:
- fix(native): reprove stale filesystem preservation receipts (#20438)
  > - Reuse the prior controller archive through the existing authenticated readback path.
  > - Require the old receipt to match every current immutable binding before a facts refresh. Reconstruct its exact digest from unchanged bindings and the prior proof fields.
  > - Recheck current database facts, assets, controller and local files under the existing finalizer, selection, task and run fences.
- fix(native): let a resume claimed after a restart write the run's existing log (#20427)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-finalization-reconciler.ts`: `dispatchNativeSessionResumptions` writes the claiming process's controller identity (boot id, pid, host hash, process start) along with its lease owner and expiry. This matches the restart-recovery claim, so ownership proofs and the run-log writer check see the process that actually owns the resume.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/native-session-resumption.test.ts`: a regression test for this case.
- fix(workspaces): preserve recovery for verified archived Git sources (#20436)
  > - Change three conditions to accept a non-null canonical archived-Git source instead of requiring its recipe field.
  > - Use the same typed recovery inspector, sorted native finalization locks, fresh reinspection, and source-task publication locks for all accepted variants.
  > - Reuse the existing Git source validator, private scratch materializer, encrypted snapshot restore, journal, and recovery markers. No source-proof schema or provider protocol changes.
- fix(ui): stop reporting cancelled queries to Sentry (#20430)
  > - `ui/src/api/errors.ts`: `isAbortError` now recognizes React Query's `CancelledError` by class. The old check compared `name` with `"CancelledError"`, and that comparison could never match.
  > - `ui/src/api/errors.test.ts`: add a `classifyError` case for a real `CancelledError`.
  > - `ui/src/lib/query-client.test.ts`: add a regression test. It cancels an in-flight query with `revert: false` and asserts that the Sentry reporter is not called.
- fix(workspaces): preserve archived native recovery during migration (#20424)
  > - Permit the existing online recovery owner to inspect recipe-backed archived Git sources.
  > - Acquire the existing native finalization locks for those sources. Reinspect their exact proofs after lock acquisition and before publication.
  > - Lock related source-task ancestry before publication.
- fix(workspaces): refresh saved cohort source proofs on resume (#20429)
  > - Admit a changed aggregate scope proof only when every saved member has a running journal and its current fingerprint matches both saved fingerprints.
  > - Keep partial reservations on the original strict aggregate-proof path. Keep exact cohort and operation membership checks.
  > - Run the existing source, lifecycle, recovery and finalizer admission before refresh. Lock member rows, inspect the current scope again, and compare every journal in one transaction.
- fix(storage): wait out a store outage on a run's critical path instead of failing it (#20299)
  > - New `server/src/storage/storage-unavailable.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-completion.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/retries.ts`: `storage_unavailable` joins the transient infrastructure codes. It gets its own retry budget and policy, like `runner_revision_mismatch`.
- fix(opencode): attribute unreadable tool-result images to the tool (#20399)
  > - Add `packages/paperclip-runner/src/drivers/opencode/tool-media-attribution.ts`. It finds the OpenCode synthetic attachment message only when it directly follows tool results. It moves each "Cannot read ..." or "empty or corrupted" notice into the last tool result of that step as a runner note. It keeps readable media in the synthetic message. It removes the message when no media remains.
  > - The OpenCode provider proxy applies this rewrite before it forwards a request. It re-serializes the body only when something changed.
  > - Messages written by a person are never changed. A person who attaches an image to a text-only model still gets "Inform the user", which is correct for that case.
- fix(workspaces): preserve unmaterialized repository intent during migration (#20416)
  > - Accept unchanged pending requests and canonical preparation failures only when no pin or branch exists and the contained checkout path is absent.
  > - Require a proven online source or the existing offline writer-stop contract.
  > - Leave companion workspace requests under their existing strict blocker. Classify the current workspace request with its filesystem check.
- fix(server): tell agents who owns a plugin-managed routine and where it is changed (#20409)
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: add a shared `PLUGIN_MANAGED_ROUTINE_GUIDANCE` description to `GET /api/routines/{id}` and `PATCH /api/routines/{id}`. It names `managedByPlugin`, links `[plugin settings](/<prefix>/company/settings/instance/plugins/<pluginId>)`, and tells agents to reply with that link instead of rewriting, detaching or duplicating the routine. The `PATCH` entry also states the existing agent rule (own routines only, no reassignment).
  > - `server/src/__tests__/plugin-managed-routine-guidance.test.ts`: new regression test. The queries the agent actually used now find these entries with the guidance, and the linked settings route is mounted in `ui/src/App.tsx`.
- fix(server): redact invitation tokens from agent run logs (#20360)
  > - Add `redactInvitationTokens()` in `server/src/middleware/invitation-token-redaction.ts`, next to the HTTP log redactor from #20343. It removes the token from `/invite/<token>` segments and from raw `pcp_invite_` tokens inside free text and JSON text. The character classes stop at JSON, Markdown and URL delimiters, so the record structure stays valid. `redact-sensitive.ts` stays byte-identical, because the chat eval pins its exact bytes before it accepts a real invitation link.
  > - Apply it in `compactRunLogChunk()` and `redactCanonicalNativeRunLogChunk()`. These are the two functions that format every agent run-log chunk before the store writes it. The run excerpt columns use the same chunk, so they are also redacted.
  > - `canonicalNativeRunLogRedactionForms()` also yields the earlier, unredacted projection. A run log written by an earlier release still verifies after an upgrade.
- fix(agents): repair queued setup through task connection cards (#20361)
  > - Share owned account-selection failure evidence through a pure library contract. Lifecycle policy parks those configuration failures with fixed, redacted guidance; temporary infrastructure errors retain their retries.
  > - Batch setup-state reads for unstarted queued turns. Distinguish manual repair from automatic setup retries and preserve Stop, started/history state and lifecycle holds.
  > - Reuse the existing `connection_intent` card, account selection and completion APIs. Its controller-only `agentSetup` marker accepts only never-started legacy turns; runtime tokens still require a running run.
- fix(accounting): prevent usage receipt stalls during streamed run updates (#20385)
  > - Allow aggregate-lock-protected accounting writers to select READ COMMITTED. Keep repeatable read as the default.
  > - Use READ COMMITTED only for standalone usage receipt writes. Keep the existing run lock, immutable receipt journal, replay checks and transactional budget-work publication.
  > - Add PostgreSQL regressions for concurrent log appends, concurrent finalization, duplicate delivery and the unchanged default snapshot.
- fix(server): let a native reviewer approve its review stage (#20373)
  > - `PATCH /api/issues/:id`: when the requester is the native run that holds the task lock and is the active participant, a `status: "done"` request on the final stage now records the approval decision (`executionState` completed, decision row, activity). The route removes `status` from the write, so the task stays `in_review` and the run keeps its execution lock. The run's `paperclip_finish` report then sets `done` through the normal status arbiter.
  > - An intermediate-stage approval (which would hand the task to the next participant) and every other status change from such a run keep the existing `native_run_status_reported_by_finish` refusal.
  > - `paperclip_finish` from the active participant (`done` or `needs_review`) while its stage is still pending is now rejected with the exact decision path, instead of being accepted with "stage still pending".
- fix(workspace): refresh source admission proofs on import resume (#20394)
  > - Compare the complete `legacyRecoverySources` records at resume, neutralizing only a valid fingerprint under the supported proof schema. Preserve every other field and refuse unsupported proof versions by name.
  > - Reuse the current canonical full proofs. Persist them with an operation metadata compare-and-swap only after the existing finalization lock and strict reinspection succeed.
  > - Keep strict full-proof checks within each attempt and before publication. Preserve all journal, snapshot, archive, nested-child, and cohort identities.
- fix(lifecycle): align stop proof for pre-dispatch resource waits (#20384)
  > - Add one shared classifier for matching workspace and AI-capacity wait receipts. Use it in legacy recovery and cancellation stop proof.
  > - Reuse the existing dispatch-event predicate instead of maintaining another event list.
  > - Retain checks for native execution references, unresolved workspace obligations, controller leases, and resource cleanup. A live controller or cleanup still in flight cannot pass the proof.
- fix(workspace): bound queued source-lock admission for imports (#20370)
  > - Add optional `sourceLockWaitMs` to singleton and cohort migration APIs. Omitted options keep current admission behavior.
  > - Queue the sorted source keys with one decreasing wait budget. Abort cancels only the dedicated backend identified by PID and backend start time.
  > - Use an independent source transaction for queued singleton imports. Keep lifecycle checks, fresh source checks, journal ownership, and publication after commit.
- fix(api-search): stop function words from pushing case lookup off page one (#20382)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/runner-api-catalog.ts`: the search tokenizer drops a small set of function words (`a`, `an`, `the`, `for`, `of`, `to`, `in`, `on`, `at`, `by`, `with`, `and`, `or`, `from`) before scoring. This applies to the query and to the catalog text.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/runner-api.test.ts`: a regression test. "list comments for an issue" returns the comments route first. "queued comments", "activity", "runs" and "case for task" return their routes on the first page. A query of only function words returns no results.
- fix: deliver saved answers to dependency-blocked tasks (#20363)
  > - Verify the stored response by company, task, requesting agent, status, kind, human resolver, and resolution time. A context flag alone is insufficient.
  > - Allow a verified response through dependency admission and claim in the existing bounded interaction mode.
  > - Recheck that response during scheduled retry promotion and final provider dispatch.
- fix(ui): show submitted screenshots and Markdown in steering messages (#20367)
  > - Hide submitted attachment previews as soon as Send clears the text. Keep their upload receipts until the request settles.
  > - Use the shared Markdown renderer in queued rows. Show up to three compact screenshots with a count for more images. Strip standalone image syntax from the text preview.
  > - Keep paragraph boundaries. Wrap thumbnails and controls within narrow rows.
- fix(workspaces): migrate task directories with legacy shared mode (#20371)
  > - Permit `shared_workspace` beside `isolated_workspace` in the existing physical task-source admission check. All other checks remain in their existing owners.
  > - Extend the existing migration and archive-restore fixture with the legacy shared-mode task path and null provider reference.
  > - Use the truthful task-case lookup summary “Find the cases for a task”. This restores its existing first-page discovery contract without changing search scores, pagination or tests.
- fix(ui): keep new-task composer stable during submission (#20365)
  > - Keep the header absent during a normal send when it has no title, subtask context, or notice.
  > - Keep the existing title, subtask context, connection retry notice, and error message.
  > - Extend the submit test to check stable composer sections for description-only tasks, titled tasks, and subtasks.
- fix(api): make case lookup and audit history discoverable to agents (#20354)
  > - `server/src/routes/experimental-api-paths.ts`: add `experimentalApiDocs` with summaries and descriptions for the case list, task-to-case lookup, case read, case events and case document revision routes.
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: use those summaries and descriptions when present. Other experimental routes keep the generated summary. The experimental notice stays in every description.
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: the Cases chat directive now names `GET /api/issues/{issueId}/cases` to find a task's case and `GET /api/cases/{id}/events` for who changed its facts.
- fix(ci): prepare native binaries for Runner chaos checks
- fix(chat): answer "my inbox" from the person's Mine list (#20334)
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: `GET /api/agents/me/inbox/mine` now has a summary and description that say it is a person's Mine list, not the agent's own queue. It documents the required `userId` query parameter and the optional `status` parameter, and the 400 response.
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: one chat guidance paragraph. For inbox questions, read the Mine route with the `authorUserId` of the person's message, answer from those tasks only, and link `[Mine](/<prefix>/inbox/mine)` (it opens the Mine view in every layout). If the read fails, say so and link Mine.
  > - Regression tests in `openapi-routes.test.ts` and `agent-conversations.test.ts`.
- fix(sentry): report a reviewed chat setup wait only after its retry decision (#20205)
  > - `RunFailureReportOptions` has a new `reviewedChatRetry` field: `pending`, `scheduled` or `not_scheduled`.
  > - `reportRunFailure` does not report a `pending` write, in any mode. The setup path reports that run again with its decision.
  > - In `unexpected` mode, a `reviewed_chat_execution_binding_not_ready` failure with `reviewedChatRetry: "scheduled"` stays local. Other codes and `all` mode are unchanged.
- fix(recovery): end a remote workspace epoch whose writer died during preparation (#20346)
  > - New `settlePreparationOnlyRemoteWorkspaceEpoch` (`recovery/remote-workspace-unsaved-files-settlement.ts`), wired as the settle of the existing `remote_workspace_unsaved_files` ownership kind. Once the writer run is proven gone, it ends the epoch with the existing `abandonUnverifiedRemoteWorkspaceEpoch` only when all of these are true:
  > - After that, the lease no longer matches a prepared source, so the existing environment lease settle releases it and the workspace writer hold is free for the retry.
  > - The ownership inventory row for this kind names the new settle.
- fix(api): send call_api mutations once when responseText asks only for the first window (#20344)
  > - `validateRunnerApiCall`: for a non-GET operation, drop a first-window `responseText` (no offset or offset 0) and continue. A later page still returns 400 with "This request was not sent" and the reason.
  > - New `assertRunnerApiDispatchable`: reject a new PATCH with a required request body when `body` and `files` are both absent. The message lists up to 12 allowed fields and the received argument keys. `call_api` runs it inside its receipt reservation, after saved and inherited receipts are read and before it writes a pending receipt, so a rejected call leaves no receipt. `executeRunnerApi` also runs it for direct callers. Recovery and receipt replay use only `validateRunnerApiCall`, so a body-less PATCH that an older server completed still returns its saved result.
  > - Tests: first-window `responseText` on a PATCH dispatches once with the body (three window shapes). A later page is rejected with no dispatch. A body-less PATCH is rejected with no dispatch, and `body: {}` is still accepted. The catalog-wide dispatch tests now send `body: {}` for PATCH operations with a required body.
- fix(tasks): keep a budget-held Retry's failure visible and refresh the open task (#20338)
  > - `ExecutionBlockerNotice.tsx`: a Retry held by a budget hard stop presents as `waiting_for_user` with `userAction: "budget"`. The notice now shows "Couldn't finish this attempt. Your message is saved. It is waiting for the budget to allow new work. Raise the budget in Costs." (`data-testid="task-execution-waiting"`). It shows no second Retry, because the saved Retry starts by itself. Other person-owned decisions, such as approvals and run-stopped confirmation, keep their own surfaces.
  > - `deferred-execution-wait.ts`: when the wait reason of a deferred request changes, its task records an `issue.execution_waiting` activity. Its live event refreshes an open task page. Callers inside a transaction (`heartbeat/queue.ts`) pass their post-commit publication list, so the event is published only after commit.
- fix(api): publish agent-grant access operations as board_or_agent in the API catalog (#20349)
  > - Remove six operations from `BOARD_ONLY_OPERATIONS`: `POST`/`GET /companies/{companyId}/invites`, `GET /join-requests`, `POST /join-requests/{requestId}/approve` and `/reject`, and `GET /members`. Their routes use `assertCompanyPermission`, which admits agents with the grant.
  > - Add descriptions that name the grant for each operation. The invite description also says it returns a shareable `inviteUrl` (no email field), and that membership starts only after acceptance.
  > - Member edits (`PATCH /members/{memberId}`, `/role-and-grants`, `/permissions` and `POST /archive`) stay board-only. The route guard requires board access, and a test keeps the catalog in line with that guard.
- fix: keep tool approvals valid through OAuth refresh and delayed review (#20340)
  > - Add versioned approval target comparison. New snapshots bind credential identity epochs, consent, permissions, ownership, connection configuration, tool contracts, and credential references. Authenticated OAuth refresh preserves identity. Manual credential replacement does not.
  > - Remove the implicit one-hour deadline from ordinary app action reviews. Keep explicit deadlines and terminal-task expiry. Synchronize the nullable interaction deadline in shared contracts and UI.
  > - Keep accepted, undispatched actions when sign-in is required. Show a saved-approval repair state. Resume on committed connection changes after target and access revalidation, without an idle retry loop.
- fix(runner): settle saved handoffs after verified shutdown (#20336)
  > - Add an optional driver shutdown-settlement operation. Runner returns proof only after its existing stop, drain, tool settlement and suspension barrier passes.
  > - Bind the receipt to the run, normalized session and turn. Persist it with the saved wait and validate it during recovery.
  > - Complete the same yielded handoff after verified shutdown. Retire the stopped session from warm reuse. Do not invent a provider terminal or complete usage.
- fix(server): redact invitation bearer paths from HTTP logs (#20343)
  > - Remove the bearer segment from `/api/invites/<token>/...`, `/invites/<token>/...`, and `/invite/<token>` URLs, including mounted paths and absolute URLs.
  > - Apply the same URL policy to `referer` and `referrer` request headers.
  > - Keep record IDs on the non-token revoke route `/invites/<inviteId>/revoke` and on company-scoped invite routes.
- fix(runner): share the bounded ACPX cleanup proof (#20332)
  > - Use `await_provider_lifetime_fence` for a recovered executor with no live session and unconfirmed provider exit. Delete the unused one-shot helper.
  > - Keep the acquired quorum held through the existing durable state write. A failed proof leaves `providerExitUnconfirmed` set.
  > - Give the shared timeout message wording that applies to shutdown and suspension.
- fix(issues): name the existing order in dependency-cycle denials (#20342)
  > - `server/src/services/issues.ts`: the cycle check now records the path it finds. The 422 message names the existing chain by task identifier (for example "COM-2 waits for COM-1, so COM-1 cannot also wait for COM-2"). It never includes titles, because the chain can pass through tasks the caller cannot read. Identifiers are the same fields that locked relation stubs already show. It says no dependencies changed and the existing ones are kept. It says not to remove or reverse an existing dependency, and to ask the requester which order they want.
  > - The 422 `details` now carry `code: "blocking_cycle"`, the updated issue, the requested blocker, and the existing chain as ids and identifiers.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/issues-service.test.ts`: a regression test checks the message and details for a direct cycle, the chain text for a two-step cycle through a task with a private title, that no title appears in the error, and that the existing dependency is unchanged.
- fix(recovery): settle a remote workspace save whose process died mid-save (#20326)
  > - `saveRemoteWorkspace` records the controller process stamp of each invocation in `saveWork.owners` when it registers the invocation. It removes that entry when the invocation settles. A module-level set marks invocations that are still running in this process (`isRemoteWorkspaceSaveInvocationLive`).
  > - The ownership registry owns a `remote_snapshot_save` with active invocations by that stamp. A save whose active invocations have no stamps was written before this change and is owned by its run, as with finalize attempts. A mix of stamps, or more than one process, is unprovable.
  > - `settleAbandonedRemoteSnapshotSave` settles a save with active invocations once its owner is proven gone:
- fix: refresh preserved harness receipt facts without copying archives (#20322)
  > - Reuse the exact prior archive for existing WEB receipts. Recheck current source bytes, controller history, checkpoint material and archive bindings. Read back ciphertext and verify its size and hash.
  > - Permit only database/local-observation freshness changes. Selected session, source run, controller, archive, WEB entries, and assets must still match the prior proof. Local inode and timestamp observations may change across attempts; preserved bytes, paths and modes must match.
  > - Recheck current facts and source files under the existing finalizer, task and run fences. Accept a matching concurrent winner or require the exact previous receipt digest before replacement.
- fix(ci): handle missing runner output and await import fixture readiness (#20339)
  > - Add `ubuntu-latest` as the fallback only for `verify` and `e2e` when the routing runner is empty.
  > - Extend the existing workflow contract test to require a usable fallback and execute each real summary script with a failed, cancelled or skipped gate and absent routing outputs.
  > - Wait up to 30 seconds for the recovery child to finish imports and construct storage before starting the existing 15-second checkpoint poll. Keep the 30-second work abort, 60-second test timeout, and every kill, key, state and proof assertion.
- fix(chat): tell agents that instruction saves keep no history (#20328)
  > - `AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE` now says three things:
  > - The `get_agent_instruction_history` contract now says that it lists only pre-upgrade revisions, and that an empty list means Paperclip retains no earlier version.
  > - The `restore_agent_instructions` contract now says that only listed pre-upgrade revisions can be restored.
- fix(recovery): move stranded legacy subtasks off a parent's held workspace (#20314)
  > - `server/src/services/execution-workspaces.ts`: extract the binding release into `releaseAncestorWorkspaceBinding(tx, …)`, which runs inside the caller's transaction. Admission uses it unchanged (same guards: no other running run of the task, no unsettled workspace recovery, only a relative's workspace).
  > - New `server/src/services/recovery/legacy-workspace-rehome.ts` (`rehomeStrandedLegacyWorkspaceTasks`). It selects a board-owned stranded escalation whose latest run failed setup on the workspace writer hold before any provider work, for a Blocked task bound to a relative's workspace. In one transaction it releases the binding, resolves the escalation with the outcome `legacy_shared_workspace_rehomed`, sets the task to `todo`, records a pending wake (`legacyWorkspaceRehome.wake: "pending"`), and records `issue.legacy_workspace_rehomed` system activity. After commit it wakes the assignee once (`issue_recovery_action_restored`, with a fresh session) and marks the wake `delivered` only when a run is created. A wake lost to a crash, refused (`null`), or failed stays pending and backs off (5 minutes, doubling, at most an hour); the pass returns the earliest owed wake as its retry deadline, so the recovery worker schedules it. Candidates are paged by action id.
  > - Guards: the Blocked status must come from that escalation (set after the failed run, and no status decision at or after the block), the shared dependency-readiness rule must allow work (cancelled blockers and done blockers that still save files keep it blocked), the failed run must belong to the current assignee, and the binding release must be allowed. Otherwise nothing changes.
- fix(runner): allow Codex interruption to settle governed waits (#20330)
  > - Give Codex a 30-second governed-wait terminal drain through the existing backend option.
  > - Keep other providers and the caller's shorter execution bound unchanged.
  > - Add real Runner protocol cases for prompt, delayed, and absent terminal delivery. Check retained wait state and one provider turn.
- fix(storage): retry small uploads and resume broken downloads (#20313)
  > - `server/src/storage/s3-multipart.ts`: `sendFileRequestWithRetry` resends a single file request and records throttles and backoff in the save timing. It uses the same throttle backoff as multipart parts for 429/503/SlowDown and up to the same part attempt count for other failures. It honours the caller's abort signal.
  > - `server/src/storage/s3-provider.ts`:
  > - Tests:
- fix(chat): give project and workspace tool results their page links (#20324)
  > - `list_projects` rows now carry `href: /<prefix>/projects/<id>`. Both the native runner tool authority and the MCP project-tools route pass the company prefix.
  > - `get_workspace` and `list_workspaces` now carry `href: /<prefix>/execution-workspaces/<id>`, only while isolated workspaces are enabled. The workspace route redirects to the dashboard when the feature is off, so no href is offered then.
  > - The chat link guidance (`AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE`) adds the project link format. It tells the agent to link a returned `href`, and to name a workspace that has no `href` instead of linking it.
- fix(native): accept a completed child's file in the parent's completion report (#20325)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-deliverable-feedback.ts`: when a cited attachment is not on the task, `validateNativeDeliverableEvidence` also looks for it on a direct child task with status `done`. If it is there, the citation is accepted.
  > - This applies to the `paperclip_finish` check and to the two finalization checks, because they use the same function.
  > - `doc/AGENT-ARTIFACTS.md`: the native runner section states that a completion report can cite a file on a completed direct child task.
- fix(recovery): re-prove an exhausted save's agent stop and free the workspace (#20295)
  > - New `server/src/services/workspace-recovery-stop-proof.ts` (`rearmExhaustedStopProof`). For an exhausted save whose last failure is `agent_stop_unconfirmed` (`recheck_evidence`), it starts a new bounded system recovery generation. It does this only when the source run is provably over: the run is terminal, no local execution remains, and no controller lease or finalizer lease is live. The new generation repeats the strict stop proof (a provider stop receipt) and the save. It records an `issue.workspace_recovery_rearmed` system activity.
  > - Re-arms are bounded: at most 4. The first is due at once. The next ones are due 30 minutes, 2 hours and 8 hours after the previous re-arm. The `stopProofRecovery` marker in the recovery action evidence records the count and the next due time.
  > - `workspace-recovery-fallback-discovery.ts`: new discovery conditions (`stopProofRearmPendingCondition`, `stopProofRearmDueAt`, `stopProofRearmDueCondition`) and `retainedHoldFallbackEligible`. `retainedWorkspaceCandidates` (worker pass) and `nextWorkspaceRecoveryAt` use them, so the recovery worker wakes when a re-arm is due.
- fix(issues): let the board confirm an unidentified run stopped instead of an endless Retry wait (#20310)
  > - `server/src/services/native-local-process-stop.ts`: new `confirmUnidentifiedLocalRunnerStopped`. It accepts only a finished native run of the task with no recorded process and whose latest launch evidence is an unconfirmed launch on this host in a local environment. It still refuses while a runner with `--run-id <run>` is running. It records `native.local_process_stopped` with `basis: "operator_attested"` and the attesting user. That is the receipt the existing continuation gate already accepts.
  > - `server/src/routes/issues.ts`: `POST /issues/:id/recovery-actions/confirm-run-stopped` (`runId`, `stopEvidence` of 20 to 12,000 characters). It is board-only and requires issue read plus `runtime:confirm_owner_stop`, like the other stop attestations. It logs `issue.run_stop_confirmed` with the evidence, promotes deferred intents for the issue, and resumes each parked task Retry, so work starts at the click. Refusals return 409 with a code.
  > - `packages/shared`: `confirmRunStoppedSchema`, and the presentation user action `confirm_run_stopped`.
- fix(workspaces): preserve import verification across process restarts (#20311)
  > - Add optional opaque storage namespace identity. S3 binds it to the explicit endpoint, region, bucket, normalized prefix and addressing style. Implicit endpoints retain full verification.
  > - Save a versioned child-snapshot receipt only after canonical crypto, manifest and file restoration succeed and a second HEAD confirms the same strong ETag and size.
  > - Bind reuse to the complete snapshot reference, key material, child lineage, source operation and storage namespace. Require a fresh strong HEAD and canonical validation that the current secret provider can unwrap the saved snapshot key.
- fix(chat): tell overviews to state when the picture is from (#20301)
  > - `AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE` status-update guidance: when the agent describes what the company, a project or agents are doing now, it states a UTC clock time, such as "As of 15:26 UTC". It uses the latest message's timestamp unless a record it read gives a later time.
  > - A regression test checks that the directive contains this guidance.
- fix(chat): send a task whose automatic recovery stopped to the person's Retry (#20306)
  > - `execution-blocker.ts`: the text that is added when a person can retry is now `EXECUTION_BLOCKER_PERSON_RETRY_ACTION`: "A person can use Retry on this task, or send it a new message, to continue once the previous execution has stopped. Agents cannot retry it for them."
  > - `deferred-execution-wait.ts`: when admission records a new reason why a deferred request (such as a person's Retry) cannot start, the request's task now records an `issue.execution_waiting` activity. Before, admission saved the reason but published nothing that the UI listens to. After a Retry under a budget hold, the open task page stayed blank (no notice, no Retry) until someone reloaded it. The activity's live event refreshes the open page, which then shows the kept failure, Retry and "Retry is waiting for the budget ...". Callers inside a transaction (`heartbeat/queue.ts`) pass their post-commit publication list, so the event is published only after commit.
  > - `ExecutionBlockerNotice.tsx`: the task's own Retry has `data-testid="task-execution-retry"`.
- fix(recovery): settle a remote workspace save that stopped without finishing (#20291)
  > - `settleAbandonedRemoteSnapshotSave` (`recovery/workspace-finalize-attempt-settlement.ts`) settles a `remote_workspace_snapshot` row in `running` or `pending` only when all of these are true:
  > - The ownership registry wires it as the settle of `remote_snapshot_save`.
  > - In the same transaction it re-arms blocked fallbacks. For every open hold whose fallback check last refused with `workspace_operation_running` and whose source run used this save's workspace, it clears the check and wakes the workspace-recovery queue. A hold that had already spent its 12 checks (no next check) continues as well.
- fix(issues): make every Retry start work or say why (#20283)
  > - `server/src/services/task-retry.ts`: when the Retry coalesces into the task's latest run and that run is `scheduled_retry` (with no other Retry already active), the receipt returns `scheduledRetryRunId`.
  > - `server/src/routes/issues.ts`:
  > - `ui/src/hooks/useTaskRetry.ts`: new `taskRetryNotStartedReason`. When a Retry did not start work (stale, or a scheduled retry that is gate-suppressed, waiting, or gone), the hook returns that reason as its error. `ExecutionBlockerNotice` and `StalledDependencyNotice` already display it.
- fix(runner): stop a lost runnerd's provider and tools from a durable lineage journal (#20270)
  > - `process_lineage.rs`: runnerd journals each supervised process's lineage to `<runner state>/process-lineage/lineage-<runnerd pid>-<n>.json`. The journal holds the root, every member, and every group and session a member leads, each with its start time. The first record is written when the provider is spawned, before any tool can run. Later records are written by the existing 50 ms sampler only when the record changes. A teardown that proves the lineage gone removes the journal.
  > - `process_lineage/journal.rs` (new module): the on-disk format, the write rules and the reaper `reap_journals`. The reaper refuses when runnerd never journalled (no writer marker) or when runnerd is still alive. It restores each journal into the same `LineageState` rules, sends TERM and then KILL only to processes whose PID and start time still match, and reports `verified` only when none remain. A journal that it cannot read refuses the proof.
  > - Unsampled escapes: each record stores `sampled_through`, the newest process start that its read listed. The value is refreshed at most once a second while the membership is unchanged. The reaper refuses (`unattributed_orphan`) when a same-user orphan (parent PID 1) that is in no journal started after that point. Such a process can be a tool that started after the last read, so the reaper neither signals it nor ignores it.
- fix(runner): keep a Claude session resumable when its turn-end provider restart fails (#20285)
  > - `acpx_park_preparation.rs`:
  > - `acpx_provider_backend.rs`:
  > - Tests (`tests/support/acpx_turn_end_restart.rs`):
- fix(workspaces): preserve remote recovery holds during online import (#20298)
  > - Add a distinct remote-only preservation proof. Keep remote runs excluded from legacy export sources.
  > - Use the canonical source inspector and prepared-reference matcher. Bind the run, finalizer, action, task, accepted result, authority, allocation and prior committed snapshot.
  > - Acquire the held run's finalizer fence through the existing sorted lock path. Existing source-proof reinspection rejects changes before publication.
- fix(chat): hold a chat message at the agent's daily limit instead of dropping it (#20292)
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: add `conversationDeliveryHold`, which applies the same daily run/cost limit check as queue admission. The outbox returns `deferred` at the limit and writes no receipt, so the message stays owed. It reports the next UTC day start through a new optional `onDeferredDeadline` option.
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/service.ts`: stranded-work recovery passes its `onDeferredDeadline` to the outbox, so the held message is retried when the limit resets.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/queue.ts`: if the limit is reached while an outbox wake is in admission (a race), admission throws a 409 with `reason: heartbeat.daily_*` for `conversation-comment:` keys instead of writing a `skipped` receipt. The outbox already maps a matching 409 to `deferred`. Other wakes keep the current skip behavior.
- fix(recovery): verify cross-task warm sandbox handoffs and say plainly when recovery refuses (#20282)
  > - `retiredReusableLeasePredecessors` (`environment-lease-handoff.ts`) requires the same task only when the lease has no execution workspace. This matches the binding that `acquireLease` uses when it replaces a reusable lease.
  > - `inspectWorkspaceRecoverySource` (`workspace-recovery-source.ts`) checks each predecessor run and its finalization against the predecessor lease's own task, not the recovering task. A predecessor from another task is accepted only when both leases have the same execution workspace. All other checks stay: same agent, terminal run, no live controller or lease owner, a settled finalization, and a saved snapshot at an older epoch on the same allocation, workspace, and remote directory.
  > - The other callers of the chain walk (remote termination resolution and retired backup restore) get the same rule.
- fix(ui): keep responder avatars visible across stale execution outcomes (#20293)
  > - Record the previous intent identity. Its later resolution cannot acknowledge the new message unless the observed run belongs to that message.
  > - Pass the existing reopen request into shared submission state on both chat surfaces.
  > - Keep startup feedback through the previous terminal status. Restore ordinary completion handling once the reopened status arrives.
- fix(environments): honor stopped-task proof after warm preparation (#20289)
  > - Remove the startup-marker exclusion from the existing status-first path. Keep the existing shared confirmation check. It still requires `preparationStopped: true` for startup work.
  > - Keep stop followed by status when the first status lacks the required proof. Keep the startup drain, credential writeback, complete, and cleanup failure handling.
  > - Add retained-task coverage with the startup metadata that warm preparation saves. Cover a startup cleanup whose first status lacks preparation proof.
- fix(runner): allow warm release to drain after provider restart (#20290)
  > - Align the release drain timeout with the existing 30-second warm admission budget for every provider. Close paths keep their explicit remaining deadlines.
  > - Keep the existing immediate completion when the drain receipt proves settlement. Add no sleep, retry, provider branch or dispatch work.
  > - Add a real Runner regression with a fake ACPX sidecar whose replacement start takes 1.5 seconds. One release call must park the Runner, then a follow-up must claim the same Runner process.
- fix(recovery): let a new task wait for its manually paused assignee (#20281)
  > - `server/src/services/recovery/service.ts`: in `reconcileStrandedAssignedIssues`, skip a `todo` task with no run when its assignee is paused with a `manual` lifecycle hold. The existing rule above it already skips work whose run the pause cancelled. Other pause causes, such as a budget hold, keep the existing escalation.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/heartbeat-paused-assignee-new-task.test.ts`: a PostgreSQL test. The task stays `todo` with no comment and no run during two recovery sweeps. After resume it starts exactly once and finishes. A budget-held assignee still escalates.
- fix(chat): tell agents which CLI commands a person runs in their own terminal (#20267)
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: add a chat guidance paragraph for terminal and CLI requests. It names the supported commands for task creation, document download, run read and cancel, and context profiles. It asks for one command per line, real ids, and the `<your Paperclip URL>` placeholder.
  > - `cli/src/__tests__/chat-cli-guidance.test.ts`: a new test reads the guidance and resolves every `npx paperclipai` command against the registered commander commands. It fails on an unknown command, flag or missing argument.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/agent-conversations.test.ts`: check that the paragraph is in the directive and in the chat prompt.
- fix(server): give remote MCP tool calls a 30 second default deadline and record stream failure evidence (#19768)
  > - `server/src/services/remote-http-fetch.ts`: the default response timeout is exported as `DEFAULT_REMOTE_HTTP_RESPONSE_TIMEOUT_MS` (30,000 ms, unchanged value).
  > - `server/src/services/tool-gateway.ts`:
  > - `server/src/services/mcp-http.ts`: `readMcpHttpResponse` takes an `onProgress` callback. It reports bytes, chunks and events received, whether the matching response arrived, and whether the stream ended. As on master, the read returns as soon as the response with the matching id arrives.
- fix(chat): tell chat agents how to link a case page while Cases is enabled (#20265)
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: new `CASES_CHAT_DIRECTIVE`. It says what a case is, names the `/<prefix>/cases/<case identifier>` page, and asks for a Markdown link when a reply concerns a case.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/task-markdown.ts`: new `casesEnabled` input. A conversation prompt includes the directive under "Cases:" only when it is true.
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/task-context.ts`: sets `casesEnabled` from the `enableCases` experimental setting, the same way as `statusCardsEnabled`.
- fix(recovery): stop stall wakes from failing setup on a held workspace (#20273)
  > - `remote-workspace-writer-hold.ts`: add `remoteWorkspaceHoldRefusesStart`, the one rule for a hold that reservation refuses outright (unattributed writer, or a writer silent past the cutoff with no live owner fence).
  > - `workspace-preparation.ts`: use that rule instead of an inline copy, so reservation and wake senders cannot drift.
  > - `blocker-stall-wakeups.ts`: before waking a waiter, check for proof that its run would be refused. The proof is that the assignee's own latest run on the task failed setup with the unfinished-writer error, and the bound workspace hold still refuses a run. Then do not wake it; count it as deferred and re-check at most hourly. A different assignee (for example a controller-only agent that never uses the workspace), a later run, or a settled hold lets the wake through. Reservation already tried and failed the unchanged-writer release for that run, so the sweep does not repeat that proof.
- fix(recovery): return a settled restore hold's warm sandbox to reuse (#20278)
  > - `remote-workspace-writer-hold.ts`: add `remoteWorkspaceHoldRefusesStart`, the one rule for a hold that reservation refuses outright (unattributed writer, or a writer silent past the cutoff with no live owner fence).
  > - `workspace-preparation.ts`: use that rule instead of an inline copy, so reservation and wake senders cannot drift.
  > - `blocker-stall-wakeups.ts`: before waking a waiter, read its bound remote workspace hold. When the hold refuses a run, do not wake it; count it as deferred and re-check at most hourly. A workspace with an unchanged-release baseline is left to reservation, which can release it.
- fix(issues): refuse agent status changes that detach another run's live execution (#20271)
  > - `server/src/routes/issues.ts`: new `assertAgentStatusChangeKeepsLiveRunBound`. For an agent actor, it refuses a status change when `executionRunId` or `checkoutRunId` names a different run in the same company that is `queued` or `running`. It runs in `PATCH /issues/:id` after the final status and assignee are known (execution-policy transitions included) and before any run stop or write. It also runs in recovery resolution when the source status changes.
  > - The guard applies to any status other than `in_progress`, also to the current status, because the service clears the locks for those too.
  > - The write is conditional on the lock the guard checked. The route re-reads the lock and passes it to `svc.update` as `expectedExecutionLock`. The service compares it under the row lock and returns `409 issue_execution_lock_changed` if a checkout landed in between.
- fix(native): open a remote replacement as a new runner after archiving the reattached one (#20242)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts` `openReplacementSession`, on a `reattach_remote_runner` backend: archives the continuity state (local and remote, as before), then calls `createRunnerdBackendWithinSessionClaim` for the same run with the claim re-kinded to `bootstrap_incomplete` (minus `remote` and `stagedAttachment`) and without warm recovery, and opens the replacement on that backend. Root migration then quarantines the empty archived root (the #20241 rule) and every launch input resolves fresh.
  > - A backend that adopts only through warm recovery (no claim to re-kind) throws `native_runner_replacement_requires_fresh_launch` **before** archiving, so its saved state stays intact.
  > - Tests: a classic Daytona reattach backend for `codex` and `acpx` (Claude). The replacement comes from a second backend: the original backend's `openSession` is never called, and the new transport has no adopted runner, uses `turn-<runId>`/`item-<runId>`, has a new still-pending spawn barrier, and (for `acpx`) carries the packaged provider paths. The bundled-image replacement test now expects a new backend and its own pack admission, not the recovered session's cold admission.
- fix(workspaces): stop running Paperclip's worktree setup before every agent turn (#20269)
  > - Rename `scripts/provision-worktree.sh` to `scripts/setup-paperclip-worktree.sh`. The content is unchanged.
  > - `scripts/provision-worktree-runtime.sh` calls the new name when the worktree has no config.
  > - Tests read the real script from its new path (`provision-worktree-self-heal`, `workspace-runtime`, `worktree-seed-server-spawn`). Server fixtures still copy it into their test repositories as `scripts/provision-worktree.sh`, so the harness default stays covered.
- fix(chat): send a task-tree pause to the person's Pause subtree control (#20261)
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: add one paragraph to the chat directive. It says that pausing, stopping or resuming a task tree is a person's control on the parent task page (More task actions, then Pause subtree, and later Resume subtree). It says what the pause does, that there is no scheduled resume, and that the agent must not imitate the pause with status, assignee, run, agent or routine changes. The agent links the parent task and replies.
  > - `server/src/__tests__/agent-conversations.test.ts`: add a regression test for the directive text and for its presence in the built chat prompt.
- fix(chat): name the managed-profile CLI and the dev-only labs in agent guidance (#20236)
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: the GET and POST managed-agent-profile routes now say that no web page exists. The POST description now names `paperclipai managed-agent setup --company-id <companyId> --profile-key <key> --display-name <name> --api-key-secret-id <secretId> --acknowledge-retention`, the `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` requirement, the `--probe` option and the Secrets page.
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: the chat directive has a new short paragraph. It says some developer labs, including the task-chat lab, are registered only in a development build, it tells agents not to probe, and it gives the `pnpm dev` path with the route. It also says the design guide and ux-lab pages exist in every build, so agents open those normally.
  > - Tests for both texts in `openapi-routes.test.ts` and `agent-conversations.test.ts`.
- fix(native): require publication for files named under a Deliverable label (#20231)
  > - `explicitlyRequestsFileOutput` now also returns true when a `Deliverable:` / `Deliverables` label line (plain, bold or heading), or a bullet or numbered item in the list below it (blank lines between items allowed), names a file. Relative paths such as `dist/index.html` count.
  > - Under the label, web page files (`.html`, `.htm`, `.css`) count with the existing file types.
  > - Items that name an input ("a summary of input.csv", "a comparison of input.csv and baseline.csv"), describe a repository change, pull request, commit or branch, forbid the named file (`do not create report.pdf`), name a file to edit in place (`update styles.css`) without asking to publish it, send the file inline (`index.html and styles.css inline in the reply`), or forbid creating files, do not count. An inline or no-upload instruction about another output in the same item (`index.html and a short summary in the reply`, `index.html; do not upload it externally`) does not cancel the named file. The repository-change check ignores file names, so `branch-report.pdf` still counts.
- fix(recovery): continue a run refused by an inert cleanup quarantine with no click (#20260)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: new `verifyStoppedNativeSessionForAutomaticReplacement`. It returns the existing replacement proof, or, for a `native_session_cleanup_quarantined` run with no replacement proof, the continuation proof that the board Retry already uses (Codex and ACPX, including an inherited owner run).
  > - `server/src/index.ts`: the safe-replacement sweep uses that verifier.
  > - The existing sweep then retires the quarantine, records `native.stopped_text_turn_verified` and queues a successor with `forceFreshSession: true` at the failed run's attempt number, so no attempt is charged.
- fix(accounting): settle a native run that only inherited its predecessor's runner identity as no provider work (#20250)
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/runtime-selection.ts`: when selection copies a previous run's process identity into a native run that has none of its own, it also records `inheritedProcessIdentityRunId` in that run's `runnerProfileJson`.
  > - `server/src/services/run-cost-accounting.ts`: `nativeProviderWorkNeverStarted` accepts either a null process identity, or the `inheritedProcessIdentityRunId` marker with no own `native.process_start_requested` / `native.process_identity_recorded` event. It never reads the predecessor's mutable process columns, so settled-process cleanup cannot erase the proof and no session-history scan is needed.
  > - Tests:
- fix(accounting): a run's unproven accounting never holds the company (#20254)
  > - `run-cost-accounting.ts`: a verified local process stop closes the receipt source for `cancelled`, `timed_out` and `interrupted` runs as well as `failed` ones. The inherited-pid run (0b4b404f) is covered by #20250, so this PR does not change the never-started rule.
  > - `budget-exposure.ts`: a final charge that accounting settled without its receipt (receipt unavailable, closure timed out, capture failed, or lost on restore) never trips the blocking unpriced-usage policy. Unpriced usage from a complete receipt still does. A capture failure stays exempt even if a later report adds usage, because its receipt is never certified complete. This removes the restart-derived special case, because the rule now covers every accounting-settled charge.
  > - `task-interruption-presentation.ts`: a `budget.blocked` wait caused by the unpriced-usage refusal is shown as `accounting.unpriced_usage`, with accounting wording, for both a held Retry and a never-started task. Only the fixed refusal phrase is matched; the stored message is never shown.
- fix(api-catalog): mark task-tree controls board-only and name the Pause subtree page (#20252)
  > - Added the six tree-control operations (`tree-control/state`, `tree-control/preview`, `tree-holds` list/create/get, `tree-holds/{holdId}/release`) to `BOARD_ONLY_OPERATIONS`.
  > - Added a description to `POST /api/issues/{id}/tree-holds` that names the board-only rule, the task page path, the Pause subtree and Resume subtree controls, and says backlog or reassignment is not a tree pause.
  > - Added an `openapi-routes.test.ts` case for the board-only authorization and the description.
- fix(chat): name the addressee on human-only card denials and list team requirements (#20245)
  > - `server/src/services/issue-thread-interaction-resolution.ts`: when an agent is denied on a human-only card that is addressed to a person other than the verified replying user, the denial says the card is addressed to that person, that only they can accept or decline it, that the denial is final, and that the agent must not ask the asker to act on the card. The details include `addresseeUserId`. Cards without an addressee keep the old message.
  > - `server/src/routes/issues.ts`: the route adds the addressee's display name to that denial (`details.addresseeName` and the message). If the name lookup fails, the generic addressee text stays.
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: the teams catalog paragraph asks agents to name a recommended team's roles (`agentSlugs`), every required skill (`requiredSkills`, by ref) and every configuration input (`envInputs`), and to say that a listing does not mean accounts are configured.
- fix(runner): record a runner exit mid-turn as runner loss, and stop the lineage sampler panicking (#20259)
  > - `nativeSessionFailureSourceCode` returns `native_runner_process_exited` for a `NativeProviderTerminalFailure` with code `notification_transport_failed` when its message starts with `Notification transport failed closed: native_runner_process_exited: `. Only the runner transport writes that prefix. Other closed-stream causes stay `native_provider_terminal_failed`.
  > - `paperclip-process-table` has a new `session(pid)` function. Linux reads the session field of `/proc/<pid>/stat`. macOS calls `getsid`. A session of 0 or an error gives `None`.
  > - `process_lineage` reads sessions through `paperclip_process_table::session` instead of rustix `getsid`.
- fix(workspaces): close a retained local task directory as archived, not cleanup_failed (#20248)
  > - `cleanupExecutionWorkspaceArtifacts` reports `cleaned: true` for a `local_fs` directory that the runtime did not create. It never removes that directory, so nothing is left to clean.
  > - Added `server/src/__tests__/workspace-cleanup-retained-local-dir.test.ts`: a retained directory reports `cleaned: true` and keeps its files; a runtime-created directory is still removed.
- fix(runner): fail closed with a recorded cause when runner event commits stall (#20235)
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/control-plane/durable-prp-control-plane.ts`:
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/contracts/native-session-backend.ts`: new reason `runner_event_commit_stalled`, with an optional `detail` in the message.
- fix(chat): tell chat agents what a status card is and to reply now after queuing one (#20230)
  > - Add `STATUS_CARDS_CHAT_DIRECTIVE` in `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`. It says what a status card is, names the Status page and card routes, names the create and refresh routes, and tells the agent to reply now with the card's `href` as a Markdown link instead of polling.
