  > - Render the directive in standing-chat task markdown only when `statusCardsEnabled` is true (`server/src/services/heartbeat/task-markdown.ts`). Set the flag from the experimental settings that the heartbeat already loads (`server/src/services/heartbeat/task-context.ts`). No new query runs before the turn starts.
  > - Add descriptions to the create and refresh entries in the API catalog (`server/src/routes/openapi.ts`). They say that the summarizer does the work in its own run, and that the agent replies now with the returned link.
- fix(chat): point agents at the teams catalog for ready-made teams (#20239)
  > - `server/src/services/agent-conversations.ts`: the chat directive has a new short paragraph. It says that ready-made teams are packages in the teams catalog. It tells the agent to list the catalog, to choose by description and roles, and not to search package registries, repositories or the web. It names the preview route and says that preview creates no resources. It says who can install. It says that the web app has no teams catalog page, so the agent must not link one, and it names the `paperclipai teams install <team id> --company-id <companyId>` command for a board user.
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: `GET /api/teams/catalog` now has a description and documents its `q`, `kind` and `category` query parameters. The preview route says that it creates no resources. The install route says who can install and names the CLI command.
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/runner-api-catalog.ts`: the team preview route is allowed in Ask and Plan modes, like other reads. It computes a plan and saves no data. Install stays in standard mode only.
- fix(runner): answer held Paperclip tool calls after a local restart before the next turn (#20227)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-session-executor.ts`: the runnerd transport now always gets `recoverSemanticToolInput`, not only when `controllerPersistence` (remote) is set. It uses the same epoch check and `SessionAuthorityEpoch.recover`. Recovery re-authorizes and re-runs reads, and replays receipts for mutations; it never repeats an uncertain mutation.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/live/runnerd-codex-transport.ts` (`#startTurnWork`): when the control plane has unsettled semantic inputs, it runs `recoverPendingSemanticInputs()` before queueing `turn.start`, and waits for that pass, bounded by the turn-start deadline. Recovered results are queued as commands ahead of `turn.start`, and the Runner applies commands in order. If recovery has not finished by the deadline, the turn start fails with `native_turn_start_held_tool_calls_unresolved` instead of sending `turn.start` behind it. When everything is settled there is no wait, so a normal turn start is unchanged. Live calls of a turn being replaced are not waited on; the replaced start still rejects them.
  > - `packages/paperclip-runner/src/control-plane/durable-prp-control-plane.ts`: `recoverPendingSemanticInputs()` passes are serialized. A call made while the startup pass is still answering a held call waits for that pass, then runs its own, so the turn-start gate cannot finish early by skipping a call that is already being recovered.
- fix(native): take over a remote runner at once when its old controller is proven gone (#20240)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-restart-recovery.ts`, `evaluateNativeControllerTakeover` with `remoteLeaseFenced`:
  > - `native-restart-recovery.test.ts`: a new test, red on master, covers:
- fix(recovery): bootstrap a remote follow-up again when a restart cut it off after retiring its finished owner (#20241)
  > - `native-restart-recovery.ts`: the `remote_runner_never_launched` proof also holds when the run carries only its continued session. That means a session checkpoint with a matching identity and no projected provider event, active turn, semantic result, terminal or pending runtime request, plus the predecessor's `sessionIdBefore`. The proof still requires no `native.process_start_requested`, no controller checkpoint for this run, no runner process identity and no `sessionIdAfter`. The classifier then sees no checkpoint for this run.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: a `bootstrap_incomplete` claim over a root with no runner identity and no controller state quarantines that root and bootstraps again for a remote target too. Previously only a local target with no live runner process could do this. Such a root was never authenticated by a controller on this host.
  > - Tests: four classifier cases (restart; a requested runner start, a pending runtime request and a projected provider event stay held). Two executor cases (created and replacement sandbox) restart the exact state the kill left after the retire.
- fix(runner): drop a tool target path that breaks a line so the controller can acknowledge its event (#20238)
  > - `runner-core/src/tool_file_changes.rs`: new `is_single_line_path`, which refuses control characters and U+2028/U+2029 (the characters ECMAScript `.` does not match). `normalize_relative` uses it, so file-change paths are covered too.
  > - `runner-core/src/provider_events.rs`: `safe_acpx_location` uses the same check (it replaces the NUL-only check).
  > - `src/drivers/acpx/safe-locations.ts`: the sidecar drops a location whose path contains a control character or U+2028/U+2029.
- fix(recovery): settle a local workspace command whose server process died mid-command (#20233)
  > - `workspace-operations.ts`: a local command phase (`worktree_prepare`, `workspace_config_freshness`, `workspace_provision`, `workspace_seed`, `workspace_runtime_provision`, `workspace_repair`, `workspace_teardown`, `worktree_cleanup`) without a runtime-control stamp now records `commandOwner` (the controller process stamp). This process tracks the operation as live while it runs. Finalize attempts and command operations share one live-id set.
  > - `workspace-finalize-attempt-settlement.ts`: the finalize settle is now a factory. A second instance settles abandoned command operations with `abandonedCommand` provenance and an activity entry. Both instances skip a row with a runtime-control stamp.
  > - `ownership-registry.ts`: new owner-gone-attestable kind `workspace_command_operation`. The owner is the `commandOwner` process. A row without a stamp is owned by its run.
- fix(chat): make task-not-found, unreachable-MCP and board denials final for agents (#20208)
  > - `GET /api/issues/:id`: for an agent, the 404 body adds `code: "issue_not_visible"` and a `remediation`. It says that the lookup is final, that the agent must not search other routes or open a similarly named task, and that it should reply now and offer sharing or pasting. A missing task, a private task and another company's task get the same body, so the body reveals nothing. People and other routes keep the plain body.
  > - MCP health check and catalog refresh: when Paperclip cannot reach the server (connection refused, DNS, network, reset, timeout or TLS), an agent's error adds a `remediation`. It says the person must correct the URL or start the server, that the agent must not contact other hosts, ports or network addresses, and it links Connectors (`/<prefix>/apps`). Status, `error` and `code` do not change.
  > - `AGENT_CHAT_DIRECTIVE`: after a board-only denial, the agent replies now instead of making more calls. "Complete other permitted work" is removed.
- fix(secrets): delete a secret that a binding proposal referenced (#20193)
  > - `packages/db`: `company_secret_proposals.secret_id` now uses `ON DELETE CASCADE`, the same as `secret_proposal_id`. Migration `0452_secret_proposal_secret_cascade.sql` replaces the foreign key (`DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS`, then `ADD CONSTRAINT`).
  > - `server/src/services/secrets.ts`: `remove` accepts an optional `beforeDelete` hook. The hook runs after the secret is marked deleted and before its row is removed.
  > - `server/src/services/secret-proposals.ts`: new `rejectPendingBindingsForSecret`. It rejects pending binding proposals for a secret with a reason and returns them. This closes their review cards and writes the normal activity entries. `createBinding` now holds the secret row `FOR SHARE` in its insert transaction and refuses a deleted secret. So no new pending proposal can start after the delete marks the secret deleted.
- fix(native): an inherited checkpoint with a pending runtime request is evidence (#20226)
  > - `native-restart-recovery.ts`: `admissionCheckpointOnly` additionally requires `pendingRuntimeRequests` to be absent or empty.
  > - `native-runner-restart-recovery.integration.test.ts`: a held case, an unclaimed remote follow-up whose inherited checkpoint carries a pending runtime request.
- fix(chat): post a turn's answer when a card it did not create is pending (#20220)
  > - `server/src/services/native-runtime/native-governance.ts`: `conversationTurnAnswered()` now checks only pending cards whose `sourceRunId` is the turn's run. `pendingNativeInteraction()` gets an optional `createdByRun` filter for this. The status gate (`pendingNativeGovernance()`) does not change, so another person's card still holds the task.
  > - Both callers get the fix through the one shared rule `conversationTurnPresentsAnswer()`: live run completion (`run-completion.ts`) and background presentation recovery (`recoverCommittedNativeChatResponse`).
  > - `server/src/services/heartbeat/run-completion.test.ts`: new live cases "someone else's confirmation card" (board-created, human-only, addressed to another user, no source run) and "earlier turn's question card" (created by an earlier run of the same agent). Each case now also checks that the conversation moves to `waiting`.
- fix(native): roll back a warm rotation cut off before its new control plane once its runner is proven dead (#20218)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`:
  > - `native-recovered-owner-followup.test.ts`: #20146's rotation test adds the "cut off before its new control plane" shape. With a dead-runner claim it is admitted from the restored previous owner; without the claim it still fails closed.
- fix(native): restart an unclaimed remote follow-up that carries only its inherited checkpoint (#20214)
  > - `native-restart-recovery.ts`:
  > - `native-inherited-runner-retry.ts` / `native-restart-recovery.ts`: a warm continuation carries its settled predecessor's runner process identity until it launches its own runner, and t26's row still held pid 596 at the restart.
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`: `restorePerTurnRunnerdController` now also runs for a `bootstrap_incomplete` claim, but only when the root's reference identity belongs to a previous run. A claim on the run's own root returns early, as before.
- fix(native-runtime): a stopped run cannot close a task another run continues; deliver a pre-restart comment at once (#20216)
  > - `status-arbiter.ts`:
  > - `native-run-finalizer.ts` and `native-status-reassessment.ts` pass the run id to that guard.
  > - `status-decision-committer.ts` rechecks the same rule under the issue lock: a done decision for a non-conversation issue held by another run raises `NativeStatusRaceError`. The finalizer then re-arbitrates and keeps status.
- fix(daytona): wait out a sandbox state change before starting it for workspace realize (#20212)
  > - `packages/plugins/sandbox-providers/daytona/src/plugin.ts`: `ensureSandboxStarted` now polls `refreshData` while the sandbox is in a transitional state.
  > - `plugin.test.ts`:
- fix(authz): require board authority for agent budget changes on every route (#20213)
  > - `PATCH /api/agents/:id`: a changed `budgetMonthlyCents` requires a board actor. An unchanged amount with other edits still works.
  > - Configuration rollback: a rollback that changes the budget requires a board actor.
  > - `POST /api/companies/:id/agents` and an immediate `agent-hires`: a positive budget requires a board actor. A hire that needs board approval keeps the proposed budget and does not activate it before approval.
- fix(native): retire a finished remote owner whose suspend never finished once its sandbox leases ended (#20211)
  > - `native-session-executor.ts`:
  > - `native-recovered-owner-followup.test.ts`: a red/green test with lease variants. A lease that ended or was released on another sandbox is admitted. A retained lease, an ended lease on the same sandbox, or no lease record stays held.
- fix(guidance): tell agents who acts and which page to link for person-only steps (#20206)
  > - `server/src/routes/openapi.ts`: add catalog descriptions that say who acts, link the page, and say not to search further:
  > - `server/src/services/connection-intents.ts`:
  > - `server/src/routes/chat-channels.ts`: `POST /api/companies/{companyId}/chat-endpoints` returns 403 with the Experimental settings page while the provider's experiment is off (Chat connectors, or GitHub review bots for GitHub). AgentMail is unchanged. The catalog entry says the same. The UI already hides the setup pages in this state.
- fix(runner): keep discarded OpenCode token deltas from delaying tool calls (#20209)
  > - `shouldForwardOpenCodeProxyItem` now takes the event type and payload. For `item.delta`, it forwards only assistant text (`agentMessage` or `text`), which runnerd maps to `item/agentMessage/delta`.
  > - The proxy keeps reasoning and tool-output token deltas. Completed reasoning and tool items still go to runnerd as `item/completed`. Tool progress still goes as `item/updated`.
  > - New regression test: a step with 1,000 streamed reasoning and tool-output tokens puts the same number of frames before the tool call request as a step with 1 token, and no `item/delta` frame is written.

## Other changes

- Measure database and projection costs in runtime tool discovery (#22347)
  > - Add a transaction span with children for the entry read and the matched-connection read.
  > - Attach the two returned row counts to that transaction.
  > - Time availability checks and descriptor construction under the existing catalog discovery span.
- chore(lockfile): refresh pnpm lockfiles (#22231)
  > - Refresh the dependency lock from the manifests on master.
  > - Refresh the sandbox provider lockfiles from their manifests on master.
  > - Include the matching offline report correction and hashes.
- refactor(wake-queue): own blocked admission deferral (#22332)
  > - Added a transaction-bound deferral operation to wake-queue recovery. It handles existing receipts, saved input, and replaceable system signals.
  > - Scoped retry updates to the company, issue, agent, deferred status, and receipt ID. A stale receipt cannot confirm admission or cause a new receipt to be inserted.
  > - Kept case refusals and issue state callbacks in heartbeat. A saved comment uses the existing delivery worker and post-commit effects.
- perf(wake-queue): skip detailed recovery reads on tasks without history (#22290)
  > - Preserve the independent ownership and lease check.
  > - Use the existing detailed recovery logic when history exists. Include resolved records in the existence check.
  > - Run the probe after acquiring the issue lock, in its own statement. Cover both orderings of a concurrent recovery insert and a record moved onto the issue.
- refactor(wake-queue): enforce recovery before task admission (#22292)
  > - Bind the recovery read to the issue and transaction selected by wake-queue. Read the current issue lock again after durable chat authorization when required.
  > - Expose the busy writer after the preliminary continuation check passes. Expose the fresh writer after the transaction applies any required continuation.
  > - Preserve heartbeat’s existing ordering for system fallback, budget and daily limits, task retry, and durable receipts.
- perf(wake-queue): preflight task admission without loading execution context (#22289)
  > - Give heartbeat a preflight decision. Keep storage and the five-field query inside wake-queue.
  > - Reuse the conversation and retry predicates for all callers. Keep the locked recheck after preflight, including durable chat authorization.
  > - Supply the task's responsible user, parent and routine origin from the current locked row. A company-only admission supplies no issue.
- perf(heartbeat): skip unused session and holder reads for queued input (#22282)
  > - Keep explicit resume lookup early because it determines task scope.
  > - Use the stored execution key if present. Scope the fallback agent lookup to the company.
  > - When binding an existing run, use the same normalized key for the issue write and the coalescing decision.
- perf(wake-queue): reuse the task row locked by admission (#22280)
  > - Return the admission projection from the row lock. Repeat the locking read after durable authorization or an initial miss.
  > - Use the task projection only for the same company and task. Keep every recovery-action, ownership and lease read.
  > - Cover changes inside durable authorization and a concurrent insert after the initial lock missed.
- perf(wake-queue): skip recovery lookup when no saved receipt can be adopted (#22279)
  > - Query the same receipt scope under the existing issue lock.
  > - Keep the handoff owner, receipt order, and reconstruction exception.
  > - Exercise empty and nonempty exact scopes with an active recovery hold and verify the lock from a competing database session.
- Use one automatic goal steering decision for task comments (#22277)
  > - Keep the assignee, self-comment, and external-conversation guards together with the active goal decision.
  > - Preserve the comment-derived command ID, the route's selected agent, and completion-error reporting.
  > - Drive both HTTP routes with a single comment through success, unavailable transport, and rejected completion.
- Admit explicit queued steering with durable acknowledgement in wake-queue (#22276)
  > - Use the shared board queue read and lock for pending messages and acknowledgement replay.
  > - Keep the target run available while waiting for the provider. Re-read and lock the scoped run after acknowledgement and preserve concurrent result updates.
  > - Leave HTTP access, activity logging, response redaction and error translation with the route.
- refactor: share the board queue snapshot and lock in wake-queue (#22273)
  > - Move the existing board snapshot and lock into a Postgres adapter.
  > - Use the shared reader from GET, Interrupt and Steer, including acknowledgement replay.
- Own the issue admission lock and dispatch after commit in wake-queue (#22272)
  > - Bind the busy and fresh admission operations to a single company and issue in Postgres. Remove the transaction scope object.
  > - Keep specialized heartbeat gates and continuation writes on the acquired transaction.
  > - Buffer the workspace preflight activity publication until the transaction commits.
- Admit saved task comments with the fresh issue run (#22268)
  > - Keep exact resumed receipts separate from ordinary board comments. Adopt a prior assignee’s comments only when the interrupted run proves a handoff.
  > - Use the same canonical comment ID codecs for admission and dispatch.
  > - Test the assignment path with one or two saved comments and independent chat and interaction receipts.
- Move fresh issue run and receipt writes into wake-queue (#22266)
  > - Move fresh receipt, run, and binding writes to a transaction-bound Postgres adapter.
  > - Keep explicit continuation and reconciled retry lineage in the application layer.
  > - Scope receipt updates by company and durable promotion by agent. Cover both predicates with a database test.
- Fix project sidebar navigation and pinned task expansion (#22264)
  > - Open recent and pinned projects at `/projects/:ref/issues`.
  > - Share the project task disclosure between both lists. Load at most 12 tasks when expanded, ordered by latest update.
  > - Request full task rows with read state. Preserve monitoring status, unread markers, and task navigation prefetch.
- Let plugin connectors declare their tool timeout (#22249)
  > - `packages/shared`: `timeoutMs` is a new optional field on `ConnectionMethodDef` and on `PluginConnectorToolDef`. The validator accepts integers from 1000 to 60000 only. It rejects `timeoutMs` on methods that do not use `transport: "plugin"`. New exported constants: `PLUGIN_CONNECTOR_TOOL_TIMEOUT_MIN_MS`, `PLUGIN_CONNECTOR_TOOL_TIMEOUT_MAX_MS`, and `PLUGIN_CONNECTOR_TOOL_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS` (30000).
  > - `server/src/services/plugin-connector-runtime.ts`: `pluginConnectionToolTimeoutMs()` resolves the timeout in this order: the tool's value, then the method's value, then 30 s. The worker `executeTool` RPC now gets the gateway timeout plus 1 s. Thus the gateway's `tool_timeout` error is what callers see, and the 30 s RPC default no longer cuts off a longer declared timeout.
  > - `server/src/services/tool-gateway.ts`: `executePluginConnectionTool` now applies the timeout itself, after it loads the connection. All three plugin paths use it: session, assigned-tool and direct calls (`executeTool`), test calls (`executeTestCall`), and approved action execution. If a caller sends `timeoutMs`, that value wins, as before. It is still clamped to 60 s. Approved actions keep their fixed 60 s budget.
- test(release-smoke): follow onboarding into the standing chat (#22259)
  > - Expect the launch to land on `/chats/<lead agent id>`, not on `/issues/`.
  > - Assert that the task list and the project list are empty after launch.
  > - Assert that exactly one chat exists, with `originKind: "onboarding_chat"`, no project, and the lead as `conversationAgentId`.
- Fix sidebar Recents task order and archive visibility (#22251)
  > - Render task, agent chat, and project rows directly in Standard Recents. Keep the existing controls to pin each kind of row.
  > - Render standalone task rows with the existing Classic action menus. Remove sidebar child loading and refresh code.
  > - Derive the viewer’s archive state using the existing inbox rule. Filter archived tasks out of Recents while retaining authorized pinned shortcuts.
- docs(release): canonicalize stable notes for v2026.1117.0 (#22247)
  > - `releases/beta/v2026.1110.0-beta.0.md` is renamed to `releases/v2026.1117.0.md`. Git reports a 100% rename
  > - No line of the file changes. The blob is `dff612e41f915a5a2d9f3e572142e2fee56068a8`, the same blob as `releases/v2026.1117.0.md` at the release source commit `c0f132898d2a2677312d8c72096187b27a3d9a38` (tag `v2026.1117.0`)
  > - This pull request does not change the release workflow
- Show live provider reset countdowns in the viewer's timezone (#22225)
  > - Add an optional timezone label to the existing `formatDateTime` helper. Existing callers keep their output.
  > - Share relative and exact local time formatting between provider-limit messages and the usage popover. Unrelated text and invalid timestamps stay intact.
  > - Reuse the existing shared one-second ticker. Countdown updates pause in hidden tabs, catch up when shown, and stop at the deadline or on unmount. No API polling is added.

  > - Include the matching offline report correction and hashes.

- Add bulk keyed issue document reads for native helpers (#22105)
  > - Add a bulk keyed document route and a company-scoped document reader. Reuse the current batched task-read policy and completed-task read receipts.
  > - Preserve request order and deduplicate IDs. Return `document: null` for readable tasks without the key. Omit unreadable, hidden, foreign and missing tasks without revealing which exist.
  > - Bound requests to 200 IDs and responses to 8 MiB of JSON bytes. Check encoded body sizes in SQL before fetching bodies in the same read snapshot. Build JSON within the byte budget. Oversized responses fail with 413 and are never saved as partial artifacts.
- Start onboarding in chat and defer execution until the user chooses work (#22077)
  > - Finish onboarding in agent chat for default and team-template companies. Create no execution task or project.
  > - Queue one live welcome after visible chat content paints. Pass company goals and the initial idea as context. Preserve access, budget, and hiring controls.
  > - Instruct the agent to gather a goal and exit criteria, offer an interview, and propose tasks for acceptance. Allow direct creation on an explicit user request.

Synchronize `AWS_CI_ROUTING_USER_IDS_SOURCE` and `AWS_CI_ROUTING_USER_IDS`. The other three users were already listed. This settings change is complete; the protected master owner check still controls test execution.

- perf(workspaces): hold kept plugin workspaces instead of re-inspecting them every sweep (#22054)
  > - `server/src/services/terminal-workspace-retry.ts`: add the `terminalInspectionHold` workspace metadata record (generation, fingerprint, reason, HEAD, check count, check time, next check time). The write is fenced on the lifecycle generation. Add the recheck delay: 1 hour, doubling, capped at 24 hours.
  > - `server/src/services/execution-workspaces.ts` (`sweepTerminalWorkspaces`):
  > - Core owns the new metadata key. Workspace metadata updates keep it, like `terminalCleanupRetry`.
- perf(runner): start ACPX once on parked checkpoint relaunch (#22064)
  > - Keep a relaunched park within the existing host claim command fence.
  > - Load a settled provider checkpoint that qualifies for host parking without opening the provider during readiness or polling.
  > - Open the provider with the incoming run's settings. Verify the session identity and that it has no active work before publishing the new provider checkpoint.
- Expose continuation retry keys and preserve account-repair resumes (#22046)
  > - Express the single provider continuation condition as direct `if` and `then` fields. This is equivalent to its previous `allOf` wrapper.
  > - Keep the canonical output schema unchanged. Keep required retry keys and case handoff IDs.

  > - Refresh the dependency lock from the manifests on master.
